gautamapraṇītadharmasūtrāṇi
haradattakṛtamitākṣarāvṛttisahitāni
tatra prathamapraśne prathamo ‘dhyāyaḥ
namo rudrāya
yad dharmaśāstraṃ gautamanirmitam |
kriyate haradattena tasya vṛttir mitākṣarā ||
vedo dharmamūlam || 1.1 ||
karmajanyo ‘bhyudayaniḥśreyasahetur apūrvākhya ātmaguṇo dharmaḥ | tasya mūlaṃ pramāṇam | vedo mantrabrāhmaṇātmakaḥ | jātāv ekavacanam | catvāro vedā ṛgyajuḥsāmātmakās ta eva dharme pramāṇam | na yogipratyakṣaṃ nānumānaṃ nārthāpattir na śākyādyāgamaḥ | tena tanmūlā evopanayanādayo dharmā vakṣyante na caityavandanakeśolluñcanādaya iti | dharmagrahaṇam upalakṣaṇam | adharmasyāpi pratiṣedhātmako veda eva mūlam | niṣedhavidhayo hi brahmahatyādau viṣaye pravṛttaṃ nivartayanti | na ca rāgadveṣādinā viṣaye pravṛttas tato nivartayituṃ śakyaḥ | yady asau viṣayo ‘nuṣṭhitaḥ pratyavāyahetur na syād iti niṣadhavidhir eva pratyavāyahetutāṃ gamayati || 1.1 ||
atha yatra pratyakṣo vedo mūlabhūto nopapadyate tatra katham |
tadvidāṃ ca smṛtiśīle || 1.2 ||
tadvidāṃ vedavidāṃ manvādīnāṃ yā smṛtis tatpraṇītaṃ dharmaśāstraṃ yac ca teṣāṃ śīlam anuṣṭhānaṃ te smṛtiśīle asmadādīnāṃ pramāṇam | na ca teṣām anuṣṭhānaṃ nirmūlaṃ saṃbhavati | saṃbhavati ca vaidikānām utsannapāṭhe vedānubhava iti | teṣāṃ tu tadānīṃ vidyamānatvena saṃpradāyāvicchedāc ca vaidikānuṣṭhānaṃ vedamūlam eva | yathāha āpastambaḥ: “teṣām utsannāḥ pāṭhāḥ prayogād anumīyante” iti || 1.2 ||
yadi śīlaṃ pramāṇam atiprasaṅgaḥ syāt | katham | katakabharadvājau vyatyasya bhārye jagmatuḥ; vasiṣṭhaś caṇḍālīm akṣamālām; prajāpatiḥ svāṃ duhitaram; rāmeṇa pitṛvacanād avicāreṇa mātuḥ śiraś chinnam – ityādisāhasam api pramāṇam syāt | nety āha –
dṛṣṭo dharmavyatikramaḥ sāhasaṃ ca mahatām || 1.3 ||
mahatām etādṛśaṃ sāhasam api dharmavyatikrama eva dṛṣṭo na tu dharmaḥ, rāgadveṣanibandhanatvāt || 1.3 ||
na ca teṣām evaṃvidhaṃ dṛṣṭam ity etāvatāsmadādīnām api prasaṅgaḥ | kutaḥ –
avaradaurbalyāt || 1.4 ||
avareṣām asmadādīnāṃ durbalatvāt | tathā ca śrūyate |
teṣāṃ tejoviśeṣeṇa pratyavāyo na vidyate |
tad anvīkṣya prayuñjānaḥ sīdaty avarako janaḥ || iti || 1.4 ||
atha yatra dve viruddhe tulyabale pramāṇe upanipatataḥ | yathā “atirātre ṣoḍaśinaṃ gṛhṇāti, nātirātre ṣoḍaśinaṃ gṛhṇāti | udite juhoty anudite juhoti” iti śrutiḥ | “nityam abhojyaṃ keśakīṭāvapannam” [GDh 17.8–9] iti gautamaḥ |
pakṣijagdhaṃ gavāghrātam avadhūtam avakṣatam |
keśakīṭāvapannaṃ ca mṛtprakṣepeṇa śudhyati || [MDh 5.125]
iti manuḥ | tatra kiṃ kartavyam |
tulyabalavirodhe vikalpaḥ || 1.5 ||
tulyapramāṇaprāpitayor evaṃjātīyakayor arthayor vikalpaḥ | tad vedaṃ vety anyatarasvīkāraḥ | na samuccayo ‘saṃbhavāt | prakarṣabodhane tu śrutismṛtivirodhe smṛtyartho nādaraṇīyaḥ, atulyabalatvāt | ata eva jābālir āha |
śrutismṛtivirodhe tu śrutir eva garīyasī |
avirodhe sadā kāryaṃ smārtaṃ vaidikavat sadā || iti || 1.5 ||
athedānīṃ darmān vakṣyann upanayanapūrvakatvāt teṣām upanayanaṃ tāvad āha |
upanayanaṃ brāhmaṇasyāṣṭame || 1.6 ||
upanayanānantarabhāvini brāhmaṇatve ‘tra [brāhmaṇagrahaṇam] | brāhmaṇagrahaṇaṃ tu brāhmaṇasya sata evopanayanaṃ na tūpanayanādisaṃskarajanyabrāhmaṇyam iti jñāpanartham | kiṃ ca “brāhmaṇo na hantavyaḥ,” “brāhmaṇo na surāṃ pibet” iti niṣedhaśrutir anupanītaviṣaye(yā) na syāt | brāhmaṇasyāṣṭamaṃ varṣaṃ mukhyam upanayanakālaḥ | prathamabhāvino garbhādhānādīn saṃskārān ullaṅghyopanayanaṃ vyācakṣāṇas tasya prādhānyaṃ darśayati | tena daivānupapattyā garbhādhānāder akaraṇe ‘py upanayanaṃ bhavati | tasyākaraṇe tu vivāhādiṣv anadhikāra iti siddham || 1.6 ||
navame pañcame vā kāmyam || 1.7 ||
kāmanimittaṃ kāmyam | tan navame pañcame vā bhavati | “navame tejaskāmam” [ĀpDh 1.1.23] ity āpastambaḥ |
brahmavarcasakāmasya kāryo viprasya pañcame | [MDh 2.37]
iti manuḥ || 1.7 ||
garbhādi saṃkhyā varṣāṇām || 1.8 ||
varṣāṇāṃ saṃkhyā garbhādir eva bhavati, na jananādiḥ || 1.8 ||
tad dvitīyaṃ janma || 1.9 ||
tad upanayanaṃ dvitīyaṃ janma | atrāsya mātā sāvitrī pitā tv ācāryaḥ | tena dvijanmatvasiddhiḥ || 1.1.9 ||
tad yasmāt sa ācāryaḥ || 1.10 ||
tad upanayanaṃ pitur abhāve yasmāt puruṣād bhavati sa ācāryaḥ || 1.10 ||
na tu kevalād upanayanāt | kasmāt tarhi –
vedānuvacanāc ca || 1.11 ||
anuvacanam adhyāpanam | atra manuḥ |
upanīya tu yaḥ śiṣyaṃ vedam adhyapayed dvijaḥ |
sakalpaṃ sarahasyaṃ ca tam ācāryaṃ pracakṣate || [MDh 2.140] iti || 1.11 ||
ekādaśadvādaśayoḥ kṣatriyavaiśyayoḥ || 1.12 ||
nityo ‘yam anayoḥ kalpaḥ | kāmyas tu manunā darśitaḥ |
rājño balārthinaḥ ṣaṣṭhe vaiśyasyārthārthino ‘ṣṭame | [MDh 2.37] | iti || 1.12 ||
athāpatkalpān āha |
ā ṣoḍaśād brāhmaṇasyāpatitā sāvitrī || 1.13 ||
abhividhāv ākāraḥ | ā ṣoḍaśād varṣād brāhmaṇasya sāvitry apatitā apracyutā | sāvitrīśabdena tadupadeśanimittam upanayanaṃ lakṣyate | tad upanayanasya kālaḥ ity arthaḥ || 1.1.13 ||
dvāviṃśate rājanyasya dvyadhikāyā vaiśyasya || 1.14 ||
ubhayatrāpy āṅ anuvartate | pūraṇapratyayasya lopo draṣṭavyaḥ | ā dvādiṃśād varṣād rājanyasya ā caturviṃśād vaiśyasyāpatitā sāvitrī || 1.14 ||
mauñjījyāmaurvīsautryo mekhalāḥ krameṇa || 1.15 ||
muñjo darbhaviśeṣas tadvikāro mauñjī | mūrvā araṇyauṣadhiviśeṣaḥ | (saralīti draviḍabhāṣāyām) | tadvikāro maurvīḥ | jyā cāsau maurvī ceti karmadhārayaḥ | jyāśabdena dhanuṣo grāhyeti yāvat | sautrī sūtravikāraḥ | etā varṇakrameṇa mekhalā bhavanti || 1.15 ||
kṛṣṇarurubastājināni || 1.16 ||
kṛṣṇaḥ kṛṣṇasāraḥ | rurur bindumān mṛgaḥ | bastaś chāgaḥ | eteṣām ajināny uttarīyāṇi krameṇa, “ajinaṃ tv evottaraṃ dhārayet” (ĀpDh 1.3.10) ity āpastambīye darśanāt || 1.16 ||
vāsāṃsi śāṇakṣaumacīrakutapāḥ sarveṣām || 1.17 ||
śaṇavikāraḥ śāṇaḥ | kṣumā atasī tadvikāraḥ kṣaumam | śvetapaṭṭa ity anye | darbhādinimitaṃ cīram | ūrṇānirmitaḥ kambalaḥ kutapaḥ | catvāry etāni vāsāṃsi sarveṣām || 1.17 ||
kārpāsaṃ vāvikṛtam || 1.18 ||
avikṛtaṃ kārpāsaṃ vāsaḥ sarveṣām | kusumbhādirāgadravyair varṇāntarakalpanaṃ vikṛtis tadrahitam || 1.18 ||
anumānāny āha |
kāṣāyam apy eke || 1.19 ||
eke tv ācāryāḥ kaṣāyeṇa raktam api dhāryaṃ manyante || 1.19 ||
tatrāpi niyamaḥ |
vārkṣaṃ brāhmaṇasya māñjiṣṭhahāridre itarayoḥ
|| 1.20 ||
vṛkṣakaṣāyeṇa raktaṃ vārkṣam | tad brāhmaṇasya | mañjiṣṭhayā raktaṃ māñjiṣṭham | haridrayā raktaṃ hāridram | te itarayoḥ | kṣatriyavaiśyayor iti yāvat || 1.20 ||
bailvapālāśau brāhmaṇadaṇḍau || 1.21 ||
bailvaḥ pālāśo vā brāhmaṇasya daṇḍo na punaḥ samuccitau || 1.21 ||
aśvatthapailavau śeṣe || 1.22 ||
pīlur vṛkṣaviśeṣaḥ | utā iti prasiddhaḥ | śeṣe kṣatriyavaiśyaviṣaye || 1.22 ||
yajñiyo vā sarveṣām || 1.23 ||
sarveṣām uktālābhe yajñiyo yajñiyavṛkṣo vā daṇḍaḥ syāt || 1.23 ||
apīḍitā yūpavakrāḥ saśalkāḥ || 1.24 ||
apīḍitāḥ kīṭādibhir adūṣitāḥ | yūpavakrā yūpavad agre vakrāḥ | saśalkāḥ śalkāḥ [tvacaḥ?] satvacaḥ | evaṃvidhā daṇḍāḥ sarveṣām || 1.24 ||
mūrdhalalāṭanāsāgrapramāṇāḥ || 1.25 ||
yathāsaṃkhyam atreṣyate | mūrdhapramāṇo brāhmaṇasya daṇdaḥ | lalāṭāvadhiḥ kṣatriyasya | nāsāvadhir vaiśyasyeti || 1.25 ||
muṇḍajaṭilaśikhājaṭāś ca || 1.26 ||
atra na yathāsaṃkhyam | muṇḍā luptasarvakeśāḥ | jaṭilāḥ keśadhāriṇaḥ | jaṭā keśasaṃhatiḥ | śikhāmātraiva jaṭā yeṣāṃ te śikhājaṭāḥ | sarveṣām ayaṃ sāmānyadharmaḥ | chandogāpekṣayā muṇḍaśabdagrahaṇam || 1.26 ||
dravyahastaś ced ucchiṣṭo ‘nidhāyācāmet || 1.27 ||
mūtrapurīṣayoḥ karma bhojanādi cocchiṣṭatvanimittam | dravyahastaḥ sann ucchiṣṭaś cet tad dravyam anidhāyācāmet | ucchiṣṭaḥ san dravyahastaś ced dravyaṃ nidhāyācāmet | tathā ca manuḥ |
ucchiṣṭena tu saṃspṛṣṭo dravyahastaḥ kathaṃcana |
anidhāyaiva tad dravyam ācāntaḥ śucitām iyāt || iti | [MDh 5.143]
kiṃ ca bhakṣyabhojyādidravyaviṣaye tad dravyaṃ nidhyaiva mūtrapurīṣayoḥ karma kṛtvā punas tat pātraṃ nidhāyācāmet | vastradaṇḍādiviṣaye tv anidhāyaivācāmet || 1.27 ||
atha dravyaśuddhir ucyate |
dravyaśuddhiḥ parimārjanapradāhatakṣaṇanirṇejanāni
taijasamārtikadāravatāntavānām || 1.28 ||
taijasādīnāṃ dravyāṇāṃ yathākramaṃ parimārjanādiśuddhayaḥ | taijasaṃ kāṃśyādi | mārtikaṃ mṛnmayādi | dāravaṃ dārumayādi | tāntavaṃ tantumayādi | teṣāṃ krameṇa parimārjanam | tatra bhasmanā kāṃsyasya, śakṛtā sauvarṇarājatayoḥ, āmlena tāmrasya | idam ucchiṣṭaliptānām | “taijasānām evaṃbhūtānāṃ bhasmādibhiḥ” iti kaṇvaḥ | rajasvalācaṇḍālādispṛṣṭānām ekadinaṃ pañcagavyaṃ nikṣipyaikaviṃśatikṛtvo mārjanāc chuddhiḥ | mārtikānāṃ pradāhaḥ | prakṛṣṭo dāho varṇāntarāpattir yathā syāt tathāvidho dāhaḥ śodhanam | idaṃ sparśopahatānām | atra vasiṣṭhaḥ |
madyamūtrapurīṣais tu śleṣmapūyāśruśoṇitaiḥ |
saṃspṛṣṭaṃ naiva śudhyeta punardāhena mṛnmayam || iti | [VaDh 3.59]
dāravāṇāṃ takṣaṇāc chuddhiḥ | idam amedhyādivāsitānām | anyatra prokṣaṇaprakṣālanādi | tāntavānāṃ nirṇejanāc chuddhiḥ | idaṃ sparśadūṣitānām | malādidūṣitānāṃ dhāvanaṃ tanmātracchedanaṃ vā | sparśadūṣitānāṃ bahūnāṃ prokṣaṇāc chuddhir iti || 1.28 ||
taijasavad upalamaṇiśaṅkhamuktānām || 1.29 ||
upalādīnāṃ taijasavac chuddhiḥ parimārjanam iti || 1.29 ||
dāruvad athibhūmyoḥ || 1.30 ||
asthi hastidantādi | bhūmir gṛhādi | tayor dāruvac chuddhis takṣaṇam iti | dāravavad iti vaktavye dāruvad iti nirdeśād vikārasya yā śuddhir vikāriṇo ‘pi saiva śuddhir ity uktam || 1.30 ||
āvapanaṃ ca bhūmeḥ || 1.31 ||
āvapanam anyata ānīya pūraṇam adhikā śuddhir bhūmeḥ | atra vasiṣṭhaḥ |
khananād dahanād adbhir gobhir ākramaṇena ca |
caturbhir śudhyate bhūmiḥ pañcamāt tūpalepanāt || [VaDh 3.57] iti || 1.31 ||
cailavad rajjuvidalacarmaṇām || 1.32 ||
vidalaṃ vetraveṇuvidalādinirmitam | picchanirmitam apy anye | rajjvādīnāṃ trayāṇāṃ cailavad vastravac chuddhir nirṇejanam iti | paiṭhīnasis tu: “rajjuvidalacarmaṇām aspṛśyaspṛṣṭānāṃ prokṣaṇāc chuddhiḥ” iti || 1.32 ||
utsargo vātyantopahatānām || 1.33 ||
idaṃ vāsiṣṭhena samānaviṣayaṃ “madyamūtrapurīṣaiḥ” [VaDh 3.59] ityādinā | vāśabdaḥ pakṣavyāvṛttau || 1.33 ||
prāṅmukha udaṅmukho vā śaucam ārabheta || 1.34 ||
icchāto vikalpa ārabheteti vacanāt pādaprakṣālanaprabhṛtidiṅniyamaḥ | āpastambas tu “pratyakpādāvanejanam” [ĀpDh 1.31.2] ity āha | śaucagrahaṇam ācamana eva mā bhūn mūtrapurīṣādiśauce 'pi diṅniyamajñāpanārtham || 1.34 ||
śucau deśa āsīno dakṣiṇaṃ bāhuṃ jānvantarā kṛtvā yajñopavīty ā maṇibandhanāt pāṇī prakṣālya vāgyato hṛdayaspṛśas triś catur vāpa ācāmet || 1.35 ||
idam ekaṃ vākyam | ācamanakāle śucau deśe ‘nupahata āsīna ity upalakṣaṇam āsīnas tiṣṭhan prahvo veti | jānvantarā jānunor madhye dakṣiṇabāhuṃ kṛtvā | dakṣiṇaṃ bāhum ity uktatvād vāmahastasya nāvaśyaṃbhāvaḥ | yajñopavītīti pūrvaṃ svasthānastham api yathāsthānaniveśanārtham | atha vottarīyavinyāsārtham | tathā ca āpastambaḥ: “upāsane gurūṇāṃ vṛddhānām atithīnāṃ home japyakarmaṇi bhojana ācamane svādhyāye ca yajñopavītī syāt” | “api vā sūtram evopavītārthaḥ” [ĀpDh 1.15.1; 2.4.22] iti | ā maṇibandhād yasmin deśe maṇir badhyata ā tasmāt pāṇī prakṣālya | vāgyataḥ śabdam akurvan | hṛdayaspṛśaḥ parimāṇārtham idaṃ yāvatyaḥ pītā hṛdayaṃ spṛśanti yāsu māṣo majjati tāvatīr apa ācāmet triś catur vā | yatra mantravad ācamanaṃ vihitaṃ tatra tena saha catuḥ | anyatra trir iti vikalpaḥ || 1.35 ||
dviḥ parimṛjyate || 1.36 ||
pratiyogaṃ sodakena pāṇinauṣṭhayoḥ parimārjanam || 1.36 ||
pādau cābhyukṣet || 1.37 ||
cakārāc chiraś ca || 1.37 ||
khāni copaspṛśec chīrṣaṇyāni || 1.38 ||
śīrṣe bhavāni śīrṣaṇyāni | śirobhavānīti yāvat | khāni indriyāṇi | tāny upaspṛśet | atra cakāraḥ pratīndriyopasparśanārthaḥ || 1.38 ||
mūrdhani ca dadyāt || 1.39 ||
cakārān nābhau mūrdhani ca sarvābhir aṅgulībhir upaspṛśed ity arthaḥ || 1.39 ||
suptvā bhuktvā kṣutvā ca punaḥ || 1.40 ||
svāpādinimitte punar dvir ācāmed iti yāvat || 1.40 ||
dantaśliṣṭeṣu dantavad anyatra jihvābhimarśanāt
|| 1.41 ||
dantaśliṣṭeṣūcchiṣṭalepeṣu jihvābhimarśanād anyatra dantavan nāśucitvam || 1.41 ||
tatrāpi –
prāk cyuter ity eke || 1.42 ||
saty api jihvābhimarśane yāval lepāḥ svasthānān na cyavante tāvan nāśucitvam iti || 1.42 ||
cyuteṣv āsrāvavad vidyān nigirann eva tacchuciḥ
|| 1.43 ||
āsrāva āsyajalam | nigaraṇam antaḥpraveśanam | cyuteṣu nigirann eva tacchucir iti vaktavya āsrāvavad vidyād iti vacanam āsrāve ca nigaraṇād eva śucir iti sūcanārtham || 1.1.43 ||
na mukhyā vipruṣa ucchiṣṭaṃ kurvanti | na ced aṅge
nipatanti || 1.44 ||
mukhe bhavā mukhyāḥ | vipruṣa āsrāvabindavaḥ | bhūmyādiṣu patitā nocchiṣṭatāṃ nayanti
|| 1.44 ||
lepagandhāpakarṣaṇaṃ śaucam amedhyasya || 1.45 ||
vasā śukram asṛṅ majjā mūtraviṭkarṇaviṇnakhāḥ |
śleṣmāśru dūṣikā svedo dvādaśaite nṛṇāṃ malāḥ || [MDh 3.135]
iti manuḥ | etat sarvam amedhyaśabdena vivakṣitam | asya yāvatā gandho lepaś cāpakṛṣyate ‘panīyate tāvatā śaucam iti | tatra yasya malasya gandhamātraṃ tasya tadapakarṣaṇam | yasya gandho lepaś ca tasya tadubhayāpakarṣaṇam || 1.45 ||
tad adbhiḥ pūrvaṃ mṛdā ca || 1.46 ||
tat pūrvaṃ gandhavan malāpakarṣaṇam adbhir lepagandhavan malāpakarṣaṇaṃ mṛdā cādbhiś ceti | idaṃ hastapādāder amedhyaliptasya śaucam, taijasādiṣu viśeṣasya pūrvam uktatvāt
|| 1.46 ||
mūtrapurīṣasnehavisraṃsanābhyavahārasaṃyogeṣu
ca || 1.47 ||
cakāraḥ pūrvoktasamuccaye | sneho retaḥ | mūtrapurīṣasnehānāṃ visraṃsanaṃ nirasanam | abhyavahāram avyavahāryadravyaṃ tena saṃyogaḥ | eṣu nimitteṣu pūrvavan mṛdā cādbhiḥ śaucam iti || 1.47 ||
yatra cāmnāyo vidadhyāt || 1.48 ||
yatra viṣaye yac chaucam āmnāyo vidadhyāt tatra tad eva bhavati | yathā camasānām ucchiṣṭaliptānāṃ mārjālīyādbhiḥ prakṣālanam iti || 1.48 ||
atha gurūpasadanavidhiḥ |
pāṇinā savyam upasaṃgṛhyānaṅguṣṭham adhīhi bho ity āmantrayed guruṃ tatracakṣurmanāḥ prāṇopasparśaṇam darbhaiḥ || 1.49 ||
pāṇinā svena dakṣiṇena | savyam iti viśeṣanād dakṣiṇeneti gamyate | guroḥ savyaṃ pādam anaṅguṣṭham aṅguṣṭhavarjaṃ gṛhītvā, adhīhi bho iti gurum āmantrayet | tatra gurau manaścakṣuṣī ca nidhāyāvahitaḥ syād iti | prāṇāḥ śīrṣaṇyānīndiryāṇi | teṣām ātmīyānām ācamanoktakrameṇa darbhair upasparśanaṃ kartavyaṃ māṇavakena || 1.49 ||
prāṇāyāmās trayaḥ pañcadaśamātrāḥ || 1.50 ||
kāryā iti śeṣaḥ | jānupārśvataḥ parimṛjya truṭim ekāṃ kuryāt saikā mātrā | tāḥ pañcadaśa pūryante yāvatā kālena tāvantaṃ kālaṃ prāṇavāyuṃ dhārayet sa ekaḥ prāṇāyāmaḥ | te trayaḥ kāryāḥ | manuḥ |
savyāhṛtikāṃ sapraṇavāṃ gāyatrīṃ śirasā saha |
triḥ paṭhed āyataprāṇaḥ prāṇāyāmaḥ sa ucyate || iti |
[=VaDh 25.13; BDh 4.1.28] || 1.50 ||
prākkūleṣv āsanaṃ ca || 1.51 ||
prāgagreṣu darbheṣv āsanaṃ cakārāt kartavyam iti śeṣaḥ || 1.51 ||
oṃpūrvā vyāhṛtayaḥ pañca satyāntāḥ || 1.52 ||
vyāhṛtir nāma (?) bhūr bhuvaḥ svaḥ satyaṃ puruṣa iti pañca | atra tu puruṣavyāhrtiś caturthī satyavyāhṛtiḥ pañcamī vaktavyā | tāś ca pratyekaṃ praṇavapūrvā vaktavyāḥ || 1.52 ||
guroḥ pādopasaṃgrahaṇaṃ prātaḥ || 1.53 ||
ahar ahaḥ prātar guroḥ pādopasaṃgrahaṇaṃ kāryam | manuḥ |
vyatyastapāṇinā kāryam upasaṃgrahaṇaṃ guroḥ |
savyena savyaḥ spraṣṭavyo dakṣiṇena tu dakṣiṇaḥ || [MDh 2.72] iti || 1.53 ||
brahmānuvacane cādyantayoḥ || 1.54 ||
brahma vedaḥ | anuvacanam adhyāpanam | tatrādyantayoś ca gurupādopasaṃgrahaṇaṃ kāryam
|| 1.54 ||
anujñāta upaviśet prāṅmukho dakṣiṇataḥ śiṣya
udaṅmukho vā || 1.55 ||
ācāryeṇānujñātas taddakṣiṇataḥ prāṅmukha udaṅmukho vopaviśet | kāryānuguṇo vikalpaḥ
|| 1.55 ||
sāvitrī cānuvacanam || 1.56 ||
tat savitur vareṇyam ity eṣā na tv anyā savitṛdevatyā | sā vānuvacanaṃ pratyadhyayanaṃ paṭhanīyeti || 1.56 ||
ādito brahmaṇa ādāne || 1.57 ||
“pāṇinā savyam upasaṃgṛhya” [GDh 1.49] ityādi sāvitryanuvacanāntaṃ yad uktaṃ tad idaṃ brahmaṇo vedasya guroḥ sakāśād ādita ādānakāle kartavyam | upanayanād anantaraṃ sāvitryupadeśakāle ca, pratyahaṃ tu tatra cakṣurmanastvam | prātaradhyayanādyantayoś ca guroḥ pādopasaṃgrahaṇam anujñātopaveśanaṃ ca kartavyam || 1.57 ||
oṃkāro ‘nyatrāpi || 1.58 ||
sāvitryanuvacanād anyatrāpy oṃkāro vaktavyaḥ | pratyaham adhyayanakāla ity arthaḥ || 1.58 ||
antarāgamane punar upasadanam || 1.59 ||
guroḥ śiṣyasya ca madhye gamanam antarāgamanam | yasya kasyāpy antarāgamane punar upasadanaṃ kartavyam | “pāṇinā savyam” [GDh 1.49] ityādy “oṃkāro ‘nyatrāpi” [GDh 1.58] ity antam upasadanam || 1.59 ||
śvanakulasarpamaṇḍūkamārjārāṇāṃ tryaham
upavāso vipravāsaś ca || 1.60 ||
śvādīnām antarāgamane tryaham upavāso vipravāsaś ca kartavyaḥ | vipravāsa ācāryakulād anyatra vāsaḥ | manus tu |
paśumaṇḍūkamārjāraśvasarpanakuleṣu ca |
antarāgamane vidyād anadhyāyam aharniśam || iti | [MDh 4.126]
tad dhāraṇādhyayanaviṣayam | gautamīyaṃ tu grahaṇādhyayanaviṣayam || 1.60 ||
prāṇāyāmā ghṛtaprāśanaṃ cetareṣām || 1.61 ||
itareṣāṃ śvādivyatiriktānāṃ paśvādīnām antarāgamane prāṇāyāmās trayaḥ kāryā ghṛtaprāśanaṃ ca kāryam | etat sarvaṃ śiṣyasya prāyaścittaṃ na guroḥ, | ubhayor ity apare || 1.61 ||
śmaśānābhyadhyayane caivam || 1.62 ||
abhir uparibhāve śmaśānasyopary adhyayane caivaṃ prāyaścittam | prāṇāyāmā ghṛtaprāśanaṃ ceti | dviruktir adhyāpanaparisamāptyarthā || 1.62 ||
iti śrīgautamīyavṛttau haradattaviracitāyāṃ mitākṣarāyāṃ
prathamapraśne prathamo ‘dhyāyaḥ
atha dvitīyo ‘dhyāyaḥ
upanītaprasaṅgenānupanītadharmā ucyante |
prāg upanayanāt kāmacāraḥ kāmavādaḥ
kāmabhakṣaḥ || 2.1 ||
ā ṣoḍaśād brāhmaṇasyety āpatkalpopanayanaviṣayam | kāmacāra icchācaraṇam | apaṇyāny api vikrīṇīyāc chvavṛttyāpi jīved iti | kāmavādo ‘ślīlānṛtādivacanam | kāmabhakṣo laśunaparyuṣitānnādibhakṣaṇaṃ catuḥ pañcakṛtvo vā bhojanam ity etāvad yasya sa tathoktaḥ | na tu brahmahatyāsurāpānādyatiprasaṅgaḥ || 2.1 ||
ahutāt || 2.2 ||
hutaśeṣaṃ puroḍāśādi | tad attīti hutāt | tadviparīto ‘hutāt | anupanīto hutaṃ nādyād iti || 2.2 ||
brahmacārī || 2.3 ||
kāmacārāder ayam apavādaḥ | ā ṣoḍaśād ity uktatvāt strīṣu prasaṅgayogyatāsti | ato brahmacārī jitendriyaḥ syād iti | tathā ca smṛtyantare: “prāyaścittaṃ viluptam avakīrṇivratena śuddham upanayen na ūrdhvaṃ vrātyāvakīrṇivratābhyām” iti || 2.3 ||
yathopapāditamūtrapurīṣo bhavati || 2.4 ||
mūtrapurīṣe yathopapadyete yasya sa tathoktaḥ prāṅmukhādir api kuryāt | “na bhūmāv anantardhāya” (GDh 9.38) ityādisthānaniyamo ‘pi nāsti || 2.4 ||
nāsyācamanakalpo vidyate || 2.5 ||
kalpaniṣedhād ācamanam anujñātaṃ strīśūdravat || 2.5 ||
anyatrāpamārjanapradhāvanāvokṣaṇebhyaḥ || 2.6 ||
apamārjanādīni varjayitvā, ācamanakalpo nāsti | apamārjanādikam astīti yāvat | yady apy apamārjanādīny ācamanakalpe nāntarbhavanti tathāpi paryudāsamukhena tāni vidhīyante | atramārjanaṃ (atrāpamārjanaṃ) sodakena pāṇinā parimārjanam ucchiṣṭādiliptasya | pradhāvanam amedhyādiliptasyādbhir mṛdā ca kṣālanam | avokṣaṇaṃ rajasvalādispṛṣṭasya | idam atyantabālaviṣayam | ṣaḍvarṣād ūrdhvaṃ snānam icchanti | asyānupanītasyaitāvad uttamātra (?) kāmacārādivyatikrame prāyaścittam asti | tatra smṛtyantare |
aśītir yasya varṣāṇi bālo vāpy ūnaṣoḍaśaḥ |
prāyaścittārdham arhanti striyo vyādhita eva ca ||
ūnaikādaśavarṣasya pañcavarṣāt parasya ca |
cared guruḥ suhṛc caiva prāyaścittaṃ viśuddhaye ||
ato bālatarasyāsya nāparādho na pātakam |
rājadaṇḍaś ca tasyātaḥ prāyaścittaṃ ca neṣyate || iti || 2.6 ||
na tadupasparśanād aśaucam || 2.7 ||
tadupasparśanāt tasyākṛtopanayanasyodakyādispṛṣṭasyāpy upasparśanād aśaucaṃ na syāt | spṛṣṭāspṛṣṭir upasparśanam | tena snānaṃ na kartavyam | bhuktocchiṣṭasya kṛtamūtrapurīṣa(sya) sparśanād api nācamanam | idam api ṣaḍvarṣāt prāg eva | kimarthaṃ tarhi tasya śaucaṃ vihitam | na tāvad anuṣṭhānārthaṃ nāpi sparśayogyatārtham, akṛtaśaucasyāpi sparśayogyatvāt | rakṣaṇārtham iti brūmaḥ | tathā ca smṛtyantaram |
bālasya pañcamād varṣād rakṣārthaṃ śaucam ācaret | iti || 2.7 ||
na tv evainam agnihavanabaliharaṇayor niyuñjyāt || 2.8 ||
enam anupanītam agnihavana aupāsanahomādau baliharaṇe vaiśvadevādau na niyuñjyān na niyuñjīteti yāvat | tuśabdād uktād anyatrāpi samantrake karmaṇi na niyuñjīteti | evakāro ‘vadhāraṇe | atha āśvalāyanaḥ: “pāṇigrahaṇādi gṛhyaṃ paricaret svayaṃ patny api vā putraḥ kumāry antevāsī vā” iti | chandogāś ca patnī juhuyād iti patnīkumāryādyanujñāte ‘smin pakṣe na tv evainam ity arthaḥ || 2.8 ||
na brahmābhivyāhārayed anyatra svadhāninayanāt || 2.9 ||
brahma vedaḥ | enam anupanītaṃ brahma nābhivyāhārayen noccārayet | kim aviśeṣeṇeti | neti āha | anyatra svadhāninayanāt | pitryasya sarvasya karmaṇa upalakṣaṇam | “anyatrodakakarmasvadhāpitṛsaṃyuktebhyaḥ” (VaDh 2.7) iti vāsiṣṭhe darśanāt | agṛhītākṣaraḥ putraḥ pitroḥ saṃskāram arhatītyādi ca | anyasyāsaṃbhave sarvaṃ pitryaṃ karma tadānīṃ mantrān grāhayitvāsau kārayitavyaḥ || 2.9 ||
upanayanādir niyamaḥ || 2.10 ||
agnīndhanādir yo niyamo vakṣyate sa upanayanādir eva | anupanītādhikāreṇa vicchinnatvād upanītādhikārārtham idam || 2.10 ||
uktaṃ brahmacaryam || 2.11 ||
anupanītasya yad uktaṃ brahmacaryaṃ tad upanītasyāpi samānam | nanu ca “strīprekṣaṇālambhane” [GDh 2.16] iti neṣedho vakṣyate | tathāpi smaraṇakīrtanādiniṣedhārtham idam || 2.11 ||
agnīndhanabhakṣacaraṇe || 2.12 ||
agnīndhanaṃ samiddhomaḥ | bhikṣāṇāṃ samūho bhaikṣam | tadarthaṃ pratigṛhaṃ caraṇaṃ bhaikṣacaraṇam | te pratyahaṃ kartavye | tatra manuḥ |
dūrād āhṛtya samidhaḥ saṃnidadhyād vihāyasi |
sāyaṃ prātaś ca juhuyāt tābhir agnim atandritaḥ ||
akṛtvā bhaikṣacaraṇam asamidhya ca pāvakam |
anāturaḥ saptarātram avakīrṇivrataṃ caret || iti | [MDh 2.186–87]
āpastambas tu: “sāyam evāgnipūjety eke” iti [ĀpDh 1.4.17] || 2.12 ||
satyavacanam || 2.13 ||
upanītena satyam eva vaktavyam || 2.13 ||
apām upasparśanam || 2.14 ||
upasparśanam snānam | tad apy ahar ahaḥ kartavyam || 2.14 ||
eke godānādi || 2.15 ||
godānaṃ nāma ṣoḍaśe varṣe kartavyaṃ vratam | tad vrateṣu dvitīyam | chandogānām eke ācāryā godānādi snānam icchanti na tataḥ prāg dīkṣitavad asyāpi brahmacaryadīkṣāniyuktatvāt | nityasnānasyāyaṃ pratiṣedhaḥ | naimittikaṃ tu kartavyam, tatra daṇḍavad āplavanam “nāpsu ślāghamānaḥ snāyāt” ity āpastambasmaraṇāt [ĀpDh 1.2.30] || 2.15 ||
bahiḥsaṃdhyatvaṃ ca || 2.16 ||
sāyaṃprātar dve saṃdhye yasya grāmād bahir bhavataḥ sa bahiḥsaṃdhyas tasya bhāvaḥ | grāmād bahir eva saṃdhyopāsanaṃ kartavyam iti || 2.16 ||
tat kadā kathām cety āha |
tiṣṭhet pūrvām āsītottarāṃ sajyotiṣyā jyotiṣo
darśanād vāgyataḥ || 2.17 ||
prātaḥsaṃdhyāṃ tiṣṭhet sāyaṃsaṃdhyām āsīta | atyantasaṃyoge dvitīyā | sthānāsanayor uprakramopasaṃhārau kathayati – sajyotiṣi kāle samārabhya ā jyotirantaradarśanāt | prātar nakṣatrajyotir ārabhya ā sūryajyotirdarśanāt sāyam ādityajyotir ārabhya ā nakṣatradarśanād iti | tāvantaṃ kālaṃ vāgyataś ca syāt | tathā ca manuḥ |
pūrvāṃ saṃdhyāṃ japaṃs tiṣṭhet sāvitrīm ārkadarśanāt |
paścimāṃ tu samāsīta samyag ṛkṣavibhāvanāt || iti [MDh 2.101] || 2.17 ||
nādityam īkṣeta || 2.18 ||
brahmacāriṇo ‘yaṃ sadādityadarśane pratiṣedhaḥ | snātakasya tu māna(vam) |
nekṣetodyantam ādityaṃ nāstaṃ yāntaṃ kadācana |
noparaktaṃ na vāristhaṃ na madhyaṃ nabhaso gatam || iti [MDh 4.37] || 2.18 ||
varjayen madhumāṃsagandhamālya-divāsvāpābhyañjanayānopānacchatrakāmakrodha-lobhamohavādavādanasnānadantadhāvanaharṣa-nṛtyagītaparivādabhayāni || 2.19 ||
madhvādīni varjayet | madhu mākṣikam | māṃsaṃ mṛgādeḥ | gandhaś candanādiḥ | mālyāni puṣpāṇi | divāsvapno divānidrā | añjanam akṣṇoḥ | abhyañjanaṃ tailābhyaṅgaḥ | yānaṃ śakaṭādi | upanacchatre prasiddhe | kāmaḥ strīsaṅgaḥ | krodhaḥ kopaḥ | lobho dravyābhilāṣaḥ | moho vivekaśūnyatā | vado bahujalpaḥ | vādanaṃ vīṇādīnām | snānaṃ sukhārtham uṣṇatoyādinā kaṇṭhād adhaḥ prakṣālanam | dantadhāvanaṃ dantamalāpakarṣaṇam | harṣo ‘bhimatalābhāc cittodrekaḥ | nṛtyagīte prasiddhe | privādaḥ paradoṣakathanam | bhayaṃ bhayahetuḥ kāntārapraveśādiḥ | idaṃ harṣe ‘pi draṣṭavyam || 2.19 ||
gurudarśane kaṇṭhaprāvṛtāvasikthakāpāśrayaṇa-
pādaprasāraṇāni || 2.20 ||
guravaḥ pitrācāryādayaḥ | teṣāṃ darśanayogye deśe kaṇṭhaprāvṛttādīni varjayet | kaṇṭhaprāvṛtaṃ kaṇṭhaprāvaraṇaṃ vastrādinā | avasakthikā ūrau pādam āropyāvasthānam | apāśrayaṇaṃ kuḍyastambhādyāśrityāsanam | pādaprasāraṇaṃ prasiddham | gurujanasakāśe vinayasaṃkocena tiṣṭhed ity arthaḥ || 2.20 ||
niṣṭhīvitahasitavijṛmbhitāvasphoṭanāni || 2.21 ||
varjayed iti | niṣṭhīvitaṃ kaṇṭhac chleṣmaṇaḥ saśabdaṃ bahir nirasanam | hasitaṃ hāsaḥ | vijṛmbhitaṃ jṛmbhikā | avasphoṭanam aṅgulīnāṃ saśabdam upamardanam || 2.21 ||
strīprekṣaṇālambhane maithunaśaṅkāyām || 2.22 ||
strīṇāṃ prekṣaṇam avayavaśo nirūpaṇaṃ na yādṛcchikaṃ darśanam | ālambhanaṃ sparśanam | te ‘pi varjayet | maithunaśaṅkāyām iti vacanād bālavṛddhāturāsu svayaṃ ca tathāvidhasya na doṣaḥ
|| 2.22 ||
dyūtaṃ hīnasevām adattādānaṃ hiṃsām || 2.23 ||
dyūtaṃ varjayed iti | dvividhaṃ [dyūtam] pāṇyaprāṇibhedāt | prāṇidyūtaṃ meṣayuddhādi | aprāṇidyūtam akṣakrīḍādi | hīnasevāṃ hīnasya sevām adhojātiprabhṛteḥ | hīnā cāsau sevā ca śaucādijalāharaṇam | adattādānaṃ kenāpy adattasyotsṛṣṭasyāpy asvāmikasyādānam | hiṃsā prāṇipīḍā || 2.23 ||
ācāryatatputrastrīdīkṣitanāmāni || 2.24 ||
ācāryasya tatputrasya tatstriyā dīkṣitasya nāmāni varjayet | parokṣe ‘py aupādhikanāmagrahaṇaṃ kartavyam iti || 2.24 ||
madhumāṃsādyetatparyantaṃ varjayed iti kriyānvayo ‘syāpi sūtrasya |
śuklavāco madyaṃ nityaṃ brāhmaṇaḥ || 2.25 ||
brāhmaṇaḥ śuklā aślīlāḥ parodvegakāriṇyaḥ | madyaṃ madakaraṃ dravyam | tāś ca tac ca nityaṃ varjayet | nityaṃ brāhmaṇa iti vacanāt kṣatriyavaiśyayor gṛhasthayoḥ paiṣṭīvyatiriktamadyopayoge na pratyavāya iti || 2.25 ||
adhaḥśayyāsanī pūrvotthāyī jaghanyasaṃveśī || 2.26 ||
asyārtho mānave spaṣṭaḥ |
nīcaṃ śayyāsanaṃ cāsya nityaṃ syād gurusaṃnidhau |
uttiṣṭhet prathamaṃ cāsya caramaṃ caiva saṃviśet || iti [MDh 2.194] || 2.23 ||
vāgbāhūdarasaṃyataḥ || 2.27 ||
vāksaṃyamo bahupralāpavirahaḥ | bāhusaṃyamo loṣṭamardanādyabhāvaḥ | udarasaṃyamo mitabhojanam || 2.27 ||
nāmagotre guroḥ samānato nirdiśet || 2.28 ||
ātmano nāmagotre guroḥ samānato nirdiśet | samānato yathāvad apalāparahitam ity arthaḥ | apara āha – guror nāmagotre samānataḥ samyag ānataḥ prahvo bhūtvā nirdiśed iti || 2.28 ||
arcite śreyasi caivam || 2.29 ||
arcito loke pūjitaḥ | śreyān vidyādibhir adhikaḥ | tayor apy evam eva samyag ānata iti | atra smṛtyantaram |
ācāryaṃ caiva tatputraṃ tadbhāryāṃ dīkṣitaṃ gurum |
pitaraṃ vā pitṛvyaṃ ca mātulaṃ mātaraṃ tathā ||
hitaiṣiṇaṃ ca vidvāṃsaṃ śvaśuraṃ patim eva ca |
na brūyān namato vidvān mātuś ca bhaginīṃ tathā ||
arcite śreyasi cety evaṃśabdo yac ca yāvac ca gurāv uktaṃ tat sarvam atidiśati | tena śayyāsanādikam api tayoḥ saṃnidhau nīcaṃ bhavatīti || 2.29 ||
śayyāsanasthānāni vihāya pratiśravaṇam || 2.30 ||
gurāv ājñāpayati sati pratiśravaṇaṃ prativacanaṃ kurvañ śayyāsanasthānāni vihāyābhigacchan kuryāt || 2.30 ||
abhikramaṇaṃ vacanād adṛṣṭena || 2.31 ||
yadi bahiḥsthito gurur apaśyann eva śiṣyaṃ bravīti tadā śiṣyeṇābhikramaṇam upasarpaṇaṃ kartavyaṃ na punar adṛṣṭo ‘smīty anādaraḥ kartavyaḥ || 2.31 ||
adhaḥsthānāsanas tiryagvātasevāyāṃ gurudarśane
cottiṣṭhet || 2.32 ||
yadā gurur nīcaiḥ sthānam āsanaṃ cādhitiṣṭhati svayam uccaiḥ sthānāsanasthas tadā guruṃ dṛṣṭvottiṣṭhet | tiryagvātasevāyāṃ mūtrapurīṣotsargādau ca guruṃ dṛṣtvottiṣṭhet | cakāraḥ pūrvāpekṣayā samuccayārthaḥ || 2.32 ||
gacchantam anuvrajet || 2.33 ||
gacchantaṃ gurum anugacchet || 2.33 ||
karma vijñāpyākhyāya || 2.34 ||
yat kiṃcid asya śiṣyasya kartavyaṃ tasya niṣkṛtir idaṃ kariṣyāmīty ācāryāya vijñāpya yac cācāryaupayikam udakumbhaharaṇādi tat svayam eva jñātvā kṛtvā ca tasmai kṛtam ity ākhyāya vartitavyam ity arthaḥ || 2.34 ||
āhūto ‘dhyāyī || 2.35 ||
guruṇāhūtaḥ sann adhīyīta na tu svayaṃ codayed iti || 2.35 ||
yuktaḥ priyahitayoḥ || 2.36 ||
ācāryasya yat priyaṃ hitaṃ ca tatra yuktas tatparaḥ syāt | priyaṃ tatkālaprītikaram | hitaṃ kālāntare --- tatkaram (?) || 2.36 ||
tadbhāryāputreṣu caivam || 2.37 ||
tasyācāryasya bhāryāputrāś ca teṣu caivam ācāryavad vartitavyam || 2.37 ||
asyāpavādaḥ |
nocchiṣṭāśanasnāpanaprasādhanapāda-
prakṣālanonmardanopasaṃgrahaṇāni || 2.38 ||
ucchiṣṭāśanaṃ bhuktaśeṣāśanam | snāpanaṃ snānīyādibhiḥ śiro’ṅgamardanapūrvakam abhiṣekaḥ | prasādhanam alaṃkaraṇam | pādaprakṣālanaṃ prasiddham | unmardanam abhyaṅgaśarīrasaṃvāhanādi | upasaṃgrahaṇaṃ “vyatyastapāṇinā” [MDh 2.72] ityādi pūrvoktam | etāni guror bhāryāputreṣu ca na kartavyāni | ata evācārye kartavyānīti siddham || 2.38 ||
athopasaṃgrahaṇasya pratprasavaḥ |
viproṣyopasaṃgrahaṇaṃ gurubhāryāṇām || 2.39 ||
viproṣya pravāsaṃ gatvā pratyāgatena gurubhāryāṇām upasaṃgrahaṇaṃ kāryam || 2.39 ||
tatrāpi –
naike yuvatīnāṃ vyavahāraprāptena || 2.40 ||
eke tv ācāryā yuvatīnāṃ gurubhāryāṇāṃ vyavahāraprāptena ṣoḍavarṣaprāyeṇa śiṣyeṇa viproṣyāpy upasaṃgrahaṇaṃ na kāryam iti manyante || 2.40 ||
agnīndhanabhaikṣacaraṇa ity uktam | tatrāgnīndhanasya pratigṛhyaṃ vyavasthitatvāt sādhāraṇabhaikṣacaraṇe vidhim āha |
sārvavarṇikabhaikṣyacaraṇam
abhiśastapatitavarjam || 2.41 ||
sarveṣu varṇeṣu bhavaṃ sārvavarṇikam | abhiśastān patitāṃś ca varjayitvā sarveṣu varṇeṣu bhaikṣyaṃ caritavyam | abhiśastā upapātakinaḥ || 2.41 ||
ādimadhyānteṣu bhavacchabdaḥ prayojyo
varṇānukrameṇa || 2.42 ||
bhikṣāṃ dehīti padadvayasyādimadhyānteṣu varṇakrameṇa bhavacchabdaḥ saṃbuddhyantaḥ prayoktavyaḥ | strīṣu strīliṅgaḥ | brāhmaṇasya bhavan bhikṣāṃ dehi | brahmaṇyāṃ bhavati bhikṣāṃ dehi | kṣatriyasya bhikṣāṃ bhavan dehi | bhikṣāṃ bhavati dehi | vaiśyasya bhikṣāṃ dehi bhavan | bhikṣāṃ dehi bhavati || 2.42 ||
ācāryajñātigurusveṣv alābhe ‘nyatra || 2.43 ||
ācārya uktaḥ | jñātiḥ pitṛvyādiḥ sapiṇḍaḥ | gurur mātulādiḥ | svam ātmīyagrahaṇam | anyatra bhikṣāyā abhāve, ācāryādigṛheṣu bhaikṣyaṃ caritavyam || 2.43 ||
teṣāṃ pūrvaṃ pūrvaṃ pariharet || 2.44 ||
teṣām ācāryādīnāṃ yo yaḥ prathamanirdiṣṭas taṃ taṃ pariharet | anyatrālābhe svagṛhe, tatrālābhe guruṣu, tatrālābhe jñātiṣu, tatrālābha ācāryagṛha iti || 2.44 ||
nivedya gurave ‘nujñāto bhuñjīta || 2.45 ||
idam ānītaṃ bhaikṣyam iti gurave nivedya tadanujñāto bhuñjīta | yadi guruḥ svayaṃ gṛhṇīyāt tato ‘nyad āharet || 2.45 ||
asaṃnidhau tadbhāryāputrasabrahmacāribhyaḥ || 2.46 ||
ācāryāsaṃnidhāne tadbhāryādibhyo yathāsaṃbhavaṃ nivedya tair anujñāto bhuñjīta || 2.46 ||
vāgyatas tṛpyann alolupyamānaḥ
saṃnidhāyodakam || 2.47 ||
yāvadbhukti vācaṃyamaḥ | tṛpyann annadarśanena hṛṣyan | alolupyamāno ‘tispṛhām akurvan | saṃnidhāyāntarbhāvitaṇyarthaḥ | saṃnidhāpyeti | udakam udakabhājanam iti || 2.47 ||||
śiṣyaśāsanaprakāram āha |
śiṣyaśiṣṭir avadhena || 2.48 ||
vadhas tāḍanam | atāḍyatā guruṇā bhartsanādibhiḥ śiṣyaḥ śāsyaḥ || 2.48 ||
aśaktau rajjuveṇuvidalābhyāṃ tanubhyām || 2.49 ||
yadi bhartsanādibhiḥ śāsitum aśakyas tato rajjvā tanvā, tanunā veṇuvidalena veti | dvaṃdvanirdiṣṭayor api vikalpo “rajjvā veṇudalena vā” [MDh 8.229] iti mānave darśanāt | tābhyāṃ durbalābhyāṃ tāḍayitvāpi śāsanīyaḥ || 2.49 ||
anyena ghnan rājñā śāsyaḥ || 2.50 ||
hastādinā krodhavaśena tāḍayan rājñā śāsya ācāryaḥ | evaṃ śiṣyasya gurukule vāsa uktaḥ || 2.50 ||
kimantaṃ kālam ity ata āha |
dvādaśa varṣāṇy ekavede brahmacaryaṃ caret || 2.51 ||
yady apy ekaikasya vedasya bahvyaḥ śākhāḥ | ekaviṃśatidhā bahvṛca ekaśataṃ yajuḥśākhāḥ sahasravartmā sāmavedo navadhātharvaṇo veda iti | tathāpi tatra tatra vede pūrvair adhyayanānuṣṭhānābhyāṃ parigṛhītā yavatī śākhā tāvaty atra vedaśabdena vivakṣitā | ya ekaṃ vedam adhīte sa dvādaśa varṣāṇi gurukule brahmacaryaṃ caret || 2.51 ||
pratidvādaśa vā sarveṣu || 2.52 ||
yas tu caturo vedān adhyetuṃ śaktaḥ sa pratidvādaśa prativede dvādaśa varṣāṇīty arthaḥ | yathāha āpastambaḥ: “upetasyācāryakule brahmacārivāso ‘ṣṭācatvāriṃśad vatsarāṇi” [ĀpDh 1.2.11–12] iti || 2.52 ||
grahaṇāntaṃ vā || 2.53 ||
yāvatā kālenaiko vedo dvau trayaś caturo vā grahītuṃ śakyās tāvantaṃ kālam iti || 2.53 ||
vidyānte gurur arthena nimantryaḥ || 2.54 ||
vidyāsamāptau gurur arthena prayojanena nimantryaḥ praṣṭavyaḥ | guro, idaṃ dhanam āharāṇīti || 2.54 ||
kṛtvānujñātasya vā snānam || 2.55 ||
tata āharety ācāryoktaṃ kṛtvā snānaṃ kartavyam | vatsa tvadguṇair evāham asmi toṣito dhanenālam iti tenānujñātasya vā, snānaṃ samāvartanaṃ kartavyam iti || 2.55 ||
ācāryaḥ śreṣṭho gurūṇāṃ mātety eke mātety eke || 2.56 ||
gurūṇāṃ pitrādīnāṃ madhya uktalakṣaṇa ācāryaḥ śreṣṭhaḥ | sa hi vidyātas taṃ janayati tac chreṣṭhaṃ janma | tenānekagurusamavāye sa eva prathamaṃ pūjyaḥ | eke tv ācāryā mātā śreṣṭheti manyante | tathā ca vasiṣṭhaḥ |
upādhyāyād daśācārya ācāryāṇāṃ śataṃ pitā |
pitur daśaguṇaṃ mātā gauraveṇātiricyate || [VaDh 13.48]
āpastambo ‘pi: “mātā putratvasya bhūyāṃsi karmāṇy ārabhate tasyāṃ śuśrūṣā nityā patitāyām api” [ĀpDh 1.28.9] | dviruktir adhyāyaparisamāptyarthā || 2.56 ||
iti śrīgautamīyavṛttau haradattaviracitāyāṃ mitākṣarāyāṃ
prathamapraśne dvitīyo ‘dhyāyaḥ
atha tṛtīyo ‘dhyāyaḥ
tasyāśramavikalpam eke bruvate || 3.1 ||
tasyaivam adhītavedasya brahmacāriṇo vakṣyamāṇāś catvāra āśramā vikalpyanta ity eka ācāryā bruvate | anye tu samuccīyanta iti | tatra āpastambaḥ: “teṣu sarveṣu yathopadeśam avyagro vartamānaḥ kṣemaṃ gacchati” [ĀpDh 2.21.2] iti | “buddhvā karmāṇi yat kāmayeta tad ārabheta” [ĀpDh 2.21.5] iti ca | tathā ca brahmacaryāśramam uktvā “ata eva brahmacaryavān pravrajati” [= ĀpDh 2.21.8] iti baudhāyanaḥ | manunā tu samuccayo darśitaḥ |
ṛṇāni trīṇy apākṛtya mano mokṣe niveśayet |
anapākṛtya mokṣaṃ tu vrajamānaḥ pataty adhaḥ || [MDh 6.35]
sarve ‘pi kramaśas tv ete yathāśāstraṃ niveṣitāḥ |
yathoktakāriṇaṃ vipraṃ nayanti paramāṃ gatim || [MDh 6.88] iti || 3.1 ||
ke punas ta āśramāḥ |
brahmacārī gṛhastho bhikṣur vaikhānasaḥ || 3.2 ||
yady apy asau pūrvam api brahmacaryāśrama uktas tathāpi prapitsitanaiṣṭhikabrahmacāritvam atra vivakṣitam | bhikṣuḥ saṃnyāsī | vaikhānaso vānaprasthaḥ | vaikhānasaproktena mārgeṇa vartata iti | tena sa āśramaḥ prādhānyena darśitaḥ | śāstrāntareṣu vaikhānasas tṛtīyo bhikṣus caturtha āśramaḥ | iha tu kramabhedaḥ “prāguktās traya āśramiṇaḥ” [GDh 28.49] ity atra vaikhānasavarjanārthaḥ || 3.2 ||
teṣāṃ gṛhastho yonir aprajanatvād itareṣām || 3.3 ||
teṣāṃ caturṣv apy āśrameṣu vartamānānāṃ gṛhastho yonir upasatti[utpatti]sthānam | gṛhasthenaivotpāditāś caturbhir āśramair adhikriyante | gṛhasthavyatiriktāśramasthānāṃ prajotpādanasya niṣiddhatvāt | tatra śātātapaḥ |
caṇḍālāḥ pratyavasitāḥ parivrājakatāpasāḥ |
teṣāṃ jātāny apatyāni caṇḍālaiḥ saha vāsayet || iti || 3.3 ||
idānīm āśramadharmān vakṣyan prathamanirdiṣṭasya brahmacāriṇa āha |
tatroktaṃ brahmacāriṇaḥ || 3.4 ||
tatra teṣāṃ madhye brahmacāriṇo naiṣṭhikasya yad upakurvānasya “upanayanādir niyamaḥ” [GDh 2.10] ity ārabhyoktam tad evāsyāpīty uktaṃ bhavati || 3.4 ||
tatra viśeṣaḥ |
ācāryādhīnatvam āntam || 3.5 ||
āntam ā dehapātam | ācāryakula eva tacchuśrūṣayā varteta || 3.5 ||
guroḥ karmaśeṣeṇa japet || 3.6 ||
ācārye prakṛte guruśabdaḥ pitror api grahaṇārthaḥ | tataś cācāryaṃ pitarau ca śuśrūṣamāṇas tadvyatirikte kāle japed vedam adhīyīta | na tu svādhīno bhavet || 3.6 ||
gurvabhāve tadapatyavṛttis tadabhāve vṛddhe
sabrahmacāriṇy agnau vā || 3.7 ||
ācārye yā vṛttir abhihitā sā tadabhāve tatputre, tatputrābhāve vṛddhe vidyayā vayasā vādhike, vṛddhābhāve sabrahmacāriṇi, sabrahmacāryabhāve ‘gnau vā kartavyā | samidādhānādibhir agnau vṛttiḥ || 3.7 ||
evaṃvṛtto brahmalokam avāpnoti jitendriyaḥ || 3.8 ||
spaṣṭo ‘rthaḥ | jitendriyatvaṃ manunā darśitam |
śrutvā spṛṣṭvā ca dṛṣṭvā ca bhuktvā ghrātvā ca yo naraḥ |
na hṛṣyati glāyati vā sa vijñeyo jitendriyaḥ || MDh 2.98] || 3.8 ||
uttareṣāṃ caitad avirodhi || 3.9 ||
uttareṣām apy āśramāṇām asmin vṛtte yad aviruddhaṃ tat samānam | yathā dyūtādivarjanam | viruddhaṃ yathā – agnikāryaṃ pravrajitasya, gurukulavāso vaikhānasasya, brahmacaryaṃ gṛhasthasyety aparā vṛttiḥ | uttareṣāṃ cāśramāṇāṃ dharmajātam etasya draṣṭavyam | kim aviśeṣeṇa | na | etad avirodhi | etad āśramadharmāvirodhi “na mlecchāśucyadhārmikaiḥ saha saṃbhāṣeta” [GDh 9.16] ity evamādy asyāpi bhavati || 3.9 |||
bahuvaktavyatvāt kramaprāptam api gṛhastham ullaṅghya bhikṣor dharmān āha |
anicayo bhikṣuḥ || 3.10 ||
nicayo dravyasaṃgrahas tadrahitaḥ syāt || 3.10 ||
ūrdhvaretāḥ || 3.11 ||
“uttareṣāṃ caitad avirodhi” [GDh 3.9] iti jitendriyatve siddhe ‘pi punar ūrdhvaretā iti retasaḥ srotobhaṅgo yathā bhavet tathā prayatetety evamartham || 3.11 ||
dhruvaśīlo varṣāsu || 3.12 ||
varṣāśabdo nityaṃ bahuvacanāntaḥ | varṣartau sati dhruvaśīlaḥ syād ekatra tiṣṭhed iti || 3.12 ||
bhikṣārthī grāmam iyāt || 3.13 ||
bhikṣākāla eva grāmaṃ praviśet | śeṣakālaṃ devālayādau vṛkṣamūleṣu vā vaset || 3.13 ||
jaghanyam anivṛttaṃ caret || 3.14 ||
bhikṣākāle yad gṛham anupapattyā vilambitaṃ na tad bhūyas tadahaḥ praviśet | tatra manuḥ |
vidhūme sannamusale vyaṅgāre bhuktavajjane |
vṛtte śarāvasaṃpāte bhikṣāṃ nityaṃ yatiś caret || [MDh 6.56] || 3.14 ||
nivṛttāśīḥ || 3.15 ||
adhikabhikṣālābhārthaṃ gṛheṣv āśīrvādaparo na syāt || 3.15 ||
vākcakṣuḥkarmasaṃyataḥ || 3.16 ||
vāksaṃyamo maunam | cakṣuḥsaṃyamaḥ pādavikṣepapradeśād anyatra cakṣuṣor apravartanam | karmasaṃyamo bhikṣoś coditakarmānatikramaḥ | atra vāksaṃyamavirodhe tu smṛtyantaram |
dharmayogaṃ pathipraśnaṃ svādhyāyaṃ ca tathaiva ca |
bhikṣārthaṃ dehivacanaṃ na nindati yater api || iti || 3.16 ||
kaupīnācchādanārthe vāso bibhṛyāt || 3.17 ||
kaupīnam iti guhyapradeśasya nāma | tad ācchādyate yāvatā tāvad eva vāso bibhṛyāt | adhikaṃ tu prāvaraṇādi na bibhṛyāt || 3.17 ||
prahīṇam eke nirṇijya || 3.18 ||
eke manyante tad api kaupīnācchādanaṃ prahīṇaṃ jīrṇaṃ tathānyais tyaktaṃ prakṣālya bibhṛyāt || 3.18 ||
nāviprayuktam oṣadhivanaspatīnām aṅgam
upādadīta || 3.19 ||
vṛkṣalatādīnām aṅgaṃ phalapatrādyaviprayuktaṃ tato ‘pracyutaṃ nopādadīta na gṛhṇīyāt | svayaṃ patitaṃ tu gṛhṇīyāt || 3.19 ||
na dvitīyām apartu rātriṃ grāme vaset || 3.20 ||
yatra varṣartau dhruvaśīlatoktā tam ṛtuṃ varjayitvā ṛtvantareṣu yatraikāṃ rātrim uṣitas tatra grāme na dvitīyāṃ vaset | grāmaikarātraḥ syād iti || 3.20 ||
muṇḍaḥ śikhī vā || 3.21 ||
sarvān eva keśān saha śikayā vāpayet | śikhāvarjaṃ vāpayed vā | muṇḍaḥ śikhī veti vikalpenaikadaṇḍatridaṇḍagrahaṇavikalpo ‘py uktaḥ | atra śrutismṛtī |
agner iva śikhā nānyā yasya jñānamayī śikhā |
sa śikhīty ucyate vidvān netare keśadhāriṇaḥ || iti | [Brahma Up 87]
saśikhaṃ vapanaṃ kṛtvā bahiḥsūtraṃ tyajed budhaḥ | [Brahma Up 86]
ekadaṇḍaṃ gṛhītvā ca bhikṣudharmaṃ samācaret |
śikhī yajñopavītī ca yad vā samyak prabodhitaḥ |
tridaṇḍagrahaṇaṃ kṛtvā bhikṣudharmaṃ samācaret || 3.21 ||
varjayed bījavadham || 3.22 ||
bījāni vrīhyādīni | teṣāṃ vadho musalādināvaghātaḥ | taṃ na kuryāt | tena taṇḍulasyaudanīkaraṇam apy upalakṣitam, pakvānnasyaiva svāmitvād asya || 3.22 ||
samo bhūteṣu hiṃsānugrahayoḥ || 3.23 ||
hiṃsāyām anugrahe ca bhūteṣu samo yo hinasti yo vānugṛhṇāti tatra tatra nirvikāra iti || 3.23 ||
anārambhī || 3.24 ||
aihikaṃ pāratrikaṃ ca na kaṃcid ārambhaṃ kuryāt | yathāha āpastambaḥ: “aniho ‘namutraś caret” [ĀpDh 2.21.10] iti || 3.24 ||
atha vaikhānasasyāha |
vaikhānaso vane mūlaphalāśī tapaḥśīlaḥ || 3.25 ||
vaikhānaso vānaprastho vane vasan mūlāni phalāni ca pakvāni vāśnīyān na punar odanam | tapaḥ kāyapariśoṣaṇam | tataś ca mūlaphalāny api svalpāny evāśnīyād iti || 3.25 ||
śrāvaṇakenāgnim ādhāya || 3.26 ||
śrāvaṇakaṃ nāma vaikhānasaṃ śāstram | taduktena prakāreṇāgnim ādhāya sāyaṃ prātar juhuyād iti śeṣaḥ || 3.26 ||
agrāmyabhojī || 3.27 ||
phalamūlāny api grāmyāṇi na bhuñjīta || 3.27 ||
devapitṛmanuṣyabhūtarṣipūjakaḥ || 3.28 ||
vanyair eva phalamūlair ahar ahaḥ pañca mahāyajñān kuryāt | atra manuḥ |
āraṇyair vividhair medhyaiḥ śākamūlaphalena vā |
etān eva mahāyajñān nirvaped vidhipūrvakam || iti [MDh 6.5] || 3.28 ||
sarvātithiḥ pratiṣiddhavarjam || 3.29 ||
ya enam upāgacchanti te sarve ‘syātithayaḥ | na punar “brāhmaṇasyānatithir abrāḥmaṇaḥ” [GDh 5.39] ity ayaṃ niyamo ‘sti | tatrāpi stenapatitādīn varjayet pratiṣiddhavarjam || 3.29 ||
vaiṣkam apy upayuñjīta || 3.30 ||
viṣkā duṣṭamṛgā vyāghrādayaḥ, tair hataṃ māṃsaṃ vaiṣkam | tad apy upayuñjīta | apiśabdo gauṇārthaḥ | phalamūlādyabhāve tad api bhakṣyam iti | tatrāpi “pañca pañcanakhā bhakṣyāḥ” ity etad vātiriktaṃ varjayitvā | “pratiṣiddhavarjam” iti padaṃ kākākṣinyāyenobhayatra saṃbadhyate || 3.30 ||
na phālakṛṣṭam adhitiṣṭhet || 3.31 ||
araṇye vasan halena kṛṣṭaṃ pradeśaṃ nādhivaset || 3.31 ||
grāmaṃ ca na praviśet || 3.32 ||
vane vasato ‘pi yādṛcchikopagrāmapraveśo niṣiddhaḥ || 3.32 ||
jaṭilaś cīrājinavāsāḥ || 3.33 ||
jaṭilaḥ keśaśmaśrulomanakhadhārī | cīraṃ darbhādinirmitaṃ vāsaḥ, ajinam uttarīyam, tathā ca smṛtyantare vyavasthādarśanāt || 3.33 ||
nātisaṃvatsaraṃ bhuñjīta || 3.34 ||
saṃvatsaram atikrāntam atisaṃvatsaram, tad āraṇyam api nāśnīyāt | atra manuḥ |
tyajed āśvajuye māsi hy utpannaṃ pūrvasaṃcitam |
jīrṇāni caiva vāsāṃsi puṣpamūlaphalāni ca || iti [MDh 6.15] || 3.34 ||
uktā āśramās teṣāṃ vikalpasamuccayāv api darśitau | teṣāṃ prādhānyaṃ darśayati |
aikāśramyaṃ tv ācāryāḥ pratyakṣavidhānād
gārhasthyasya gārhasthyasya || 3.35 ||
tuśabdo viśeṣavācī | sarveṣu vedaśāstretihāsapurāṇeṣu gṛhasthadharmā evāgnihotrādayaḥ prācuryeṇa vidhīyante | tataḥ sarva evācāryā gārhasth[y]asyaikāśramyaṃ prādhānyaṃ manyante | tatrāśaktānām itarāśramadharmā vidhīyante | pratyakṣavidhānād itarāśramāṇāṃ pratyakṣeṇopajīvyatvāt | dviruktir vyākhyātā || 3.35 ||
iti śrīgautamīyavṛttau haradattaviracitāyāṃ mitākṣarāyāṃ
prathamapraśne tṛtīyo ‘dhyāyaḥ
atha caturtho ‘dhyāyaḥ
gṛhasthadharmā ucyante |
gṛhasthaḥ sadṛśīṃ bhāryāṃ vindetānanyapūrvāṃ
yavīyasīm || 4.1 ||
gṛhastha iti bhāvisaṃjñāvyapadeśaḥ | atha vā gṛhasthasya ye dharmās te vivāhāt prāg api snātakasyāpi samā iti darśanārthaṃ ca | jātyā kulena ca sadṛśīm | anyasmai vācāpy adattām | avaravayasīm | evaṃbhūtāṃ bhāryāṃ vindeta udvahet || 4.1 ||
asamānapravarair vivāhaḥ || 4.2 ||
samāna ekaḥ pravaro yeṣāṃ taiḥ saha na vivāhaḥ | tad yathā haritakutsapiṅgaśaṅkhadarbhahaimakabhavānām āṅgirasāmbarīṣayauvanāś ceti | hārītaḥ kautsīṃ nodvahed ityādipravaraprapañca āpastambīye draṣṭavyaḥ || 4.2 ||
ūrdhvaṃ saptamāt pitṛbandhubhyo bījinaś ca
mātṛbandhubhyaḥ pañcamāt || 4.3 ||
pitaram ārabhya tadbandhuvarge gaṇyamāne saptamāc chirasa ūrdhvaṃ jātāṃ kanyakām udvahet | mātaram ārabhya tadbandhuvarge gaṇyamāne pañcamāc chirasa ūrdhvaṃ jātām udvahet | bījinaś ca saptamād ūrdhvam iti cakārāt sidhyati | yathā kṣetrī badhnyo rugṇo vā devaraṃ prārthayate mama kṣetre putram utpādayeti | yad vā saṃtānakṣaye vidhavāṃ guravo niyuñjate, dṛṣṭaṃ vicitravīryakṣetre satyavatīvākyād vyāso dhṛtarāṣṭrādīn utpāditavān iti | yathāha yājñavalkyaḥ |
aputreṇa parakṣetre niyogotpāditaḥ sutaḥ |
ubhayor apy asau rikthī piṇḍadātā ca dharmataḥ || iti | [YDh 2.127]
tadviṣayam etad “bījinaś ca” iti || 4.3 ||
atha vivāhabhedāḥ |
brahmo vidyācāritrabandhuśīlasaṃpannāya dadyād
ācchādyālaṃkṛtām || 4.4 ||
vidyā vedavidyā | cāritraṃ coditakarmānuṣṭhānam | bandhavo jñātayo mātulādayaś ca | śīlaṃ vihiteṣu śraddhā | etair guṇaiḥ saṃpannāya vastrayugulenācchādya yathāvibhavam alaṃkṛtāṃ kanyāṃ dadyāt | evaṃvidhasya vivāhasya brāhmasaṃjñā || 4.4 ||
saṃyogamantraḥ prājāpatye saha dharmaś
caryatām iti || 4.5 ||
prājāpatyasaṃjñake vivāhe “saha dharmaś caryatām” iti pradānamantraḥ | yady api brāhmādiṣv api sahadharmacaryā bhavati tathāpy āntād anayā sahā dharmaś caritavyaḥ | nāśramāntaraṃ praveṣṭavyaṃ nāpi stryantaram upayantavyam iti mantreṇa samayaḥ kriyate | eṣa brāhmādeḥ prājāpatyasya viśeṣaḥ | ācchādyālaṃkṛtām iti samānam || 4.5 ||
ārṣe gomithunaṃ kanyāvate dadyāt || 4.6 ||
ārṣasaṃjñake vivāhe gomithunaṃ strīpūṃrūpaṃ kanyāvate dadyād varas tadbandhur vā kaścit | ācchādyālaṃkṛtām iti samānam || 4.6 ||
antarvedy ṛtvije dānaṃ daivo ‘laṃkṛtya || 4.7 ||
antarvedi, vedyā [vedyāṃ?] dakṣiṇākāla ṛtvije karma kurvate yad alaṃkṛtya kanyāyā dānaṃ sa daivo vivāhaḥ | ācchādyālaṃkṛtām iti | prakṛte punar alaṃkṛtyeti vacanaṃ varasyāpy aṅgulīyakādibhir alaṃkārārtham || 4.7 ||
icchantyāḥ svayaṃ saṃyogo gāndharvaḥ || 4.8 ||
icchantyā vadhvā icchato varasya saṃyogo gāndharvo vivāhaḥ | svayam iti vacanād varecchā gamyate || 4.8 ||
vittenānatiḥ strīmatām āsuraḥ || 4.9 ||
yatra strīmatāṃ kanyāvatāṃ pitrādīnāṃ vittena dhanapradānenānatir ārjanaṃ kriyate sa āsuro vivāhaḥ | atra yājñavalkyaḥ: “āsuro dravaṇādānād” [YDh 1.61] iti | manuś ca |
jñātibhyo draviṇaṃ datvā kanyāyai ca svaśaktitaḥ |
kanyāpradānaṃ svāc chandyād āsuro dharma ucyate || [MDh 3.31]
strīmatām iti vacanān na kevalaṃ kanyāyai dhanapradānam āsuratvanibandhanam | tathā ca smṛtyantaram |
yāsāṃ nādadate śulkaṃ jñātayo na sa vikrayaḥ |
arhaṇaṃ tat kumārīṇām ānṛśaṃsyān na kevalam || iti || 4.9 ||
prasahyādānād rākṣasaḥ || 4.10 ||
balātkāreṇa kanyāvato nirjitya yad ādānaṃ sa rākṣaso vivāhaḥ || 4.10 ||
asaṃvijñātopasaṃgamāt paiśācaḥ || 4.11 ||
suptā māttā pramattā vā yatrāsaṃvijñātam upagamyate sa paiśāco vivāhaḥ || 4.11 ||
evam aṣṭau vivāhā uktās teṣu,
catvāro dharmyāḥ prathamāḥ || 4.12 ||
āditaś catvāro vivāhāḥ sarvavarṇānāṃ dharmyā dharmād anapetāḥ praśastā bhavanti || 4.12 ||
ṣaḍ ity eke || 4.13 ||
eke smartāraḥ ṣaḍ dharmyā ity āhuḥ | gāndharvāsurayor api dharmād anapetatvam
icchanti || 4.13 ||
krayavivāhe kṣatriyādiṣu strīṣu brāhmaṇādibhyo jātānāṃ putrāṇāṃ śāstreṣu saṃketitaṃ saṃjñābhedam āha |
anulomānantaraikāntaradvyantarāsu jātāḥ
savarṇāmbaṣṭhograniṣādadauṣmantapāraśavāḥ || 4.14 ||
brāhmaṇasyānantarā kṣatriyā tasyāṃ jātaḥ savarṇaḥ | kṣatriyasya vaiśyā tasyāṃ tasmād ambaṣṭhaḥ | vaiśyasyānantarā śūdrā tasyāṃ tasmād ugrah | brāhmaṇasyaikantarā vaiśyā tasyāṃ tasmān niṣādaḥ | kṣatriyasyaikantarā śūdrā tasyāṃ tasmād dauṣmantaḥ | brāhmaṇasya dvyantarā śūdrā tasyāṃ tasmāt pāraśavaḥ | prapañco jātinirṇayasya smṛtyantare draṣṭavyaḥ || 4.14 ||
prātilomyena jātān āha |
pratilomās tu sūtamāgadhāyogavakṛtavaidehaka-
caṇḍālāḥ || 4.15 ||
anantaraikāntaradvyantarāsu jātā ity anyvartate | kṣatriyasyānantarā brāhmaṇī tasyāṃ tasmāt sūtaḥ | vaiśyasyānantarā kṣatriyā tasyāṃ tasmān māgadhaḥ | śūdrasyānantarā vaiśyā tasyāṃ tasmād āyogavaḥ | vaiśyasaikāntarā brāhmaṇī tasyāṃ tasmāt kṛtaḥ | śūdrasyaikāntarā kṣatriyā tasyāṃ tasmād vaidehakaḥ | śūdrasya dvyantarā brāhmaṇī tasyāṃ tasmāc caṇḍāla iti || 4.15 ||
anyeṣāṃ matena teṣām eva prativarṇaṃ saṃgṛhya saṃjñābhedān āha —
brāhmaṇy ajījanat putrān varṇebhya ānupūrvyād
brāhmaṇasūtamāgadhacaṇḍalān || 4.16 ||
spaṣṭo ‘rthaḥ | atrānupūrvyagrahaṇaṃ varṇakramavivakṣāparam | na tv anulomaparam || 4.16 ||
tebhya eva kṣatriyā mūrdhavasiktakṣatriyadhīvara-
pulkasāṃs tebhya eva vaiśyā bhṛjjakaṇṭhamāhiṣya-
vaiśyavaidehān pāraśavayavanakaraṇaśūdrāñ
chūdrety eke || 4.17 ||
eke smartāra ity uktakrameṇa brāhmaṇy ajījanad ity ārabhya brāhmaṇīkṣatriyāvaiśyāśūdrāsu brāhmaṇādivarṇebhyaḥ kramena jātānāṃ saṃjñābhedān manyante || 4.17 ||
varṇāntaragamanam utkarṣāpakarṣābhyāṃ saptame
pañcame vācāryāḥ || 4.18 ||
manyanta iti vākyaśeṣaḥ | teṣām eva savarṇādīnām anulomajātānām utkarṣeṇa pitṛdvārā saptamapuruṣād utkṛṣṭavarṇāntaraprāptir bhavati | apakarṣeṇa mātṛdvārā pañcamapuruṣād apakṛṣṭavarṇāntaraprāptir bhavati | tad yathā – brāhmaṇenoḍhāyāṃ kṣatriyāyām utpāditā savarṇā sāpi brāhmaṇenoḍhā tasyām utpāditā cety evam ā saptamyāḥ saptamī tu brāhmaṇenoḍhā yad apatyaṃ sūte tad brāhmaṇajātīyam eva bhavati | evaṃ brāhmaṇena kṣatriyāyām utpāditaḥ putraḥ savarṇaḥ so ‘pi kṣatriyām udvāhya putram utpādayati so ‘pi kṣatriyām ity evam ā pañcamāt pañcamas tu kṣatriyāyāṃ yad apatyam utpādayati tat kṣatriyajātīyam eva bhavati | vikalpasyaivaṃ cārthaḥ | tatrāpi varṇāntaragamane vṛttasvādhyāyabāhulye sati pañcamenotkṛṣṭaṃ bhavati | hīnavṛttyā pañcamenāpakṛṣṭaṃ ca bhavatīti | evaṃ kṣatriyasya vaiśyāyāṃ vaiśyasya śūdrāyām api draṣṭavyam || 4.18 ||
sṛṣṭyantarajātānāṃ ca || 4.19 ||
cāturvarṇyam antareṇa cānulomajātānāṃ savarṇāmbaṣṭhādīnām apy utkarṣāpakarṣābhyām anyonyavarṇāntaragamanaṃ bhavati | tad yathā – savarṇenoḍhāyām ambaṣṭhyām utpāditā duhitā punaḥ savarṇenohyate | tasyām apy utpāditā savarṇenety ā saptamāt saptamī tu savarṇenoḍhā yad apatyaṃ sa eva bhavati | evaṃ saivāmbaṣṭhenoḍhāyāṃ duhitaraṃ sūte sāpy ambaṣṭheneti saptamī tv ambaṣṭhenoḍhā yad apatyam utpādayati so ‘mbaṣṭha eva bhavati | evam ambaṣṭhayor api draṣṭavyam || 4.19 ||
pratilomās tu dharmahīnāḥ || 4.20 ||
pratilomāj jātāḥ sūtādayo dharmahīnā upanayanādidharmahīnāḥ | tatra sūtasyaikasyopanayanamātraṃ śāstrāntare ‘ṅgīkṛtam || 4.20 ||
śūdrāyāṃ ca || 4.21 ||
ānulomyenāpi śūdrāyām utpannaḥ pāraśavādir dharmahīnaḥ | evaṃ ca savarṇādīnām anulomānāṃ siddho dharmāṅgīkāraḥ | tathā ca manuḥ |
svajātijātyantarajāḥ ṣaṭ sutā dvijadharmiṇaḥ |
śūdrāṇāṃ tu sadharmāṇaḥ sarve ‘padhvaṃsajāḥ smṛtāḥ || [MDh 10.41] || 4.21 ||
asamānāyāṃ tu śūdrāt patitavṛttiḥ || 4.22 ||
śūdrād asamānāyāṃ vaiśyādistriyām utpādita āyogavādiḥ patitavṛttiḥ patitavad darśanasparśanapratigrahādau varjanīyaḥ | evaṃ ca vaiśyāt kṣatriyāyāṃ kṣatriyād brāhmaṇyāṃ jāto na patitavṛttiḥ || 4.22 ||
antyaḥ pāpiṣṭhaḥ || 4.23 ||
śūdrād asamānāj janiteṣu teṣu yo ‘ntyo brāhmaṇyāṃ jātaś caṇḍālaḥ pāpiṣṭho ‘tyantaṃ varjanīyaḥ | tathā ca smṛtyantaram: “caṇḍālam ājagovālavyajanān pariharet” iti || 4.23 ||
atha prakṛtān vivāhān stauti |
punanti sādhavaḥ putrāḥ || 4.24 ||
acchāsu [asmāsu] jātāḥ sādhavaḥ sādhuvṛttayaḥ putrā janayituḥ kulaṃ punanti || 4.24 ||
tatra viśeṣaḥ |
tripuruṣam ārṣāt || 4.25 ||
ārṣavivāhoḍhāyāṃ jātaḥ putras trīn puruṣān punāti narakād uddharati || 4.25 ||
daśa daivād daśaiva prājāpatyāt || 4.26 ||
upasamastam api puruṣapadam atra daśaśabdena saṃbadhyate | evakāro nirdhāraṇaparaḥ || 4.26 ||
daśa pūrvān daśa parān ātmānaṃ ca brāhmīputro
brāhmīputraḥ || 4.27 ||
brāhmavivāhenoḍhā brāhmī tasyāṃ jātaḥ putraḥ pitrādīn daśa pūrvān daśa parān bhaviṣyataḥ putrādīṃś ca daśātmānaṃ caikaviṃśaṃ punāti | tasmād brāhmo vivāhaḥ praśastatamaḥ || 4.27 ||
iti śrīgautamīyavṛttau haradattaviracitāyāṃ mitākṣarāyāṃ
prathamapraśne caturtho ‘dhyāyaḥ
atha pañcamo ‘dhyāyaḥ
garbhādhānakālaṃ prastauti |
ṛtāv upeyāt || 5.1 ||
rajodarśanād ārabhya ṣoḍaśāhorātrā ṛtuḥ strīṇāṃ garbhagrahaṇakālas tatropagacched bhāryām | tatra āpastambīye viśeṣaḥ: “caturthīprabhṛty ā ṣoḍaśīm uttarām uttarāṃ yugmāṃ prajāniḥśreyasam ṛtugamanam ity upadiśanti” | mānavaṃ tu |
ṛtuḥ svābhāvikaḥ strīṇāṃ rātrayaḥ ṣoḍhaśa smṛtāḥ |
caturbhir etaiḥ sārdham ahobhiḥ sadvigarhitaiḥ ||
tāsām ādyāś catasras tu nindyā ekādaśī tu yā |
trayodaśī ca śeṣās tu praśastā daśa rātrayaḥ || [MDh 3.46–47]
amāvāsyām aṣṭamīṃ ca paurṇamāsīṃ caturdaśīm |
brahamacārī bhaven nityam apy ṛtau snātako dvijaḥ || [MDh 4.128] iti |
yājñavalkyas tu |
evaṃ gacchan striyaṃ kṣāmāṃ maghāṃ mūlaṃ ca varjayet | [YDh 1.80]
yugmāsu putrā jāyante striyo ‘yugmāsu rātriṣu | [MDh 3.48]
iti ca | tad iha ṣoḍaśasu rātriṣv āditas tisraḥ sarvathā varjyāḥ | itarāsu gacched iti sarvasmṛticoditaniṣedān parityajya gacchann utkṛṣṭaṃ putraṃ janayati | dveṣādinā ṛtāv anupayan pratyaveyād iti | tathā ca devalaḥ |
yaḥ svadārān ṛtusnātān svasthaḥ san nopagacchati |
bhrūṇahatyām avāpnoti garbhaṃ prāptaṃ vināśya saḥ || iti |
smṛtyantaraṃ ca |
ṛtusnātāṃ tu yo bhāryāṃ saṃnidhau nopagacchati |
tasyā rajasi taṃ māsaṃ pitaras tasya śerate || iti |
ayaṃ tu rāgataḥ prāptatve saty apy akaraṇe pratyavāyaśravaṇād dvividhaś ca bhavati | ṛtāv evopeyad eveti kasyacin matena niyamaś ca bhavati || 5.1 ||
ācāryas tu parisaṃkhyānaṃ ca pratipādayati |
sarvatra vā pratiṣiddhavarjam || 5.2 ||
sarveṣu vā kāleṣūpeyād ṛtāv anṛtau ca pratiṣiddhadivasān varjayitvā |
yathākāmī bhaved vāpi strīṇāṃ varam anusmaran | iti | [YDh 1.81]
varaś ca – kāmam ā vijanitoḥ saṃbhavāmeti | yad apy ātmano jitendriyatve saty api “dharmadārāḥ sarvadā rakṣaṇīyāḥ” iti smaryate | “apramattā rakṣata tantum enaṃ mā vaḥ kṣetre parabījāni vāpsuḥ” [VaDh 17.9] iti || 5.2 ||
atha pañca mahāyajñāḥ |
devapitṛmanuṣyabhūtarṣipūjakaḥ || 5.3 ||
atra pūjakaśabdo devādiṣu pratyekaṃ saṃbadhyate | dvandvāntyaṃ śrūyamāṇaṃ pratyekam abhisaṃbadhyate | gṛhastho nityaṃ devādipūjakaḥ syāt | tatra devapūjā vaiśvadevasakalahomādyagnikāryaṃ ca | pitṛpūjāṃ manur āha |
ekam apy āśayed vipraṃ pitrarthe pāñcayājñike |
na caivātrāśayet kiṃcid vaiśvadevaṃ prati dvijam || [MDh 3.83] iti |
dadyād ahar ahaḥ śrāddham annādyenodakena ca |
payomūlaphalair vāpi pitṛbhyaḥ prītim āvahan || [MDh 3.82] iti ca |
manuṣyapūjā atithipūjā | bhūtapūjā baliharaṇam | ṛṣipūjā brahmayajñaḥ | ṛṣiyajño brahmayajña iti paryāyaḥ || 5.3 ||
te ca pratipādyante | atra kramo na vivakṣitaḥ | brahmayajñas tu |
nityasvādhyāyaḥ || 5.4 ||
bahuvrīhir ayam | tatprakāraś ca “brahmayajñena yakṣyamāṇa” ity ārabhya taittirīyake ‘bhihitaḥ | atra nityaśabdād brahmayajñavyatiriktakāle ‘pi yathāvasaram adhīyīteti darśitam || 5.4 ||
pitṛyajñas tu |
pitṛbhyaś codakadānaṃ yathotsāham anyat || 5.5 ||
pitṛbhyo nityam udakaṃ dadyāt | anyad bhojanaphalamūlādi yathotsāhaṃ yathāśakti dadyāt | atra cakārān nityaṃ devarṣīṇām api tarpaṇaṃ kartavyam iti darśitam | tatra manur brahmacāriṇaṃ prakṛtyāha |
nityaṃ snatvā śuciḥ kuryād devarṣipitṛtarpaṇam || iti | [MDh 2.176]
kātyāyanaḥ |
devatānāṃ pitṝṇāṃ ca jale dadyāj jalāñjalim |
asaṃskṛtapramītānāṃ sthale dadyāj jalāñjalim || iti |
bhṛguḥ |
nodakeṣu na pātreṣu na kruddho naikapāṇinā |
nopatiṣṭhati tat toyaṃ yad bhūmyāṃ na pradīyate ||
uśanā |
āpo devagaṇāḥ sarva āpaḥ pitṛgaṇāḥ smṛtāḥ |
tasmād apsu jalaṃ deyaṃ pitṝṇāṃ dattam akṣayam || iti |
bhṛguḥ prakārāntaram āha |
nābhimātre jale sthitvā cintayann ūrdhvamānasaḥ |
āgacchantu me pitara imaṃ gṛhṇantv apo ‘ñjalim ||
tris trir añjalim ākāśam uccair uccataraṃ budhaḥ |
uktvā coktvā kṣipan vāri vāgyato dakṣiṇāmukhaḥ || iti || 5.5 ||
devayajñasyāgnikāryamūlatvād agniparigrahakālaṃ tāvad āha |
bhāryādir agnir dāyādir vā || 5.6 ||
bhāryāśabdena vivāho lakṣyate | yasminn agnau bhāryohyate tam agnim ārabhya vā yasminn ahani pitṛbhrātrādibhir dāyavibhāgaḥ kriyate tad ahar ārabhya vā sāyam upakramam agniṃ paricaret | dāyavibhāgāt pūrvaṃ pitur jyeṣṭhabhrātur vā karmaṇy upajīvato na pratyavāyaḥ || 5.6 ||
tasmin gṛhyāṇi karmāṇi || 5.7 ||
tasminn evaṃparigṛhīte ‘gnau gṛhye manuṣyabhūtāni puṃsavanādīni karmāṇi kartavyānīti || 5.7 ||
devapitṛmanuṣyayajñāḥ svādhyāyaś ca balikarma || 5.8 ||
tasminn ity anuvartate | tatra devayajñasyāgnisaṃbandhaḥ prasiddhaḥ | pitṛmanuṣyabhūtayajñānāṃ tu tadartham annam asmin gṛhyāgnau pacyata iti | brahmayajñasyāgnisaṃbandha uśanasā pakṣe darśitaḥ: “agnisamīpa ity ekeṣām” iti | aparigṛhītāgner api pañcamahāyajñavidhānād ete laukike ‘gnau kartavyāḥ || 5.8
atha devayajñavaiśvadevaprayogam āha |
agnāv agnir dhanvantarir viśve devāḥ prajāpatiḥ
sviṣṭakṛd iti homaḥ || 5.9 ||
atra “juhoticodanāḥ svāhākāraprad(h)ānāḥ” ity āpastambasmaraṇād dhomaśabdena svāhākāra uktaḥ | agnyādibhiḥ svāhākārāntair ganau juhuyāt | sviṣṭakṛc cāgnyupapado draṣṭavyaḥ | homatvād evāgnisiddhāv agnāv iti vacanaṃ baliharaṇavad bhūmau na kartavyam iti vacanārthm | tasmād dhomadharmaḥ svāhākāro baliharaṇeṣv api bhavati | tathā ca āśvalāyanaḥ: “svāhety atha baliharaṇam” iti | āpastambīyānāṃ svāhākārāntā eva mantrāḥ paṭhitāḥ || 5.9 ||
atha bhūtayajñabaliharaṇam āha |
digdevatābhyaś ca yathāsvam || 5.10 ||
yasyā devatāyā yā dik tasyāṃ diśi tasyai devatāyai balir hartavyaḥ | indrāya svāhetīśānaparyantaṃ prāgādi pradakṣiṇaṃ kartavyam || 5.10 ||
dvārṣu mahadbhyaḥ || 5.11 ||
gṛhasya yāvatyo dvāras tāsu mahadbhyaḥ svāheti baliharaṇam || 5.11 ||
gṛhadevatābhyaḥ praviśya || 5.12 ||
antaḥ praviśya gṛhadevatābhyaḥ svāheti baliharaṇam | praviśyeti vacanād dvārabahiṣṭhena na kartavyam || 5.12 ||
brahmaṇe madhye || 5.13 ||
gṛhasya madhye brahmaṇe svāheti balir hartavyaḥ | digdevatābhyaś ceti cakārāt pṛthivī vāyuḥ prajāpatir viśve devā iti sūtrakāroktadevatābhyaś ca brahmaṇo ‘nantaraṃ balir hartavyaḥ || 5.13 ||
ākāśāyety antarkṣe balir utkṣepyaḥ || 5.14 ||
viśvebhyaś caiva devebhyo balir ākāśa utkṣipet |
divācarebhyo bhūtebhyo naktaṃcāribhya eva ca || [MDh 3.90]
iti manuḥ || 5.14 ||
naktaṃcarebhyaś ca sāyam || 5.15 ||
sāyaṃ baliharaṇe ‘yaṃ viśeṣaḥ | naktaṃcarebhyaḥ svāheti balir hartavyaḥ | cakārāt pūrvokābhyaś ca bhavati || 5.15 ||
svastivācya bhikṣādānam appūrvam || 5.16 ||
baliharaṇānantaraṃ bhikṣādānaṃ kartavyam | svasty astv iti svastivacanam uktvā bhikṣor haste pūrvam apo datvā ceti | parivrājake viśeṣaḥ |
yatihaste jalaṃ datvā bhaikṣaṃ datvā punar jalam |
bhaikṣaṃ parvatamātraṃ syāt tajjalaṃ sāgaropamam || iti |
etad bhikṣādānam atithipūjābhāve manuṣyayajñaḥ syāt | ete pañca mahāyajñā brahmayajñavarjāḥ kartavyāḥ sāyaṃ prātaś ca | kasmāt | “atha sāyaṃ prātaḥ siddhasya haviṣyasya juhuyāt” ity āśvalāyanasmarṇāt | “sāyaṃ prātar bhūtam ity ukta” ityādi kātyāyanasūtravacanāc ca || 5.16 ||
dadātiṣu caivaṃ dharmyeṣu || 5.17 ||
dadātayo dānāni yāni dānāni dharmyāni na bhayādinimittāni teṣu caivam appūrvadānam iti || 5.17 ||
dānaprasaṅgāt phalaviśeṣam āha |
samadviguṇasāhasrān ānantyāni phalāny
abrāhmaṇabrāhmaṇaśrotriyavedapāragebhyaḥ || 5.18 ||
abrāhmaṇaḥ kṣatriyādiḥ brāhmaṇo jātimātram | śrotriyo ‘dhītavedaḥ | sāṅgaṃ sakalpaṃ sarahasyaṃ cādhītavedo vedapāragaḥ | ebhyo dattaṃ yathākramaṃ samadviguṇasāhasram ānantyaṃ ca phalaṃ dadāti | tathā ca manuḥ |
samam abrāhmaṇe dānaṃ dviguṇaṃ brāhmaṇabruve |
śrotriye śatasāhasram anantaṃ vedapārage || [MDh 7.85] iti || 5.18 ||
dānaprasaṅgād yatrāvaśyadeyam adāne ca pratyavāyas taṃ viṣayam āha |
gurvarthaniveśauṣadhārthavṛttikṣīṇa-        yakṣyamāṇādhyayanādhvasaṃyogavaiśvajiteṣu
dravyasaṃvibhāgo bahirvedi || 5.19 ||
yajñe dakṣiṇākāle sadasyebhyo yad dānaṃ tad antarvedi | tato ‘nyatra bahirvedi | dravyasaṃvibhāgo hiraṇyāder dānam | tadgurvādiviṣaye ‘vaśyaṃ kartavyam | adhītavedasya dakṣiṇārthaṃ gurvartham | niveśauṣadhārthaṃ niveśo vivāhaḥ | sa ca prathamas tadartham | auṣadhārthaṃ rugṇasya bheṣajārtham ¦ vṛttyā tadahar jīvanena hīno vṛttikṣīṇaḥ | yakṣyamāṇo yajñaṃ kariṣyan | adhyayanādhvasaṃyogaḥ | adhyayanena saṃyogo yasya so ‘dhyayanasaṃyogaḥ | adhvanā saṃyogo yasya so ‘dhvasaṃyogaḥ | bahuvrīhiḥ | vaiśvajitaḥ kṛtaviśvajidyāgaḥ | sarvasvadānena nirdravyaḥ | etair yācito ‘vaśyaṃ yathāśakti hiraṇyādi dadyāt | adadat pratyaveyād iti | bahirvedigrahaṇena sadīkṣitaviṣayam idam antarvedy anyebhyo ‘pi deyam || 5.19 ||
bhikṣamāṇeṣu kṛtānnam itareṣu || 5.20 ||
itareṣūktavyatirikteṣu bhikṣamāṇeṣu kṛtānnaṃ pakvānnam avaśyaṃ deyam | dravyāder adāne na doṣaḥ | kṛtānnaviṣaye ‘pi vasiṣṭhaḥ |
avratā hy anadhīyānā yatra bhaikṣacarā dvijāḥ |
taṃ grāmaṃ daṇḍayed rājā coradaṇḍavrato hi saḥ || [VaDh 3.4] iti || 5.20 ||
atha dānāpavādaḥ |
pratiśrutyāpy adharmasaṃyuktāya na dadyāt || 5.21 ||
dāsyāmīti pratiśrutyāpy adharmasaṃyuktaviṣaye na dadyāt | yadi tena dravyeṇādharmasaṃyuktaṃ veśyāgamanādy asau kariṣyatīti vijānīyat || adharmasaṃyukta iti vacanād anyatra pratiśrutam adadat pratyaveyād iti darśayati || 5.21 ||
pratiśravaṇaviṣaye viśeṣam āha |
kruddhahṛṣṭabhītārtalubdhabālasthaviramūḍha-
mattonmattavākyāny anṛtāny apātakāni || 5.22 ||
kruddhādivākyāny anṛtāny ayathārthāny apy apātakāni na pāpaṃ janayanti | kruddhaḥ krodhāviṣṭaḥ | hṛṣṭo harvāviṣṭaḥ | bhīto bhayāviṣṭaḥ | eteṣāṃ guṇāntarair āviṣṭatvād vākyam apramāṇam | tasmāt pratiśrutyādāne ‘pi teṣām adoṣaḥ || 5.22 ||
atha gṛhasthapūrvabhojyān āha |
bhojayet pūrvam atithikumāravyādhitagarbhiṇī-
svavāsinīsthavirāñ jaghanyāṃś ca || 5.23 ||
atithir vakṣyamāṇaḥ | kumārā bālāḥ | vyādhitaḥ saṃjātavyādhiḥ | garbhiṇyaḥ prasiddhāḥ | svavāsinyo duhitaro bhaginyaś ca | sthavirā vṛddhāḥ | jaghanyāḥ paricārakādayaḥ | etān ātmanaḥ pūrvaṃ bhojayet, paścāt svayaṃ bhuñjīta | jaghanyānāṃ pṛthakpadatvaṃ teṣām ānantaryasūcanārtham || 5.23 ||
ācāryapitṛsakhīnāṃ ca nivedya vacanakriyā|| 5.24 ||
yadi bhojanakāla ācāryādya āgaccheyus tadā siddham annaṃ tebhyo nivedya tadannakriyā tadicchātaḥ kartavyāḥ | na teṣu saṃnihiteṣu svatantro bhaved ity arthaḥ || 5.24 ||
ṛtvigācāryaśvaśurapitṛvyamātulānām upasthāne
madhuparkaḥ || 5.25 ||
ṛtvigādiṣu gṛham āgateṣu madhuparko deyaḥ || 5.25 ||
saṃvatsare punaḥ || 5.26 ||
pūjitās te yadi saṃvatsarāt punar āgaccheyuḥ punar api madhuparko deyo nārvāg iti || 5.26 ||
yajñavivāhayor arvāk || 5.27 ||
saṃvatsarād arvāg api yajñavivāhayor āgatebhyas tebhyo madhuparko deyaḥ | madhuparkavidhir gṛhyokto draṣṭavyaḥ || 5.27 ||
rājñaś ca śrotriyasya || 5.28 ||
śrotriyasya sato rājñaś caivaṃ madhuparko deyaḥ || 5.28 ||
aśrotriyasyāsanodake || 5.29 ||
aśrotriyasya rājña āsanodakamātreṇa pūjanaṃ madhuparkaḥ || 5.29 ||
śrotriyasya tu pādyam arghyam annaviśeṣāṃś ca
prakārayet || 5.30 ||
tuśabdo na brāhmaṇaṃ vyāvartayati | śrotriyasya brāhmaṇasyātitheḥ pādyaṃ pādodakam | arghyaṃ phalopahāratāmbūlādi | annaviśeṣāḥ pāyasāpūpādayas tāṃś ca prakarṣeṇa kārayet samarthaḥ || 5.30 ||
asamartha tu |
nityaṃ vā saṃskāraviśiṣṭam || 5.31 ||
yad asya gṛhe nityaṃ vidyamānaṃ tad eva marīcajīrakādisaṃskāraviśiṣṭaṃ sādhayet || 5.31 ||
madhyato ‘nnadānam avaidye sādhuvṛtte || 5.32 ||
yas tv atithir vidyārahito ‘pi sādhuvṛtto bhavati tasminn upasthite madhyamena saṃskāreṇānnaṃ deyam || 5.32 ||
viparīteṣu tṛṇodakabhūmi svāgatam antataḥ
pūjānatyāśaś ca || 5.33 ||
viparīto vidyāyukto ‘pi na sādhuvṛttaḥ | tasminn ihāsyatām iti bhūmiṃ tṛṇam āsanam udakaṃ ca dadyāt | svāgatam antato ‘nantaraṃ svāgataṃ ca prayuñjīta | saṃbhāśaṇena pūjā kartavyā | anatyāśaś ca | atyāśaḥ pāyasāpūpādiviśiṣṭakramas tadanyo madhyamarītyā kartavyaḥ | āśo ‘śanam iti yāvat | 5.33 ||
śayyāsanāvasathānuvrajyopāsanāni sadṛkśreyasoḥ
samānāni || 5.34 ||
yo ‘tithir vidyāvṛttādinātmanā sadṛśo yaś ca śreyāṃs tayor dvayor apy ātmanā tulyāni śayyāsanādīni deyāni | āvasatho gṛhe sthānaviśeṣaḥ | anuvrajyopāsanayor ātmany asaṃbhavāt tulyatvaṃ na saṃbhavati te api kārye ity arthaḥ || 5.34 ||
alpaśo ‘pi hīne || 5.35 ||
ātmano kiṃcid ūne ‘py atithāv āgate samāny eva śayyādīni deyānīty eke | vayaṃ tu brūmaḥ | hīne ‘tithāv āgate ‘lpaśo ‘pi śayyādīni deyāni na tu hīna iti kṛtvātyantalopaḥ kartavyaḥ || 5.35 ||
atithilakṣaṇam āha |
asamānagrāmo ‘tithir aikarātriko
‘dhivṛkṣasūropasthāyī || 5.36 ||
asamānagrāmo ‘nyagrāmavāsī | aikarātrika ekāṃ rātriṃ vasatīty aikarātrikaḥ | vṛkṣāṇām upari yadā sūryaḥ so ‘dhivṛkṣasūryaḥ kālo madhyāhnaḥ | atha vā vṛkṣāṇām upari sūryaraśmayo yadā bhavanti sa kālaḥ sāyaṃ vā | tasmin kāla upasthito ‘tithiḥ sarvathā mānyatamaḥ || 5.36 ||
kuśalānāmayārogyāṇām anupraśnaḥ || 5.37 ||
brāhmaṇādiṣu triṣu varṇeṣu pathyādisaṃgateṣu kuśalādīnām ānupūrveṇa praśnaḥ kartavyaḥ | api kuśalam āyuṣmann iti brāhmaṇaḥ praṣṭavyaḥ | apy anāmayaṃ tatra bhavata iti kṣatriyaḥ | apy arogo bhavān iti vaiśyaḥ || 5.37 ||
antyaṃ śūdrasya || 5.38 ||
kuśalādiṣu yad antyaṃ tac chūdrasya prayoktavyam apy arogo ‘stīti || 5.38 ||
brāhmaṇasyānatithir abrāhmaṇaḥ || 5.39 ||
abrāhmaṇaḥ kṣatriyādir brāhmaṇasyātithir na bhavati | pūrvoktā atithidharmās tatra na prayojyāḥ | kevalam udakānnadānādināṅgīkāryaḥ || 5.39 ||
yajñe saṃvṛtaś cet || 5.40 ||
yajñakāla āhūtaś ced atithivat pūjyaḥ || 5.40 ||
tatrāpi |
bhojanaṃ tu kṣatriyasyordhvaṃ brāhmaṇebhyaḥ || 5.41 ||
tasyātithipakṣe ‘pi brāhmaṇeṣu bhuktavatsu paścād bhojanaṃ deyam || 5.41 ||
anyān bhṛtyaiḥ sahānṛśaṃsyārtham
ānṛśaṃsyārtham || 5.42 ||
anyāñ śūdrādīn ātithyakāla āgatān bhṛtyaiḥ karmakarādibhiḥ saha sati vibhave bhojayet | yady api teṣām atithitvaṃ na bhavati tathāpy anṛśaṃsyārtham | nṛśaṃsatā pratyakṣakrauryaṃ tadrāhityāya | “ānṛśaṃsyaṃ paro dharmaḥ” ity ānṛśaṃsyam api paro dharma eveti [abhyāso 'dhyāyasamāptyarthaḥ] || 5.42 ||
iti śrīgautamīyavṛttau haradattaviracitāyāṃ mitākṣarāyāṃ
prathamapraśne pañcamo ‘dhyāyaḥ
atha ṣaṣṭho ‘dhyāyaḥ
uktātithipūjā | anyeṣām api pūjāprakāram āha |
pādopasaṃgrahaṇaṃ samavāye ‘nvaham || 6.1 ||
vakṣyamāṇānāṃ mātrādīnāṃ samavāye saṃgame pratidinaṃ pādopasaṃgrahaṇaṃ kāryam | “vyatyastapāṇinā kāryam” [MDh 2.72] iti pūrvoktaprakāreṇa || 6.1 ||
abhigamya tu viproṣya || 6.2 ||
tuśabdaḥ prakṛtavyāvṛttau | viproṣya svayaṃ vipravāsaḥ kṛtvā teṣāṃ vipravāse vā te mātṛpitrādayo yatrāsthitās tatrābhigamya pādopasaṃgrahaṇaṃ kāryam iti || 6.2 ||
tanmātṛpitrādīn āha |
mātṛpitṛtadbandhūnāṃ pūrvajānāṃ vidyāgurūṇāṃ
tadgurūṇāṃ ca || 6.3 ||
mātāpitarau prasiddhau | tadbandhavo mātulamātṛṣvasṛpitṛvyapitṛṣavasrādayaḥ | pūrvajā jyeṣṭhabhrātaraḥ | vidyāgurava ācāryopādhyāyādayaḥ | tadgurava [pr-?]ācāryādayaḥ | tadviṣayaṃ pūrvasūtradvayam iti || 6.3 ||
saṃnipāte parasya || 6.4 ||
mātrādīnāṃ yugapatsaṃnipāte samāgame parasyotkṛṣṭasya prathamam upasaṃgrahaṇaṃ kāryam | “ācāryaḥ śreṣṭho gurūṇāṃ mātety eke” [GDh 2.56] ity utkarṣaḥ pūrvoktaḥ | āpastambena tu: “ācāryaprācāryasaṃnipāte prācār[yā]yopasaṃgṛhyopasaṃjighṛkṣed ācāryam” [ĀpDh 1.8.19] ityādinopasaṃgrahaṇam uktam || 6.4 ||
abhivādanavidhim āha |
svanāma procyāham ayam ity abhivādo
jñasamavāye || 6.5 ||
yaḥ pratyabhivādanābhijñas tena saṃgame svanāma procya vyāvahārikaṃ prasiddhaṃ nāma procyāham ayam iti prakarṣeṇoccair uktvābhivādaḥ kāryaḥ | abhivādo ‘bhivādanaṃ ṇyantāder ac | evaṃ cārthajñānāṃ jñātavarasamavāye ‘bhivādanakrameṇāyam aham iti svanāma guhyaṃ procyābhivādanaṃ kāryam | hīnavyatirikābhivādyaviṣayam | tad yathā – “abhivādaye haradattaśarmā nāmāham asmi bho” iti | tatra pratyabhivādanavidhir manunā darśitaḥ |
āyuṣmān bhava saumyeti vācyo vipro ‘bhivādane |
akāraś cāsya nāmno ‘nte vācyaḥ pūrvākṣaraḥ plutaḥ || [MDh 2.125]
asyārthaḥ | vipraśabdena brāhmaṇaviṣayam idam | abhivādayitā vipra āyuṣmān bhava saumyeti vācyaḥ | asya nāmno ‘nte pūrvākṣarapluto ‘kāraś ca vācyaḥ | pūrvākṣarapluta ity akārasya viśeṣaṇam | yasmāt pūrvo yo ‘ntyaḥ sa pluto vācyaḥ | tena vyañjanavyavadhāne ‘pi bhavati | “āyuṣmān bhava saumya haradattā3a” | vyañjanavyavadhānena yathā “āyuṣmān bhava saumyāgni3da” | iti prayogaḥ | vasiṣṭhas tu saṃdhyakṣare viśeṣam āha: “āmantrite yo ‘ntyaḥ svaraḥ sa plavate saṃdhyakṣaram apragṛhyam āhuḥ” [VaDh 13.46] [i] ā u bhāvaṃ cāpadyata iti | “eco ‘pragṛhyasyādūrād ghūte pūrvasyārdhasyād uttarasyedutau” iti vaiyākaraṇaḥ | tatrānte ‘kāre prayukte tayor yvāv aci saṃhitāyām iti yakāravakārau | āyuṣmān bhava saumya pinākapāṇā3yeti viṣṇā3veti ca prayogaḥ | ajñasamavāya iti pakṣe nāyam abhivādanaprakāraḥ | tatra smṛtyantaram |
avidvāṃsaḥ pratyabhivāde nāmno ye na plutiṃ viduḥ |
kāmaṃ teṣu tu viproṣya strīṣv ivāyam ahaṃ vadet || iti |
yathā strīṣūktaprakāraṃ vinā tādātmikena deśabhāṣādinā yena kenāpi śabdenābhivādanaṃ tadvat teṣv api bhavati | abhivādanam iti sāmānyopalakṣaṇam, prakāravarjitasya stryādiprayuktasyāpy abhidhānāt | abhivādanaprakāre tv āpastambaḥ: “dakṣiṇaṃ bāhuṃ śrotrasamaṃ prasārya brāhmaṇo ‘bhivādayīta uraḥsamaṃ rājanyo madhyamaṃ vaiśyo nīcaiḥ śūdraḥ prāñjaliḥ” [ĀpDh 1.5.16] iti || 6.5 ||
strīpuṃyoge ‘bhivādato ‘niyamam eke || 6.6 ||
strīpuṃyoge jāyāpatisamavāye ‘bhivādataḥ sārvavibhaktikas tasiḥ | abhivādane prāpte ‘niyamam eke manyante | yady api bhartā pratyabhivādajñas tathāpi tadabhivādane bhāryāyā niyamaṃ necchanti | abhivādaye ‘ham iyam ityādikramo niyamas taṃ necchanti | sāmānyābhivādanamātram eva | evaṃ ca bhāryayā bhartur ahar ahar namaskāraḥ kāryaḥ | eka iti vacanād gautamasya pakṣe niyama eva || 6.6 ||
nāviproṣya strīṇām amātṛpitṛvyabhāryābhaginīnām || 6.7 ||
samavāye ‘nvaham ity asyāpavādo ‘yam | mātṛpitṛvyabhāryābhaginīvyatiriktānāṃ strīṇām aviproṣyopasaṃgrahaṇam abhivādanaṃ ca na kāryam | kiṃ tu viproṣya pratyāgamana eva kāryam | mātrādīnāṃ tv aviproṣyāpi pratyaham | tathā ca smṛtyantaram |
upasaṃgrahaṇaṃ kuryād bhaginyā mātur eva ca |
tathā pitṛvyabhāryāṇāṃ samavāye ‘nvahaṃ dvijaḥ || iti || 6.7 ||
nopasaṃgrahaṇaṃ bhrātṛbhāryāṇāṃ svasṝṇām || 6.8 ||
viproṣya pratyāgatenāsām upasaṃgrahaṇaṃ na karyam | abhivādanaṃ tu bhavaty eva | tatrātyantagurusthānīyānāṃ mātulānyādīnām upasaṃgrahaṇam anyāsām abhivādanam iti || 6.8 ||
ṛtvikchvaśurapitṛvyamātulānāṃ tu yavīyasāṃ
pratyuttānam abhivādyāḥ || 6.9 ||
ṛtvigādīnām ātmano yavīyasāṃ pratyutthānamātreṇa pūjā kāryā na punas te ‘bhivādyāḥ || 6.9 ||
tathānyaḥ pūrvaḥ pauro ‘śītikāvaraḥ śūdro ‘py
apatyasamena || 6.10 ||
ṛtvigādayo yathā pratyuttheyā nābhivādyās tathāyam api | anyas tebhyo ‘nyaḥ | pūrvo vayasādhikaḥ | pauraḥ puravāsī | vayasādhikye ‘pi puravāsād apakarṣa uktaḥ | aśītir evāśītikā tayāvaro ‘śītikāvaraḥ | nyūnāśītika ity arthaḥ | evaṃvidhaḥ śūdro ‘py apatyasamena pratyuttheyo nābhivādyaḥ | apatyasamenety antayavīyastā darśitā | śūdragrahaṇam avaravarṇopalakṣaṇam | tataś ca śūdras tribhir api varṇair vaiśyo dvābhyāṃ kṣatriyas tu brāhmaṇeneti sidhyati || 6.10 ||
avaro ‘py āryaḥ śūdreṇa || 6.11 ||
nyūnāśītikena śūdreṇāvaro ‘py āryo yavīyān apy āryas traivarṇikaḥ pratyuttheyo nābhivādyaḥ | atrāpi śūdragrahaṇam avaravarṇopalakṣaṇam | tataś ca śūdreṇa trayo varṇāḥ | vaiśyena dvau | kṣatriyeṇa brāhmaṇa ity avaravayasaḥ pratyuttheyā nābhivādyā iti sidhyati || 6.11 ||
nāma vāsya varjayet || 6.12 ||
asyeti atra vīpsālopaḥ | asyāsyotkṛṣṭotkṛṣṭasyāpakṛṣṭo na nāma gṛhṇīyāt | kiṃ tv aupacārikaṃ nāma gṛhṇīyāt || 6.12 ||
rājñaś cājapaḥ preṣyaḥ || 6.13 ||
ajapo ‘śrotriyaḥ | preṣyaḥ preṣakaraḥ | sa utkṛṣṭavarṇo brāhmaṇo ‘pi rājño ‘bhiṣiktasya nāma varjayet || 6.13 ||
bho bhavann iti vayasyaḥ samāne ‘hani jātaḥ || 6.14 ||
vayasā tulyo vayasyaḥ | samāne ‘hani jātaḥ | atrāhaḥśabdaḥ saṃvatsaravācakaḥ | ekasmin saṃvatsare jātaḥ sa bho bhavann ity anayor anyatareṇa śabdena saṃbhāṣyaḥ || 6.14 ||
daśavarṣavṛddhaḥ pauraḥ pañcabhiḥ kalābharaḥ
śrotriyaś cāraṇas tribhiḥ || 6.15 ||
pure vasan guṇahīno daśavarśavṛddhaś ca tatrāpi kalābharaś catuḥṣaṣṭikalāsv anyatamayā jīvan pañcabhir varṣair vṛddhaś ca | śrotriyo ‘dhītavedaḥ | cāraṇaḥ sahādhyāyī | ete sarve ‘pi bho bhavann iti saṃbhāṣyāḥ | āpastambas tu sarvatrābhivādanam icchati |
daśavarṣaṃ paurasakhyaṃ pañcavarṣaṃ tu cāraṇam |
trivarṣapūrvaḥ śrotriyo ‘bhivādanam arhati || iti | [ĀpDh 1.14.13]
rājanyavaiśyakarmā vidyāhīnāḥ || 6.16 ||
karmaśabdaḥ pratyekam abhisaṃbadhyate | rājanyakarmā vaiśyakarmā | brāhmaṇo ‘pi rājanyakarmaṇā vaiśyakarmaṇā vā jīvann atyantavṛddho ‘pi bho bhavānn iti saṃbhāṣyaḥ | vidyāhīnaś ca vṛddho ‘pi vidyādhikena tathā bhāṣyaḥ || 6.16 ||
dīkṣitaś ca prākkrayāt || 6.17 ||
vayasyaviṣayam idam | dīkṣitaś ca vayasyaḥ somakrayāt pūrvaṃ tathā bhāṣyaḥ | tataḥ paraṃ vṛddhavan mānyaḥ | uttamāśramaviṣaya uśanā: “śrotriyavat prāśitaḥ sarveṣāṃ gurur bhavati” iti || 6.17 ||
vittabandhukarmajātividyāvayāṃsi mānyāni
parabalīyāṃsi || 6.18 ||
vittādīnāṃ sākṣāt mānyatvāsaṃbhavāt tadvanto mānyā ity upalakṣyante | vittavān āḍhyaḥ | bandhumān viśiṣṭaiḥ sodaryādibhir yuktaḥ | karmavān yathoktakarmakārī | jātimān abhijanayuktaḥ | vidyāvān adhītavedaśāstraḥ | vayasvān vayasādhikaḥ | etādṛśā atādṛśair mānyāḥ | parasparasamavāye tu paraḥ paro balīyān prathamamānyaḥ | mānye ‘bhivādanādisaṃmānaḥ || 6.18 ||
śrutaṃ tu sarvebhyo garīyaḥ || 6.19 ||
śrutaṃ mantrabrāhmaṇavibhāgena vedārthaparijñānam | tatsarvebhyo vittādibhyo garīyo gurutaram | pūrvasūtre parabalīyāṃsīti śrutam aparam upanyastaṃ tadvyāvṛttyarthaṃ pṛthaksūtram || 6.19 ||
kutaḥ punaḥ śrutaṃ sarvebhyo garīya ity ata āha |
tanmūlatvād dharmasya śruteś ca || 6.20 ||
śrutamūlam anuṣṭhānam anuṣṭhānamūlo dharma iti śruteś cāpy anucchinnasaṃpradāyo mūlam | tasmāc chrutasya garīyastvam | śrutasya garīyastvaṃ chāndogye pratipāditam: “brāhmaṇaṃ śaiśavaṃ bhavati śiśur vai āṅgiraso mantrakṛtāṃ mantrakṛd āsīt” iti | manur api |
adhyāpayāmāsa pitṝñ śiśur āṅgirasaḥ kaviḥ |
putrakā iti hovāca jñānena parigṛhya tān || [MDh 2.151] iti || 6.20 ||
cakridaśamīsthānugrāhyavadhūsnātakarājabhyaḥ
patho dānam || 6.21 ||
cakri cakravac chakaṭādi | tatsthaś cakristhaḥ | daśamyāṃ daśāyāṃ sthito daśamīstho vṛddhaḥ | anugrāhyo rogārtaḥ | vadhūr garbhiṇī | snātako vidyāvratasnātaḥ | rājābhiṣiktaḥ | pathi saṃgama etebhyo ‘nyaiḥ panthā deyaḥ || 6.21 ||
tatra viśeṣaḥ |
rājñā tu śrotriyāya śrotriyāya || 6.22 ||
śrotriyasamāgame rājñaiva panthā deyaḥ | abhyāso ‘dhyāyasamāptyarthaḥ || 6.22 ||
iti śrīgautamīyavṛttau haradattaviracitāyāṃ mitākṣarāyāṃ
prathamapraśne ṣaṣṭho ‘dhyāyaḥ
atha saptamo ‘dhyāyaḥ
śrutaṃ tu sarvebhyo garīya iti vidyāprādhānyam uktam | sā vidyā brāhmaṇād adhigantavyeti prathamaḥ kalpaḥ | tadabhāve vidyāyā avaśyādhigantavyatvād āpatkalpam āha |
āpatkalpo brāhmaṇasyābhrāhmaṇād
vidyopayogaḥ || 7.1 ||
aṅgāni vedāś catvāro mīmāṃsā nyāyavistaraḥ |
purāṇaṃ dharmaśāstraṃ ca vidyā hy etāś caturdaśa ||
upayogo niyamapūrvakaṃ grahaṇam | abrāhmaṇaḥ kṣatriyo vaiśyaś ca, tasmād brāhmaṇena vidyopayogaḥ kāryaḥ sa āpatkalpaḥ | āpadvidhir brāhmaṇasyety upalakṣaṇam | tena kṣatriyeṇāpi vaiśyād vidyopayogaḥ kārya iti sidhyati | āpatkalpa ity adhyāyaparisamāpter adhikriyate || 7.1 ||
anugamanaṃ śuśrūṣā || 7.2 ||
tatra yāvad adhyayanakālam anugamanam eva śuśrūṣā nānyat pādasaṃvāhanādi || 7.2 ||
samāpte brāhmaṇo guruḥ || 7.3 ||
samāpte tv adhyayane brāhmaṇa eva guruḥ || 7.3 ||
yājanādhyāpanapratigrahāḥ sarveṣām || 7.4 |
yājanādayo brāhmaṇasya vṛttayas tā āpadi sarveṣām anujñāyante na tu brāḥmaṇasyaiveti | apara āha – āpadi sarve yājayitavyāḥ sarve ‘dhyāpyāḥ sarvataś ca pratigrāhyaṃ na tu garhādoṣo ‘stīti | tathā ca manuḥ |
nādhyāpanād yājanād vā garhitād vā pratigrahāt |
doṣo bhavati viprāṇāṃ jvalanāmbusamo hi saḥ || [MDh 10.103] iti || 7.4 ||
pūrvaḥ pūrvo guruḥ || 7.5 ||
eteṣāṃ yājanādīnāṃ yo yaḥ pūrvanirdiṣṭaḥ sa sa uttarasmād gurur jñeyaḥ | āpadi pratigraheṇa jīvet tadasaṃbhave ‘dhyāpanena tadasaṃbhave yājaneneti || 7.5 ||
tadalābhe kṣatravṛttiḥ || 7.6 ||
idaṃ brāhmaṇaviṣayam | garhitayājanāder apy alābhe kṣatravṛttiḥ syāt | brāhmaṇaḥ sevādinā jīvet | āpadi nivṛttāyāṃ nāradaḥ |
āpadaṃ brāhmaṇas tīrtvā kṣatravṛttyā bhṛte jane |
utsṛjet kṣātravṛttiṃ tāṃ kṛtvā pāvanam ātmanaḥ || [NSm 1.55] iti || 7.6 ||
tadalābhe vaiśyavṛttiḥ || 7.7 ||
kṣatravṛtter apy alābhe vaiśyavṛttyāpi jīved brāhmaṇaḥ | alābhagrahaṇaṃ vṛttisaṃkaro mā bhūd iti | kṣatriyasya vaiśyavṛttyupajīvanaṃ daṇḍāpūpanyāyena siddham || 7.7 ||
tasyāpaṇyam || 7.8 ||
tasya vaiśyavṛtter brāhmaṇasyāpaṇyena vikreyaṃ vakṣyate | tasyeti vacanāt kṣatriyasya vaiśyavṛttyupajīvino vakṣyamāṇam apaṇyaṃ na bhavati || 7.8 ||
gandharasakṛtānnatilaśāṇakṣaumājināni || 7.9 ||
gandhaś candanādiḥ | rasas tailaghṛtalavaṇaguḍādiḥ | kṛtānnaṃ modakāpūpādi | tilāḥ prasiddhāḥ | śāṇaṃ śaṇavikāro goṇyādiḥ | kṣaumaṃ kṣumodbhūtaṃ paṭṭavastraviśeṣaḥ | ajinaṃ carma kaṭādi | etāny avikreyāṇi | śāṇakṣaumayor vikāraniṣedhāt prakṛter apartiṣedhaḥ || 7.9 ||
raktanirṇikte vāsasī || 7.10 ||
raktaṃ lākṣādinā vikṛtam | nirṇiktaṃ rajakādinā dhautam | evaṃbhūte api vāsasī apaṇye || 7.10 ||
kṣīraṃ savikaram || 7.11 ||
dadhyādibhir vikāraiḥ saha kṣīram apaṇyam || 7.11 ||
mūlaphalapuṣpauṣadhamadhumāṃsatṛṇodakāpathyāni
|| 7.12 ||
mūlam ārdrakaharidrādi | phalaṃ pūgādi | puṣpaṃ cambakādi | auṣadhaṃ pippalyādi | madhu mākṣikam | māṃsatṛṇodakāni prasiddhāni | apathyaṃ viṣādi | etāny apaṇyāni | rasaśabdena pūrvam eva niṣiddhe ‘pi punar madhugrahaṇaṃ sarvathā vṛttir aśaktāv ityādipakṣe niṣedhartham || 7.12 ||
paśavaś ca hiṃsāsaṃyoge || 7.13 ||
paśavo ‘jādayaḥ | hiṃsāsaṃyoge saunikādibhyo hiṃsārthe na vikreyāḥ || 7.13 ||
puruṣavaśākumārīvehataś ca nityam || 7.14 ||
puruṣāḥ dāsādayaḥ | vaśā vandhyā gauḥ | kumārī vatsatarī | vehad garbhopaghātinī | ete nityam apaṇyāḥ | nityam ity uktatvād dhiṃsāsaṃyogād anyatrāpi niṣedhaḥ | apara āha – iha nityagrahaṇāt pūrveṣu tilādiṣv anityaḥ pratiṣedha iti | tatra vasiṣṭhaḥ: “kāmaṃ vā svayam utpādya tilān vikrīṇīran” [VaDh 2.31] iti || 7.14 ||
bhūmivrīhiyavājāvyaśvaṛṣabhadhenvanaḍuhaś
caike || 7.15 ||
bhūmir gṛham | vrīhiyavājāvyaśvāḥ prasiddhāḥ | ṛṣabhaḥ secanasamartho gauḥ | dhenuḥ sakṛtprasūtā | anaḍvān anovāhanayogyo balīvardaḥ | ete cāpaṇyā ity eke manyante | ekaśabdād vayaṃ tv anujānīmaḥ | atrāpy ajāvigrahaṇaṃ hiṃsāsaṃyogaviṣayaparam || 7.15 ||
niyamas tu || 7.16 ||
niyamo vinimayaḥ parivartanaṃ tuśabdena niyamo ‘nujñāyata iti || 7.16 ||
rasānāṃ rasaiḥ || 7.17 ||
tailaghṛtaguḍādīnāṃ rasair eva vinimayaḥ karyaḥ | tad yathā: tailaṃ datvā ghṛtaṃ grāhyam iti | “rasaiḥ samato hīnato vā” iti vasiṣṭhaḥ [VaDh 2.37] || 7.17 ||
paśūnāṃ ca || 7.18 ||
paśūnāṃ catuṣpadāṃ paśubhir vinimayaḥ karyaḥ || 7.18 ||
na lavaṇakṛtānnayoḥ || 7.19 ||
lavaṇasya kṛtānnasya ca vinimayo ‘pi pratiṣiddhaḥ || 7.19 ||
tilānāṃ ca || 7.20 ||
tilānāṃ ca vinimayo na kāryaḥ | lavaṇakṛtānnatilānāṃ dravyāntarasvīkāreṇa pradānaṃ niṣiddham, samānadravyaviṣaye pravṛttasaṃbhavāt || 7.20 ||
samenāmena tu pakvasya saṃpratyarthe || 7.21 ||
samena samaparimāṇenāmena taṇḍulena saṃpratyarthe tādātmikopayogārthe pakvānnasya niyamaḥ kāryaḥ | manus tu “tilā dhānyena tatsamā” [MDh 10.94] iti samena dhānyena tilānāṃ niyamam anujānāti | apaṇyam iti vikrayaniṣedhāt sarvatra yāvad upayogakraye niṣedho na syāt | rasādīnām api niyamaśabdena pradānam eva vivakṣitam | anyathā tv avidyamānena rasāntarāder dravyāṇy āvṛttyasaṃbhavāt || 7.21 ||
sarvathā vṛttir aśaktāv aśaudreṇa || 7.22 ||
uktena prakāreṇa kuṭumbadhāraṇasyāsaṃbhavo ‘śaktiḥ | tasyāṃ satyāṃ sarvathā vṛttiḥ | prakāravacane thāl, uktena sarvaprakāreṇa niṣiddhenāpi jīvet | tatrāpi na śaudreṇa karmaṇā jīved iti || 7.22 ||
tad apy eke prāṇasaṃśaye || 7.23 ||
eke tv ācāryāḥ prāṇasaṃśaye sati tad api śaudraṃ karmāpy anumanyante | yathāha vyāsaḥ:
dharmārthakāmamokṣāṇāṃ prāṇāḥ saṃsthitihetavaḥ |
tān nighnatā kiṃ na hataṃ rakṣatā kiṃ na rakṣitam || iti || 7.23 ||
tadvarṇasaṃkarābhakṣyaniyamas tu || 7.24 ||
niyamo varjanam | śūdravṛttisthitenāpi brāhmaṇena tena śūdravarṇena sahāsanāṅgasaṃmelanādiḥ saṃkaraḥ | abhakṣyaṃ ca laśunādi | tadubhayavarjanaṃ kartavyaṃ na tu śūdravṛttir asmīti yathākāmyam iti || 7.24 ||
prāṇasaṃśaye brāhmaṇo ‘pi śastram ādadīta || 7.25 ||
prāṇasaṃśaye sati brāhmaṇo ‘pi rakṣārthaṃ śastram ādadīta | tadalābhe kṣatravṛttir iti śastragrahaṇe siddhe punar upādanaṃ brāhmaṇavṛtteḥ sato ‘py aniṣedhārtham | apiśabdāt kiṃ punar vaiśyaśūdrau || 7.25 ||
rājanyo vaiśyakarma [vaiśyakarma] || 7.26 ||
prāṇasaṃśaye rājanyo vaiśyakarmādadīta | tenātmānaṃ rakṣet [abhyāso ‘dhyāyasamāptyarthaḥ] || 7.26 ||
iti śrīgautamīyavṛttau haradattaviracitāyāṃ mitākṣarāyāṃ
prathamapraśne saptamo ‘dhyāyaḥ
athāṣṭamo ‘dhyāyaḥ
āpadvṛttim āśrito yadi tatraiva rameta kenāsau nivāryata ity āha |
dvau loke dhṛtavratau rājā brāhmaṇaś ca
bahuśrutaḥ || 8.1 ||
loko rāṣṭram | vīpsālopaś cātra draṣṭavyaḥ | loke loke dhṛtavratau vratānāṃ karmaṇāṃ dhārayitārau dvau rājā bahuśrutaś ca brāhmaṇaḥ | tau sarvasya sarvāpado daṇḍopadeśābhyāṃ nivārayitārau || 8.1 ||
tayoś caturvidhasya manuṣyajātasyāntaḥsaṃjñānāṃ
calanapatanasarpaṇānām āyattaṃ jīvanam || 8.2 ||
caturvidhasya manuṣyajātasya cāturvarṇyasyāntaraprabhā(bha)vās tv anulomādayas tanmūlatvāt pṛthaṅ noktāḥ | antaḥsaṃjñā vṛkṣādayaḥ sthāvarā vṛddhikṣayavantaḥ | yeṣām antaḥsaṃjñā na bahis te tathoktāḥ | tathā ca manuḥ |
tamasā bahurūpeṇa ceṣṭitāḥ karmahetunā |
antaḥsaṃjñā bhavanty ete samaduḥkhasamanvitāḥ || iti | [MDh 1.49]
calanāḥ paśvādayaḥ | patanāḥ pakṣiṇaḥ | sarpaṇāḥ sarīsṛpā bhujagādayaḥ | eṣāṃ manuṣyādīnāṃ jīvanaṃ tayo rājabrāhmaṇayor āyattaṃ tadadhīnam | rājā tu paripanthinigrahādinā teṣāṃ jīvanahetuḥ | itaras tu kathaṃ bahuśruta ity ata āha |
agnau prāstāhutiḥ samyag ādityam upatiṣṭhate |
ādityāj jāyate vṛṣṭir vṛṣṭer annaṃ tataḥ prajāḥ || [MDh 3.76]
ityādinyāyena jīvane hetuḥ || 8.2 ||
na ca jīvanamātram eva tadadhīnaṃ kiṃ tarhi –
prasūtirakṣaṇam asaṃkaro dharmaḥ || 8.3 ||
prasūtir abhivṛddhiḥ | daṇḍopadeśābhyāṃ yathoktakāritayā vṛṣṭyādidvāreṇa rogādyupadravaśāntyā cābhivṛddhir bhavati | coranigrahād rakṣaṇam api | daṇḍaprāyaścittopadeśābhyāṃ bhavati varṇānām asaṃkaro ‘saṃmelanam api | vihitopadeśāt pratiṣiddhasevāyāṃ daṇḍadhāraṇāc ca dharmo ‘pi bhavati | etat sarvaṃ tayor āyattam || 8.3 ||
bahuśruta ity uktaṃ pratipādayati |
sa eṣa bahuśruto bhavati || 8.4 ||
sa eṣa iti vakṣyamāṇanirdeśaḥ || 8.4 ||
lokavedavedāṅgavit || 8.5 ||
lokaśabdena lokavyavahārasiddhā janapadādidharmā ucyante | teṣāṃ vedāś catvāra ṛgyajuḥsāmātharvaṇāḥ | aṅgāni ṣaṭ | vyākaraṇaṃ śikṣā chando jyotiṣaṃ kalpasūtrāṇi niruktam api | teṣāṃ vettā pāṭhato ‘rthathaś ca || 8.5 ||
vākovākyetihāsapurāṇakuśalaḥ || 8.6 ||
vedaśāstropayogīni tarkoktipratyuktirūpāṇi vākyāni | yathā mahābhārate – kaḥ svid ekākī carati sūrya ekākī caratītyādīni vākovākyam | bhāratarāmāyaṇādīnītihāsaḥ | purāṇaṃ viṣṇupurāṇaśivapurāṇādyaṣṭādaśavidham | teṣu kuśalaḥ samarthaḥ || 8.6 ||
tadapekṣas tadvṛttiḥ || 8.7 ||
yāny etāni lokādīny anūktāni tāny apekṣata iti | tadvṛttis tadabhihitānāṃ karmaṇām anuṣṭhātā || 8.7 ||
catvāriṃśatsaṃskāraiḥ saṃskṛtaḥ || 8.8 ||
catvāriṃśatsaṃskārā garbhādhānādayo vakṣyante | taiḥ saṃskṛtaḥ || 8.8 ||
triṣu karmasv abhirataḥ || 8.9 ||
ijyādhyayanadānāni trīṇi karmāṇi | teṣv abhirataḥ | teṣāṃ sātatyenānuṣṭhātā | tadvṛttir ity anenaiva siddhe punarvacanam atidārḍhyārtham || 8.9 ||
ṣaṭsu vā || 8.10 ||
yājanādhyāpanapratigrahaiḥ saha ṣaṭ karmāṇi, teṣv abhirataḥ | vāśabdena pūrvokteṣu niyamaḥ
|| 8.10 ||
sāmayācārikeṣv abhivinītaḥ || 8.11 ||
pauruṣeyī vyavasthā samayaḥ | tanmūlā ācārāḥ samayācārās teṣu bhavāḥ sāmayācārikāḥ smārtā dharmās teṣv abhivinītaḥ pitrādibhiḥ samyak śikṣitaḥ || 8.11 ||
sa evaṃrūpo brāhmaṇaḥ –
ṣaḍbhiḥ parihāryo rājñā || 8.12 ||
ṣaḍbhir vakṣyamāṇair vadhādibhiḥ parihāryo rājñā bhavati | parihāro varjanam || 8.12 ||
tān vadhādīn āha |
avadhyaś cābandhyaś cādaṇḍyaś cābahiṣkāryaś
cāparivādyaś cāparihāryaś ceti || 8.13 ||
vadhas tāḍanam | bandho nigaḍanam | daṇḍo ‘rthāpahāraḥ | bahiṣkāro grāmādibhyo nirasanam | parivādo doṣasaṃkīrtanam | parihāras tyāgaḥ | ṣaḍ ete vadhādaya evaṃbhūte bahuśrute brāhmaṇe saty abuddhipūrvāparādhe rājñā varjyāḥ | buddhipūrvasya tu prasaṅgābhāvāt | itiśabdhaḥ prakāravacane | yac cānyad evaṃrūpasaṃbhāṣādi tad api varjyam iti || 8.13 ||
catvāriṃśatsaṃskārair ity uktam, tān āha \
garbhādānapuṃsavanasīmantonnayanajātakarma-
nāmakaraṇānnaprāśanacaulopanayanam || 8.14 ||
samāhāradvandvaḥ | garbhādhānam “ṛtāv upeyāt” [GDh 5.1] ityādikālaniyamena sūtrakāroktavidhānaniṣekaḥ | garbhādhānādayaḥ saṃskārās tattadgṛhyeṣūktāḥ | iha tu saṃskāragaṇanārthaṃ svarūpanirdeśamātraṃ kṛtam || 8.14 ||
catvāri vedavratāni || 8.15 ||
etāni prativedaṃ pratiśākhaṃ ca gṛhyeṣūktāni || 8.15 ||
snānaṃ sahadharmacāriṇīsaṃyogaḥ || 8.16 ||
snānaṃ samāvartanam | sahadharmacāriṇīsaṃyogo vivāhaḥ || 8.16 ||
pañcānāṃ yajñānām anuṣṭhānaṃ
devapitṛmanuṣyabhūtabrahmaṇām || 8.17 ||
pañcānāṃ devānāṃ yajñās teṣām anuṣṭhānam | etat pañcamahāyajñānuṣṭhānam ahar ahaḥ kartavyam | na tu sakṛtkṛtena saṃskārasiddhiḥ | pañcagrahaṇāt pañcaite pṛthaksaṃskārā na punaḥ samuditā ekaḥ saṃskāraḥ || 8.17 ||
eteṣāṃ ca || 8.18 ||
vakṣyamāṇānām aṣṭakādīnāṃ ca pākayajñānām anuṣṭhānaṃ saṃskāra iti || 8.18 ||
