tān āha |
aṣṭakā pārvaṇaḥ śrāddhaṃ śrāvaṇy āgrahāyaṇī        caitry
āśvayujīti sapta pākayajñasaṃsthāḥ || 8.19 ||
ūrdhvam āgrahāyaṇyās trayo ‘parapakṣās teṣv ekaikasminn ekāṣṭakā bhavatīti chandogāḥ | hemantaśiśirayoś caturṇām aparapakṣāṇām aṣṭamīṣv aṣṭakaikasyāṃ cety āśvalāyanaḥ | yā mādhyāḥ paurṇamāsyā upariṣṭān madhyāṣṭakā tasyām aṣṭamī jyeṣṭhayā saṃpadyate | tām ekāṣṭakety ācakṣata ity āpastambaḥ | evaṃbhūtāṣṭakā | parvaṇi bhavaḥ sthālīpākaḥ pārvaṇaḥ | śrāddhaṃ māsi śrāddham | śrāvaṇī sarvabaliḥ | śrāvaṇyāṃ paurṇamāsyāṃ gṛhyam agnim atipraṇīyetyādi chandogābhihitaḥ | śrāvaṇyāṃ paurṇamāsyām astamite sthālīpāka ity āpastambaḥ | āgrahāyaṇī mārgaśīrṣyāṃ paurṇamāsyāṃ kriyamāṇaḥ sarpabalir utsargahomaḥ | hemante pratyavarohaṇākhyaṃ ca karmāgrahāyaṇīśabdenocyate | caitrī śūlagavaḥ | īśānabalir ity āpastambīyānāṃ prasiddhaḥ sa caitryāṃ paurṇamāsyāṃ bhavati | atha śūlagavaḥ śaradi vasante cety āśvalāyanaḥ | āśvayujīṃ rudyāya sa iti chandogāḥ | āśvayujyāṃ paurṇamāsyāṃ tat karmaniveśanam alaṃkrtya snātāḥ śucivāsasaḥ paśupataye sthālīpākaṃ nirupya juhuyur ity āśvalāyanaḥ | anāhitāgner āgrayaṇam api tatraiva bhavati | tad idaṃ dvayam āśvayujīśabdena vivakṣitam | pākayajña iti gāryāṇāṃ karmaṇām ākhyā | yathāha āpastambaḥ: “laukikānāṃ pākayajñaśabdaḥ” iti | saṃsthāvidhāḥ pākayajñavidhāḥ saptety arthaḥ || 8.19 ||
agnyādheyam agnihotraṃ darśapūrṇamāsāv āgrayaṇaṃ
cāturmāsyāni nirūḍhapaśubandhaḥ sautrāmaṇīti sapta
haviryajñasaṃsthāḥ || 8.20 ||
agnyādheyādayaḥ śrutisiddhāḥ saṃskāreṣu gaṇyante | saptagrahaṇād darśapūrṇamāsau samudāyatayaikaḥ saṃskāraḥ | somasaṃbandhābhāvād dharviryajñā iti || 8.20 ||
agniṣṭomo ‘tyagniṣṭoma ukthyaḥ ṣoḍaśī vājapeyo ‘tirātro
‘ptoryāma iti sapta somasaṃsthāḥ || 8.21 ||
agniṣṭomo rājanyasya | ṣoḍaśigraho gṛhyate yatra so ‘tyagniṣṭomaḥ | brāhmaṇasya katham ayaṃ saṃskāra iti cintyam | anye prasiddhāḥ || 8.21 ||
ity ete catvāriṃśat saṃskārāḥ || 8.22 ||
ity uktopasaṃhāraḥ | catvāriṃśadgrahaṇād eva tāvanta eva saṃskārāḥ | anyāni smārtakarmāṇi kāmyādīni ceti || 8.22 ||
athāṣṭāv ātmaguṇāḥ || 8.23 ||
vakṣyanta iti śeṣaḥ | athaśabdaḥ saṃbhāvanāyām || 8.23 ||
dayā sarvabhūteṣu kṣāntir anasūyā śaucam anāyāso
maṅgalam akārpaṇyam aspṛheti || 8.24 ||
ātmavat sarvabhūteṣu yad dhitāya śivāya ca |
vartate satataṃ hṛṣṭaḥ kṛtsnā hy eṣā dayā smṛtā || 1 ||
ākruṣṭo ‘bhihato vāpi na krośen na ca tāḍayet |
aduṣṭo vāṅmanaḥkāyaiḥ sā titikṣā kṣamā smṛtā || 2 ||
yo dharmam arthaṃ kāmaṃ ca labhate mokṣam eva ca |
na dviṣyāt taṃ sadā prājñaḥ sānasūyā smṛtā budhaiḥ || 3 ||
dravyaśaucaṃ manaḥśaucaṃ vācikaṃ kāyikaṃ tathā |
śaucaṃ caturvidhaṃ proktam ṛṣibhis tattvadarśibhiḥ || 4 ||
yadārambhe bhavet pīḍā nityam atyantam ātmanaḥ |
tad varjayed dharmyam api so ‘nāyāsaḥ prakīrtitaḥ || 5 ||
praśastācaraṇaṃ nityam apraśastavivarjanam |
etad dhi maṅgalaṃ proktaṃ munibhis tattvadarśibhiḥ || 6 ||
āpady api ca kaṣṭāyāṃ bhaved dīno na kasyacit |
saṃvibhāgaruciś ca syāt tad akārpaṇyam ucyate || 7 ||
vivarjayed asaṃtoṣaṃ viṣayeṣu sadā naraḥ |
paradravyābhilāṣaṃ ca sāspṛhā kathyate budhaiḥ || 8 ||
ity uktaprakāreṇāṣṭāv ātmaguṇāḥ || 8.24 ||
eṣām utkarṣam āha |
yasyaite catvāriṃśat saṃskārā na cāṣṭāv ātmaguṇā
na sa brāhmaṇaḥ sāyujyaṃ sālokyaṃ gacchati || 8.25 ||
sālokyaṃ samānalokavāsitvam | ekadeśasaṃyogāt sālokyaṃ samastayogāt sāyujyam iti || 8.25 ||
yasya tu khalu saṃskārāṇām ekadeśo ‘py aṣṭāv
ātmaguṇā atha sa brahmaṇaḥ sāyujyaṃ sālokyaṃ ca
gacchati [gacchati] || 8.26 ||
tuśabdo viśeṣavācī | khaluśabdaḥ prasiddhau | yasya catvāriṃśatsaṃskāreṣu dvijatvamūlakatipayasaṃskārasaṃbandhe ‘py aṣṭāv ātmaguṇāḥ santi | athaśabdo nirdhāraṇe brahmaṇaḥ sāyujyaṃ sālokyaṃ ca gacchaty eva | [abhyāso ‘dhyāyasamāptyarthaḥ] || 8.26 ||
iti śrīgautamīyavṛttau haradattaviracitāyāṃ mitākṣarāyāṃ
prathamapraśne ‘ṣṭamo ‘dhyāyaḥ
atha navamo ‘dhyāyaḥ
sa vidhipūrvakaṃ snātvā bhāryām adhigamya yathoktān
gṛhasthadharmān prayuñjāna imāni vratāny
anukarṣet || 9.1 ||
tacchabdena pūrvādhyāyoktarājā brāhmaṇaś ca parāmṛśyate | vaiśyasya tu snātakaviṣaye viśeṣaṃ vakṣyati | vidhipūrvaṃ vidhiṃ puraskṛtya snātvā vedavratāni pāraṃ nītvā samāvartanaṃ krtvā bhāryām adhigamya vivāhaṃ kṛtvā tadanantaraṃ yathoktān atithipūjādigṛhasthadharmān anutiṣṭhann imāny api vakṣyamāṇāni vratāny anukarṣet | ātmānaṃ prāpayed anutiṣṭhed iti || 9.1 ||
snātakaḥ || 9.2 ||
calopo draṣṭavyaḥ | snātakaś caitāni gṛhasthavratāny anutiṣṭhet | brahmacaryān nivṛttatvād gārhasthyavyatiriktāśramāntarabhāvāc ca bhāryādhigamād ūrdhvam iti pūrvasūtram ārabdham | snātakasya tu bhāryādhigamāsaṃbhave yāvajjīvaṃ gṛhasthadharmā evānuṣṭheyā iti sūtrāntaram ārabdham | etac ca rājabrāhmaṇayor eva snātakavratānuṣṭhānaṃ tadatikrame prāyaścittaṃ ca vidhīyate | tathā ca smṛtyantaram: “rājabrāhmaṇayor eva netareṣāṃ kathaṃcana” iti || 9.2 ||
kāni punas tāni vratāni |
nityaṃ śuciḥ sugandhiḥ snānaśīlaḥ || 9.3 ||
ācamanādinā nityaṃ śuciḥ śaktiviṣaye na muhūrtam apy aprayataḥ syāt | sugandhiś candanādyanuliptena surabhitāṅgaḥ | yad vā gandhaḥ śīlaṃ suśīlaḥ syād iti | snānaśīlo nityasnāyī syāt | atra snātakaviṣaye vasiṣṭhaḥ |
snātakānāṃ tu nityaṃ syād antarvāsas tathottaram |
yajñopavīte dve yaṣṭiḥ sodakaś ca kamaṇḍaluḥ || iti | [VaDh 12.14]
manuḥ |
vaiṇavīṃ dhārayed yaṣṭiṃ sodakaṃ ca kamaṇḍalum |
yajñopavītaṃ vedaṃ ca śubhe raukme ca kuṇḍale || iti | [MDh 4.36]
vedo dharbhamuṣṭiḥ || 9.3 ||
sati vibhave na jīrṇamalavadvāsāḥ syāt || 9.4 ||
vibhave ‘nyasya saṃbhave sati jīrṇaṃ malavac ca vāso na dhārayet || 9.4 ||
na raktam ulbaṇam anyadhṛtaṃ vāso bibhṛyāt || 9.5 ||
kusumbhādirāgayuktam ulbaṇaṃ bahumūlyam anyadhṛtaṃ guruvarjam anyaiḥ pūrvadhṛtam evāmvidhāni vāsāṃsi na dhārayet | sati vibhava ity anuvartate || 9.5 ||
na sragupānahau || 9.6 ||
sragupānahāv apy anyadhṛte na dhārayet || 9.6 ||
nirṇiktam aśaktau || 9.7 ||
anyasyālābho ‘śaktiḥ | aśaktāv anyadhṛtaṃ vāsaḥ sragupānahau ca nirṇijya dhārayet | tatra vāsonirṇejanam ūṣarodake pakvaṃ kāryam || 9.7 ||
na rūḍhaśmaśrur akasmāt || 9.8 ||
śmaśrugrahaṇaṃ nakhādīnām apy upalakṣaṇārtham | akāraṇān na rūḍhaśmaśruḥ syāt | kāraṇe sati rūḍhaśmaśruḥ syāt | kāraṇaṃ tu smṛtyantare paṭhitam |
ṣaṣṭhābde ṣoḍaśābde ca vivāhābde tathaiva ca |
antarvatnyāṃ ca jāyāyāṃ kṣaurakarma vivarjayet || iti |
vapanasyāpi “gaṅgāyāṃ bhāskarakṣetre” ityādinā coditakālatvāt kathaṃ tarhi syāt | tatra manuḥ “kḷptakeśanakhaśmaśruḥ” [MDh 4.35] iti | kalpanā kartanena samīkaraṇam | yājñavalkyaś ca |
śuklāmbaradharo nīcakeśaśmarunakhaḥ śuciḥ | [YDh 1.131] iti || 9.8 ||
nāgnim apaś ca yugapad dhārayet || 9.9 ||
ekena hastenāgnim apareṇāpaś ca yugapan na dhārayet | atra vyāghro viśeṣam āha |
na dhāreyed apaś cāgnim apaś cānnaṃ tathaiva ca |
yugapat snātako nityaṃ tadbhāryāpi tathaiva ca || iti || 9.9 ||
nāñjalinā pibet || 9.10 ||
yat kiṃcit kṣīrodakādi peyam añjalinā na pibet | saṃyuktau hastāv añjaliḥ || 9.10 ||
na tiṣṭhann uddhṛtodakenācāmet || 9.11 ||
uddhṛtodakena tiṣṭhan nācāmet | āsīna evācāmet | uddhṛtodakeneti vacanāt taṭākādiṣu tīrapradeśasyāsucitve jānudaghne jale tiṣṭhato ‘py ācamanam apratiṣiddham | ācāro ‘py evam eva śiṣṭānām || 9.11 ||
na śūdrāśucyekapāṇyāvarjitena || 9.12 ||
śūdreṇa śucināpy aspṛśyasparśādidūṣitena dvijenāpy ekena pāṇinā ca yad āvarjitaṃ tenodakena nācāmet | svayaṃ tu vāmahastāvarjitenodakenācamanaviṣaya ekapāṇyāvarjitatvaṃ samānam iti cen, na, hastadvayasyāpy ācamanakarmasaṃbandhāt tathā ca śiṣṭācāradarśanāt || 9.12 ||
na vāyvagniviprādityāpo devatā gāś ca prati paśyan vā
mūtrapurīṣāmedhyān vyudasyet || 9.13 ||
apśabdānte dvandva ārṣatvāt samāsānto na kṛtaḥ | anityāḥ samāsāntā iti keṣāṃcit pakṣaḥ | devatāḥ pratimāḥ | vāyvādīn prati mūtrādīni na vyudasyet paśyan vā na kuryād iti pratipaśyan vety arthaḥ | prati na kuryād ity ābhimukhyavarjanam | paśyan na kuryād iti niyamād ābhimukhye saty apy anavalokanam | mūtrapurīṣayoḥ pṛthag upādānād amedhyaśabdena niṣṭhīvanocchiṣṭādi vivakṣitam | tarhy amedhyaśabdenaivālam | mūtrapurīṣagrahaṇaṃ tu tayor atiśayena varjanārtham || 9.13 ||
naitā devatāḥ prati pādau prasārayet || 9.14 ||
etā vāyvādyā devatāḥ prati pādau na prasārayet | pādāv ity upalakṣaṇaṃ pādaṃ ca na prasārayet | goṣu vipreṣu ca devatāpadaprayogas tadvad upacārārthaḥ || 9.14 ||
na parṇaloṣṭāśmabhir mūtrapurīṣāpakarṣaṇaṃ
kuryāt || 9.15 ||
na parṇādibhir mūtrapurīṣayor karṣaṇam apamārjanaṃ kuryāt | anyais tu kuryād iti || 9.15 ||
na bhasmakeśanakhatuṣakapālamedhāny
adhitiṣṭhet || 9.16 ||
bhasmādīni nākrāmet | tuṣā vrīhyādīnāṃ tvacaḥ | anye prasiddhāḥ | teṣām upari na tiṣṭhet | adhitiṣṭhed ity anena yādṛcchikasparśamātre na doṣaḥ || 9.16 ||
na mlecchāśucyadhārmikaiḥ saha saṃbhāṣeta || 9.17 ||
varṇāśramadharmarahite deśe siṃhaladvīpādau ye vasanti te mlecchāḥ | aśucaya āryā api vihitāni saṃdhyāvandanādīni ye na kurvanti te tathoktāḥ | adhārmikāḥ patitādayaḥ | taiḥ saha na saṃbhāṣeta | saṃśabdaprayogād eva siddhe sahagrahaṇaṃ taiḥ sahaikakāryo bhūtvā na saṃbhāṣetety evamartham | tena mārgapraśnādau na doṣaḥ || 9.17 ||
saṃbhāṣya puṇyakṛto manasā dhyāyet || 9.18 ||
yadi kāraṇavaśāt taiḥ saha saṃbhāṣeta tataḥ puṇyakṛto vasiṣṭhādīn manasā dhyāyet | manaseti dhyānasvabhāvānuvādaḥ || 9.18 ||
brāhmaṇena vā saha saṃbhāṣeta || 9.19 ||
prakaraṇād brāhmaṇo ‘pi puṇyakṛd eva || 9.19 ||
adhenuṃ dhenubhavyeti brūyat || 9.20 ||
dhenuḥ payasvinī gauḥ | adhenus tadviparītā | tām api dhenubhavyeti brūyān na punar adhenur iti || 9.20 ||
abhadraṃ bhadram iti || 9.21 ||
abhadram api vastu bhadram ity eva brūyat || 9.21 ||
kapālaṃ bhagālam iti || 9.22 ||
kapālaṃ bruvan bhagālam iti brūyāt || 9.22 ||
maṇidhanur itīndradhanuḥ || 9.23 ||
indradhanur iti bruvan maṇidhanur iti brūyāt || 9.23 ||
gāṃ dhayantīṃ parasmai nācakṣīta || 9.24 ||
“dheṭ pane” [Dhātu Pāṭha 902]  | vyatyayenāyaṃ karmaṇi kartṛpratyayaḥ | vatsena dhīyamānāṃ gāṃ parasmai svāmine na brūyāt | yasya haviṣe vatsā apākṛtā dhayeyur ityādike nimitte tv ākhyātavyam eva “saṃsṛṣṭāṃ ca vatsena” [ĀpDh 1.31.10] ity āpastambīye viśeṣāt || 9.24 ||
na caināṃ varayet || 9.25 ||
na ca svayam apy enāṃ vārayed iti || 9.25 ||
na mithunī bhūtvā śaucaṃ prati vilambeta || 9.26 ||
mithunībhūya śtriyam upagamya śaucaṃ prati na vilambeta | tatkṣaṇa eva kuryat | śaucaṃ tv āpastambenābhihitam: “udakopasparśanam api vā lepān prakṣālyācamya prokṣaṇam aṅgānām” iti [ĀpDh 2.123; 2.2.1] || 9.26 ||
na ca tasmin śayane svādhyāyam adhīyīta || 9.27 ||
yasmin mithunam ācaritam || 9.27 ||
na cāpararātram adhītya punaḥ pratisaṃviśet || 9.28 ||
yaḥ pūrvarātre suptvāpararātra utthāyādhīte, na sa punaḥ pratisaṃviśet | kāladairghye sati punar na svapyāc cheṣāṃ rātriṃ jāgṛyād eveti | punargrahaṇāt pūrvarātre ‘suptasya svāpe na doṣaḥ || 9.28 ||
nākalyāṃ nārīm abhiramet || 9.29 ||
akalyāṃ rogādināsvasthāṃ nārīṃ nābhiramet | nānayā mithunī bhavet || 9.29 ||
na rajasvalām || 9.30 ||
rajasvalām api nārīṃ nābhiramayet | “udakyāgamane trirātram” [GDh 23.33] iti prāyaścittaṃ vakṣyati | tenaiva siddhe vacanam idaṃ trirātrād ūrdhvam apy anivṛtte rajasi gamanapratiṣedhārtham || 9.30 ||
na caināṃ śliṣyen na kanyām || 9.31 ||
enāṃ rajasvalāṃ kanyām anūḍhām api na śliṣyen nāliṅget || 9.31 ||
agnimukhopadhamanavigṛhyavādabahirgandhamālya-
dhāraṇapāpīyasāvalekhanabhāryayāsahabhojanāñjanty-
aveṣaṇakudvārapraveśanapādapādadhāvanāsandīstha-
bhojananadībāhutaraṇavṛkṣaviṣamārohaṇāvarohaṇa-
prāṇavyāyacchanāni varjayet || 9.32 ||
upadhamanam upadhmānaṃ nāgniṃ mukhenopadhamayen na jvalayet | vigṛhyavādo vākkalahaḥ | gandhamālyayor bahirdhāraṇaṃ prakāśadhāraṇam iti | “anāviḥsraganulepanaḥ” syād ity āpastambaḥ [ĀpDh 1.32.5] | pāpīyasāvalekhanam aśucinā kāṣṭhādinā śiraḥprabhṛteḥ kaṇḍūyanam | tṛtīyāyā alukchāndasaḥ | bhāryayā saha bhojanaṃ bhāryayā sahaikasmin bhājane bhojanam | keṣucid deśeṣv ācārāt prāptau satyāṃ niṣedhaḥ | anye tv ekasmin kāle bhojanaṃ sahabhojanam icchanti | añjantyavekṣaṇam | añjantī tailābhyaṅgaṃ kurvaty añjanādibhir alaṃkriyamāṇā vā, tasyā avekṣaṇam | tac ca bhāryāviṣayam ity eke | strīmātraviṣayam ity anye | kudvārapraveśanaṃ dvāravyatiriktapradeśena devālayagṛhādeḥ praveśanam aprasiddhamārgeṇa nagaragrāmādeḥ praveśanam iti | yathā ca āpastambaḥ: “na kusṛtyā grāmaṃ praviśed yadi praviśen namo rudrāya vāstoṣpataya ity etām ṛcaṃ japed anyāṃ vā raudrīm” iti [ĀpDh 1.31.21] | pādapādadhāvanaṃ pādena pādaprakṣālanam | āsandīsthabhojanam, āsandī pīṭhikā tatrasthasyānnasya bhojanam āsandīsthabhojanam | yad vā yatrāsīno bhuṅkte tatrāsane bhojanapātraṃ nidhāya yad bhojanaṃ tad vā | nadībāhutaraṇaṃ bāhubhyāṃ nadyāstaraṇaṃ pāragamanam | bāhutaranāt plavādau na doṣaḥ | nadīgrahaṇaṃ taḍāgādīnām apy upalakṣaṇam | vṛkṣaviṣamārohaṇāvarohaṇe vṛkṣasyārohaṇaṃ viṣamasya kūpāder avarohaṇaṃ ca | vṛkṣaviṣamagrahaṇenātyunnatanimnasthalaṃ lakṣyate | prāṇavyāyacchanaṃ prānoparodhyullaṅghanajalayantrādyadhirohaṇam | etāny agnimukhopadhamanādīni varjayet || 9.32 ||
na saṃdigdhāṃ nāvam adhirohet || 9.33 ||
pāragamane saṃdigdhām asamarthāṃ nāvaṃ nādhitiṣthet || 9.33 ||
pratipadapāṭhasyāśakyatvāt saṃkṣipyāha |
sarvata evātmānaṃ gopāyet || 9.34 ||
sarvebhya upāyebhya ātmānaṃ rakṣayet | “eko na gacched adhvānam” [cf. MDh 4.60] ityādibhyaḥ || 9.34 ||
na prāvṛtya śiro ‘hani paryaṭet || 9.35 ||
prāvṛtyāśiraso divā caṅkramaṇapratiṣedhaḥ | āsīnasya yathāruci | mārge varṣātapādibādhe prāvṛtyāpi caṅkramaṇe na doṣaḥ | “sarvata evātmānaṃ gopāyet” [GDh 9.34] ity uktatvāt | 9.35 ||
prāvṛtya rātrau || 9.36 ||
rātrau tu śiraḥ prāvṛtyaiva paryaṭet || 9.36 ||
mūtroccāre ca || 9.37 ||
mūtraṇaṃ mūtra uccāraḥ purīṣakarma tayoḥ samāhāradvandvaḥ | tatra ca śiraḥ prāvṛtya prāvṛtaśirāḥ karma kuryād iti śeṣaḥ || 9.37 ||
na bhūmāv anantardhāya || 9.38 ||
mūtrapurīṣakarmaṇī bhūmau tṛṇādibhir antardhāyaiva kuryāt | “ayajñiyais tṛṇaiḥ” [VaDh 12.13] iti smṛtyantare || 9.38 ||
nārāc cāvasathāt || 9.39 ||
āvasatho gṛham | tatsamīpe na kuryāt || 9.39 ||
na bhasmakarīṣakṛṣṭacchāyāpathikāmyeṣu || 9.40 ||
karīṣaṃ gomayam | chāyopajīvyāḥ pathikādayo yatra viśāmyanti | kāmyaṃ kamanīyaḥ pradeśaḥ | bhasmādiṣv eteṣu mūtrapurīṣakarmaṇī na kuryāt || 9.40 ||
ubhe mūtrapurīṣe tu divā kuryād udaṅmukhaḥ || 9.41 ||
mūtrapurīṣe divā ced udaṅmukha eva kuryāt || 9.41 ||
saṃdhyayoś ca || 9.42 ||
udaṅmukhaḥ kuryād iti || 9.42 ||
rātrau dakṣiṇāmukhaḥ || 9.43 ||
spaṣṭam || 9.43 ||
pālāśam āsanaṃ pāduke dantadhāvanam iti ca
varjayet || 9.44 ||
itikārā[dā?]dyarthād yac cānyad evaṃ yuktam rathādi tad api pālāśaṃ varjayet | atra paṭhanti |
āsanaṃ śayanaṃ yānaṃ gṛhopakaraṇaṃ tathā |
varjayet pādukāṃ caiva pālāśaṃ dantadhāvanam || iti || 9.44 ||
sopānatkaraś cāsanābhivādananamaskārān
varjayet || 9.45 ||
abhivādanaṃ pūrvoktam, namaskāro devatāpraṇāmaḥ | abhivādanādīni sopānatko na kuryāt | upānadgrahaṇaṃ pādukāder apy upalakṣaṇam || 9.45 ||
na pūrvāhṇamadhyaṃdināparāhṇān aphalān kuryād
yathāśakti dharmārthakāmebhyaḥ || 9.46 ||
tṛtīyārthe caturthī pañcamī vā | pūrvāhṇādīn ahnas trīn bhāgān dharmādibhis tribhir yathāśakty aphalān na kuryāt | kiṃ tarhi | saphalān eva kuryād yathāsaṃkhyam || 9.46 ||
teṣu tu dharmottaraḥ syāt || 9.47 ||
tuśabdo ‘navasthāṃ pariharati | teṣu dharmārthakāmeṣu dharmottaraḥ syād dharmapradhānaḥ syāt | dharmāvirodhenārthakāmau seveteti | tathā ca manuḥ |
parityajed arthakāmau yau syātāṃ dharmavarjitau || [MDh 4.176] iti || 9.47 ||
na nagnāṃ parayoṣitam īkṣeta || 9.48 ||
parā cāsau yoṣic ca parayoṣit | anyathā vidhavānūḍhāveśyādayo na syuḥ tām nagnāṃ satīṃ nekṣeta || 9.48 ||
na padāsanam ākarṣet || 9.49 ||
pādenāsanam ātmasamīpaṃ na prāpayet || 9.49 ||
na śiśnodarapāṇipādavākcakṣuścāpalāni kuryāt || 9.50 ||
cāpalaśabdaḥ pratyekaṃ saṃbadhyate | śiśnacāpalam akāle maithunecchā | udaracāpalaṃ sarvadā bibhakṣayiṣā | pāṇicāpalaṃ śilpakarmaśikṣābhilāṣaḥ | pādacāpalaṃ paryaṭanam | vākcāpalaṃ “nāpṛṣṭaḥ kasyacid brūyān na cānyāyena pṛcchataḥ” [MDh 2.110] ity etad atikrameṇa vyavahāraḥ | cakṣuścāpalaṃ nṛtyādididṛkṣā | etāni na kuryāt || 9.50 ||
chedanabhedanavilekhanavimardanāvasphoṭanāni
nākasmāt kuryāt || 9.51 ||
chedanaṃ tṛṇādīnām | bhedanaṃ ghaṭādeḥ | vilekhanaṃ kuḍyabhūmyādau nakhādibhir vilekhanam | vimardanaṃ loṣṭādīnāṃ cūrṇīkaraṇam | avasphoṭanam aṅgulīnāṃ saśabdaṃ prasāraṇam | etad akasmān na kuryāt | kāraṇe tv avasphoṭanādiṣu na doṣaḥ | chedanādiṣv api yathāsaṃbhavaṃ mṛgyam || 9.51 ||
nopari vatsatantīṃ gacchet || 9.52 ||
vatsabandhanī rajjur vatsatantī | tām upari na gacchet | vatsaśabdo gojāter upalakṣaṇam || 9.52 ||
na kulaṃkulaḥ syāt || 9.53 ||
kulam eva kulaṃ yasya sa kulaṃkulaḥ | chāndaso mumāgamaḥ | evaṃvidho na syāt | anyatra gamane ‘dhyayanādilābhe sati svakula eva na tiṣṭhed iti | apara āha – kulāt kulāntaragāmī kulaṃkulo dattādirūpeṇa tathāvidho na syāt | svasūtraparityāgena parasūtraṃ na bhajed iti | tatra smṛtyantaram |
yaḥ svasūtraṃ parityajya parasūtraṃ niṣevate |
śākhāraṇḍaḥ sa vijñeyaḥ sarvakarmabahiṣkṛtaḥ || iti || 9.53 ||
na yajñam avṛto gacchet || 9.54 ||
avṛto ‘nupāmantrito yajñaṃ na gacchet || 9.54 ||
darśanāya tu kāmam || 9.55 ||
avṛto ‘pi kāmaṃ darśanāya yajñaṃ gacchet | na tv ārtvijyādilipsayā || 9.55 ||
na bhakṣān utsaṅge bhakṣayet || 9.56 ||
bhakṣāḥ pṛthukādayas tān utsaṅge kṛtvā na bakṣayet || 9.57 ||
na rātrau preṣyāhṛtam || 9.57 ||
rātrau preṣyeṇa kiṃkareṇa yad ānītaṃ tad yat kiṃcd api na bhakṣayet | na bhakṣān eva, ekavacananirdeśāt || 9.57 ||
uddhṛtasnehavilapanapiṇyākamathitaprabhṛtīni
cāttavīryāṇi nāśnīyāt || 9.58 ||
āttavīryāṇy upāttasārāṃśāni nāśnīyāt | kāni punas tāni teṣām udāharaṇaprapañcaḥ | uddhṛtasnehe upāttāgramaṇḍe dadhipayasī | vilapanaṃ navanītamalam | yantre pīḍitānāṃ tilānāṃ kalkaḥ piṇyākam | yasya mathanamātraṃ nāmbusaṃsargas tad api mathitam | yad āhur naighaṇṭikāḥ:
takraṃ hy udaśvin mathitaṃ pādāmbv ardhāmbu nirjalam || iti |
tac ca dvividham, āttanavanītam itarac ca | tatrāpy asyeha grahaṇaṃ tad dhy āttavīryasyodāharaṇam | prabhṛtigrahaṇena yac cānyad evaṃvidhaṃ kalkarūpaṃ tasya grahaṇam | uddhṛtanavanītaṃ takram āśyam anāśyam iti cintyam | ācāras tva aśanam eva | abhakṣyaprakaraṇe vaktavya iha vacanāt snātakavratalope yat prāyaścittaṃ tad evaiṣām aśane bhavati nābhakṣyabhakṣaṇanimittam || 9.58 ||
sāyaṃprātas tv annam abhipūjitam aninditam
[anindan?] bhuñjīta || 9.59 ||
tuśabdas tv avadhāraṇe | sāyaṃ rātriḥ prātas ahas tayor dvayor ekakālayor aśanaṃ bhuñjīta | nāntareti prāptasya bhojanasya parisaṃkhyeyam | tatra guṇavidhir abhipūjitam anindann iti | abhipūjitaṃ rocata iti | anindan kadannatvādidoṣeṇākutsayan | “sāyaṃ prātar aśanāny abhipūjayet” [VaDh 3.69] iti vasiṣṭhaḥ || 9.59 ||
na kadācid rātrau nagnaḥ svapet || 9.60 ||
supyād rātrau na tu nagnaḥ svapet | tad evaṃ rātrau nagnasya svāpapratiṣedho divā tu sarvatheti | kadācid grahaṇād idaṃ labhyate | anyathā rātrau na nagnaḥ svaped ity eva vācyaṃ syāt || 9.60 ||
snāyād vā || 9.61 ||
na nagna ity eva | “nagno jalaṃ nāvataret” iti smṛtyantaram || 9.61 ||
ānantyād ācārāṇāṃ pratipadapāṭho na śakya iti saṃkṣipyāha |
yac cātmavanto vṛddhāḥ samyag vinītā dambhalobha-
mohaviyuktā vedavida ācakṣate tat samācaret || 9.62 ||
ātmavanto jitendriyāḥ | vṛddhāḥ pariṇatavayaso yauvane viṣayavaśyatāsaṃbhavāt | samyag vinītā gurubhiḥ śikṣitāḥ | dambho dharmacchalena lokavañcanam | lobho ‘nyāyena paradravyāditsā | moho ‘jñānaṃ lokaviruddhajñānaṃ vā tena tyaktāḥ | vedavidaḥ pāṭhataś cārthataś ca vedānāṃ coditāraḥ | atra vṛddhā iti viśeṣyam | evaṃbhūtā vṛddhā yad ācakṣate tat kartavyam iti | bahuvacananirdeśād bahūnām aikamatye tad bhavati || 9.62 ||
yogakṣemārtham īśvaram adhigacchet || 9.63 ||
alabdhasya lābho yogaḥ | labdhasya paripālanaṃ kṣemaḥ | tadartham īśvaraṃ rājānam adhigacchet | adhiśabdaprayogād adhir aiśvarya ity asmād akārpaṇyena svatantro gacched iti
|| 9.63 ||
nānyam anyatra devagurudhārmikebhyaḥ || 9.64 ||
anyaṃ rājavyatiriktaṃ yogakṣemasamartham api nādhigacched ity anuvādaḥ | devā indrādayaḥ | guravaḥ pitrādayaḥ | dhārmikā dharmācaraṇaśīlāḥ | etebhyo ‘nyatra | etān adhigacched eveti
|| 9.64 ||
prabhūtaidhodakayavasakuśamālyopaniṣkramaṇam
āryajanabhūyiṣṭham analasasamṛddhaṃ
dhārmikādhiṣṭhaṃ niketanam āvasituṃ yateta || 9.65 ||
edhaḥ kāṣṭham udakaṃ snānapānayogyaṃ yavasaṃ tṛṇāni gavārtham | kuśāḥ prasiddhāḥ | mālyāni puṣpāṇi devādyarcanārtham | upaniṣkramyate yatra tad upaniṣkramaṇaṃ bahiravakāśaḥ saṃcārādyartham | evamādīni prabhūtāni yatra | āryās traivarṇikās ta eva janās tair bhūyiṣṭhaṃ vyāptam | alasāḥ kṛtyeṣu nirudyamās tadviparītā analasās tair samṛddham | dhārmikā dharmaśīlās tair adhiṣṭhātṛbhir adhiṣṭhitam | evaṃbhūtaṃ niketanam āvasituṃ yateta | evaṃbhūte sthāne yatnenāpi vased iti || 9.65 ||
praśastamaṅgalyadevatāyatanacatuṣpadaṃ
pradakṣiṇam āvarteta || 9.66 ||
nirgamanapraveśādiṣu yathā te dakṣiṇapārśve bhavanti tathā kuryād iti || 9.66 ||
manasā vā tatsamagram ācāram anupālayed
āpatkalpaḥ || 9.67 ||
saṃbhave tu sākṣād anuṣṭhānam eveti || 9.67 ||
satyadharmā || 9.68 ||
satyavacanasvabhāvaḥ | syād iti vakṣyamāṇam apekṣate || 9.68 ||
āryavṛttaḥ || 9.69 ||
pūrvabhāṣī priyaṃvada ityādyāryāṇāṃ vṛttam iva vṛttaṃ yasya sa tathā | uṣṭramukhavan madhyamapadalopaḥ || 9.69 ||
śiṣṭādhyāpakaḥ || 9.70 ||
satām adhyāpayitā na tv ayogyānām || 9.70 ||
śaucaśiṣṭaḥ || 9.71 ||
śiṣṭaṃ śāstravihitaṃ śaucaṃ yasyāsti sa tathā | niṣṭhān tasya paranipātaḥ | śāstravihitena śaucena tadvān | śaucasya punaḥ punar vacanaṃ tātparyārtham || 9.71 ||
śrutinirataḥ syāt || 9.72 ||
vedābhyāsarataḥ || 9.72 ||
nityam ahiṃsro mṛdur dṛḍhakārī damadānaśīlaḥ || 9.73 ||
nityaṃ nimitte saty apy ahiṃsro ‘hiṃsāśīlaḥ | mṛduḥ kṛtāparādhe ‘pi sahakaḥ | dṛḍhakārī pārabdhasya samāpayitā na prākramikaḥ | dama indriyanigrahaḥ | dānaṃ saṃvibhāgaḥ | tacchīlaḥ syād iti sarvatrāpekṣyate || 9.73 ||
evamācāro mātāpitarau pūrvāparāṃś ca saṃbandhān
duritebhyo mokṣayiṣyan snātakaḥ śaśvad brahmalokān
na cyavate na cyavate || 9.74 ||
evam uktaprakāra ācāro yasya sa evamācāraḥ | evaṃbhūtaḥ snātako mātāpitarau pūrvasaṃbandhāḥ pitāmahādayaḥ | aparasaṃbandhāḥ putrādayaḥ | tāṃś ca pūrvāparasaṃbandhān duritebhyaḥ pāpebhyo mokṣayiṣyan ye pūrvaṃ bhūtās tāṃs tadaiva narakādibhyo mocayati ye tu bhaviṣyantaḥ putrādayas tāṃś ca mokṣayiṣyan | sapratyayasyārtho mṛgyaḥ (?) | mocayiṣyan bhavati | sa evaṃbhūtaḥ snātakaḥ śaśvad bahukālaṃ brahmalokān na cyavate | dviruktir adhyāyasamāptyarthā | punaḥsnātakagrahaṇaṃ snātakadharmāṇām evaitat phalaṃ na gṛhasthadharmasahitānām ity evamartham || 9.74 ||
iti śrīgautamīyavṛttau haradattaviracitāyāṃ mitākṣarāyāṃ
navamo ‘dhyāyaḥ
prathamaḥ praśnaḥ samāptaḥ
------------------
atha dvitīyaḥ praśnaḥ
[tatra prathamo ‘dhyāyaḥ]
uktāḥ prāyaśa āśramadharmāḥ | atha varṇadharmān āha |
dvijātīnām adhyayanam ijyā dānam || 10.1 ||
yathāsaṃkhyam atra na bhavati | uttaratrādhikagrahaṇāt tatraiva vaktavyaṃ bhaviṣyati | adhyayanaṃ vedagrahaṇābhyāsarūpam | ijyā yāgo devapitṛpūjā | dānaṃ pātre dravyatyāgaḥ | dvijātīnām iti vacanād yadā dvijātayaḥ saṃpannāḥ kṛtopanayanās tata ārabhyaite dharmāḥ | tenānupanītānāṃ dāne ‘py adhikāro nāstīti kecit | neti ca vayam | dvijātīnām ity upalakṣaṇaṃ yeṣāṃ dvijātir janma teṣām iti | tenānupanītasyāpy arthavato hitaiṣibhiḥ pravartitasya dānaṃ bhavaty eva || 10.1 ||
brāhmaṇasyādhikāḥ pravacanayājanapratigrahāḥ || 10.2 ||
pravacanam adhyāpanam | yājanam ārtvijyam | pratigrahaḥ prasiddhaḥ | ete brāhmaṇasyādhikāḥ pūrvebhyo 'dhyayanādibhiḥ | te cāmī ca samuccitā ity uktaṃ bhavati | atrāpy anupanītasyāpi pratigraho bhavati | yājanādhyāpane tv asaṃbhavān na bhavataḥ | brāhmaṇasya pravacanayājanapratigrahā ity eva siddhe ‘dhikagrahaṇaṃ pūrvatra yathāsaṃkhyaṃ mā bhūd iti pūrve tāvad avasthitāḥ || 10.2 ||
pūrveṣu niyamas tu || 10.3 ||
niyamo ‘vaśyakartavyatā | pūrvāṇy adhyayanādīny avaśyakartavyāni | akurvan pratyavaiti kurvaṃś cābhyudeti | pravacanādīni tu vṛttyarthāni | ato ‘karaṇe na pratyavāyaḥ karaṇe nābhyudayaḥ || 10.3 ||
ācāryajñātipriyagurudhanavidyāniyameṣu brahmaṇaḥ
saṃpradānam anyatra yathoktāt || 10.4 ||
brahmaḥ vedaḥ | tasya saṃpradānaṃ samyak pradānam | “anujñāta upaviśet” [GDh 1.55] ity ārabhya śuśrūṣavo ‘dhyāpyā ity uktaṃ sa yathoko niyamaḥ | tasmād anyatra vināpīti tenācāryādibhyo brahma pradeyam ity ucyate | ācārya uktaḥ | jñātayo bhrātṛpitṛvyādayaḥ | priyaḥ sakhā | guravo mātulādayaḥ | eteṣu brahma samyak pradeyam | tathā dhanavidyāniyameṣu | dhanena vidyāyāḥ parivartanaṃ dhananiyamaḥ | vidyāntareṇa parivartanaṃ vidyāniyamaḥ | teṣv api brahma saṃpradeyam | dhanavidyāniyamo ‘pi yogyaviṣaya eva, “śiṣṭādhyāpakaḥ” [GDh 9.70] ity uktatvāt | na ca tasyāpy ayam apavādaḥ | yathāvad grahaṇavidhinādhyayanam uktaṃ tasyaivāyam apavāda iti || 10.4 ||
kṛṣivāṇijye vāsvayaṃkṛte || 10.5 ||
kṛṣiḥ karṣaṇena sasyotpādanaṃ vāṇijyā krayavikrayavyavahāraḥ | te ca brāhmaṇasyādhike yady asvayaṃkṛte | anyena kārayituṃ śakyete || 10.5 ||
kusīdaṃ ca || 10.6 ||
kusīdam upacayārtho dhanaprayogaḥ | tad apy asvayaṃkṛtaṃ ced brāhmaṇasyādhikam || 10.6 ||
rājño ‘dhikaṃ rakṣaṇaṃ sarvabhūtānām || 10.7 ||
rājño ‘bhiṣiktasya sarvabhūtānāṃ rakṣaṇam adhikam | sarvagrahaṇāt sthāvarādīnām apy aśvatthādīnāṃ chedananirodhena || 10.7 ||
nyāyyadaṇḍatvam || 10.8 ||
nyāyād anapeto nyāyyaḥ śāstrāviruddho daṇḍo yasya tadbhāvo nyāyyadaṇḍatvam | nyāyadaṇḍatvam ity api pāṭha eṣa evārthaḥ | sa ca rājño dharmaḥ | rāgadveṣādinā na nyūnādhikadaṇḍaḥ syād iti || 10.8 ||
bibhṛyād brāhmaṇāñ śrotriyān || 10.9 ||
śrotriyā adhītavedās tān brāhmaṇān annādidānena bibhṛyāt || 10.9 ||
nirutsāhāṃś ca brāhmaṇān || 10.10 ||
jīvanārtham utsāhaṃ kartum asamarthā nirutsāhās tān brāhmaṇān api bibhṛyāt | kiṃ punar brāhmaṇān | pūrvasūtre sarjane samarthān api śrotriyān samam āhūya bibhṛyād iti || 10.10 ||
akarāṃś ca || 10.11 ||
ye pūrvair dattā akarā brāhmaṇādibhyas tāṃś ca yathāpūrvaṃ bibhṛyād bādhakādinirāsena | svayaṃ ca nāpūrvaṃ karam utpādayed iti || 10.11 ||
upakurvāṇāṃś ca || 10.12 ||
ādhīyānā [adhī-?] brahmacāriṇa upakurvāṇās tāṃś ca bibhṛyād annādidānena | yady arthinaḥ svayaṃ jīvitavanto vanasūkarādivyāvartanena | apara āha – upakurvāṇā lokopakurvāṇā vaidyādaya iti || 10.12 ||
yogaś ca vijaye || 10.13 ||
yoga upāyo vijayaviśayaś ca yogaḥ kāryaḥ | ayam api rājño ‘dhiko dharma iti || 10.13 ||
bhaye viśeṣeṇa || 10.14 ||
anyābhibhavādinimitte viśeṣeṇa yogaḥ kāryaḥ || 10.14 ||
caryā ca rathadhanubhyām || 10.15 ||
caraṇaṃ caryā | bahiḥpradeśe caran ratham ārūḍho dhanurhastaś ca caret | rathagrahaṇaṃ hastyaśvāder upalakṣaṇaṃ dhanurgrahaṇaṃ ca khaḍgādeḥ || 10.15 ||
saṃgrāme saṃsthānam anivṛttiś ca || 10.16 ||
saṃgrāmo yuddhaṃ tatra saṃsthānaṃ prāṇātyayaḥ | nivṛttiḥ palāyanaṃ tadabhāvo ‘nivṛttiḥ | etau ca rājño ‘dhikau dharmau || 10.16 ||
na doṣo hiṃsāyām āhave || 10.17 ||
yatra parasparam āhvayante sa āhavaḥ | tādṛśe yuddhe śatrūṇāṃ hiṃsāyām api na doṣaḥ | nityam ahiṃsra ity asyāyam apavādaḥ || 10.17 ||
anyatra vyaśvasārathyāyudhakṛtāñjaliprakīrṇakeśa-
parāṅmukhopaviṣṭasthalavṛkṣādhirūḍhadūtago-
brāḥmaṇavādibhyaḥ || 10.18 ||
viśabdaś ca tribhiḥ saṃbadhyate | vyaśvo visārathir vyāyudha iti | yasyāśvo hataḥ sa vyaśvaḥ | yasya sārathir hataḥ sa visārathiḥ | yasyāyudhaṃ kṛttaṃ patitaṃ vā sa vyāyudhaḥ | kṛtāñjalir bhayena | prakīrṇakeśaḥ keśān api niyantum akṣamaḥ | parāṅmukho bhayena pṛṣṭhīkṛtya palāyamānaḥ | upaviṣṭaḥ palāyitum apy asamartha āsīnaḥ | sthalavṛkṣādhirūḍhaḥ | sthalam unnatapradeśas tam vṛkṣaṃ vārūḍhaḥ | dūto vārtāharaḥ | gaur asmi brāhmaṇo ‘smīti ye vadanti te gobrāhmaṇavādinaḥ | etebhyo ‘nyatrāhave hiṃsāyāṃ na doṣaḥ | eteṣu doṣa iti || 10.18 ||
kṣatriyaś ced anyas tam upajīvet tadvṛttyā || 10.19 ||
anyaś cet kṣatriyas taṃ rājānaṃ deśopaplavādinopajīvet tadā tadvṛttyā tasya rājño yā vṛttiś caryā rathadhanurbhyām ityādikā tayā yuktaḥ saṃjīvet | tena rājñaivam asau saṃmata iti || 10.19 ||
jetā labheta sāṃgrāmikaṃ vittam || 10.20 ||
rājñā niyukto rājabhṛtyādiḥ saṃgrāme śatrūn nirjitya yad vittaṃ labhate tat sa eva jetā labheta na rājā || 10.20 ||
vāhanaṃ tu rājñaḥ || 10.21 ||
vāhanaṃ hastyaśvādikaṃ nirjitya labdhaṃ rājño bhavati na jetuḥ || 10.21 ||
uddhāraś cāpṛthagjaye || 10.22 ||
yadi sarve sainikāḥ saṃbhūya jayeyur jitvā ca kim api labheraṃs tasminn apṛthagjaye rājñaḥ uddhāro viśeṣadravyaṃ svayaṃ vṛto deyaḥ || 10.22 ||
anyat tu yathārhaṃ bhājayed rājā || 10.23 ||
yat svayaṃ vṛtaṃ māṇikyādi tato ‘nyad yathārhaṃ yasya yāvān vyāpāro yāvad vā śauryaṃ tadanurūpeṇa bhājayet | yathaite tadanurūpaṃ bhajeraṃs tathā kārayed iti || 10.23 ||
rājño balidānaṃ karṣakair daśamam aṣṭamaṃ
ṣaṣṭhaṃ vā || 10.24 ||
karṣakaiḥ kṣetre yal labdhaṃ tasya daśamabhāgo ‘ṣṭamaḥ ṣaṣṭho vāṃśo rājño balidānaṃ kararūpeṇa deyaḥ | asya rājñaḥ karṣakaiḥ kṣetre yal labdhaṃ tad rakṣaṇanimittā vṛttir eṣā | kṛṣṭāyā bhūmer atibhogamadhyamabhogālpabhogaviṣayo ‘yaṃ vyavasthito vikalpaḥ | atibhoge daśamāṃśo madhyamabhoge ‘ṣṭamāṃśo ‘lpabhoge ṣaṣṭhāṃśa iti || 10.24 ||
paśuhiraṇyayor apy eke pañcāśadbhāgaḥ || 10.25 ||
ye paśubhir jīvanti ye vā hiraṇyaprayoktāro vārdhuṣikās taiḥ pañcāśattamo bhāgo rājñe deya ity eke | tad yathā – yasya pañcāśat paśavaḥ santi sa pratisaṃvatsaram ekaṃ paśuṃ rājñe dadyāt | yasya vā pañcāśan niṣkair vṛddhiprayogaḥ sa pratisaṃvatsaram ekaikaṃ niṣkaṃ rājñe balirūpeṇa dadyād iti || 10.25 ||
viṃśatibhāgaḥ śulkaḥ paṇye || 10.26 ||
yad vaṇigbhir vikrīyate tat paṇyam | tatra viṃśatitamo bhāgo rājñe deyas tasyaiva dīyamānasya śulka iti saṃjñā | śulkapradeśāḥ pratibhāvyaṃ vaṇikśulkam ityādayaḥ || 10.26 ||
mūlaphalapuṣpauṣadhamadhumāṃsatṛnendhanānāṃ
ṣaṣṭhaḥ || 10.27 ||
mūlaṃ haridrādi | phalam āmrādi | puṣpam utpalādi | auṣadhaṃ bilvādi | śiṣṭāni prasiddhāni | eteṣu paṇyeṣu ṣaṣṭi(ṣṭha)tamo bhāgo rājñe deyo vikretrā || 10.27 ||
kasmāt punar evaṃ rājñe deya ity ata āha |
tadrakṣaṇadharmitvāt || 10.28 ||
teṣāṃ karadāyināṃ rakṣaṇarūpeṇa dharmeṇa tadvattvāt teṣām ayaṃ rakṣaka iti kṛtveti || 10.28 ||
teṣu tu nityayuktaḥ syāt || 10.29 ||
teṣu karṣakādiṣu nityayuktaḥ syād rakṣaṇe nityam avahitaḥ syāt | apara āha – teṣu balyādiṣu nityayuktaḥ syāt | tātparyeṇādadīta śulkam | hyasyaitad (?) dhanam iti || 10.29 ||
adhikena vṛttiḥ || 10.30 ||
rājño ‘dhikaṃ rakṣaṇam iti yad uktaṃ taddvāreṇa yad āgataṃ dhanaṃ tad adhikaṃ tenātmanaḥ poṣyavargasya ca hastyaśvādīnāṃ ca vṛttiḥ syān na tu pūrvair yat saṃcitya khātaṃ kośarūpena tena jīvet | āpadi tu tenāpi jīvet | tathā ca vyāghraḥ |
kuṭumbapoṣaṇaṃ kuryān nityaṃ kośaṃ ca dhārayet |
āpado ‘nyatra kośāt tu na gṛhṇīyāt kadācana || iti || 10.30 ||
śilpino māsi māsy ekaikaṃ karma kuryuḥ || 10.31 ||
ekenāhnā sādhyam ekaṃ karma | śilpino lohakārādayaḥ | te ‘pi pratimāsaṃ rājñe svīyam ekam ahaḥ karma kuryuḥ | eṣa eṣāṃ śulkaḥ || 10.31 ||
etenātmanopajīvino vyākhyātāḥ || 10.32 ||
ātmopajīvino ye śarīrāyāsena jīvanti kāṣṭhavāhādayas te ‘py ete ca śilpiṣūktaprakāreṇa vyākhyātā māsi māsy ekaikaṃ karma kuryur iti | nartakādiṣv apy eṣaiva gatiḥ || 10.32 ||
naucakrīvantaś ca || 10.33 ||
nauś ca cakraṃ ca naucakre | cakraśabdena tadvacchakaṭaṃ lakṣyate | tadvanto naucakrīvantaḥ | āsandīvad aṣṭhīvad ityādinā kathaṃcid rūpasiddhiḥ | nauvanto naujīvinaḥ cakra(krī)vantaḥ śakaṭajīvinaḥ | te ‘pi rājña ekam ahas tatkarma kuryuḥ || 10.33 ||
bhaktaṃ tebhyo dadyāt || 10.34 ||
śilpino māsi māsīty ārabhya ye ‘nukrāntās tebhyaḥ karma kurvadbhyo bhaktam annaṃ divā bhojanaṃ dadyād rājā || 10.34 ||
paṇyaṃ vaṇigbhir arthāpacayena deyam || 10.35 ||
māsi māsy ekaikam ity anuvartate | viṃśatibhagaḥ śulkaḥ paṇya ity uktam | tataḥ śulkād adhikam idaṃ māsi māmsy ekaṃ paṇyam arthāpacayena prāptasya mūlyasya kiṃcin nyūnatāṃ kalpayitvā vaṇijo rājñe dadyuḥ | tatra bṛhaspatiḥ |
śulkaṃ dadus tato māsam ekaikaṃ paṇyam eva ca |
ardhāvaraṃ ca mūlyena vaṇijas te pṛthak pṛthak || iti || 10.35 ||
pranaṣṭam asvāmikam adhigamya rājñe
prabrūyuḥ || 10.36 ||
pranaṣṭaṃ svāmisakāśāt prabhraṣṭam | asvāmikam ajñāyamānasvāmikam | adhigamya bhūmau patitam upalabhya janapadapālane niyuktā ete rājñe prabrūyuḥ | anye vā kecid dṛṣṭavantas te ‘pi brūyuḥ || 10.36 ||
tataḥ kiṃ kartavyaṃ rājñā |
vikhyāpya saṃvatsaraṃ rājñā rakṣyam || 10.37 ||
vikhyāpya – “idam evaṃjātīyakaṃ vastv āsāditaṃ rakṣyate | yasyaitat sa āgacchatu” iti nagare paṭahena ghoṣayitvā saṃvatsaraṃ rakṣyam | prāk cet saṃvatsarāt svāmy āgacchati tato lakṣaṇāni pṛṣṭvā sāmyaṃ cet tat tasmai deyam | vaiṣamye sa daṇḍyaḥ | tathā ca yājñavalkyaḥ |
pranaṣṭādhigataṃ deyaṃ nṛpeṇa dhanine dhanam |
vibhāvayen na cel liṅgais tatsamaṃ daṇḍam arhati || iti |
evam adhigamyāprabruvato daṇḍyāḥ || 10.37 ||
atha saṃvatsarād ūrdhvaṃ kiṃ kāryam ity āha |
ūrdhvam adhigantuś caturthaṃ rājñaḥ śeṣaḥ || 10.38 ||
yenādhigamyākhyātaṃ tasmai caturtham aṃśaṃ datvā śeṣo rājñā grāhyaḥ || 10.38 ||
svāmī rikthakrayasaṃvibhāgaparigrahādhigameṣu
|| 10.39 ||
rikthaṃ pitrādīnām abhāve prāptam | krayo mūlyena svīkāraḥ | saṃvibhāgo bhrātrādīnāṃ sādhāraṇasya parasparavibhāgaḥ | parigraho vanyeṣv asvāmikeṣu vṛkṣādiṣu pūrvasvīkāraḥ | adhigamaḥ pranaṣṭasyājñātasvāmikasya nidhyādeḥ svīkāraḥ | eteṣu kāraṇeṣu dravyasvīkartā svāmī bhavati | tena pranaṣṭo ‘dhigate rājño ‘dhigantuś ca svāmyam upapannam iti prakaraṇasaṃgatiḥ | kṣetreṣūtpannāni sasyādīni kṣetravad eva kṣetravataḥ svāni | etenākareṣūtpannaṃ lavaṇādi vyākhyātam | etāni sarvavarṇasādhāraṇāni svāmyakāraṇāni ||
|| 10.39 ||
brāhmaṇasyādhikaṃ labdham || 10.40 ||
yal labdhaṃ dānarūpeṇa tad brāhmaṇasyādhikaṃ svāmyamūlam || 10.40 ||
kṣatriyasa vijitam || 10.41 ||
vijayena labdhaṃ kṣatriyasyādhikaṃ svam || 10.41 ||
nirviṣṭaṃ vaiśyaśūdrayoḥ || 10.42 ||
nirviṣṭaṃ karmaṇopāttam | kṛṣyādinā vaiśyasya śuśrūṣādinā śūdrasya | tad adhikam anayoḥ || 10.42 ||
atha pranaṣṭādhigatādhigantuś caturtham ity asyāpavādam āha |
nidhyadhigamo rājadhanam || 10.43 ||
nidhiś ced adhigatas tad rājadhanam eva bhavati | adhigantre ‘nugrahānurūpaṃ kiṃcid deyam iti || 10.43 ||
brāhmaṇasyābhirūpasya || 10.44 ||
abhirūpaḥ ṣaṭkarmanirataḥ | tasya brāhmaṇasya cen nidhyadhigamo na tad rājadhanam | kiṃ tarhi | adhigantur brāhmaṇasyaiveti || 10.44 ||
abrāhmaṇo ‘py ākhyātā ṣaṣṭhaṃ labhetety eke || 10.45 ||
abrāhmaṇo ‘pi nidhim adhigamya yady ācaṣṭa idam ittham āsāditam iti sa tasya nidheḥ ṣaṣṭaṃ labhetey eke smartāro manyante | brāhmane ‘nabhirūpe kalpyaḥ || 10.45 ||
caurahṛtam apajitya yathāsthānaṃ gamayet || 10.46 ||
caurair hṛtaṃ dravyaṃ tān apajitya yathāsthānaṃ gamayet | svāmina eva dadyāt | jetus tu jayaphalaṃ kiṃcit || 10.46 ||
kośād vā dadyāt || 10.47 ||
yady anviṣyāpi corā na dṛṣṭās ta eva vā jitvā gatās tadā svakośād ādāya tāvad dhanaṃ svāmine dadyād yāvad apahṛtaṃ caurair iti || 10.47 ||
rakṣyaṃ bāladhanam ā vyavahāraprāpaṇāt || 10.48 ||
bālo ‘prātpaṣoḍaśavarṣaḥ | tasya yadi hitaiṣiṇo rakṣakāś ca pitrādayo na santi santo vā mūrkhāś cādhārmikāś ca tadā taddhanaṃ rājñā rakṣyam | ā kutaḥ | vyavahārapāpaṇāt | yāvad asau vyavahāraprāptaḥ ṣoḍaśavarṣo bhavati || 10.48 ||
samāvṛtter vā || 10.49 ||
āṅ anuvartate | adhītavedasya gurukulān nivṛttiḥ samāvṛttiḥ | ā vā tasyā iti || 10.49 ||
evaṃ rājño ‘dhikaṃ svatvamūlam uktam | sāṃprathaṃ vaiśyasyāha |
vaiśyasyādhikaṃ kṛṣivaṇikpāśupālyakusīdam || 10.50 ||
kṛṣiḥ prasiddhā | vaṇig iti vāṇijyam | paśupālasya karma pāśupālyam | kusīdaṃ vṛddhyartho dhanaprayogaḥ | kṛṣyādibhir yal labdhaṃ tad adhikaṃ svaṃ vaiśyasya || 10.50 ||
śūdraś caturtho varṇa ekajātiḥ || 10.51 ||
caturtho varṇa iti | varṇasāmānyatve saty api caturthagrahaṇaṃ pūrveṣāṃ trayāṇāṃ brāhmaṇādivarṇānāṃ pṛthagvarṇatvopapādanārtham | traivarṇikā api siddhatvād ekajatir upanayanaṃ pūrveṣāṃ dvitīyajanma tad asya nāstīti | upanayanapratiṣedhāt tatpūrvakam adhyayanam api na bhavati | tadviṣaye gṛhyakāra āha: “śūdrasyāpi niṣekapuṃsavanasīmantonnayanajātakarmanāmakaraṇānnaprāśanacaulāny amantrakāṇi yathākālam upadiṣṭāni” iti | vivāho ‘py amantrako yathācāraṃ bhavati || 10.51 ||
tasyāpi satyam akrodhaḥ śaucam || 10.52 ||
upanayanādhyayanarahitatve ‘pi yathāvṛttikatvaṃ mā bhūd iti tasyāpi śūdrasya satyādayo dharmā bhavanti | satyaṃ yathādṛṣṭārthavāditvam | akrodhaḥ parānabhidrohabuddhiḥ | śaucaṃ pūrvoktadravyaśaucaṃ manaḥ śaucam ityādi | vasiṣṭhas tu: “sarveṣāṃ satyam akrodho dānam ahiṃsā prajananaṃ ca” [VaDh 4.4] iti || 10.52 ||
ācamanārthe pāṇipādaprakṣālanam evaike || 10.53 ||
pūrveṣāṃ varṇānāṃ yatrācamanam uktaṃ tasmin viṣaye śūdrasya pāṇipādaprakṣālanam eva bhavati nānya ācamanakalpa ity eke manyante | manus tu sakṛd ambupānam icchati: “strīśūdrau tu sakṛt sakṛt” [MDh 5.139] iti | nityasnānaviṣaye tu uśanā: “sacchūdraḥ snāyād asacchūdraḥ pāṇipādaṃ prakṣālayet” iti || 10.53 ||
śrāddhakarma || 10.54 ||
“amāvāsyāyām” [GDh 16.35] ity ārabhya yac chrāddhakarma vakṣyate tad api śūdrasya kartavyaṃ mantravarjam || 10.54 ||
bhṛtyabharaṇam || 10.55 ||
bhṛtyo bharaṇīyaḥ poṣyavargaḥ | tasya ca bharaṇaṃ kartavyam | tena tadanurūpam arthārjanam apy asya kartavyam iti || 10.55 ||
svadāravṛttiḥ || 10.56 ||
sveṣv eva dāreṣv asya vṛttiḥ | sajātīyeṣv api paradāreṣu veśyāsu ca prasañjan daṇḍya iti | apara āha – svadāravṛttir evāsya bhavati nāśramāntaraprāptir iti || 10.56 ||
paricaryā cottareṣām || 10.57 ||
uttareṣāṃ trayāṇāṃ varṇānāṃ paricaryā śuśrūṣā ca || 10.57 ||
saiṣā vṛttyarthety āha |
tebhyo vṛttir lipseta || 10.58 ||
tebhyaḥ paricaritebhyo jīvanaṃ lipseta || 10.58 ||
tatra pūrvaṃ pūrvaṃ paricaret || 10.59 ||
tathā ca āpastambaḥ: “pūrvasmin pūrvasmin varṇe niḥśreyasaṃ bhūyaḥ” iti [ĀpDh 1.1.8] | tad evaṃ yathā yājanādhyāpanapratigraheṣu brāhmaṇasya pratigraho mukhyā vṛttis tathā śūdrasya paricaryā | tatrāpi pūrvasmin pūrvasmin varṇa iti || 10.59 ||
jīrṇāny upānacchatravāsaḥkūrcādīni || 10.60 ||
kūrcaṃ tṛṇādi | śeṣaṃ prasiddham | jīrṇāny upabhuktāny upānadādīni paricarate śūdrāya deyāni | ayaṃ tu śuśrūṣāvṛtteḥ śūdrasya niyamo na gṛhasthavṛtteḥ | tasya tu vṛttyanapakṣaṃ sāmānyākāreṇa viśeṣatvam || 10.60 ||
punaḥ prakṛtam anusarati |
ucchiṣṭāśanam || 10.61 ||
bhojanapātre yad bhuktāvaśiṣṭaṃ tad asyāśanam | “nābrāhmaṇāyocchiṣṭaṃ prayacchet” [ĀpDh 1.31.22] ity etat tu dāsaviṣayam | gṛhasthaśūdraviṣayam anye | tathā ca vyāghraḥ |
ucchiṣṭam annaṃ dātavyaṃ śūdrāyāgṛhamedhine |
gṛhasthāya tu dātavyam anucchiṣṭaṃ dine dine || iti || 10.61 ||
śilpavṛttiś ca || 10.62 ||
śilpāni citrakarmādīni | tair apy ayaṃ varteta | atra mānavo viśeṣaḥ |
aśaknuvaṃs tu śuśrūṣāṃ śūdraḥ kartuṃ dvijanmanām |
putradārātyayaṃ prāpto jīvet kārukakarmabhiḥ || [MDh 10.99] iti || 10.62 ||
yaṃ cāyam āśrayed bhartavyas tena kṣīṇo ‘pi || 10.63 ||
paricaryayā vartamānaḥ śūdro yadi kṣīṇaḥ karma kartum asamartho bhavati tathāpi (tadāpi) yam asau pūrvam āśritaḥ karmāṇy akarot tenāsau bhartavyaḥ pūrvakṛtāpekṣayā || 10.63 ||
tena cottaraḥ || 10.64 ||
tena ca śūdreṇottaro vṛttikṣīṇo bhartavyaḥ śilpādibhiḥ pūrvakṛtāpekṣayaiva | atra jātūkarṇyaḥ |
yo nīcam āśrayed ārya ātmānaṃ darśayet sadā |
ātmānaṃ dāsavat kṛtvā caren nīco ‘pi taṃ prati ||
daridro brāhmaṇo dānto vedānāṃ caiva pāragaḥ |
śūdreṇāpi sadāpy eṣa bhartavyo ‘nāśrito ‘pi san ||
bibhṛyād brāhmaṇaṃ nityaṃ sarvayatnena buddhimān |
anyaṃ cāpy ānṛśaṃsyārthaṃ śūdro ‘pi dravyavān bhavet || iti || 10.64 ||
tadartho ‘sya nicayaḥ syāt || 10.65 ||
asya śūdrasya nicayo ‘rthasaṃcayas tadarthaḥ syāt tasyottarasya poṣaṇārthaḥ syāt | pūrvasūtrasya hetur ayam || 10.65 ||
anujñāto ‘sya namaskāro mantraḥ || 10.66 ||
asya śūdrasya vaiśvadevadiṣu tattaddevatāpadaṃ caturthyantaṃ manasā dhyātvā namo nama ity evaṃrūpo mantro ‘nujñāto dharmajñaiḥ | apara āha |
devatābhyaḥ pitṛbhyaś ca mahāyogibhya eva ca |
namaḥ svadhāyai svāhāyai nityam eva namo namaḥ ||
ity ayaṃ mantro namaskāraśabdena vivakṣitaḥ | sa pitryeṣu karmasu bhavati | tac cāha gṛhyakāraḥ: “brāhmaṇān upaveśya devatādikaṃ mantraṃ japet” || 10.66 ||
pākayajñaiḥ svayaṃ yajetety eke || 10.67 ||
pakvaguṇakeṣv apakvaguṇakeṣu ca gārhyeṣu karmasu pākayajñaśabdaḥ prasiddhaḥ | yathāha āpastambaḥ: “laukikānāṃ pākayajñaśabdaḥ” iti || 10.67 ||
sarve cottarottaraṃ paricareyuḥ || 10.68 ||
sarva eva vaiśyādayo ‘py uttaram uttaraṃ varṇaṃ paricareyur na kevalaṃ śūdra eva | brāhmaṇasya tūttaro nāsti | madhye kṣatriyavaiśyau | tathāpi sarvaśabde bahuvacanam avāntaraprabhavāṇāṃ grahaṇārtham | apara āha – samāne ‘pi varṇe yo yo ‘pi guṇata uttaras taṃ tam avaro ‘varaḥ paricared ity evamartham || 10.68 ||
āryānāryayor vyatikṣepe karmaṇaḥ sāmyaṃ
sāmyam || 10.69 ||
āryas traivarṇikaḥ | anāryaḥ śūdraḥ | tayoḥ karmaṇa ācārasya vyatikṣepe vyatyāse sati tayoḥ sāmyam eva bhavati na paricāryaparicārakabhāvaḥ | brāhmaṇādir apy anāryakarmā cen na śūdreṇa paricaraṇīyaḥ | śūdro ‘py āryakarmā ced anāryakarbhir itarair jātyapakarṣeṇa nāvamantavya iti | etena brāhmaṇakṣatriyau kṣatriyavaiśyau ca vyākhyātau | [abhyāso ‘dhyāyasamāptyarthaḥ] || 10.69 ||
iti śrīgautamīyavṛttau haradattaviracitāyāṃ mitākṣarāyāṃ
dvitīyapraśne prathamo ‘dhyāyaḥ
atha ekādaśo ‘dhyāyaḥ
āryānāryayor vyatikṣepe nivārayitā rājā | atas taddharmān āha |
rājā sarvasyeṣṭe brāhmaṇavarjam || 11.1 ||
rājā abhiṣiktaḥ sarvasya svajanapadavartino janasya īṣṭe nigrahānugrahādiṣu | kim aviśeṣeṇa | nety āha – brāhmaṇavarjaṃ brāhmaṇān varjayitvā | tatas te cyavanto ‘pi svadharmāt sāntvena sthāpyāḥ | svakriyāsu svātantryakhyāpanārthaṃ vacanam | yathāha nāradaḥ |
asvatantrāḥ prajāḥ sarvāḥ svatantraḥ pṛthivīpatiḥ || [NSm 1.29] iti || 11.1 ||
śāstrāviruddheṣv evāsya svātantryam ity āha |
sādhukārī sādhuvādī || 11.2 ||
sādhukārī śāstrāviruddhācaraṇaśīlaḥ | sādhuvādī vyavahārakāle svapakṣāparapakṣasamavādī || 11.2 ||
trayyām ānvīkṣikyā vābhivinītaḥ || 11.3 ||
ṛgyajuḥsāmātmakās trayo vedās trayī | atharvaṇaś ca vedas teṣv antarbhavati | tatrāpi hi mantrā ṛco yajūṃṣi vā bhedavyavahāras tu pravacananimittaḥ | śāntikapauṣṭikādiprameyabhedanibandho vā | ānvīkṣikī nyāvavidyā | tayor abhivinīto gurubhiḥ samyak śikṣitaḥ | manus tu |
traividebhyas trayīṃ vidyāṃ daṇḍanītiṃ ca śāśvatīm |
ānvīkṣikīṃ cātmavidyāṃ vārtārambhaṃ ca lokataḥ || [MDh 7.43] iti || 11.3 ||
śucir jitendriyo guṇavatsahāyopāyasaṃpannaḥ || 11.4 ||
śuciḥ antaḥ paradravyādiṣv aspṛhaḥ bahiḥ snānādiparaḥ | jitendriyaḥ striyo ‘kṣā mṛgayā pānam ityādivyasanarahitaḥ | guṇāḥ śāntyādayaḥ | tadvadbhis trisāmādibhiḥ (?) sahāyaiḥ saṃpannaḥ samavetaḥ | sāmādibhiś copāyaiḥ saṃpanno deśakālāvasthānurūpaṃ teṣāṃ prayoktā | sarvatra syād iti vakṣyamāṇam apekṣyate || 11.4 ||
samaḥ prajāsu syāt || 11.5 ||
vyavahārakāle dveṣye priye ca samaḥ syāt || 11.5 ||
hitam āsāṃ kurvīta || 11.6 ||
āsāṃ prajānāṃ yogakṣemayor avahitaḥ syāt || 11.6 ||
tam upary āsīnam adhastād upāsīrann anye
brāhmaṇebhyaḥ || 11.7 ||
tam evaṃguṇaṃ rājānam upari siṃhāsanādāv uccair āsīnam adhastād bhūmāv evāsīran | kim aviśeṣeṇa | na | anye brāhmaṇebhyo brāhmaṇavyatiriktāḥ | adha upāsīrann ity eva siddha upary āsīnam iti svabhāvānuvādaḥ | sarvadāyam upary āsīno bhavati na tu rahasy api bhūmāv iti
|| 11.7. ||
te ‘py enaṃ manyeran || 11.8 ||
te ‘pi brāhmaṇā enaṃ rājānaṃ manyerann āśīrvādādibhiḥ pūjayeyuḥ || 11.8 ||
varṇān āśramāṃś ca nyāyato ‘bhirakṣet || 11.9 ||
varṇā brāhmaṇādayaḥ | āśramā brahmacaryādayaḥ | tān nyāyato yathāśāstraṃ ṣaṣṭhāṃśādibhāgasvīkāreṇābhirakṣed abhito rakṣet | yathā varṇāśramadharmānuṣṭhānena nirapāyās te bhaveyuḥ | atha vā nyāyata iti yathā deśādidharmāṇāṃ bhaṅgo na bhavati tathā rakṣed iti | anulomādayo ‘vāntaraprabhavā varṇā eṣv evāntarbhūtāḥ | rakṣaṇaṃ sarvabhūtānām iti corādibhyo rakṣaṇaṃ pūrvoktam | idaṃ tu vacanaṃ varṇāśramadharmeṣu saṃkaro mā bhūd iti || 11.9 ||
calataś caitān svadharme sthāpayet || 11.10 ||
te yady ālasyādinā svadharmāc caleyus tataś calata etān svadharma eva nigṛhya sthāpayed iti
|| 11.10 ||
kasmāt punar evam asau karotīty āha |
dharmasya hy aṃśabhāg bhavatīti || 11.11 ||
vijñāyate hi yasmād rakṣato dharmasyāṃśo bhavati | upalakṣaṇam etat | arakṣato ‘py adharmasyāṃśo bhavatīti jñeyam | atra manuḥ |
sarvato dharmaṣaḍbhāgo rājño bhavati rakṣaṇāt |
adharmasyāpi ṣaḍbhāgo bhavaty asya hy arakṣataḥ || [MDh 8.304] iti || 11.11 ||
brāḥmaṇaṃ ca purodadhīta vidyābhijanavāgrūpa-
vayaḥśīlasaṃpannaṃ nyāyavṛttaṃ tapasvinam || 11.12 ||
“sa eṣa bahuśruto bhavati” [GDh 8.4] ity ārabhyoktā vidyā | viśiṣṭakule janmābhijanaḥ | vāk saṃskṛtā bhāratī | rūpaṃ manoharam | vayo madhyamaṃ nātibālo nātisthavira iti | śīlam antaḥkaraṇaśuddhir bāhyaṃ vānuṣṭhānam | etair vidyādibhiḥ saṃpannaṃ samṛddham | nyāyavṛttaṃ lokāviruddhācāram | tapasvinam abhogaparam | evaṃbhūtaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ purodadhīta purohitaṃ kurvīta || 11.12 ||
sarveṣu karmasu puro dhīyata iti purohitaḥ, tad darśayati |
tatprasūtaḥ karmāṇi kurvīta || 11.13 ||
tena purohitena prasūto ‘nujñāta idam itthaṃ kartavyam iti kṛtopadeśaḥ karmāṇi śrautasmārtādīni paurāṇikāni nityanaimittikāni śāntikapauṣṭikāny ābhicārikāṇi kurvīta | tatprasūta ity asya mūlatvena brāhmaṇam ākarṣati || 11.13 ||
brahmaprasūtaṃ hi kṣatrm ṛdhyate na vyathata iti
ca vijñāyate || 11.14 ||
brahma brāhmaṇas tena prasūtam anujñātaṃ hi kṣatraṃ kṣatriyam ṛdhyate [kṣatriyajātir ṛdhyate] samṛddhaṃ bhavatīti na vyathate na kutaścid bibheti | nirapāyaṃ syād ity arthaḥ | ity evaṃ prakāreṇa vijñāyate paramparayā dṛśyate || 11.14 ||
yāni ca daivotpātacintakāḥ prabrūyus tāny
ādriyeta || 11.15 ||
daivacintakā jyotirvidaḥ | utpātacintakāḥ śakunajñāḥ | utpātānāṃ cāgre phalāni jānate | te yat prabrūyur idam anyagrahavaikṛtam idam adya duḥśākunam ayam adyotpāto ‘yam eṣāṃ parihāra iti ca tāny api sarvāṇy ādriyeta nopekṣeta || 11.15 ||
kim artham |
tadadhīnam api hy eke yogakṣemaṃ pratijānate || 11.16 ||
na kevalaṃ rakṣaṇādivihitānuṣṭhānaṃ kiṃ tarhi tadadhīnam api daivotpātacintakair grahavaikṛtādau yat kartavyatayā proktaṃ tadadhīnam api yogakṣemaṃ bhavati | alabdhasya lābho yogaḥ | labdhasya rakṣaṇaṃ kṣemaḥ | tayoḥ samāhāradvandvaḥ | “āyogaprajā vindet,” “yogakṣemo naḥ kalpantām” [TaitS 7.5.18.1] ityādāv ekaviṃśatyādivat paraval liṅgatā | tad yathā – ekaś ca viṃśatiś vaikaviṃśatiḥ | taṃ yogakṣemaṃ pratijānata eka ācāryā iti || 11.16 ||
śāntipuṇyāhasvastyayanāyuṣmanmaṅgalasaṃyuktāny
ābhyudayikāni vidveṣaṇasaṃvananābhicāra-
dviṣadvyṛddhiyuktāni ca śālāgnau kuryāt || 11.17 ||
tatra āpastambo “rājñas tu viśeṣād vakṣyāmaḥ” [ĀpDh 2.25.1] iti prakṛtya, veśmāvasathaḥ sabheti tristhānāny abhisaṃdhāyāha “sarveṣv evājasrā agnayaḥ syur agnipūjā ca nityā yathā gṛhamedhe” iti [ĀpDh 2.25.6–7] | teṣām anyatamo ‘tra śālāgnir naupāsano nāpi tretāgnir gārhyaṣu śrauteṣu karmasu tayor niyatatvāt | śāntisaṃyuktaṃ daivotpātacintakasūcitāpacayanivṛttyarthaṃ yat kriyate grahaśāntimahāśantyādi | puṇyāhasaṃyuktaṃ dinadoṣanāśāya vivāhādau yat kriyate | svastyayanasaṃyuktaṃ yātrādau yat kriyate | āyuṣmatsaṃyuktaṃ janmanakṣatrādāv āyurvṛddhyarthaṃ yat kriyate | dūrvāhomādi maṅgalasaṃyuktaṃ gṛhapraveśādau yat kriyate vāstuhomādi | etāny ābhyudayikāny abhyudayanimittāni | vidveṣaṇasaṃyuktaṃ yenāsya śatruḥ prakṛtīnāṃ vidveṣyo bhavati | saṃvananasaṃyuktaṃ yenāsya śatravo vaśyāḥ praṇipatanti | abhicārasaṃyuktaṃ yenāsya śatravo miryante | ṛddher abhāvo vyṛddhiḥ | dviṣatāṃ vyṛddhir dviṣadvyṛddhiḥ | yenāsya śatravo vigataiśvaryā bhavanti | uccāṭanādīny etāni ca śālāgnau kuryāt | kaḥ | rājā | tasya ca kartṛtvam idam eva | yat tat saṃvidhātṛtvam arthasaṃpradānādinā | tad yathā yo ‘py ekānte tūṣṇīm āsīno bhaktabījabalīvardaiḥ pratisaṃvidhatte  so ‘py ucyante pañcabhir halaiḥ kṛṣyatīti | apara āha – ābhyudayikāni purohitaḥ svayaṃ kuryād itarāṇi kārayed iti | yasmiṃś cāgnāv ābhyudyikāni na tatretarāṇi kurvanti kiṃ tv agnyantare pūrvoktānām anyasmin || 11.17 ||
yathoktam ṛtvijo ‘nyāni || 11.18 ||
anyāni gārhyāṇi śrautāni ca tāni karmāṇi yathoktaṃ yasmin karmaṇi yāvanta ṛtvija uktās tāvantaḥ kuryuḥ | tad yathā – aupāsane cāgihotre cādhvaryur ekaḥ | darśapūrṇamāsayoś catvāraḥ | cāturmāsye pañca | paśubandhe ṣaṭ | jyotiṣṭomādau ṣoḍaśa | atra manuḥ |
purohitaṃ ca vṛṇuyād vṛṇuyād eva cartvijaḥ || iti | [MDh 7.78]
tatra yeṣv eva ṛtvik tatra purohito ‘dhvaryur brahmety anye || 11.18 ||
tasya ca vyavahāro vedo dharmaśāstrāṇy aṅgāny
upavedāḥ purāṇam || 11.19 ||
vyavaharanty aneneti vyavahāraḥ | tasya rājñaḥ prajāpālane ‘dhikṛtasya vedādīni vyavahārasādhanāni | yathā vedādiṣv abhihitaṃ tathā vyavahared iti | vyavahāro lokamaryādāsthāpanam || 11.19 ||
deśajātikuladharmāś cāmnāyair aviruddhāh
pramāṇam || 11.20 ||
deśadharmeṣu jātidharmeṣu ca pratiniyatam anuṣṭhīyamāneṣu yady api vedādi mūlabhūtaṃ nopalabhyate tathāpi yadi vedādibhir virodho na bhavati tathaiva te paripālanīyā na tu mūlāniyogena vihantavyā iti | tatra deśadharmāḥ – meṣasthe savitari cauleṣu kumāryo nānāvarṇai rajobhir bhūmāv ādityaṃ saparivāram ālikhya sāyaṃ prātaḥ pūjayanti | mārgaśīrṣyāṃ cālaṃkṛtā grāme paryaṭya yal labdhaṃ tad devāya nivedayante | karkaṭasthe savitari pūrvayoḥ phalgunyor bhagavatīm umām ārādhya yathāvibhavam arudbhyo ‘ṅkuritaṃ mudgalavaṇaṃ ca prayacchanti | mīnasthe savitary uttarayoḥ phalgunyor gṛhamedhinaḥ śriyaṃ devīṃ pūjayanti | jātidharmāḥ śūdrā vivāhe madhe sthūṇāṃ nikhāya sahasravartīr ekasyāṃ sthālyāṃ nidhāya prativarti dīpān āropya vadhūṃ haste gṛhītvā pradakṣiṇayanti | anyad apy evaṃjātīyakaṃ draṣṭavyam | kuladharmāḥ – kecin madhyaśikhāḥ | kecit pṛṣṭaśikhāḥ | pravacanādayas tu kālabhedenobhayataḥśikhāḥ | saṃbandhaś caitais taiḥ svavargair iti | ye tv āmnāyaviruddhā mātulasutāpariṇayanam, anadhītya vedān anyatra śrama ityādayo deśadharmā neha pramāṇam
|| 11.20 ||
karṣakavaṇikpaśupālakusīdikāravaḥ sve sve
varge || 11.21 ||
karṣakāḥ kṛṣijīvinaḥ | vaṇijaḥ krayavikrayavyavahāraparāḥ | paśupālā gopālāḥ | kusīdino vārdhuṣikāḥ | kāravas takṣarajakādayaḥ | ete sve sve varge svavargasaṃvede pramāṇam || 11.21 ||
tataś ca karṣakādiṣu dharmavipratipattau satyām |
tebhyo yathādhikāram arthān pratyavahṛtya
dharmavyavasthā || 11.22 ||
tebhyas tattadvargebhyo yathādhikāraṃ ye yatra varge vyavasthāpakatvenādhikṛtās tebhyo ‘rthān ācāraprakārān pratyavahṛtya śrutvāvadhārya dharmavyavasthā kāryā | ittham asmākaṃ nikāma ācāra iti tair ukte tathaiva vyavasthāpyam iti || 11.22 ||
atha te pakṣapātena mithyā brūyus tadā kathaṃ tattvaṃ jñātavyam |
nyāyādhigame tarko ‘bhyupāyaḥ || 11.23 ||
nyāyayuktasyārthasyādhigame ‘vadhāraṇe tarko ‘numānam abhyupāyaḥ | abhir dhātvarthānuvādī | tatra manuḥ |
ākārair iṅgitair gatyā ceṣṭayā harṣitena ca |
netravaktravikāraiś ca gṛhyate ‘ntargataṃ manaḥ || [MDh 8.26] iti || 11.23 ||
tataś ca |
tenābhyūhya yathāsthānaṃ gamayet || 11.24 ||
tena tarkeṇābhyūhyaivam ayam artho bhavitum arhatīti niścitya yathāsthānaṃ yatra pakṣe ‘rthas tatra gamayet || 11.24 ||
athātmana ekākinas tarkeṇāpi duradhigamatve sati |
vipratipattau traividyavṛddhebhyaḥ pratyavahṛtya
niṣṭhāṃ gamayet || 11.25 ||
vipratipattau satyāṃ duradhigamatve sati traividyavṛddhān samānāyya taiḥ saha vicāryārthatattvaṃ tebhyaḥ pratyavahṛtya niṣṭhāṃ gamayet | yatra pakṣe ‘rtho niṣṭhitas taṃ gamayet || 11.25 ||
kim evaṃ kurvato bhavati |
tathā hy asya niḥśreyasaṃ bhavati || 11.26 ||
evam asya nirṇayaṃ kurvato niśreyasam ubhayor lokayor bhavati | iha janānurāgenāmutra dharmaprāptyā ceti hetoḥ || 11.26 ||
na kevalaṃ rājña eva siddhiḥ | kiṃ tarhi | saha nirṇetṝṇāṃ brāhmaṇānām apīti darśayituṃ śrutim udāharati |
brahma kṣatreṇa saṃpṛktaṃ devapitṛmanuṣyān
dhārayatīti vijñāyate || 11.27 ||
brahma traividyalakṣaṇaṃ kṣatreṇa saṃyuktaṃ rājñā saha dharmaṃ viviñcad devapitṛmanuṣyān dhārayantīti śrutisiddham | evaṃ nirṇaye kṛte yathokaṃ karmānutiṣṭhanti manuṣyāḥ | tac ca dharmyaṃ karma devā upajīvanti pitaraś ca na kṣīyanta iti nyāyena sarveṣāṃ dhāraṇaṃ bhavatīti || 11.27 ||
atha dauḥśīlyād vyavasthāṃ nānumanyate tataḥ |
daṇḍo damanād ity āhus tenādāntān damayet || 11.28 ||
damanayogād daṇḍaśabdasya daṇḍatvam ity āhur dharmajñāḥ | tenādāntān avaśyān damayed vaśaṃ nayet | daṇḍenādāntān damayed ity evaṃ siddhe daṇḍaḥ –
dhigdaṇḍaṃ prathamaṃ kuryād vāgdaṇḍaṃ tadanantaram |
tṛtīyaṃ dhanadaṇḍaṃ tu vadhadaṇḍaṃ tataḥ param || [MDh 8.129]
devadānavagandharvā rakṣāṃsi patagoragāḥ |
te ‘pi bhogāya kalpyante daṇḍenaiva nipīḍitāḥ || [MDh 7.23] iti || 11.28 ||
athaivaṃ śāstravaśyatayā rājñā ca svadharme sthāpyamānānāṃ varṇānām āśramāṇāṃ ca kathaṃ siddhir ity ata āha |
varṇāśramāḥ svasvadharmaniṣṭhāḥ pretya karmaphalam
anubhūya tataḥ śeṣeṇa viśiṣṭadeśajātikularūpāyuḥśruta-
vṛttavittasukhamedhaso janma pratipadyante || 11.29 ||
varṇā brāhmaṇādayaḥ | āśramā brahmacaryādayaḥ | te svadharmaniṣṭhā varṇaprayuktān āśramaprayuktān ubhayaprayuktāṃś ca dharmān anuṣthitavantaḥ pretya maraṇena lokāntaraṃ gatvā tasya tasya karmaṇaḥ phalaṃ svargādikam anubhūya tatas tadanantaraṃ śeṣeṇa bhuktāvaśiṣṭena karmaṇā viśiṣṭadeśādikān bhuktvā janma pratipadyante | tatra viśiṣṭaśabdo deśadibhiḥ sarvaiḥ saṃbadhyate | viśiṣṭo deśa āryāvartādiḥ | viśiṣṭajātir brāhmaṇajātiḥ | viśiṣṭakulam adhyayanādisaṃpannam | viśiṣṭarūpaṃ kāntimat | viśiṣṭāyuḥ sahaṣoḍaśaṃ varṣaśatam, “sahaṣoḍaśaṃ varṣaśatam ajīvat” iti darśanāt | rogarahitatvam apy āyuṣo viśeṣaḥ | viśiṣtaśrutaṃ “brāhmaṇaś ca bahuśrutaḥ” [GDh 8.1] ity atra vyākhyātam | viśiṣṭavṛttam anupādhi cāritram | viṣiṣṭavittaṃ dharmārjitaṃ dharme prayujyamānaṃ ca | sukhaṃ nirapāyasthānādhiṣṭhānenāniṣiddhamukhasevanam | viśiṣṭamedhā granthārthayor grahaṇaśaktir iti | medhāśabde sakārāntatvam ārṣaṃ sumedhaso durmedhasa ityādiṣv eva darśanāt | karmāṇi bhujyamānāni puṇyāny apuṇyāni ca saśeṣāṇy evaṃ bhujyante | aihikasya śarīragrahaṇāder api puṇyāpuṇyanibandhanatvāt || 11.29 ||
viṣvañco viparītā naśyanti || 11.30 ||
ye varṇāśramāḥ svāni karmāṇi yathāvan nānutiṣṭhanti te viparītā viṣvañco nānāyonīr gacchanto naśyanti | anarthaparamparām anubhavantīti || 11.30 ||
tān ācāryopedeśo daṇḍaś ca pālayate || 11.31 ||
tān viparītān yathoktam akurvato varṇān āśramāṃś cācāryopadeśas tāvat pālayate | tatrāpy atiṣṭhato rājadaṇḍaḥ || 11.31 ||
yata evam |
tasmād rājācāryāv anindyāv anindyau || 11.32 ||
tasmād dheto rājācāryau mānyāv anindyāv iti | yady api niyamanakāle hitaiṣitayā pramukhapuruṣau bhavatas tathāpi tayor nindā na kāryā | [abhyāso ‘dhyāyasamāptyarthaḥ] || 11.32 ||
iti śrīgautamīyavṛttau haradattaviracitāyāṃ mitākṣarāyāṃ
dvitīyapraśne dvitīyo ‘dhyāyaḥ
atha dvādaśo ‘dhyāyaḥ
daṇḍenādāntān damayed ity uktam | tatra kiyaty aparādhe kiyān daṇḍa ity ata āha |
śūdro dvijātīn abhisaṃdhāyābhihatya ca vāgdaṇḍa-
pāruṣyābhyām aṅgaṃ mocyo yenopahanyāt || 12.1 ||
śūdraś caturtho varṇaḥ | sa dvijātīn brāhmaṇādīṃs trīn varṇān | vākpāryuṣyeṇābhisaṃdhāyābhibhūya daṇḍapāruṣyenābhihatya ca | abhir abhisaṃdhipūrve buddhipūṛvaṃ tāḍayitvā | daṇḍagrahaṇaṃ hastāder apy upalakṣaṇam | evaṃ kurvann aṅgaṃ mocyo ‘vayavena viyojanīyo yenāṅgenopahanyād aparādhaṃ kuryāt tad aṅgaṃ mocyaḥ | hastena tāḍane hastacchedaḥ pādena tāḍane pādacchedo vācā jihvācchedaḥ | atra manuḥ |
yenāṅgenāvaro varṇo brāhmaṇasyāparādhnuyāt |
tad aṅgaṃ tasya chettavyaṃ tan manor anuśāsanam || iti | [MDh 8.279]
pāruṣyagrahaṇāt parihāsenāpriyavacane parihāsādinā tāḍane ca nedaṃ bhavati || 12.1 ||
āryastryabhigamane liṅgoddhāraḥ svaharaṇaṃ
ca || 12.2 ||
śūdra iti prakṛtaṃ ṣaṣṭhyantam apekṣate | āryās traivarṇikāḥ | teṣāṃ cet striyaṃ śūdro ‘bhigacchet tasya liṅgoddhāro liṅgotpāṭanaṃ kāryaṃ yac ca yāvac ca svaṃ tasya ca haraṇaṃ daṇḍaḥ | āryābhigamanam ity eva siddhe strīgrahaṇam āryagṛhītānāṃ śūdrāyām apīti sūcanārtham | tatra vaiśyastriyāṃ svaharaṇaṃ kṣatriyāyāṃ liṅgoddhāraḥ | brāhmaṇyām ubhayam iti || 12.2 ||
goptā ced vadho ‘dhikaḥ || 12.3 ||
sa yadi śūdras tāsāṃ rakṣitā bhavati tadā vadhaḥ kāryaḥ | adhikagrahaṇāt pūrvoktadaṇḍadvayam api bhavati || 12.3 ||
atha hāsya vedam upaśṛṇvatas trapujatubhyāṃ
śrotrapratipūraṇam udāharaṇe jihvācchedo dhāraṇe
śarīrabhedaḥ || 12.4 ||
atha heti vākyālaṃkāre | upaśrutya buddhipūrvam akṣaragrahaṇam upaśravaṇam | asya śūdrasya vedam upaśṛṇvatas trapujatubhyāṃ trapuṇā jatunā ca dravīkṛtena śrotre pratipūrayitavye | upaśravaṇaśabdena yadṛcchayā dhvanimātraśravaṇe na doṣaḥ | sa ced dvijātibhiḥ saha vedākṣaraṇy udāhared uccaret, tasya jihvā chedyā | dhāraṇe sati yadānyatra gato ‘pi svayam uccārayituṃ śaknoti tataḥ paraśvādinā śarīram asya bhedyam || 12.4 ||
āsanaśayanavākpatheṣu samaprepsur daṇḍyaḥ || 12.5 ||
śūdraś ced āsanādiṣu dvijātibhiḥ saha sāmyaṃ prepsati tattulyabhāvaṃ tato ‘sau daṇḍyaḥ | daṇḍaś ca āpastambena darśitaḥ: “vāci pathi śayyāyām āsana iti samī bhavato daṇḍatāḍanam” [ĀpDh 2.27.15] iti | danḍenāsau tāḍya iti | atra mānavo viśeṣaḥ |
sahāsanam abhiprepsur utkṛṣṭasyāpakṛṣṭakaḥ |
kaṭyāṃ kṛtāṅko nirvāsyaḥ sphijau vāpy asya kartayet || [MDh 8.281] iti || 12.5 ||
śataṃ kṣatriyo brāhmaṇākrośe || 12.6 ||
kṣatriyaś ced brāhmaṇam ākrośed vācā paruṣayā nindet tataḥ śataṃ daṇḍyaḥ | daṇḍaprakaraṇe sarvatra tāmrikasya kārṣāpaṇasya grahaṇam iti smārto vyavahāraḥ | śataṃ kārṣāpaṇāni daṇḍyaḥ | daṇḍapāruṣye dviguṇam | athāha bṛhaspatiḥ |
vākpāruṣye kṛte yasya yathā daṇḍo vidhīyate |
tasyaiva dviguṇaṃ daṇḍaṃ kārayen maraṇād ṛte || iti || 12.6 ||
adhyardhaṃ vaiśyaḥ || 12.7 ||
vaiśyas tu brāhmaṇākrośe ‘dhyardhaṃ śataṃ daṇḍyo ‘rdhādhikaṃ pañcāśadadhikaṃ śataṃ daṇḍyaḥ || 12.7 ||
brāhmaṇas tu kṣatriye pañcāśat || 12.8 ||
kṣatriyākrośe brāhmaṇas tu pañcāśatpaṇān daṇḍyaḥ || 12.8 ||
tadardhaṃ vaiśye || 12.9 ||
vaiśyākrośe tadardhaṃ pañcaviṃśatipaṇān daṇḍyaḥ || 12.9 ||
na śūdre kiṃcit || 12.10 ||
śūdre tv ākruṣṭe na kiṃcid api dravyaṃ brāhmaṇo daṇḍyaḥ | tad idaṃ na vaktavyam: avacanād eva daṇḍābhāvaḥ sidhyet | kiṃ tu kṣatriyavaiśyayoḥ śūdrākrośe daṇḍaprāpaṇārtham uktam | tad uktam uśanasā: “śūdram ākruśya kṣatriyaś caturviṃśatipaṇān daṇḍabhāg vaiśyaḥ ṣaṭtriṃśat” iti || 12.10 ||
brāhmaṇarājanyavat kṣatriyavaiśyau || 12.11 ||
brāhmaṇarājanyayoḥ parasparākrośe yādṛśo daṇḍas tādṛśaḥ kṣatriyavaiśyayoḥ parasparākrośe | tataś caivaṃ sūtram ūhitavyam | śataṃ vaiśyaḥ kṣatriyākrośe | kṣatriyas tu vaiśyaṃ pañcāśat | evam antaraprabhaveṣv api draṣṭavyam | atra jamadagniḥ: “mātṛtulyam anulomānāṃ pitṛtulyaṃ pratilomānām” iti || 12.11 ||
uktaḥ sāhasadaṇḍaḥ | steyadaṇḍam āha |
aṣṭāpādyaṃ steyakilbiṣaṃ śūdrasya || 12.12 ||
steyaṃ cauryam | steyopāttaṃ dravyaṃ kilbiṣanimittatvāt kilbiṣam ucyate | steyenopāttaṃ dravyam aṣṭaguṇam āpādanīyaṃ śūdrasya | kartari ṣaṣṭhy eṣā | steyakilbiṣaṃ śūdro ‘ṣṭaguṇam āpādayed rājñe daṇḍarūpeṇa pratipādayed iti | tatraiko guṇaḥ svāmine deyaḥ | śeṣo rājñe | uktaṃ ca “corahṛtam avajitya” [GDh 10.46] ityādinā || 12.12 ||
dviguṇottarāṇītareṣāṃ prativarṇam || 12.13 ||
itareṣāṃ vaiśyādīnāṃ steyakilbiṣāṇi prativarṇaṃ dviguṇottarāṇy āpādanīyāni | vaiśyasya ṣoḍaśaguṇaṃ kṣatriyasya dvātriṃśadguṇaṃ brāhmaṇasya catuḥṣaṣṭiguṇam iti || 12.13 ||
kasmād idam evam ity āha |
viduṣo ‘tikrame daṇḍabhūyastvam || 12.14 ||
yathā yathā varṇotkarṣeṇa vidyotkarṣas tathā tathā vihitātikrame daṇḍabhūyastvaṃ bhavati | niṣedhadoṣaṃ jñātvāpi pravartamānasya doṣādhikyaṃ bhavati | ajānatas tv andhakūpapatanavad anugraho ‘sti | aṣṭāpādyam ityāder apavādaḥ || 12.14 ||
phalaharitadhānyaśākādāne pañcakṛṣṇalam
alpam || 12.15 ||
phalam āmrādi | haritadhānyaṃ stambe ‘vasthitaṃ vrīhyādi | śākaṃ vāstūkādi | eteṣāṃ steyenādāne pañcakṛṣṇalaṃ daṇḍaḥ | kṛṣṇalaṃ guñjābījapramāṇam |
māṣo viṃśatibhāgas tu jñeyaḥ kārṣāpaṇasya hi |
kṛṣṇalas tu caturthāṃśo māṣasyaiva prakīrtitaḥ || iti |
pañcānāṃ kṛṣṇalānāṃ samāhāraḥ pañcakṛṣṇalam | alpaṃ tac cet phalādi alpam udarapūraṇamātram | adhike tv aṣṭāpādyam eva || 12.15 ||
paśupīḍite svāmidoṣaḥ || 12.16 ||
paśubhir upahate sasyādau paśumato doṣaḥ | daṇḍaparimāṇaṃ vakṣyati || 12.16 ||
pālasaṃyukte tu tasmin || 12.17 ||
sa cet paśuḥ pālāya svāminā samarpitas tadā tasmin pāle doṣaḥ | pālayatīti pālo gopālaḥ | idaṃ pramādakṛte, buddhipūrve tu dviguṇo daṇḍaḥ, tathā smṛtyantare darśanāt || 12.17 ||
pathi kṣetre ‘nāvṛte pālakṣetrikayoḥ || 12.18 ||
kṣetrikaḥ kṣetrāny asya kṣetraṃ pathy anāvṛtaṃ bhavati tatra paśupīḍite pālakṣetrikayor ubhayor daṇḍo ‘rdham ardham | pālasyānavadhānāt kṣetrikasya vṛtyakaraṇāc ca,
vṛtiṃ ca tatra kurvīta yām uṣṭro nāvalokayet | [MDh 8.239]
iti mānave darśanāt || 12.18 ||
daṇḍaparimāṇam āha |
pañca māṣā gavi || 12.19 ||
uśanasā māṣo darśitaḥ |
māṣo viṃśatibhāgas tu jñeyaḥ kārṣāpaṇasya hi |
kākiṇī tu caturthāṃśo māṣasyaiva prakīrtitaḥ || iti ||
māṣāḥ pañca gopīḍite sasyādau daṇḍaḥ || 12.19 ||
ṣaḍ uṣṭrakhare || 12.20 ||
dvandvaikavadbhāvaḥ | uṣṭtrakhare tūpahantari pratyekaṃ ṣaṇmāṣā daṇḍaḥ || 12.20 ||
aśvamahiṣyor daśa || 12.21 ||
liṅgam avivakṣitam | aśve mahiṣe ca pratyekaṃ daśa māṣā daṇḍaḥ || 12.21 ||
ajāviṣu dvau dvau || 12.22 ||
ajeṣv aviṣu copasaṃhantṛṣu dvau dvau māṣau | saṃbhūya carantīti bahuvacanam | pratyajaṃ pratyavikaṃ dvau dvau daṇḍaḥ || 12.22 ||
sarvavināśe śadaḥ || 12.23 ||
yathā punaḥ praroho na bhaveti tathā sarvavināśe śado daṇḍaḥ | śada iti bhāgābhidhānam | yāvāṃs tatra bhāga utpatsyate tāvat svāmine deyam | rājñe cānurūpo daṇḍaḥ || 12.23 ||
śiṣṭākaraṇe pratiṣiddhasevāyāṃ ca nityaṃ cailapiṇḍād
ūrdhvaṃ svaharaṇam || 12.24 ||
śiṣṭaṃ vihitam | nityaṃ śiṣṭasyākaraṇe nityaṃ ca pratiṣiddhasevāyāṃ cailapiṇḍād ūrdhvaṃ cailam ācchādanaṃ piṇḍo grāsas tābhyām ūrdhvaṃ yāvatā tayor nivṛttis tato ‘dhikaṃ yat svaṃ tasya haraṇaṃ kāryam | ācchādanāsanārthaṃ yat kiṃcit parihāpyāvaśiṣṭam asya svaṃ hartavyam ity evam ato nivṛtteḥ || 12.24 ||
adattādānaniṣedhaviṣaye ‘pavādam āha |
go’gnyarthe tṛṇam edhān vīrudvanaspatīnāṃ ca puṣpāṇi
svavad ādadīta phalāni cāparivṛtānām || 12.15 ||
agniḥ śrautasmārtādir na laukikaḥ | gavārthe tṛṇāni | agnyartha edhān vīrudvanaspatīnām | latānāṃ vṛkṣāṇāṃ puṣpāṇi devatārcanārthāni nopabhogārthāni | gavāgnisāhacaryād devatārthānīti gamyate | etāni tṛṇādīni svāmibhir adattāny api svavad ādadīta | yathā svāmī niḥśaṅkam ādatte tadvad ādadīta | te vīrudvanaspatayo ‘parivṛtāś cet teṣāṃ phalāny api svavad ādadīta na svāmyapekṣā | phalaviṣayam etad aparivṛtatvaṃ na tṛṇādiviṣayam, pṛthagvākyatvāt
|| 12.25 ||
kusīdavṛddhir dharmyā viṃśatiḥ pañcamāṣikī
māsam || 12.26 ||
vṛddhyarthaṃ prayuktasya dravyasya kusīdasaṃjñā | “māṣaḥ kārṣāpaṇasya viṃśatitamo bhāgaḥ” ity uśanasoktam | pañca māṣā vṛddhirūpeṇa dīyante yatra viṃśatau sā pañcamāṣikī | tad asmin vṛddhyāyalābhaśulkopadā dīyata ity atrārthe prāgvahaseṣṭhak | adhyardhapūrvadvigor lug asañjāyām iti lukprāpto na kṛtaḥ svācchandyād ṛṣiṇā | kārṣāpaṇānāṃ viṃśatiḥ pratimāsaṃ pañcamāṣikī yathā bhavati tathā bhavantī kusīdavṛddhidharmād anapetā | atra manuḥ |
vasiṣṭhavihitāṃ vṛddhiṃ sṛjed vittavivardhinīm |
aśītibhāgaṃ gṛhṇīyān māsād vārdhuṣikaḥ śataḥ || iti | [MDh 8.140]
atrāpīyam eva vṛddhir uktā | katham | paṇasya viṃśatitamo bhāgo māṣaḥ | paṇānāṃ viṃśatiś catuḥśatī māṣāṇāṃ saṃpadyate | catuḥśatyāḥ pañca māṣā vṛddhir aśīter ekaḥ | pañcaśatīti yaś caturaśatīti (?) | yājñavalkyas tu |
aśītibhāgo vṛddhiḥ syān masi māsi sabandhake |
varṇakramāc chataṃ dvitricatuḥpañcakam anyathā || iti | [YDh 2.37]
viśvāsārthaṃ yad ādhīyate suvarṇādi tad bandhakam | tad ukte dhanaprayoge varṇānupūrvyād brāhmaṇādiṣv adhamarṇeṣu dhanaṃ dvitryādiyuktaṃ kramād bhavati || 12.26 ||
nātisāṃvatsarīm eke || 12.27 ||
yeyam aśītibhāgalakṣaṇā dharmyā vṛddhis tām atisāṃvatsarīṃ saṃvatsare ‘tikrānte bhavāṃ na gṛhṇīyāt, ekasminn eva saṃvatsare pratimāsam aśītibhāgo grāhyas tata ūrdhvaṃ na kiṃcid api grāhyam eṣā dharmyā bhavatīty eke manyante | atisāṃvatsarīm iti rūpasiddhiś cintyā || 12.27 ||
svamatam āha |
cirasthāne dvaiguṇyaṃ prayogasya || 12.28 ||
yāvatā kālena prayuktaṃ dhanaṃ dviguṇaṃ bhavati tāvantam eva kālaṃ dharmyayā vṛddhyā vivardhate nātaḥ param iti | suvarṇādidravyaviṣayam etat | atra vasiṣṭhaḥ: “dviguṇaṃ hiraṇyaṃ triguṇaṃ dhānyam | dhānyenaiva rasā vyākhyātāḥ | vṛkṣamūlaphalāni ca tulādhṛtam aṣṭaguṇam” [VaDh 2.44–47] iti | ciragrahaṇāt sahasreṇāpi saṃvatsarair na dvaiguṇyāt paraṃ vardhate iti || 12.28 ||
bhuktādhir na vardhate || 12.29 ||
viśvāsārthaṃ yad ādhīyate kāṃsyābharaṇādi sa ādhiḥ | sa ced upabhuktaḥ prayukto ‘rtho na vardhate | bhoga eva tatra vṛddhir iti || 12.29 ||
ditsato ‘varuddhasya ca || 12.30 ||
dhanine dhanaṃ dātum icchato ‘dhamarṇasya dhanaṃ na vardhate | dhanī vṛddhilobhād vyājena na gṛhṇāti cet tasminn eva divase parahaste sthāpyaṃ tad ārabhya vṛddhir na vardhate tathā yo ditsann adhamarṇo rājādināvaruddhas tasyāpi dātum asamarthasya dravyaṃ tata ārabhya na vardhate || 12.30 ||
athāpadi vṛddhyantarāṇy āha |
cakrakālavṛddhiḥ || 12.31 ||
vṛddhiśabdaḥ pratyekam abhisaṃbadhyate | yāvatā kālena yāvatī vṛddhis tām api mūlīkṛtya tāvato mūlasya punar vṛddhiś cakravṛddhiḥ || yathāha nāradaḥ |
vṛddher api punar vṛddhiś cakravṛddhir udāhṛtā || iti | [NSm 1.89]
iyataḥ kālasyeyatī vṛddhir iti yatra samayena gṛhyate sā kālavṛddhiḥ || 12.31 ||
kāritākāyikāśikhādhibhogāś ca || 12.32 ||
vṛddhaya it śeṣaḥ | prayoktā gṛ(gra)hītrā ca deśakālakāryāvasthāpekṣayā prabhūtā nyūnā vā svayam eva kalpitā vṛddhiḥ kāritā | kāyikā kāyakarmasaṃśodhyā | tathā bṛhaspatiḥ: “kāyikā karmasaṃyukā” iti | vyāsas tu |
dohyavāhyakarmayuktā kāyikā samudāhṛtā | iti |
śikhāvṛddhiṃ kātyāyana āha |
pratyahaṃ gṛhyate yā hi śikhāvṛddhis tu sā smṛtā |
śikhena vardhate nityaṃ śiraśchedān nivartate ||
mūle datte tathaiveṣā śikhāvṛddhis tataḥ smṛtā || iti |
udāharaṇam – taṇḍulaprasthasya pratyahaṃ taṇḍulamuṣṭir gṛhyata iti | ādhibhoga āhitasya kṣetrasya bhogo ‘nubhavaḥ | tatrānubhava eva vṛddhiḥ | sā ca śatenāpi saṃvatsarair na nivartate | kṣetraṃ cottamarṇasya na bhavati | yadā kadācid api mūlapradāne saty adhamarṇasya bhavati | adhibhoga ity anye | bhogam adhikṛtya vartata ity adhibhogavṛddhiḥ | tatrāpy eṣa evārthaḥ | etāsu cakravṛddhyādiṣu vṛddher dvaiguṇyāt param api bhavaty eva || 12.32 ||
kusīdaṃ paśūpajalomakṣetraśadavāhyeṣu
nātipañcaguṇam || 12.33 ||
paśor upajātaṃ paśūpajaṃ ghṛtakṣīrādi | ūrṇākambalacāmaravālavyajanādi lomakṣetraśadaḥ kṣetrabhogaḥ | vāhyaṃ balīvardādi | bāhyam iti prāyeṇa paṭhanti, tatrāpy eṣa evārthaḥ | eteṣu paśūpajādiṣu prayukteṣu tat kusīdaṃ yāvat pañcaguṇaṃ vardhate pañcaguṇatāṃ nātyeti | apara āha – paśūpajātiṣu mūlatvena kalpitasya dravyasya tadānīm apradāne yāvat pañcaguṇaṃ vardhate, dharmyayāṃ ca vṛddhyā pañcaguṇatāṃ natyeti || 12.33 ||
ajaḍāpaugaṇḍadhanaṃ daśavarṣabhuktaṃ paraiḥ
saṃnidhau bhoktuḥ || 12.34 ||
jaḍa unmattaḥ paugaṇḍo vyākṛtavyavahāraḥ | yo jaḍo na bhavati paugaṇḍo vā na bhavati tasya dhanaṃ parais tatsaṃnidhāv eva ced daśa varṣāṇi bhuktaṃ bhavati tadā tad dhanaṃ bhoktur eva svam iti niścīyate | sa eva bhogaḥ svāminaḥ sakāśād dānādirūpeṇa tasya dhanasya nirgataṃ sūcayati | katham aparathaitāvantaṃ kālam evam artham aparaloke tūṣṇīm āsīteti | atra kṣetraviṣaye yājñavalkyaḥ |
paśyato ‘bruvato bhūmer hānir viṃśativārṣikī |
pareṇa bhujyamānāyā dhanasya daśavārṣikī ||  [YDh 2.24]
iti |
paśyann anyasya dadataḥ kṣitiṃ yo na nivārayet |
svāmī satāpi lekhena na sa tal labdhum arhati ||
iti bṛhaspatiḥ | atra manuḥ |
yat kiṃcid daśa varṣāṇi saṃnidhau prekṣate dhanī |
bhujyamānaṃ parais tūṣṇīṃ na sa tal labdhum arhati || iti | [MDh 8.147]
“anāgamaṃ tu yo bhuṅkte” ityādi tv asaṃnidhiviṣayāṇi jaḍādiviṣayāṇi vā || 12.34 ||
asyāpavādaḥ |
na śrotriyapravrajitarājapuruṣaiḥ || 12.35 ||
śrotriyādibhir bhujyamānaṃ na bhogamātrāt teṣāṃ bhavati, upekṣākāraṇatvopapatteḥ | śrotriyapravrajitayor dharmatṛṣṇayopekṣeti | rājapuruṣasya tu bhayena | rājapuruṣagrahaṇaṃ sarveṣāṃ balavatām upalakṣaṇam | etena sāhasikā vyākhyātāḥ | aparigrahasyāpi pravrajitasya svasvāmike śūnyagrahādāv upabhogaḥ saṃbhavati || 12.35 ||
paśubhūmistrīṇām anatibhogaḥ || 12.36 ||
paśavaś catuṣpādaḥ | bhūmiḥ kṣetrārāmādikā | striyaḥ paricārikā dāsyaḥ | paśvādīnāṃ satve  (svatve) nātibhogo ‘pekṣitaḥ | alpenāpi bhogena bhoktuḥ svaṃ bhavati | katham anantaragṛhe dṛśyamānāṃ gāṃ svayaṃ takrādi krītvopayuñjāna upekṣeta, kathaṃ vā bahuphalam ārāmam, kathaṃ vā dāsīṃ yauvanasthām anvahaṃ paricārikām || 12.36 ||
rikthabhāja ṛṇaṃ pratikuryuḥ || 12.37 ||
ye yasya rakthabhājas te tadṛṇaṃ pratidadyuḥ | putrapautrais tu rikthābhāve ‘pi deyam | tathā ca bṛhaspatiḥ |
ṛṇam ātmīyavat pitryaṃ putrair deyaṃ vibhāvitam |
paitāmahaṃ samaṃ deyaṃ na deyaṃ tatsutasya tat || iti |
nāradaḥ |
kramād abhyāgataṃ prāptaṃ putrair yan narṇam uddhṛtam |
dadyuḥ paitāmahaṃ pautrās tac caturthān nivartate || [NSm 1.4]
yājñavalkyaḥ |
pitari proṣite prete vyasanābhiplute ‘pi ca |
putrapautrair ṛṇaṃ deyaṃ nihnave sākṣibhāvitam || [YDh 2.50] iti || 12.37 ||
prātibhāvyavaṇikśulkamadyadyūtadāṇḍāḥ
putrān nābhyābhaveyuḥ || 12.38 ||
atra nāradaḥ |
upasthānāya dānāya pratyayāya tathaiva hi |
trividhaḥ pratibhūr dṛṣṭas triṣv evārtheṣu sūribhiḥ || iti || [NSm 1.104]
tasya pratibhuvi prete dāyādān api dāpayet || iti || [MDh 8.160]
viṣṇu-yājñavalkyau |
darśane pratyaye dāne prātibhāvyaṃ vidhīyate |
ādye tu vitathe dāpyāv itarasya sutā api || iti | [YDh 2.53; ViDh 6.41]
tasmād idam api dānapratibhūvyatiriktaviṣayaṃ draṣṭavyam |
aham enaṃ darśayiṣyāmīti prātibhāvyaṃ tam darśayitvā pitari prete na tatputreṇāsau darśayitavya iti | vaṇig vāṇijyārtham upāttaṃ dravyaṃ tad api na putrān abhyābhavati | yadā salābhamūlaṃ dāsyāmīti paribhāṣya kasyacit sakāśād dravyaṃ gṛhītvā vāṇijyāya deśāntaraṃ gato mriyeta tadā tatputreṇa na tat pratikartavyam iti | tathā śulkaṃ pratiśrutya vivāhaṃ kṛtvā mṛte tatputraṃ na tacchuklam abhyābhavati | tathā mūlaṃ dāsyāmīti madyaṃ bahu pītvā mṛte na tatputreṇa tad dātavyam | tathā dyūtaṃ kṛtvā parājitas tatpaṇadravyam adatvaiva yadi mriyate tatputro na dātum arhati | yathā (tathā) vyavahāre parājito rājñe daṇḍam adatvaiva yadi mriyate tadā na so ‘pi daṇḍaḥ putrān abhyābhavati || 12.38 ||
nidhyanvādhiyācitāvakrītādhayo naṣṭāḥ sarvān
aninditān (a)puruṣāparādhena || 12.39 ||
nidhir nikṣepaḥ |
svaṃ dravyaṃ yatra visrambhān nikṣipaty aviśaṅkitaḥ |
sa nikṣepaḥ | anvādhir upanidhiḥ | aupanidhikam iti smṛtyantare prasiddham | tatra yājñavalkyaḥ |
bhājanastham anākhyāya haste nyasya yad arpayet |
dravyaṃ tad aupanidhikaṃ pratideyaṃ tathaiva tat || iti | [YDh 2.65]
yācitam utsavādiṣv ābharaṇādi | avakrītam adattamaulyam ardhadattamaulyaṃ vā | ādhir gopyādhiḥ | ete nidhyādayo yadi puruṣāparādhena vinā naṣṭā bhavanti corādibhir apahṛtāḥ [vā] sarvāṃs tān aninditān āhur adoṣān āhuḥ | na kevalaṃ putrān eva nābhyābhaveyuḥ kiṃ tarhi yeṣāṃ sakāśe nidhyādayaḥ kṛtās tān api nābhyābhavanti | aninditeti te yadi pūrvaṃ dṛṣṭadoṣā bhavanti tadā pūrvam idam | puruṣāparādhas tu yadi dhārayitāraḥ svadravyavan na rakṣayeyuḥ, yady agnibhayādau svadravyaṃ gṛhītvā nidhyādy upekṣeran svadravyaṃ vā guptaṃ nidhāya bahir nidhyādi sthāpayeyuḥ | etasmin puruṣāparādhe sati dadyur eva || 12.39 ||
stenaḥ prakīrṇakeśo musalī rājānam iyāt karmācakṣāṇaḥ         || 12.40 ||
stenaḥ suvarṇasteyakṛt |
suvarṇasteyakṛd vipro rājānam abhigamya tu |
svakarma khyāpayan brūyān māṃ bhavān anuśāstv iti || [MDh 11.99]
iti mānavam | prakīrṇakeśo muktakeśaḥ | āyasaḥ khādiro vā musala iti smṛtyantaram | tadvān | “aṃse musalam ādhāya” [ĀpDh 1.25.4] ity āpastambaḥ | rājānam iyāt karmācakṣāṇaḥ | “evaṃkarmāsmi praśādhi mām” iti bruvāṇaḥ || 12.40 ||
pūto vadhamokṣābhyām || 12.41 ||
vadhas tāḍanaṃ maraṇāntikam | “tenaiva hanyāt” [ĀpDh 1.25.4] ity āpastambaḥ | sakṛd eva tāḍanam,
gṛhītvā musalaṃ rājā sakṛd dhanyāt tu taṃ svayam | [MDh 11.100]
iti smaraṇāt | mokṣo mocanam | “punar evaṃvidhaṃ mā kārṣīr gaccha” iti | tābhyāṃ ca vadhamokṣābhyāṃ stenaḥ pūto bhavati | hato ‘pi śudhyati mukto ‘pi śudhyatīti || 12.41 ||
aghnann enasvī rājā || 12.42 ||
yadi dayādinā taṃ na hanyād rājā svayam enasvī bhavati | corasya yad enas tad asya bhavatīti
|| 12.42 ||
ayaṃ tu daṇḍo brāhmaṇavarjam iti darśayati |
na śārīro brāhmaṇadaṇḍaḥ || 12.43 ||
svayam upasthitasyāpi brāhmaṇasya śārīro daṇḍo na kartavyo mokṣa eva | tathā ca manuḥ |
vadhena śudhyati steno brāhmaṇas tapasaiva ca || iti | [MDh 11.100]
atraivakārabalāt tadānīṃ tasyāpi brāhmaṇasya tapasā mokṣaḥ | na kvāpi nimitte hastacchedādikam api kartavyam ity evamarthaḥ | tathā ca manuḥ |
triṣu varṇeṣu tāni syur akṣato brāhmaṇo vrajet || iti | [MDh 8.124]
tapasvibrāhmaṇaviṣayam idam || 12.43 ||
anyasya tu yathāparādhaṃ daṇḍam āha |
karmaviyogavikhyāpanavivāsanāṅkakaraṇāni || 12.44 ||
yathā punas tat karma na karoti tathā karaṇaṃ karmaviyogaḥ | sarvasvaharaṇaṃ pratibhūgrahaṇam ityādi | vikhyāpanaṃ cauryacihnena grāmanagarādiṣv āghoṣaṇam | vivāsanaṃ nirvāsanam | yathāparādhaṃ grāmanagarād rāṣṭrād vā | aṅkakaraṇaṃ cihnakaraṇam |
tatra manuḥ |
gurutalpe bhagaḥ kāryaḥ surāpāne surādhvajaḥ |
steye tu śvapadaṃ kāryaṃ brahmahaṇy aśirāḥ pumān || iti | [MDh 9.237]
eṣāṃ karmaviyogādīnām enaḥsu guruṣu gurūṇi laghuṣu laghūnīti nyāyenāparādhānurūpā vyavasthā | etan mahāpātakaviṣayam | aṅkakaraṇaṃ tu tapasvibrāhmaṇasyāpi bhavaty eva
|| 12.44 ||
apravṛttau prāyaścittī saḥ || 12.45 ||
yas tu rājā coraviṣayeṣv evaṃdaṇḍako na vartate tasyām apravṛttau svayaṃ prāyaścittī bhavati | tatra vasiṣṭhaḥ: “daṇḍyotsarge rājāikarātram upavaset trirātraṃ purohitaḥ | kṛcchram adaṇḍyadaṇḍane purohita ekarātraṃ trirātraṃ rājā” iti [VaDh 40–43] || 12.45 ||
corasamaḥ sacivo matipūrve || 12.46 ||
sācivyaṃ pratiśrayāśanadānādi sāhāyyam | tac cen matipūrvaṃ coro ‘yam iti jñātvāpi yadi sācivyaṃ karoti sa corasamaś coravad daṇḍyaḥ | ajñāte punar ajñānam eva śaraṇam || 12.46 ||
pratigrahītāpy adharmasaṃyukte || 12.47 ||
apiśabdān matipūrva ity anuvartate | yo ‘nyasya dravyam anena coritam iti jānann eva tataḥ pratigṛhṇāti so ‘pi tasminn adharmasaṃyukte pratigrahe corasamaḥ | prakaraṇād eva siddhe ‘dharmasaṃyuktagrahaṇam anyatrāpi pāpaviṣaye pratigrahītus tat tat pāpaṃ bhavatīti jñāpanārtham || 12.47 ||
puruṣaśaktyaparādhānubandhavijñānād
daṇḍaniyogaḥ || 12.48 ||
puruṣo brāhmaṇādijātiḥ | śaktir arthadaṇḍe bahvartho ‘lpārtha iti, śarīradaṇḍe durbalaḥ prabalo veti cintā | aparādhaḥ sākṣāt kartṛtvaṃ sācivyakartṛtvaṃ veti | anubandho ‘abhyāsaḥ | etān puruṣādīn vijñāya tadanurūpo daṇḍo niyoktavya iti || 12.48 ||
anujñānaṃ vā vedavitsamavāyavacanād
vedavitsamavāyavacanāt || 12.49 ||
vedavidāṃ trayāṇāṃ caturṇāṃ vā samavāyaḥ saṃghaḥ | atra manuḥ |
catvāro vā trayo vāpi yaṃ brūyur vedaparagāḥ |
sa dharma iti vijñeyo netareṣāṃ sahasraśaḥ || iti |
tasya saṃghasya vacanād anujñānaṃ vā kartavyam | anuparodho dharmo vacanīya iti yadi te brūyus tadā vaktavyam anujānāmi tvāṃ gaccha yatheṣṭam iti | [abhyāso ‘dhyāyasamāptyarthaḥ] || 12.49 ||
iti śrīgautamīyavṛttau haradattaviracitāyāṃ mitākṣarāyāṃ
dvitīyapraśne tṛtīyo ‘dhyāyaḥ
atha trayodaśo ‘dhyāyaḥ
vipratipattau sākṣinimittā satyavyavasthā || 13.1 ||
vipratipattau sākṣiṇaḥ praṣṭavyāḥ | tair yathoktaṃ tathā satyaṃ vyavasthāpyam | atra nāradaḥ |
ekādaśavidhaḥ sākṣī śāstre dṛṣṭo manīṣibhiḥ |
kṛtaḥ pañcavidhas teṣāṃ ṣaḍvidho ‘kṛta ucyate ||
likhitaḥ smāritaś caiva yadṛcchābhijña eva ca |
gūḍhaś cottarasākṣī ca sākṣī pañcavidhaḥ kṛtaḥ ||
anye punar anuddiṣṭāḥ sākṣiṇaḥ samudāhṛtāḥ |
grāmaś ca prāḍvivākaś ca rājā ca vyavahāriṇām ||
kāryeṣv abhyantaro yaś ca arthinā prahitaś ca yaḥ |
kulyākulyavivādeṣu bhaveyus te ‘pi sākṣiṇaḥ || [NSm 1.129–132] iti || 13.1 ||
te punaḥ kīdṛśāḥ kiyanto vety āha |
bahavaḥ syur aninditāḥ svakarmasu prātyayikā rājñāṃ
niṣprītyanabhitāpāś cānyatarasmin || 13.2 ||
varṇaprayuktāny āśramaprayuktāny ubhayaprayuktāni svāni karmāṇi śrautāni smārtāni ca | teṣv aninditā akaraṇād anyathākaraṇād vā | atha yājñāvalkyaḥ |
tryavarāḥ sākṣino jñeyāḥ śrautasmārtakriyāparāḥ || iti | [YDh 2.69]
pratyayo viśvāsas tena ye caranti te prātyayikāḥ | ya evaṃbhūtā[s te] rājñām adṛṣṭadoṣatayā viśvasanīyāḥ | arthipratyarthinor anyatarasmin niṣprītayo niḥsnehā anabhitāpā akṛtadveṣāḥ | evaṃbhūtā bahavaḥ sākṣiṇaḥ syuḥ | atra yājñavalkyaḥ |
ubhayānumataḥ sākṣī bhavaty eko ‘pi dharmavit || iti | [YDh 2.72]
abhyantaras tu nikṣepe sākṣym eko ‘pi vācyate |
arthinā prahitaḥ sākṣī bhavaty eko ‘pi yācitaḥ ||
iti kātyāyanaḥ |
pramāṇam eko ‘pi bhavet sāhaseṣu viśeṣataḥ |
iti vyāsaḥ || 13.2 ||
api śūdrāḥ || 13.3 ||
śūdrā apy evaṃvidhāś cet sākṣiṇo bhaveyuḥ kiṃ punar dvijātaya iti | evaṃ ca guṇavaddvijātyabhāve śūdrā apy evaṃvidhā bhavantīti draṣṭavyam || 13.3 ||
brāhmaṇas tv abrāhmaṇavacanād anavarodhyo
‘nibaddhaś cet || 13.4 ||
brāhmaṇo nātra śrotriyaḥ | asya vṛttāntasyāsau brāhmaṇaḥ sākṣīty abrāhmaṇenokte rājñā sākṣitvena nāvarodhyo na nirbandhena grāhyaḥ, anibaddhaś cet | sa cel lekhanibaddho na bhavati | lekhyārūḍhas tu bhavaty eva sākṣī | nātra kaścid dhetur asti vacanam eva pramāṇam | atra nāradaḥ |
asākṣy api hi śāstreṣu dṛṣṭaḥ pañcavidho budhaiḥ |
vacanād doṣato bhedāt svayam ukter matāntarāt ||
śrotriyād yā vacanataḥ stenād yā doṣadarśanāt |
bhedād vipratipattiḥ syād vivāde yatra sākṣiṇām ||
svayamuktir anirdiṣṭaḥ svayam evaitya yo vadet |
mṛtāntaro ‘rthini prete mumūrṣuḥ śrāvitād ṛte || iti | [NSm 1.137–139]
tad iha śrotriyaḥ kvacid api sākṣī na bhavatīti nāradasya pakṣaḥ | ihābrāhmaṇavacanād ity uktatvād brāhmaṇenoktaḥ śrotriyo ‘pi bhavaty eva sākṣī || 13.4 ||
nāsamavetāpṛṣṭāḥ [nāsamavetā apṛṣṭāḥ] prabrūyuḥ || 13.5 ||
asamavetā asamuditā rājñā prāḍvivākena vāpṛṣṭāḥ santo na brūyuḥ | kiṃ tu samavetāḥ pṛṣṭāś ca prabrūyuḥ || 13.5 ||
avacane ‘nyathāvacane ca doṣiṇaḥ syuḥ || 13.6 ||
te caivaṃbhūtā yadi jānanta eva na [brūyur anyathā vā] brūyus tadā doṣiṇo duṣṭāḥ syuḥ | iha rājñā daṇḍyāḥ paratra ca nārākiṇaḥ || 13.6 ||
svargaḥ satyavacane viparyaye narakaḥ || 13.7 ||
bruvantas tu yadi satyaṃ bruvanti tadā svargo bhavati | viparyaye ‘satyavacane narako bhavatīti || 13.7 ||
anibaddhair api vaktavyam || 13.8 ||
nibaddhā nirdiṣṭā yūyam atra sākṣiṇa iti | tadviparītā anibaddhās tair api sākṣyaṃ vaktavyam | te ca nāradena “anye punar anirdiṣṭāḥ” ity ārabhya kathitā draṣṭavyaḥ || 13.8 ||
na pīḍākṛte nibandhaḥ || 13.9 ||
pīḍākṛtaṃ pīḍākaraṇam | nibandho nibandhanam arthasaṃbandhādi | pīḍākaraṇe hiṃsāviṣaye sākṣiṇāṃ nibandho na nirūpyaḥ | arthasaṃbandhādi na kiṃcid api dūṣaṇaṃ bhavati | āha vyāghraḥ |
steye ca sāhase caiva saṃsarge ca striyās tathā |
garādīnāṃ prayoge ca na doṣaḥ sākṣiṣu smṛtaḥ || iti || 13.9 ||
pramattokte ca || 13.10 ||
pramādo ‘navadhānam | antye pare vākye sākṣiṇā yadṛcchayā yad uktaṃ tatrāpi nibandho na bhavati | arthasaṃbandhādidūṣaṇaṃ na bhavati || 13.10 ||
viparyaye naraka uktaḥ | na sa kevalaṃ sākṣiṇa eva, kiṃ tarhi |
sākṣisabhyarājakartṛṣu doṣo dharmatantrapīḍāyām
|| 13.11 ||
tantraṃ lokavyavahāraḥ | dharmatantrayoḥ pīḍāyāṃ satyāṃ sākṣiṣu sabhyeṣu rājani kartari ca sarveṣu doṣo bhavati | kartṛgrahaṇaṃ dṛṣṭāntārtham | yāvān kartur doṣas tāvān sākṣyādīnām apīti | yady api sākṣiṇaḥ pūrvaṃ doṣa uktas tathāpīha grahaṇaṃ sabhyādīnāṃ sasākṣike ‘pi doṣagrahaṇārtham | anyathāsākṣikavyavahāre sabhyādīnāṃ doṣaḥ | sasākṣike tu sākṣiṇām evety uktaṃ syāt || 13.11 ||
śapathenaike satyakarma || 13.12 ||
yatra sākṣiṣu tathā viśvāso na bhavati tatra śapathena satyakarma śapathaṃ kārayitvā satyaṃ vācanīyam ity eke manyante || 13.12 ||
tad devarājabrāhmaṇasaṃsadi syād
abrāhmaṇānām || 13.13 ||
tac chapathena satyakarma devasaṃsadi, ugrāṇāṃ devatānāṃ saṃnidhau brāhmaṇānāṃ saṃsadi pariṣadi vā bhavati | kṣatriyādīnām arthagurutvalaghutvāpekṣo vikalpaḥ | mahaty arthe devatāsaṃnidhāv alpīyasy anyatreti | abrāhmaṇānām iti vacanād brāhmaṇānāṃ śapathakarma na bhavati | atra viṣṇuḥ: “pṛcched brūhīti brāhmaṇam | satyaṃ brūhīti rājanyam | gobījakāñcanair vaiśyam | sarvapātakaiḥ śūdram | evaṃ hi sākṣiṇaḥ pṛcched varṇānukramato nṛpaḥ” [ViDh 8.20–23, 38] iti | manus tu |
satyena śāpayed vipraṃ kṣatriyaṃ vāhanāyudhaiḥ |
gobījakāñcanair vaiśyaṃ śūdraṃ sarvais tu pātakaiḥ || [MDh 8.113] iti || 13.13 ||
viparyaye naraka iti sāmānyena sākṣiṇo doṣa uktaḥ | idānīṃ vyavahāraviśeṣe doṣaviśeṣam āha |
kṣudrapaśvanṛte sākṣī daśa hanti || 13.14 ||
kṣudrapaśavo ‘jāvikādayaḥ | tadviṣaye ‘nṛtavadane sākṣī daśa hanti | teṣāṃ daśānāṃ vadhe yāvān doṣas tāvān asya bhavatīti | daṇḍaprāyaścitte api tadanuguṇe draṣṭavye || 13.14 ||
go’śvapuruṣabhūmiṣu daśaguṇottarān || 13.15 ||
uktānām uttaraṃ daśa guṇān daśaguṇottarān | gavādiviṣaye ‘nṛte sākṣī pūrvoktād daśaguṇottaraṃ tattadvadhayuktadoṣo bhavati | etad uktaṃ bhavati | gavānṛte sākṣiṇo gośatahananadoṣaḥ | aśvānṛte ‘śvasahasrahananadoṣaḥ | puruṣānṛte ‘yutapuruṣahananadoṣaḥ | bhūmyanṛte yasya sā bhūmis tajjātīyānāṃ lakṣahananadoṣa iti |
pañca paśvanṛte hanti daśa hanti gavānṛte |
śatam aśvānṛte hanti sahasraṃ puruṣānṛte || [MDh 8.98]
ity etat tv atyantakṣudrapaśvādiviṣayam || 13.15 ||
sarvaṃ vā bhūmau || 13.16 ||
yadi vā bhūmiviṣaye ‘nṛte sarvam eva manuṣyajātaṃ hanti | grāmadeśādimahābhūmiviṣayo vikalpaḥ || 13.16 ||
haraṇe narakaḥ || 13.17 ||
prāsaṅgikam idam | bhūmer iti vipariṇāmena saṃbandhaḥ | bhūmer haraṇe narako bhavati | kālāntarāvadhiḥ śāstrāntarāvaseyaḥ || 13.17 ||
prakṛtam āha |
bhūmivad apsu || 13.18 ||
abviṣaye ‘nṛte bhūmival lakṣahananadoṣo haraṇe naraka iti ca samānam | apśabdena kūpataḍāgādirūpalakṣitaḥ || 13.18 ||
maithunasaṃyoge ca || 13.19 ||
maithunasaṃyukte cānṛte paradārān asau gacchatīty ādau bhūmivad iti cakārād gamyate || 13.19 ||
paśuvan madhusarpiṣoḥ || 13.20 ||
madhusarpirviṣaye ‘nṛte kṣudrapaśuvad doṣaḥ || 13.20 ||
govad vastrahiraṇyadhānyabrahmasu || 13.21 ||
brahma vedaḥ | vastrādiviṣaye ‘nṛte govad doṣaḥ | adhītya nāsmān mayādhītam ityādi brahmānṛtam || 13.21 ||
yāneṣv aśvavat || 13.22 ||
hastiśakaṭaśibikādīni yānāni | tadviṣaye ‘nṛte ‘śvavad doṣaḥ | anye tu kṣudrapaśvanṛta ity ārabhya sākṣiśrāvaṇe yojayanti | kṣudrapaśvanṛte sākṣiṇo daśapaśuhananadoṣaḥ | tasmāt tvayā satyam eva vaktavyam iti sākṣī śrāvayitavya iti | evaṃ sarvatropariṣād api || 13.22 ||
evam adṛṣṭaviṣaye doṣam uktvā dṛṣṭaviṣaye sākṣiṇo daṇḍam āha |
mithyāvacane yāpyo daṇḍyaś ca sākṣī || 13.23 ||
mithyāvacane dṛṣṭe sākṣī yāpyo garhyaḥ sarvair ayam asaṃvyavahārya iti, daṇḍyaś ca rājñā | atra manuḥ |
lobhāt sahasraṃ daṇḍyas tu mohāt pūrvaṃ tu sāhasam |
bhayād dvau madhyamau daṇḍyau maitryāt pūrvaṃ caturguṇam ||
kāmād daśaguṇaṃ pūrvaṃ krodhāt taddviguṇaṃ param |
ajñānād dve śate pūrṇe bāliśyāc chatam eva tu || [MDh 8.120–121]
kūṭasākṣyaṃ tu kurvāṇāṃs trīn varṇān dhārmiko nṛpaḥ |
pravāsayed daṇḍayitvā brāhmaṇaṃ tu vivāsayet || iti | [MDh 8.123]
viṣṇuḥ: “kūṭasākṣiṇāṃ sarvasvāpahāra utkocopajīvināṃ [?] ca” [ViDh 5.180–181] iti || 13.23 ||
