nānṛtavacane doṣo jīvanaṃ cet tadadhīnam || 13.24 ||
yadā satyavacanāt parasparavadho ‘nṛtavadane tu tadadhīnam anṛtavacananibandhanam anyasya jīvanaṃ bhavati na vadhas tatrānṛtavacane na pūrvokto doṣa iti | atra yājñavalkyaḥ |
varṇināṃ hi vadho yatra tatra sākṣy anṛtaṃ vadet |
tatpāvanāya nirvāpyaś caruḥ sārasvato dvijaiḥ || [YDh 2.83] iti || 13.24 ||
na tu pāpīyaso jīvanam || 13.25 ||
yadi tv anṛtavacanena pāpīyasaḥ pāpavattarasya parapīḍāratasya jīvanaṃ bhavati tadā na tu na doṣaḥ | api tu doṣa eveti || 13.25 ||
atha sākṣiṇaḥ kena praṣṭavyāḥ, tam āha |
rājā prāḍvivāko brāhmaṇo vā śāstravit || 13.26 ||
pṛcchatīt prāṭ | vivicya vaktīti vivākaḥ | nyaṅkādiṣu darśanād vṛddhikutve | rājā prāḍvivākaḥ syāt | anyapare tu tasmiṃs tena niyukto brāhmaṇo vā śāstravit | atra manuḥ |
yad vā svayaṃ na kuryāt tu nṛpatiḥ kāryanirṇayam |
tadā niyuñjyād vidvāṃsaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ kāryanirṇaye || [MDh 8.9] iti || 13.26 ||
prāḍvivākam adhyābhavet || 13.27 ||
adhir uparibhāva aiśvarye vā | āṅ āgamanārthe | enam uktalakṣaṇaṃ prāḍvivākam upary āsīnam adhaḥsthitaś ciraṃ vā guṇabhūtaḥ sann āgacchet kāryārthī | na tu prāḍvivākaḥ svayaṃ kāryam utpādyāhvayed iti | tathā ca manuḥ |
notpādayet svayaṃ kāryaṃ rājā nāpy asya pūruṣaḥ | [MDh 8.43] iti || 13.27 ||
saṃvatsaraṃ pratīkṣetāpratibhāyām || 13.28 ||
yadābhiyuktasyārthinaḥ sākṣiṇo vāpratibhā bhavati vaktavyaṃ na pratibhāti svayaṃ jāḍyādyupetatvād arthasya vā ciranirvṛttatvādinā durnirūpyatvāt tadā saṃvatsaraṃ pratīkṣeta | etāvatā kālena nirūpya brūhīti kālaṃ dadyāt | atra kātyāyanaḥ |
asvatantrajaḍonmattabāladīkṣitarogiṇām |
kālaḥ saṃvatsarād arvāk svayam eva yathepsitam ||
nāradaḥ |
gahanatvād vivādānām asāmarthyāt smṛter api |
ṛṇādiṣu haret kālaṃ kāmaṃ tattvabubhutsayā || iti | [NSm Mā. 1.38]
prajāpatiḥ |
dinam ekam atha dve vā trīṇi vā pañca sapta vā |
kālas tv ṛṇādau gahana ā tripakṣād api smṛtaḥ || 13.28 ||
dhenvanaḍutstrīprajananasaṃyukte ca śīghram || 13.29 ||
saṃyuktaśabdaḥ pratyekaṃ saṃbadhyate | dhenvādisaṃyukte vivāde śīghraṃ vivādayet | prajananaṃ vivāhas taddhetutvāt | strī dāsyādiḥ | tathāha kātyāyanaḥ |
dhenāv anuḍuhi kṣetre strīṣu prajanane tathā |
nyāse cāritrake datte tathaiva krayavikraye ||
kanyāyā dūṣaṇe steye kalahe sāhase nidhau |
upadhau kūṭasākṣye ca sadya eva vivādayet || iti || 13.29 ||
ātyayike ca || 13.30 ||
vyapaiti gauravaṃ yatra vināśas tyāga eva ca |
kālaṃ tatra na kurvīta kāryam ātyayikaṃ hi tat ||
iti kātyāyanaḥ | evamādāv ātyayike śīghraṃ vivādayen na kālaṃ dadyād iti | yājñavakyaḥ |
sāhasasteyapāruṣyago’bhiśāpātyaye striyām |
vivādayet sadya eva kālo ‘nyatrecchayā smṛtaḥ || [YDh 2.12] iti || 13.30 ||
sarvadharmebhyo garīyaḥ prāḍvivāke satyavacanaṃ
satyavacanam || 13.31 ||
śrutismṛticoditebhyaḥ sarvadharmebhyo gurutaram idaṃ yat prāḍvivāke pṛcchati sati satyaṃ brūyāt | dviruktir adhyāyasamāptyarthā || 13.31 ||
iti śrīgautamīyavṛttau haradattaviracitāyāṃ mitākṣarāyāṃ
dvitīyapraśne caturtho ‘dhyāyaḥ
atha caturdaśo ‘dhyāyaḥ
athāśaucanirṇayaḥ |
śāvam āśaucaṃ daśarātram anṛtvigdīkṣita-
brahmacāriṇāṃ sapiṇḍānām || 14.1 ||
śavanimittaṃ śāvam | aśucibhāva āśaucam | daśarātraṃ daśāhorātraṃ bhavati sapiṇḍānām ṛtvigādivarjitānām | ṛtvig yājakaḥ karmaṇi vartamānaḥ | dīkṣitaḥ kṛtadīkṣaṇīyaḥ karmaṇi vartamānaḥ | brahmacārī prasiddhaḥ | āvabhṛthaṃ pūrvayoḥ | ābrahmacaryaparyantaṃ parasya | kiṃ punar idam āśaucalakṣaṇam | karmaṇy anadhikāro ‘bhojyānnatāspṛśyatā dānādiṣv anadhikāritā | atra manuḥ |
ubhayatra daśāhāni kulasyānnaṃ na bhujyate |
dānaṃ pratigraho yajñaḥ svādhyāyaś ca nivartate ||
na spṛśeyur anāsannāḥ pretasyāsannabāndhavān || iti ca |
aṅgirāḥ |
sūtake tu yadā vipro brahmacārī viśeṣataḥ |
pibet pānīyam ajñānāt samaśnīyāt spṛśeta vā ||
pānīyamāne kurvīta pañcagavyasya bhakṣaṇam |
trirātraṃ bhojane proktaṃ spṛṣṭvā snānaṃ vidhīyate || iti |
yājñavalkyaḥ |
udakyāśaucibhiḥ snāyāt saṃspṛṣṭas tair upaspṛśet || iti | [YDh 3.30]
saṃvartaḥ |
asthisaṃcayanād ūrdhvam aṅgasparśo vidhīyate || iti |
vyāghraḥ |
maraṇād eva kartavyaṃ saṃyogo yasya nāgnibhiḥ |
dahanād eva kartavyaṃ yasya vaitāniko vidhiḥ || iti |
śaṅkhaḥ |
caturthe daśarātraṃ syāt ṣaṇniśāḥ puṃsi pañcame |
ṣaṣṭhe caturahāc chuddhiḥ saptame tu dinatrayam || iti |
etat sarvaṃ nirguṇaviṣayam | guṇavadviṣaye parāśaraḥ |
ekāhāc chudhyate vipro yo ‘gnivedasamanvitaḥ |
tryahāt kevalavedas tu nirguṇo daśabhir dinaiḥ || iti |
bṛhaspatiḥ |
trirātreṇa viśudhyeta vipro vedāgnisaṃyutaḥ |
pañcāhenāgnihinas tu daśāhād brāhmaṇabruvaḥ || iti |
atra brahmacārigrahaṇaṃ gṛhasthavyatiriktānām āśramāṇām upalakṣaṇārtham | atra bṛhaspatiḥ |
naiṣṭhikānāṃ vratasthānāṃ yatīnāṃ brahmacāriṇām |
nāśaucaṃ sūtake proktaṃ śāve vāpi tathaiva ca || iti |
dīkṣitagrahaṇaṃ cāndrāyaṇādivratapravṛttānām upalakṣaṇārtham | atra vasiṣṭhaḥ |
na rājñām atha doṣo ‘sti vratināṃ satriṇāṃ tathā |
aindrasthānam upāsīnā na cāpūtā hi te sadā || [VaDh 19.48] iti || 14.1 ||
ekādaśarātraṃ kṣatriyasya || 14.2 ||
dīkṣitabrahmacārivyatiriktasya jñātimaraṇe kṣatriyasyaikādaśarātraṃ bhavati | “dvādaśarātreṇa” [YDh 3.22] iti yājñavalkyaḥ | “pañcadaśarātreṇa” [VaDh 4.28] iti vasiṣṭhaḥ | “daśarātreṇa” iti parāśaraḥ | “ṣoḍaśāham” iti paiṭhīnasiḥ | eteṣāṃ vṛtāgnisvādhyāyasamāsavyāsāpekṣo vikalpaḥ
|| 14.2 ||
dvādaśarātraṃ vaiśyasyārdhamāsam eke || 14.3 ||
“ekādaśarātram” parāśaraḥ | “viṃśatirātram” [VaDh 4.29] vasiṣṭha-paiṭhīnasī | pūrvavad vikalpaḥ || 14.3 ||
māsaṃ śūdrasya || 14.4 ||
“sacchūdrāṇām ardhamāsam” ity uśanā | ye traivarṇikān paricarantas tebhyo vṛttiṃ lipsante te sacchūdrāḥ | sā hi teṣām uttamā vṛttir ity avocāma | dāsaviṣaye bṛhaspatiḥ |
dāsāntevāsibhṛtakāḥ śiṣyāś caikatravāsinaḥ |
svāmitulyena śaucena śudhyanti mṛtasūtake || iti |
atra kramavivāhe baudhāyanaḥ |
kṣatraviṭśūdrajātīyā ye syur viprasya bāndhavāḥ |
teṣām aśauce viprasya daśāhāc chuddhir iṣyate ||
rājanyavaiśyāv apy evaṃ hīnajātiṣu bandhuṣu |
svam evāśaucaṃ kuryātāṃ viśucchyartham asaṃśayaḥ || iti |
bṛhaspatis tu |
śudhyed vipro daśāhena janmahānyoḥ svayoniṣu |
saptapañcatrirātrais tu kṣatraviṭśūdrayoniṣu || iti |
atra viṣṇur viśeṣam āha: “brāhmaṇasya kṣatriyaviṭśūdreṣu ṣaḍrātratrirātraikarātraiḥ | kṣatriyasya viṭśūdrayoḥ ṣaḍrātratrirātrābhyām | vaiśyasya śūdre ṣaḍrātrāc chuddhiḥ” [ViDh 22.22–24] iti prakṛtam | eṣāṃ vṛttādyapekṣayā vyavasthā | adhikavarṇaviṣaye manuḥ |
sarve tūttamavarṇānām āśaucaṃ kuryur ādṛtāḥ |
tadvarṇavidhidṛṣṭena svaṃ tv āśaucaṃ svayoniṣu || iti |
uttamavarṇānāṃ maraṇaprayuktam āśaucam uktaṃ tadvarṇavidhidṛṣṭena prakāreṇa kuryuḥ, svayoniṣu tu svāśaucaṃ svajātinimittaṃ kuryur iti | ata eva jñāyate mātṛjātiyuktā anulomānāṃ dharmā iti || 14.4 ||
tac ced antaḥ punar āpatec cheṣeṇa śudhyeran || 14.5 ||
antaritasya pratiyogyapekṣāyāṃ śāvam āśaucaṃ daśarātram iti prakṛtaṃ daśarātrādy abhisaṃbadhyate | śāvasya daśarātrāder āśaucasya madhye tasmin vartamāne yady anyac chāvāśaucaṃ samānakālaṃ nyūnakālaṃ vā punar āpated āgacchet tataḥ śeṣeṇa pūrvaṃ vartamānasya daśarātrāder yāni śiṣṭāny ahāni tair eva śudhyeran | na punar āpatitasya kālapratīkṣeti | atra janane ‘py evam ity atideśāt pūrṇasya jananāśaucasya madhye samānakālaṃ vā nyūnakālaṃ vā jananāśaucam āgacchec cheṣeṇa śudhyeran | yatra nyūnakālasyāśaucasya madhye pūrṇakālam āpatet tenaiva gacchati | atra manuḥ |
antardaśāhe syātāṃ cet punar maraṇajanmanī |
tāvat syād aśucir vipro yāvat tat syād anirdaśam || iti | [MDh 5.79]
devalaḥ |
ādyānāṃ yaugapadye tu jñeyā śuddhir garīyasī || iti |
aṅgirāḥ |
mātary agre pramītāyām aśuddhau mriyate pitā |
pituḥ śeṣeṇa śuddhiḥ syān mātuḥ kuryāt tu pakṣiṇīm || iti |
sūtakād dviguṇaṃ śāvaṃ śāvād dviguṇam ārtavam |
ārtavād dviguṇā sūtis tato ‘dhi śavadāhake || iti |
vṛddhātriḥ: “anena dāhakasya sūtikāyāś ca pūrvāśaucaviśeṣeṇottarasya śuddhiḥ” iti | atra ṣaṭtriṃśanmatam |
śāvāśauce samutpanne sūtakaṃ tu yadā bhavet |
śāvena śudhyate sūtir na sūtiḥ śāvaśodhanī || iti || 14.5 ||
rātriśeṣe dvābhyām || 14.6 ||
pūrvasminn āśauce rātriśeṣe sati yady anyad āpatet tato dvābhyām ahobhyāṃ śudhyeran || 14.6 ||
prabhāte tisṛbhiḥ || 14.7 ||
atha daśāhādau vyatīte ‘paredyuḥ prabhāte saṃgave yady anyad āpater tatas tisṛbhī rātribhiḥ śudhyeran | atra manuḥ |
vigataṃ tu videśasthaṃ śṛṇuyād yo hy anirdaśam |
yac cheṣaṃ daśarātrasya tāvad evāśucir bhavet | [MDh 5.75] | iti || 14.7 ||
gobrāhmaṇahatānām anvakṣam || 14.8 ||
gavārthe brāhmaṇārthe vā hatānāṃ ye sapiṇḍās teṣām āśaucam anvakṣam | anvakṣyate pratyakṣyate śavas tāvat saṃskārānte snātvā śudhyerann iti | atra sadyaḥśaucādhikāre manuḥ |
gobrāhmaṇasya caivārthe yasya cecchati pārthivaḥ || iti | [MDh 5.95]
gavā brāhmaṇena vā ye hatās tajjñātīnām āśaucam anvakṣam iti cārthaḥ | tathāh uśanā: “gobhir hatānāṃ brāhmaṇair hatānāṃ ca sadyaḥ śaucam” iti || 14.8 ||
rājakrodhāc ca || 14.9 ||
hatānām ity upasamastam apekṣate | rājakrodhād ye hatās tajjñātīnām apy anvakṣam āśaucam
|| 14.9 ||
yuddhe || 14.10 ||
cakāro ‘nuvartate | yuddhe ca hatānām anvakṣam āśaucam | atra manuḥ |
ḍimbāhavahatānāṃ ca vidyutā pārthivena ca || iti | [MDh 5.95]
ḍimbo janasaṃmardaḥ | sadyaḥśaucaṃ prakṛtam | pāṭhāntaraṃ tv asmabhyaṃ na rocate | yadi vā sa eva pāṭhaḥ | āyuddheti padacchedaḥ | āyuddham āyodhanam | saṃgrāma iti yāvat | sarvathā nañsamāso na rocate || 14.10 ||
prāyānāśakaśastrāgniviṣodakodbandhanaprapatanaiś
cechatām || 14.11 ||
prāyo mahāprasthānam | tad anicchato ‘pi rājabhayādinā saṃbhavatītīcchatām ity uktam | evam uttareṣv api yathāsaṃbhavam icchānicche draṣṭavye | aśa bhojane ‘śanam āśaḥ | sa evāśakas tadviparyayo ‘nāśakaḥ | saty eva bhojyadravye krodhādinā bhojananivṛttiḥ | śastrāgniviṣodakodbandhanāni prasiddhāni | prapatanaṃ vṛkṣāt parvatād vā pātaḥ | etaiḥ prāyādibhir ātmavyāpādane cakārād anyair apy evaṃvidhair anvakṣam āśaucam iti | atra brahmapurāṇe |
krodhāt prāyaṃ viṣaṃ vahniḥ śastram udbandhanaṃ jalam |
girivṛkṣaprapātaṃ ca ye kurvanti narādhamāḥ ||
brahmadaṇḍahatā ye ca ye caiva brāhmaṇair hatāḥ |
mahāpātakino ye ca patitās te prakīrtitāḥ ||
patitānāṃ na dāhaḥ syān na ca syād asthisaṃcayaḥ |
na cāśrupātaḥ piṇḍo vā kāryā śrāddhakriyā na ca || iti |
atra aṅgirāḥ |
yadi kaścit pramādena mriyetāgnyudakādibhiḥ |
tasyāśaucaṃ vidhātavyaṃ kartavyā codakakriyā || iti || 14.11 ||
sapiṇḍānām ity uktam | ke te sapiṇḍās tān āha |
piṇḍanivṛttiḥ saptame pañcame vā || 14.12 ||
kūṭastham ārabhya ṣaṣṭhaparyantaṃ sāpiṇḍyam | saptame tu nivṛttiḥ | kevalaṃ saptame sodakatvam, “saptame tu nivartate” [MDh 5.60] ity uktatvāt | tataḥ paraṃ sagotratvam eva | pañcame veti yad uktaṃ tad aurasavyatiriktaviṣayam | tatrāpi yathāsaṃbhavaṃ draṣṭavyam | evaṃ cārthaḥ – pitṛpitāmahaprapitāmahebhyas tatparam api dvābhyām ity ātmanā saha ṣaṣṭhaparyantaṃ piṇḍaṃ dadyāt | saptame tu nivṛttiḥ | pañcame veti putrikāputraviṣayam etat | atra baudhāyanaḥ – “kathaṃ kalu putrikāputrasya piṇḍadānam | etat te ‘muṣyai pitaḥ, mama pitāmaha ye ca tvām anu, etat te ‘muṣyai pitāmaha mama prapitāmaha ye ca tvām anu, etat te ‘muṣyai prapitāmaha mama prapitāmaha ye ca tvām anu” iti | asyaivaṃ piṇḍaṃ dadataḥ pañcame prāpte piṇḍanivṛttiḥ | mātsyapurāṇe |
lepabhājaś caturthādyāḥ pitrādyāḥ piṇḍabhāginaḥ |
saptamaḥ piṇḍadas teṣāṃ sāpiṇḍyaṃ sāptapauruṣam || iti || 14.12 ||
janane ‘py evam || 14.13 ||
śāvam āśaucaṃ daśarātram ityādeḥ prabhāte tisṛbhir ityantasyātideśaḥ | yathā śāvam āśaucaṃ tathā janane ‘pīti draṣṭavyam || 14.13 ||
mātāpitros tan mātur vā || 14.14 ||
tajjananāśaucaṃ mātāpitror vā mātur eva vā | mukhyatvāj jananyāḥ pituḥ prāg eva | jñātīnāṃ tatra vyāghraḥ |
sūtakaṃ tu sapiṇḍānāṃ pitror vā mātur eva vā || iti |
mātāpitror vā tannimittatvād iti | manus tu |
janane ‘py evam eva syān nipuṇāṃ śuddhim icchatām |
sarveṣāṃ śāvam āśaucaṃ mātāpitros tu sūtakam || iti | [MDh 5.61–62]
yājñavalkyaḥ |
trirātraṃ daśarātraṃ vā śāvam āśaucam iṣyate |
ūnadvivarṣa ubhayoḥ sūtakaṃ mātur eva hi || iti | [YDh 3.18]
baudhāyanaḥ: “janane tāvan mātāpitror daśāham āśauce | mātur ity eke, tatpariharaṇāt | pitur ity eke, śukraprādhānyāt | ayonijā hy api putrāḥ śrūyante | mātāpitror eva tu saṃsargasāmānyāt” [BDg 1.11.19–23] | aṅgirāḥ |
nāśaucaṃ sūtake proktaṃ sapiṇḍānāṃ kathaṃcana |
mātāpitror aśaucaṃ syāt sūtakaṃ mātur eva ca ||
sarveṣāṃ śāvam āśaucaṃ mātāpitros tu sūtakam |
mātur vā sūtakaṃ tasmād upaspṛśya pitā śuciḥ || iti |
śaṅkhalikhitau: “janane ‘py evam | tatra mātāpitarāv aśucī iti | mātety eke” iti | paiṭhīnasiḥ |
janane sapiṇḍāḥ śucayo mātāpitros tu sūtakam |
sūtakaṃ mātur eva syād upaspṛśya pitā śuciḥ || iti |
atra vṛttādyapekṣo daśāho naiṣāṃ vikalpaḥ | anye bhaṇanti anadhikāralakṣaṇam āśaucaṃ sarveṣāṃ bhavati, “ubhayatra daśāhāni kulasyānnaṃ na bhujyate” iti mānave darśanāt | asparśitālakṣaṇaṃ tu mātāpitror eveti | gṛhāntare vasatas tatsaṃsargam agacchataḥ pituś ca neti | “sūtake sūtakāvarjaṃ saṃsparśo na niṣidhyate” iti ca paṭhanti || 14.14 ||
garbhamāsasamārātrīḥ sraṃsane garbhasya || 14.15 ||
ā caturthād bhavet srāvaḥ pātaḥ pañcamaṣaṣṭhayoḥ |
ata ūrdhvaṃ tu nārīṇāṃ sravaḥ prasava ucyate || iti |
tisro garbhavipadas tāḥ sarvāḥ sraṃsanaśabdenocyante | yāvatithe māse garbhasya sraṃsanaṃ tanmāsasamā rātrīr āśaucaṃ bhavati | atyantasaṃyoge dvitīyā | dvitīyamāsādiyathāmāsasaṃkhyāny ahānīti || 14.15 ||
tryahaṃ vā || 14.16 ||
dvitīye ‘pi māse tryahaṃ vāpi bhavati | caturṣu tūtkarṣaḥ || 14.16 ||
śrutvā cordhvaṃ daśamyāḥ pakṣiṇīm || 14.17 ||
daśamīgrahaṇaṃ daśāhādeḥ paripūrṇāśaucasyopalakṣaṇam | ahardvayamadhyagatā rātriḥ pakṣiṇī rātridvayamadhyagatam ahar vā | pūrvavad dvitīyāprāptir vipattiprakaraṇāt | maraṇanimittam ūrdhvaṃ daśāhād āśaucakāle ‘tikrānte yadi jñātimaraṇaṃ śṛṇuyāt tataḥ pakṣiṇīm āśaucaṃ bhavati | divā śravaṇe tadaharantarā rātriṣv aparedyuś cāhaḥ | rātrau śravaṇe sā rātrir aparedyur ahorātra iti | atra manuḥ |
atikrānte daśāhe tu trirātram aśucir bhavet || iti | MDh 5.76]
tathā jābāliḥ |
atīte sūtake sve sve trirātram aśucir bhavet || iti |
atra sūtakaśabda āśaucaparyāyaḥ | viṣṇus tu: “vyatīte tv āsaṃvatsarasyānta ekarātreṇa” [ViDh 22.40] iti | eṣā deśakāladharmāpekṣayā vyavasthā | vṛddhavasiṣṭhaḥ |
māsatraye trirātraṃ tu ṣaṇmāse pakṣiṇī bhavet |
etac ca sarvaṃ saṃvatsarād arvāk | atra manuḥ |
saṃvatsare vyatīte tu spṛṣṭvaivāpo viśudhyati || iti | [MDh 5.76]
atra paiṭhīnasiḥ |
pitarau cen mṛtau syātāṃ dūrastho ‘pi hi putrakaḥ |
śrutvā tad dinam ārabhya daśāhaṃ sūtakī bhavet || iti |
smṛtyantare |
pitṛpatnyāṃ vyatītāyāṃ mātṛvarjaṃ dvijottamaḥ |
saṃvatsare vyatikrānte trirātram aśucir bhavet ||
nirdaśaṃ jñātimaraṇaṃ śrutvā putrasya janma ca ||
etat tu samānodakaviṣayam || 14.17 ||
asapiṇḍe yonisaṃbandhe sahādhyāyini ca || 14.18 ||
sapiṇḍatā yasya nivṛttā so ‘sapiṇḍaḥ samānodakaḥ | yonisaṃbandho mātāmahamātṛṣvasṛtatputrādayaḥ strīṇāṃ prattānāṃ pitrādayaḥ svasrādayaś ca | sahādhyāyī ekasmād upādhyāyād adhītakṛtsnavedaḥ | cakārāt smṛtyantarapaṭhitāḥ pitṛṣvasṛtadapatyādayo ‘nye ca | eteṣu mṛteṣu parasparaṃ pakṣiṇīm āśaucaṃ bhavati | pakṣiṇīkāle tv atīte snānam eva | manus tu samānodake trirātram āha |
rajanyāhnaiva caikena trirātrair eva ca tribhiḥ |
śavaspṛśo viśudhyanti tryahāt tūdakadāyinaḥ || iti | [MDh 5.64]
anayoḥ pūrvavad vyavasthā | etad dvayam apy anupanītamaraṇaviṣayam | tato ‘rvāk snānam eva | janane ‘pi samānodakānāṃ manunā tryaho darśitaḥ |
janmany edodakānāṃ tu tryahāc chuddhir iheṣyate || [MDh 5.71] iti || 14.18 ||
sabrahmacārṇy ekāham || 14.19 ||
samāno brahmacārī sabrahmacārī suhṛt | tasmin mṛta ekam ahorātram āśaucaṃ bhavati || 14.19 ||
śrotriye copasaṃpanne || 14.20 ||
śrotriyo ‘dhītavedaḥ | upasaṃpanna āśrito gṛhavāsādinā | tasmin mṛta ekāham āśaucam | cakārād ekāham ity anuvartate | atra aṅgirāḥ |
gṛhe yasya mṛtaḥ kaścit tatsapiṇḍaḥ kathaṃcana |
tasyāpy aśaucaṃ vijñeyaṃ trirātraṃ nātra saṃśayaḥ || iti |
manuḥ |
śrotriye tūpasaṃpanne trirātram aśucir bhavet || iti | [MDh 5.81]
āṅgirasam api vacanaṃ śrotriyaviṣayam | atra viṣṇur api: “strīṇāṃ vivāhaḥ saṃskāraḥ saṃskṛtāsu strīṣu nāsaucaṃ pitṛpakṣe | tatprasavamaraṇe pitṛgṛhe ced bhavetāṃ tadaikarātraṃ trirātraṃ ca” [ViDh 22.32–34] iti | prasava ekarātraṃ maraṇe trirātram iti vyavasthito vikalpaḥ || 14.20 ||
pretopasparśane daśarātram āśaucam abhisaṃdhāya
cet || 14.21 ||
nātropasparśaśabdena sparśamātraṃ vivakṣitam | patitacaṇḍāletyādinā sacailasnānasya tadviṣaye vakṣyamāṇatvāt | kiṃ tarhy upasparśanaṃ pretanirharaṇam | tasmin daśarātram āśaucaṃ bhavati | tac cen nirharaṇam abhisaṃdhāya vetanādiprayojanābhisaṃdhānena bhavati na dharmārtham | saty apy āśaucādhikāre punar āśaucagrahaṇaṃ pūrvasmād āśaucād asya vaidharmyajñāpanārtham | tena vakṣyamāṇam adhaḥśayyāsanādikam asmin viṣaye na bhavati | aspṛśyatādhikāralakṣaṇam eva || 14.21 ||
uktaṃ vaiśyaśūdrayoḥ || 14.22 ||
asminn abhisaṃdhāya pretopasparśanādiviṣaye vaiśyaśūdrayor anuktam āśaucaṃ dvādaśarātram ardhamāsam iti pūrvoktam || 14.22 ||
ārtavīr vā || 14.23 ||
ṛtusamānasaṃkhyā vā rātrīr āśaucam | ṣaḍ ṛtavaḥ | pañca vā hemantaśiśirayoḥ samāsena || 14.23 ||
pūrvayoś ca || 14.24 ||
pūrvayor brāhmaṇakṣatriyayor api varṇayor uktam āśaucam ārtavīr vā rātrīr āśaucam | uktasyāpi brāhmaṇasya pūrvayor iti punar upādānam ārtavīr veti vikalpasiddhyartham | pūrvavad deśakālāvasthādyapekṣo vikalpaḥ | atra bhṛtirahite nirhāre manuḥ |
asapiṇḍaṃ dvijaṃ pretaṃ vipro nirhṛtya bandhuvat |
viśudhyati trirātreṇa mātur āptāṃś ca bāndhavān ||
yady annam atti teṣāṃ yaḥ sa daśāhena śudhyati |
anadann annam ahnaiva na cet tasmin gṛhe vaset || iti | [MDh 5.101–102]
bandhuvat snehādinā | grāmād bahir vāse voḍhṝṇāṃ sajyotiḥ | yathāha hārītaḥ: “pretaspṛśo grāmaṃ na praviśeyur ā nakṣatradarśanād rātrau ced ādityadarśanāt tataḥ śuddhiḥ” iti | grāmapraveśe tu anadann annam ahnaiveti mānavam ekāhaḥ | anāthaviṣaye parāśaraḥ |
anāthaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ pretaṃ ye vahanti dvijātayaḥ |
pade pade kratuphalam ānupūrvyāl labhanti te ||
pretasparśanasaṃskārair brāhmaṇo naiva duṣyati |
voḍhā caivāgnidātā ca sadyaḥ snātvā viśudhyati || iti || 14.24 ||
tryahaṃ vā || 14.25 ||
“pretopasparśane” [GDh 14.21] ity ārabhya sarveṣāṃ varṇānāṃ tryahaṃ vā | atyutkṛṣṭaviṣayam idam || 14.25 ||
ācāryatatputrastrīyājyaśiṣyeṣu caivam || 14.26 ||
“upanīya tu yaḥ śiṣyam” [MDh 2.140] ity uktalakṣaṇa ācāryaḥ | tatputra ācāryaputraḥ | ācāryastrī | yājyo yajanīya ṛtvigapekṣayā yajamānaḥ | śiṣyaḥ prasiddhaḥ | eteṣu mṛteṣu caivaṃ tryaham iti
|| 14.26 ||
vijātīyanirhāraviṣayam āha |
avaraś ced varṇaḥ pūrvavarṇam upaspṛśet pūrvo
vāvaraṃ tatra śavoktam āśaucam || 14.27 ||
avaro jaghanyaḥ kṣatriyādir brāhmaṇāpekṣayā | pūrvo brāhmaṇādiḥ kṣatriyādyapekṣayā | tayor anyonyanirhāre śavajātyuktam āśaucaṃ bhavati | brāhmaṇaśavanirharaṇe kṣatriyasya daśarātram | kṣatriyasya śavanirharaṇe brāhmaṇasyaikādaśarātram ityādi | atraiva bhṛtyarthe vyāghraḥ |
avaraś ced varaṃ varṇam avaraṃ vā varo yadi |
carec chāvaṃ tadāśaucaṃ dṛṣṭārthe dviguṇaṃ bhavet || iti || 14.27 ||
buddhipūrvaśavasparśamātre prāsaṅgikena saha śuddhim āha |
patitacaṇḍālasūtikodakyāśavaspṛṣṭitatspṛṣṭyupasparśane
sacailodakopasparśanāc chudhyet || 14.28 ||
patito brahmahādiḥ | caṇḍālasūtikodakyāśavāḥ prasiddhāḥ | eteṣāṃ spṛṣṭau tatspṛṣṭau spaṣṭṝṇāṃ ca spṛṣṭāv upasparśane tadupasparśane, spaṣṭṝṇāṃ sparśane ca sacaikodakopasparśanāt snānāc chudhyet | snānena sacailatvena śuddhau | ataḥ kriyāviśeṣaṇapāṭho ‘yuktaḥ | abuddhipūrve mānavam |
divākīrtim udakyāṃ ca patitaṃ sūtikāṃ tathā |
śavaṃ tatspṛṣṭinaṃ caiva spṛṣṭvā snānena śudhyati || [MDh 5.85] iti || 14.28 ||
śavānugamane ca || 14.29 ||
anugamyecchayāpy etaṃ jñātim ajñātim eva vā |
snātvā sacailaṃ spṛṣṭvāgniṃ ghṛtaṃ prāśya viśudhyati || iti | [MDh 5.103]
ghṛtaprāśanād ūrdhvam api snānaṃ kecid icchanti | tatra mūlaṃ mṛgyam | yājñavalkyo ‘pi: “spṛṣṭvāgniṃ ghṛtabhuk śuciḥ” [YDh 3.26] ity etāvad evāha | idaṃ sajātīyaviṣayam | brāhmaṇasya kṣatriyānugamane vasiṣṭhoktam: “manuṣyāsthi spṛṣṭvā trirātram āśaucam asthighne tv ahorātraṃ śavānugamane caivam” [VaDh 23.24–26] iti | evam iti trirātrāhorātrayor atideśaḥ | atra kṣatriyānugamana ekarātraṃ vaiśyānugamane trirātram iti vyavasthā | śūdrānugamane tv aṅgirāḥ |
pretībhūtaṃ tu yaḥ śūdraṃ brāhmaṇo jñānadurbalaḥ |
anugacchen nīyamānaṃ trirātraṃ so ‘śucir bhavet ||
trirātre tu tataḥ pūrṇe nadīṃ gatvā samudragām |
prāṇāyāmaśataṃ kṛtvā ghṛtaṃ prāśya viśudhyati || iti |
kṣatriyavaiśyayor vaiśyaśūdrānugamane brāhmaṇavat kalpyam | kṣtriyasya śūdrānugamana ekarātraṃ prāṇāyāmaśataṃ ca | manuḥ |
nāraṃ spṛṣṭvāsthi sasnehaṃ savāsā jalam āviśet |
ācamyaiva tu niḥsnehaṃ gāṃ spṛṣṭvā vīkṣya vā ravim || iti | [MDh 5.87]
idam abuddhipūrvaviṣayam | vṛddhamanuḥ |
dahanaṃ vahanaṃ cāpi pretasyānyasya garbhavān |
na kuryād ubhayaṃ tatra kuryād eva pituḥ sadā ||
jyeṣṭhasya vānapatyasya mātulasya sutasya vā || iti |
pitur iti mātur apy upalakṣaṇam | āturarodane pāraskaraḥ |
asthisaṃcayanād arvāg ruditvā snānam ācaret |
anatardaśāhe viprasya ūrdhvam ācamanaṃ smṛtam || iti |
viprasya mṛtasyāntardaśāhe rudatāṃ sarveṣāṃ varṇānāṃ samānam idam | atra viṣṇuḥ: “sarvasyaiva pretasya bāndhavaiḥ sahāśrupātaṃ kṛtvā snānena | akṛtāsthisaṃcaye sacailasnānena” [ViDh 22.61–62] | śuddhir iti prakaraṇād gamyate | idaṃ kṣatriyadimaraṇe samānāpakṛṣṭānāṃ rodane śūdravarjam | trivarṇaviṣayāturarodane brahmapurāṇe paṭhanti |
anasthisaṃcayo vipro rauti cet kṣatravaiśyayoḥ |
tadā snātaḥ sacailas tu dvitīye ‘hani śudhyati ||
kṛte tu saṃcaye vipraḥ snānenaiva śucir bhavet || iti |
kṣatriyasya vaiśyāturavyañjane ‘py evam evohitavyam | śūdrāturavyañjane pāraskaraḥ |
asthisaṃcayanād arvāg yadi vipro ‘śru pātayet |
mṛte śūdre gṛhaṃ gatvā trirātreṇa viśudhyati ||
asthisaṃcayanād ūrdhvaṃ māso yāvad dvijātayaḥ |
ahorātreṇa śudhyanti vāsasāṃ kṣālanena ca ||
ity alaṃ prasaktānuprasaṅgena || 14.29 ||
śunaś ca || 14.30 ||
upasamastam apy apekṣate | śunaś copasparśane sacailodakopasparśanāc chudhyet | pṛthakkaraṇaṃ tatspṛṣṭinyāyanivṛttyartham || 14.30 ||
yad upahanyād ity eke || 14.31 ||
eke tu yad aṅgaṃ śvopahanyāt tasyaiva prakṣālanam icchanti | atra āpastambīyo viśeṣaḥ: “śunopahataḥ sacailo ‘vagāheta | prakṣālya vā taṃ deśam agninā saṃspṛśya punaḥ prakṣālya pādau cācamya prayato bhavati” [ĀpDh 1.15.16–17] iti | ūrdhvāṅgasparśe snānam adhaḥ prakṣālanam iti vyavasthāṃ jātūkarṇya āha |
ūrdhvaṃ nābheḥ karau muktvā spṛśyaty aṅgaṃ kharo yadi |
snānaṃ tatra vidhātavyaṃ śeṣe prakṣālya śudhyati || iti || 14.31 ||
udakadānaṃ sapiṇḍaiḥ kṛtacūḍasya || 14.32 ||
kṛtacūḍān tasya pretasya sapiṇḍair udakadānaṃ kartavyaṃ yāvad āśaucam | na tato ‘rvāg iti | agnisaṃskāro ‘py asyaiva | yathāha laugākṣiḥ |
tūṣṇīm evodakaṃ dadyāt tūṣṇīm evāgnim eva ca |
sarveṣāṃ kṛtacūḍānām anyatrāpīcchayā dvayam || iti |
evaṃ ca kṛtacūḍasya niyato ‘gnisaṃskāra udakadānaṃ ca | akṛtacūḍasya tv aniyataṃ tad akaraṇe na pratyavāyaḥ | cūḍākaraṇena kālo lakṣyate tṛtīyaṃ varṣam, bahuṣu smṛtiṣu tathā darśanāt | manur api |
nātrivarṣasya kartavyā brāhmaṇair udakakriyā |
jātadantasya vā kuryān nāmni cāpi kṛte sati || iti | [MDh 5.70]
agnyudakagrahaṇam aurdhvadehikasyopalakṣaṇam | tatra devalo viśeṣam āha |
dvādaśād vatsarād arvāk paugaṇḍamaraṇe sati |
sapiṇḍīkaraṇaṃ na syād ekodiṣṭāni kārayet || iti || 14.32 ||
tat strīṇāṃ ca || 14.33 ||
tad udakadānaṃ strīṇāṃ ca kṛtacūḍānāṃ kāryam || 14.33 ||
eke prattānām || 14.34 ||
eke manyante prattānām eva strīṇām udakadānam aprattānāṃ tu naiveti | prattānāṃ ca bhartṛpakṣair deyam || 14.34 ||
athāśaucakāle jñātayaḥ kathaṃ varteran |
adhaḥśayyāsanino brahmacāriṇaḥ sarve || 14.35 ||
bhūmāv eva śarīrann āsīraṃś ca na kaṭāsanādiṣu | maithunaṃ ca varjayeyuḥ | sarvagrahaṇaṃ samānodakārtham || 14.35 ||
na mārjayīran || 14.36 ||
mārjanaṃ gātramalāpakarṣaṇam | tac ca na kuryuḥ || 14.36 ||
na māṃsaṃ bhakṣayeyur ā pradānāt || 14.37 ||
pradānaṃ śrāddham | ātadantaṃ māṃsaṃ na bhakṣayeyuḥ || 14.37 ||
prathamatṛtīyasaptamanavameṣūdakakriyā || 14.38 ||
prathamādiṣv ahaḥsu sapiṇḍaiḥ pretāya tilamiśram udakaṃ deyam evaṃgotrāyaivaṃśarmaṇe pretāyaitat tilodakaṃ dadāmīti | prathame trīn | tṛtīye nava | saptame triṃśat | navame trayaṃstriśat | iti pañcasaptatir jalāñjalayo deyāḥ | ācāras tu prathame ‘hni trayaḥ | dvitīyādiṣv ekottaraṃ dīyate || 14.38 ||
vāsasāṃ ca tyāgaḥ || 14.39 ||
udakadānakāle parihitāni vāsāṃsi prathamatṛtīyaspatamanavameṣu tyājyāni | anyāni krameṇa paridheyāni || 14.39 ||
antye tv antyānām || 14.40 ||
varṇeṣv antyāyāḥ śūdrās teṣam antye navame ‘hani vāsasāṃ tyāgaḥ || 14.40 ||
dantajanmādi mātāpitṛbhyām || 14.41 ||
dantajanmaprabhṛti putrasya mātāpitarau jalaṃ dadyātām | tūṣṇīṃ mātā || 14.41 ||
bāladeśāntaritapravrajitāsapiṇḍānāṃ
sadyaḥśaucam || 14.42 ||
bālo ‘kṛtacūḍaḥ | deśāntarito deśena vyavahito deśāntarasthaḥ | pravrajitā naiṣṭhikavānaprasthaparivrājakāḥ | asapiṇḍāḥ samānodakāḥ | teṣāṃ maraṇe jñātīnāṃ sadyaḥśaucaṃ snānena śuddhiḥ | bālaviṣaye yājñavalkyaḥ |
ūnadvivārṣikaṃ pretaṃ nikhanen nodakaṃ tataḥ || [YDh 3.1]
ā dantajanmanaḥ sadya ā cūḍān naiśikī smṛtā |
trirātram ā vratādeśād daśarātram ataḥ param || iti | [YDh 3.23]
āṅgirāḥ |
yady apy akṛtacūḍo vai jātadantas tu saṃsthitaḥ |
dāhayitvā tathāpy enam āśaucaṃ tryaham ācaret || iti |
manus tu |
ūnadvivārṣikaṃ pretaṃ nidadhyur bāndavā bahiḥ |
alaṃkṛtya śucau bhūmāv asthisaṃcanayād ṛte ||
nāsya kāryo ‘gnisaṃskāro nāsya kāryodakakriyā |
araṇye kāṣṭhavat tyaktvā kṣapet tryaham eva tu || iti | [MDh 5.68–69]
āśvalāyanaḥ: “adantajāte parijāta ekāham” iti | āpastambas tu: “mātuś ca yonisaṃbandhebhyaḥ pituś cā saptamāt puruṣād yāvatā vā saṃbandho jñāyate teṣāṃ pretṣūdakopasparśanaṃ garbhān parihāpyāparisaṃvatsarān mātāpitarāv eva teṣu hartāraś ca” [ĀpDh 2.15.2–4] iti | eteṣāṃ deśakuladharmāpekṣayā vyavasthā | atra kanyāviṣaya āpastambaḥ:
aprauḍhāyāṃ tu kanyāyāṃ sadyaḥśaucaṃ vidhīyate | iti |
aprauḍhā akṛtacūḍā | [yājñavalkyaḥ]:
ahas tv adattakanyāsu bāleṣu ca viśodhanam | iti | [YDh 3.24]
idaṃ caulād ūrdhvam | vyāghra āha |
bāle mṛte sapiṇḍānāṃ sadyaḥśaucaṃ vidhīyate |
daśāhenaiva dampatyoḥ sodarāṇāṃ tathaiva ca || iti |
idaṃ tu sūtakaṃ daśāhāntarmaraṇaviṣayam | tathā ca |
antardaśāhe jātasya śiśor niṣkramaṇaṃ yadi |
sūtakenaiva śuddhiḥ syāt pitroḥ śātātapo ‘bravīt || 14.42 ||
rājñāṃ ca kāryavirodhāt || 14.43 ||
rājñaś ca sadyaḥśaucaṃ kāryavirodhāt | kāryaṃ prajārakṣaṇādi | bahuvacananirdeśād ye cānye ‘mātyādayas tatkāryavantas teṣām api | “yasya cecchati pārthivaḥ” [MDh 5.95] iti manuḥ || 14.43 ||
brāhmaṇasya ca svādhyāyanivṛttyarthaṃ
svādhyāyanivṛttyartham || 14.44 ||
brāhmaṇasya ca sadyaḥśaucaṃ svādhyāyanivṛttir mā bhūd iti | bahuśiṣyasyādhyāpayata idam uktam | [abhyāso ‘dhyāyasamāptyarthaḥ] | ity āśaucam || 14.44 ||
iti śrīgautamīyavṛttau haradattaviracitāyāṃ mitākṣarāyāṃ
dvitīyapraśne pañcamo ‘dhyāyaḥ
atha pañcadaśo ‘dhyāyaḥ
atha śrāddham || 15.1 ||
athaśabdo ‘dhikārārthaḥ | śrāddhaṃ nāma karmādhikriyate | śraddhā yatra vidyate tac chrāddham | tac ca pañcavidham |
nityaṃ naimittikaṃ kāmyaṃ vṛddhiśrāddham athāparam |
pārvaṇaṃ ceti vijñeyaṃ śrāddhaṃ pañcavidhaṃ budhaiḥ ||
tatra nityaṃ manur āha |
dadyād ahar ahaḥ śrāddham annādyenodakena vā |
payomulaphalair vāpi pitṛbhyaḥ prītim āvahan ||
ekam apy āśayed vipraṃ pitrarthe pāñcayājñike |
na caivātrāśayet kiṃcid vaiśvadevaṃ pratidvijam || iti | [MDh 3.82–83]
grahaṇādiṣu yat kriyate tan naimittikam | tasya prayogaḥ pārvaṇavat | ekoddiṣṭaṃ sapiṇḍīkaraṇam aṣṭakety etāny api naimittikāny eva | tatraikoddiṣṭaṃ ṣoḍaśavidham |
mṛte ‘hani tu kartavyaṃ pratimāsaṃ tu vatsaram |
pratisaṃvatsaraṃ caivamādyam ekādaśe ‘hani || [YDh 1.256]
iti yājñavalkyaḥ | ekoddiṣṭaṃ tu kartavyam ity ādyapāde laugākṣiḥ | vyāghraḥ |
ekādaśe ‘hni kartavyaṃ tripakṣe ca tathaiva ca |
ṣaṇmāse ca tataḥ kuryād ekoddiṣṭaṃ prayatnataḥ || iti |
tataḥ saṃvatsare pūrṇe tripakṣe vā tathaiva ca |
sapiṇḍīkaraṇaṃ kuryād arvāg vā vṛddhisaṃbhave || iti |
jātūkarṇyaḥ |
caturthe pañcame caiva navamaikādaśe tathā |
yad annaṃ dīyate jantos tan navaśrāddham ucyate ||
iti ca paṭhanti | varṇānupūrvyavyavasthitāni catvāry etāni | tad eva śrāddham ekam ekādaśe ‘hanīti pradhānam | ekādaśagrahaṇam āśaucāntopalakṣaṇam | tatra ca māse tadaiva māsikam | sapiṇḍīkaraṇe pretasyaikoddiṣṭaṃ pitradibhyaḥ pārvaṇam | tatrāpi māse tad eva māsikam | madhye daśa māsikāni tripakṣaṣāṇmāsikayoḥ pratisaṃvatsaraṃ cāparaṃ mṛtāhe sāṃvatsarikam iti ṣoḍaśaikoddiṣṭāni | paṭhanti ca |
navatripakṣaṣaṇmāsyasāṃvatsarikamāsikam |
śrāddhaiḥ ṣoḍaśabhiḥ pretaḥ piśācatvaṃ vimuñcati ||
yasyaitāni na kurvanti ekoddiṣṭāni ṣoḍaśa |
piśācatvaṃ sthiraṃ tasya kṛtaiḥ śrāddhaśatair api || iti |
hemantaśiśirayoś caturṇām aparapakṣāṇām aṣṭamīṣv aṣṭakā ekasyāṃ vā | prathame ‘hani kriyamāṇe stryapatyaṃ jāyata ityādi kāmasaṃyogena kriyamāṇaṃ kāmyam | tad api pārvaṇavad eva | vivāhādivṛddhikarmasu pūrvedyuḥ kartavyaṃ vṛddhiśrāddham | tasmāt pitṛbhyaḥ pūrvedyuḥ kriyata iti brāhmaṇamūlam | tatra yugmā brāhmaṇā yavās tilārthāḥ | anyat pārvaṇavat | pārvaṇaṃ tu vakṣyate || 15.1 ||
amāvāsyāyāṃ pitṛbhyo dadyāt || 15.2 ||
sūryācandramasau yasyāṃ saha vasataḥ sāmāvāsyā | pitṛbhya iti bahuvacanāt pitṛpitāmahaprapitāmahabhyo dadyāt | dānaprakāro gṛhyeṣūkāḥ “homo brāhmaṇabhojanaṃ piṇḍanirvapaṇam” iti | iha tu brāhmaṇabhojane viśeṣaḥ || 15.2 ||
pañcamīprabhṛtiṣu vāparapakṣasya || 15.3 ||
aparapakṣaḥ kṛṣṇapakṣaḥ | tasya pañcamīprabhṛti vā dadyāt || 15.3 ||
yathāśraddhaṃ sarvasmin vā || 15.4 ||
sarvasmin vāparapakṣe dadyād yathāśraddhaṃ yathā śraddhā bhavatīti || 15.4 ||
dravyadeśabrāhmaṇasaṃnidhāne vā
kālaniyamaḥ || 15.5 ||
“tilamāṣa” [GDh 15.15][1] ity ārabhya vakṣyamāṇaṃ tat tat praśastaṃ dravyam | deśo gayāpuṣkarādiḥ |
puṣkareṣv akṣayaṃ śrāddhaṃ kurukṣetre tathaiva ca |
dadyān mahodadhau caiva hradagoṣthe girau tathā ||
iti vyāsaḥ |
yad dadāti gayāsthaś ca sarvam ānantyam aśnute | [YDh 1.261]
iti yājñavalkyaḥ | brāhmaṇāḥ paṅktipāvanā vakṣyamāṇāḥ ṣaḍaṅgavidādayaḥ | eteṣāṃ dravyādīnāṃ saṃnidhāne samavāye kālaniyamaḥ saṃnidhir eva kāla iti | vāśabdo vikalpārthaḥ
|| 15.5 ||
śaktitaḥ prakarṣed guṇasaṃskārvidhir annasya || 15.6 ||
annasya bhakṣyabhojyor guṇavidhayaḥ pāyasatvaviśadasiddhatvādayaḥ | saṃskāravidhayo bharjanamarīcījīrakalavaṇādibhiḥ surabhīkaraṇādayaḥ | etān yathāśakti prakarṣet prakṛṣṭān kuryāt || 15.6 ||
navāvarān bhojayed ayujaḥ || 15.7 ||
navasaṃkhyāvarā yeṣāṃ te navāvarās tāvanto brāhmaṇān bhojayet | ayujo ‘yugmasaṃkhyān | navapakṣe pitus trīn pitāmahasya trīn prapitāmahasya trīn | avaragrahaṇād adhikā api bhavanti pañcadaśaikaviṃśatir ityādayaḥ | ayuja iti vacanād dvādaśādivyāvṛttiḥ || 15.7 ||
yathotsāhaṃ vā || 15.8 ||
yathāsāmarthyaṃ navabhyo ‘rvāg api bhojayet | ayuja ity anuvṛttes trīn eva | tathā ca āpastambaḥ: “ayugmāṃs tryavarān” [?] iti | śāstrāntareṣu viśvebhyo devebhyo brāhmaṇabhojanam āmnātaṃ mātāmahānāṃ ca | yathāha yājñavalkyaḥ |
dvau daive prāk trayaḥ pitrya udag ekaikam eva vā |
mātāmahānām apy evaṃ tantraṃ vā vaiśvadevikam || iti | [YDh 1.228]
daive dvau brāhmaṇau prāṅmukhāv upaveśyau | pitrarthe tatrodaṅmukhāḥ | ekaikasyaikam eva veti | devānām ekaḥ pitrādīnāṃ trayāṇām eka iti | mātāmahānām apy evaṃ pitṛśrāddavat | dvau daive mātāmahādyarthe trayaḥ | vaiśvadevikaṃ tantraṃ vā bhavati pituḥ śrāddhasya mātāmahaśrāddasya ca || 15.8 ||
kīdṛśān bhojayet | tatrāha |
śrotiyān vāgrūpavayaḥśīlasaṃpannān || 15.9 ||
śrotriyān adhītavedān | vāksaṃpattiḥ suśikṣitaṃ vākyaṃ saṃskṛtabhāṣaṇādi | rūpasaṃpannān saumyaveṣān anyūnān anadhikāṅgāñ śvitrādyadūṣitān vayaḥsaṃpannān anatibālān | śīlam antaḥkaraṇaśuddhis tatsaṃpannān | evaṃguṇān bhojayet || 15.9 ||
yuvabhyo dānaṃ prathamam || 15 10 ||
evaṃguṇebhyo yuvabhyaḥ śrāddhadānaṃ mukhyaḥ kalpaḥ || 15.10 ||
eke pitṛvat || 15.11 ||
eke manyante pitṛvat pitrādyanurūpaṃ dānam iti | yathā – pitre taruṇāḥ pitāmahāya vṛddhāḥ prapitāmahāya vṛddhatarā iti || 15.11 ||
na ca tena mitrakarma kuryāt || 15.12 ||
na ca tena śrāddhena mitrakarma kuryāt | yena maitrī kāryā tasminn arthāpekṣitaṃ na bhojayet | mitralobhakārārthaṃ na bhojayed ity arthaḥ | āpastambas tu “anarthāpekṣo bhojayet” iti viśeṣeṇāha || 15.12 ||
putrābhāve sapiṇḍā mātṛsapiṇḍāḥ śiṣyāś ca
dadyuḥ || 15.13 ||
putrā dadyur iti prathamaḥ kalpaḥ | tadabhāve sapiṇḍā bhrātṛtatputrādayaḥ | tadabhāve mātṛsapiṇḍā mātṛbhrātṛtatputrādayaḥ | tadabhāve śiṣyaḥ || 15.13 ||
tadabhāva ṛtvigācāryau || 15.14 ||
śiṣyābhāva ṛtvik, tadabhāva ācāryaś ca dadyur iti || 15.14 ||
na bhojayet stenaklībapatitanāstikatadvṛtti-
vīrahāgredidhiṣupatistrīgrāmayājakājāpālotsṛṣṭāgni-
madyapakucarakūṭasākṣiprātihārikān || 15.15 ||
steno hiraṇyastenaḥ | klībo moghavīryo na tṛtīyāprakṛtiḥ | aśrotriyatvāt patito brahmahādiḥ | nāstikaḥ pretyabhāvāpavādī | tadvṛttis nāstikavṛttiḥ | pretyabhāvam aṅgīkṛtyāpi yas tadanukūlaṃ na ceṣṭate saṃsargavaśāt | vīrahā yo buddhipūrvam agnīn udvāsayīta satyām apy upapattau | śrūyate hi — “vīrahā eṣa devānāṃ yo ‘gnim udvāsayate” iti | agredidhiṣū iti dīrghāntaṃ kecit paṭhanti | patiśabdaḥ pratyekaṃ saṃbadhyate | agredidhiṣupatir didhiṣupatir iti |
jyeṣṭhāyāṃ yady anūḍhāyāṃ kanyāyām ūhyate ‘nujā |
sā tv agredidhiṣūr jñeyā pūrvā tu didhiṣūḥ smṛtā || iti |
tayoḥ patī | naighaṭukās tu –
punarbhūr didhiṣūrūḍhā dvis tasyā didhiṣuḥ patiḥ |
sa tu dvijo ‘gredidhiṣuḥ saiva yasya kuṭumbinī || iti |
strīgrāmayājakaḥ | strīṇāṃ vratānām upadeṣṭānuṣṭhāpayitā sa strīyājakaḥ | grāmayājako bahuyājakaḥ | ajāpālo ‘ajārakṣaṇajīvakaḥ | utsṛṣṭāgnir āśaucādyanupapattyā pramādād vā vicchinnāgniḥ | madyapaḥ surāvyatiriktamadakaradravyasya pātā | surāpas tu patitatvenoktaḥ | kucaraḥ kutsitācāraḥ | sākṣye ‘nṛtavaktā kūṭasākṣī | pratihāriko dvārapālavṛttiḥ | etān na bhojayet | yeṣāṃ patitādīnāṃ darśanasparśanādikaṃ pratiṣiddhaṃ teṣāṃ pratiṣedhaḥ kṛtaprāyaścittānām api varjanārthaḥ || 15.15 ||
upapatiḥ || 15.16 ||
upapatir jāraḥ || 15.16 ||
yasya ca saḥ || 15.17 ||
sa upapatir yadviṣaye sa ca sākṣāt patis tāv ubhāv api na bhojanīyau || 15.17 ||
kuṇḍāśisomavikrayyagāradāhigaradāvakīrṇi-
gaṇapreṣyāgamyāgāmihiṃsraparivittiparivettṛparyāhita-
paryādhātṛtyaktātmadurvālakunakhiśyāvadantaśvitri-
paurnarbhavakitavājaparājapreṣyaprātirūpika-
śūdrāpatinirākṛtikilāsikusīdivaṇikśilpopajīvijyāvāditra-
tālanṛtyagītaśīlān || 15.18 ||
paradāreṣu jāyete dvau sutau kuṇḍagolakau |
patyau jīvati kuṇḍaḥ syān mṛte bhartari golakaḥ || [MDh 3.174]
iti manuḥ | tasya kuṇḍasyānnam aśnātīti kuṇḍāśī | kuṇḍagrahaṇaṃ golakasyāpy upalakṣaṇam | kuṇḍādīnāṃ tu pratiṣedho daṇḍāpūpikayā siddhaḥ | apara āha – pākabhājanaṃ kuṇḍaṃ tatraiva kvacid deśe ‘śnanti tan na tyajanti te kuṇḍāśinaḥ | somavikrayī yajñe somasya vikretā | agāradāhī veśmadāhakaḥ | garado viṣasya dātā | avakīrṇī vratabhraṣṭaḥ | atha vā yo brahmacārī striyam upeyāt saḥ | gaṇapreṣyo gaṇānāṃ preṣaṇakṛt | agamyāgāmī samānapravarastrīgāmī | hiṃsraḥ prāṇivadharuciḥ |
parivettānujo ‘nūḍhe jyeṣṭhe dāraparigrahāt |
parivittis tu tajjyāyān ||
iti nighaṇṭuḥ | jyeṣṭhe ‘kṛtādhāne kṛtādhānaḥ kaniṣṭhaḥ paryādhātā, jyeṣṭhaḥ paryāhitaḥ | vasiṣṭhaḥ |
unmattaḥ kilbiṣī kuṣṭhī patitaḥ klība eva ca |
yakṣmāmayāvī ca tathā na tyājyaḥ syāt parīkṣitum || iti |
śātātapaḥ |
klībe deśavinaṣṭe ca patite pravrajite tathā |
yogaśāstrābhiyukte ca na doṣaḥ parivedane || iti ca |
tyaktātmā sāhasika udbandhanādau pravṛttaḥ | durvālaḥ khalatiḥ | veṣṭitaśephety anye | kunakhī vinā kāraṇena vivarṇanakhaḥ | vinaṣṭanakha ity anye | śyāvadantaḥ svabhāvataḥ kṛṣṇadantaḥ | śvitrī śvetakuṣṭhī | paurnarbhavo dvirūḍhā punarbhūs tasyāḥ putraḥ | kitavo dyūtakaraḥ kitaṃ vātīti paṇapūrvajīvī vā | ajapo vihitasya sāvitryādijapasyākartā | rājapreṣyo dūtādiḥ | prātirūpikaḥ kūṭatulāmānādivyatihārī | śūdrāpatiḥ saiva bhāryā yasya | nirākṛtir asvādhyāyaḥ | śrotriyān ity ukte ‘pi punaḥ pratiṣedhād vāgrūpavayaḥśīlāsaṃpattāv apy satyāṃ gatau grahaṇaṃ bhavati | kilāsas tvagdoṣo balaṃlīti draviḍānāṃ prasiddhaḥ | bhūmni matvarthīyaḥ | kusīdī vārdhuṣiko vṛddhyājīvī | vaiśyavṛttyā vāṇijyopajīvī vaṇik, vaṇigupajīvī | citrakarmādibhir upajīvī śilpopajīvī | śīlaśabdo jyādibhiḥ pratyekaṃ saṃbadhyate | jyāśīlo dhanurvedopajīvī | vāditraśīlo bheryāditāḍanavṛttiḥ | tālaśīlas tālavṛttiḥ | nṛtyagītaśīlau ca | tathaitān na bhojayet || 15.18 ||
pitrā vākāmena vibhaktān || 15.19 ||
ye cānicchatā pitrā vibhaktās tān na bhojayet || 15.19 ||
śiṣyāṃś caike sagotrāṃś ca || 15.20 ||
eka ācāryāḥ śiṣyān sagotrāṃś cābhojanīyān āhuḥ | ekagrakanād bhojanīyā iti svamatam | tatra guṇavadasaṃbhave teṣāṃ guṇavattve satīti | tathā ca āpastambaḥ: “samudetaḥ sodaryo ‘pi bhojayitavyaḥ” [ĀpDh 2.17.5] iti || 15.20 ||
bhojayed ūrdhvaṃ tribhyaḥ || 15.21 ||
yathotsāhaṃ vety anena sarvārtham ekasyāpi prasaṅgas tannivṛttyartham | tryavarān bhojayet, “tryavarān” ity āpastambīye darśanāc ca || 15.21 ||
guṇavantam || 15.22 ||
ekavacanaprayogeṇa guṇavāṃś ced ekam api bhojayet | vasiṣṭho ‘pi |
api vā bhojayed ekaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ vedapāragam |
śīlavṛttaguṇopetam avalakṣaṇavarjitam || iti | [VaDh 11.29]
manur api |
ekaikam api vidvāṃsaṃ daive pitrye ca bhojayet |
puṣkalaṃ phalam āpnoti nāmantrajñān bahūn api || [MDh 3.129] iti || 15.22 ||
sadyaḥ śrāddhī śūdrātalpagas tatpurīṣe māsaṃ
nayati pitṝn || 15.23 ||
yena śrāddhaṃ bhuktaṃ sa tasminn ahorātre śrāddhīty ucyate | śrāddham anena bhuktam iti, ata iniṭhanau | samānakālaḥ sa yadi tad ahaḥ śūdrātalpaṃ gacchet | talpagrahaṇaṃ bhāryārtham | ūḍhām api śūdrāṃ yadi gacchet sadya eva tasyāḥ purīṣe pitṝn māsaṃ nayati || 15.23 ||
itarāsu bhāryāsu kalpyam | ata āha |
tasmāt tad ahar brahmacārī ca syāt || 15.24 ||
mānave dātur api niyama uktaḥ |
nimantrito dvijaḥ pitrye niyatātmā bhavet sadā |
na ca chandāṃsy adhīyīta yasya śrāddhaṃ ca tad bhavet || [MDh 3.188] iti || 15.14 ||
śvacāṇḍālapatitāvekṣaṇe duṣṭam || 15.25 ||
śvādibhir avekṣitam annaṃ duṣṭam abhojyaṃ bhavati | śrāddhaṃ cāvekṣitaṃ duṣṭam akṛtaṃ bhavati || 15.25 ||
yasmād evam,
tasmāt pariśrite dadyāt || 15.26 ||
pariśrayaṇaṃ tiraskariṇyādinā vyavadhānam || 15.26 ||
tadaśaktau |
tilair vā vikiret || 15.27 ||
atra bhṛguḥ |
pānīyam api yad dattaṃ tilair miśraṃ dvijasya tu |
pitṛbhyaḥ kāmadhuk tat syāt pitṛguhyam idaṃ tataḥ || iti || 15.27 ||
paṅktipāvano vā śamayet || 15.28 ||
paṅktir yena pāvyate sa paṅktipāvanaḥ | śvādyavekṣaṇe yo doṣas taṃ śamayet || 15.28 ||
sa kaḥ punar asau tam āha |
paṅktipāvanaḥ ṣaḍaṅgavij jyeṣṭhasāmikas triṇāciketas
trimadhus trisuparṇaḥ pañcāgniḥ snātako
mantrabrāhmaṇavid dharmajño brahmadeyānusaṃtāna
iti || 15.29 ||
śikṣā kalpo vyākaraṇaṃ jyotiṣaṃ niruktaṃ chandovicitir iti ṣaḍaṅgāni | teṣāṃ pāṭhato ‘rthataś ca jñātā ṣaḍaṅgavit | jyeṣṭhasāmikaḥ – talavakārāṇām “ud u tyāṃ citram” ity etayor gāyako jyeṣṭhasāmagaḥ, chandogānāṃ tu tad idāsītī tīyaṃ tad yogayeti(?) jyeṣṭhaṃ sāma tadveditā jyeṣṭhasāmikaḥ | triṇāciketo – nāciketo bahuṣu śākhāsu vidhīyate taittirīye kaṭhavallīṣu śatapathe ca | taṃ yo veda brāhmaṇena saha sa triṇāciketaḥ | “madhu vātā ṛtāyate” ity etat ṛcaṃ trimadhu | tatra pratyṛcaṃ trayo madhuśabdāḥ | āśvalāyano ‘py āha: “tṛptāñ jñātvā madhumatīḥ śrāvayet” iti | iha tu tadadhyāyī puruṣas trimadhuḥ | trisuparṇa ṛgvede “ekaḥ suparṇaḥ sa samudram āviveśa” ityādikas tṛcaḥ | taittirīyake “brahmam etu mām” ityādayas trayo ‘nuvākāḥ | tatra hi “ya imaṃ trisuparṇam ayācitaṃ brāhmaṇāya dadyāt” iti śrūyate | pūrvavat puruṣe vṛttiḥ | pañcāgniḥ sabhyāvasathābhyāṃ saha pañcānām anuvākānām adhyetā | snātako vidyāvratābhyām mantrabrāhmaṇavin mantrabrāhmaṇayor arthajñaḥ | dharmajño dharmaśāstrāṇām arthajñaḥ | brahmadeyānusantāno brāhmavivāhoḍhāsantānaḥ | itikaraṇādyaś cānya evaṃyuktaḥ | ye mātṛtaḥ pitṛtaś ceti daśavarṣaṃ samanuṣṭhitā vidyātapobhyāṃ puṇyaiś ca karmabhir yeṣām ubhayato nābrāhmaṇaṃ ninayeyuḥ | pitṛta ity eka ity evamādilakṣaṇaḥ | sa eṣa sarvaḥ paṅktipāvanaḥ
|| 15.29 ||
haviḥṣu caivam || 15.30 ||
haviḥśabdena daivāni mānuṣāṇi ca karmāṇy ucyante | jyeṣṭhā apy evam uktalakṣaṇā eva brāhmaṇā bhojayitavyā na tu pratiṣiddhāḥ stenādaya iti || 15.30 ||
durvālādīñ śrāddha evaike || 15.31 ||
eke tu durvālān ārabhya ye ‘nukrāntās tāñ śrāddha eva na bhojayen na tu daivamānuṣayor iti manyante | svamate tu te tatrāpy abhojyā eveti || 15.31 ||
akṛtānnaśrāddhe caivaṃ caivam || 15.32 ||
dviruktiḥ pūrvavat || 15.32 ||
iti śrīgautamīyavṛttau haradattaviracitāyāṃ mitākṣarāyāṃ
dvitīyapraśne ṣaṣṭho ‘dhyāyaḥ
atha ṣoḍaśo ‘dhyāyaḥ
śrāvaṇādi vārṣikaṃ proṣṭhapadīṃ vopākṛtyādhīyīta
chandāṃsi || 16.1 ||
śraveṇena yuktā paurṇamāsī śravaṇā | nakṣatreṇa yuktaḥ kāla ity uktasyāṇo lubaviśeṣa iti lup | yuktavad bhāvas tu na bhavati | vibhāṣā phālgunīśravaṇeti nirdeśāt | śrāvaṇīty api bhavati | paurṇamāsyāṃ hi lubaviśeṣa iti na bhavati | phālgunī kārtikī caitrīti nirdeśāt | śravaṇaśabde tūbhayaṃ bhavatīti |
meṣādisthe savitari yo yo darśaḥ pravartate |
cāndramāsās tattadantāś caitrādyā dvādaśa smṛtāḥ ||
teṣu yā yā paurṇamāsī sā sā caitryādikā smṛtā |
kādācitkena yogena nakṣatrasyeti nirṇayaḥ ||
tad evaṃ siṃhasthe savitari yāmāvāsyā tadante cāndramase māse paurṇamāsī sā śravaṇā srāvaṇīti cocyate | śravaṇayogas tu bhavatu mā vā bhūt | etena proṣṭhapadī vyākhyātā | proṣṭhapadīm ity adhikaraṇe dvitīyā | atyantasaṃyoge vā kathaṃcit | śravaṇāyāṃ prauṣṭhapadyāṃ vā paurṇamāsyām upākṛtyopākarmākhyaṃ karma yathāgṛhyaṃ kṛtvā tadā chandāṃsi mantrabrāhmaṇalakṣaṇāny adhīyīta | ācāryo ‘dhyāpayec chiṣyā adhīyīran | tad idam adhyayanaṃ vārṣikam ity ācakṣate | varṣartau pratisaṃvatsaraṃ vā bhavatīti | adhyāpanam apy ātmāpekṣayādhyayanaṃ pārāyaṇādivat | śiṣyāpekṣayā tv adhyāpanam || 16.1 ||
kiyantaṃ kālam adhīyīta |
ardhapañcamān māsān pañca dakṣiṇāyanaṃ vā || 16.2 ||
ardhaṃ pañcamaṃ yeṣāṃ tān ardhapañcamān ardhādhikāṃś caturo māsān pūrṇān vā pañca māsān yāvad vā dakṣiṇāyanam | evam adhīyānaḥ || 16.2 ||
brahmacāry utsṛṣṭalomā na māṃsaṃ bhuñjīta || 16.3 ||
brahmacārī bhavet strīsaṅgaṃ varjayet | utsṛṣṭalomā na rūḍhaśmaruḥ | akasmād ity atroktaṃ lomakarma tad utsṛṣṭaṃ yena sa utsṛṣṭalomā | evaṃbhūto bhaven na māṃsaṃ bhakṣayet | ayam adhyāpayitur upadeśaḥ, brahmacāriṇaḥ prāptyabhāvāt | āpastambo ‘py āha: “pravacanyukto varṣāśaradaṃ maithunaṃ varjayet” [ĀpDh 1.32.1] iti | yaś ca kevalāni vratāni pāraṃ nītvā jāyām upayamya paścād adhīte so ‘py evaṃ tasyātra grahaṇārtham apy evam | āśvalāyano ‘py āha: “samāvṛtto brahmacārikalpena” iti | tatra tv ṛtugamanaṃ pākṣikam abhyupagatam | tathāha jāyopeyety eke prājāpatyam tad iti || 16.3 ||
dvaimāsyo vā niyamaḥ || 16.4 ||
dvau māsau bhūtabhāvinau vā dvimāsyaḥ | māsād vayasi yat khañau | dvigor yabavayasy api prayuñjate | sa eva dvaimāsyaḥ | ayaṃ brahmacaryādiniyamo māsadvayaṃ vā bhavati | śaktyapekṣo vikalpaḥ || 16.4 ||
athānadhyāyā ucyante |
nādhīyīta vāyau divā pāṃsuhare || 16.5 ||
pāṃsūn haratīti pāṃsuharaḥ | vāyau divā pāṃsuhare vāti sati nādhīyīta | apāṃsuhare na doṣaḥ | pāṃsuhare ‘pi rātrau na doṣaḥ || 16.5 ||
karṇaśrāviṇi naktam || 16.6 ||
vyatyayenāyaṃ karmaṇi kartṛpratyayaḥ | karṇābhyāṃ śrūyata iti karṇaśrāvī | evaṃbhūte mahāghoṣe vāyau vāti sati naktaṃ nādhīyīta || 16.6 ||
vāṇabherīmṛdaṅgagartārtaśabdeṣu || 16.7 ||
vāṇo vīṇāviśeṣaḥ, vāṇaḥ śatatantur iti mahāvrate darśanāt | bherīmṛdaṅgau prasiddhau | garto rathaḥ, “ārohataṃ varuṇa mitra gartam,” “stuhi śrutaṃ gartasadam,” ityādau darśanāt | ārto bandhumaraṇādinā duḥkhitaḥ | teṣāṃ śabde śrūyamāṇe tāvantaṃ kālaṃ nādhīyīta || 16.7 ||
śvaśṛgālagardhabhasaṃhrāde || 16.8 ||
saṃhrādaḥ sahaśabdanam | śunāṃ śṛgālānāṃ gardabhānāṃ saṃhrāde nādhīyīta | trayāṇāṃ tu sahaśabdena daṇḍāpūpikayā siddhaḥ pratiṣedhaḥ || 16.8 ||
rohitendradhanurnīhāreṣu || 16.9 ||
ākāśe lohite, indradhaniṣi dṛśyamāne, nīhāro himānī tasyāṃ ca | tāvantaṃ kālaṃ
nādhīyīta || 16.9 ||
abhradarśane cāpartau || 16.10 ||
apartur avarṣartuḥ | tatra sodakasya meghasya darśane nādhīyīta || 16.10 ||
mūtrita uccārite || 16.11 ||
saṃjātamūtre ‘lpe mūtritaḥ | uccārito ‘pi tathā | tatra śreyān api nādhīyīta | utsarge tu mānasam apy aśucir iti vakṣyati || 16.11 ||
niśāyāṃ saṃdhyodakeṣu || 16.12 ||
niśā rātrer madhyamo bhāgas tasmin saṃdhyāyām udake cāvasthito nādhīyīta || 16.12 ||
varṣati ca || 16.13 ||
varṣati ca deve tāvan nādhīyīta | dhātvarthamātraṃ vivakṣitaṃ na parimāṇaviśeṣaḥ || 16.13 ||
eke valīkasaṃtānām || 16.14 ||
eke manyante valīkasaṃtānaṃ valīkaṃ nīdhraṃ gṛhapaṭalāntas tatra varṣadhārā saṃtanyate yathā tathā varṣati deve nādhyeyam || 16.14 ||
ācāryapariveṣaṇe || 16.15 ||
ācāryau guruśukrau tayoḥ pariveṣaṇe nādhīyīta | apara āha – pariveṣaṇaṃ bhakṣabhojyādyannopaharaṇam, brāhmaṇān annena pariveṣyetyādau darśanāt | ācāryasya pariveṣaṇe nādhīyīteti || 16.15 ||
jyotiṣoś ca || 16.16 ||
prasiddhajyotiṣī sūryācandramasau | tayoś ca pariveṣaṇe nādhīyīta | pūrvasūtre dvitīyapakṣe ‘nnānuvṛttasya pariveṣaṇaśabdasyārthabhedo ‘ṅgīkaraṇīyaḥ || 16.16 ||
bhīto yānasthaḥ śayānaḥ prauḍhapādaḥ || 16.17 ||
bhīto vartamānabhayaḥ | yānastho ‘śvādyārūḍhaḥ | śayānaḥ śayyām āsevamānaḥ | prauḍhapādaḥ pāde pādāntarādhāyī pīṭhāsanādyāropitapādo vā | evaṃbhūtena nādhyeyam || 16.17 ||
śmaśānagrāmāntamahāpathāśauceṣu || 16.18 ||
śmaśānaṃ śavadāhasthānam | grāmānto grāmasīmā | mahāpathaḥ prasiddhaḥ | aśaucaṃ śaucarāhityam | eteṣu sthāneṣu nādhyeyam | atha vāśaucaṃ jananamaraṇanimittam asparśalakṣaṇaṃ tasminn api nādhyeyam || 16.18 ||
pūtigandhāntaḥśavadivākīrtyaśūdrasaṃnidhāne || 16.19 ||
pūtigandhe ghrāṇagandhe | divākīrtyaś caṇḍālaḥ | antaḥśabdaḥ ubhābhyāṃ saṃbadhyate | antaḥśave ‘ntardivākīrtye ca grāma iti | śūdrasaṃnidhāne [ca] nādhyeyam | dvandvaikavadbhāvaḥ | āpastambo ‘pi: “antaḥśave ‘ntaścaṇḍāle” [cf. ĀpDh 1.16.20] iti || 16.19 ||
bhuktake codgāre || 16.20 ||
bhukta[ka]m amlam amle codgāre vartamāne nādhīyīta || 16.20 ||
ṛgyajuṣaṃ ca sāmaśabdo yāvat || 16.21 ||
ṛk ca yajuś ca ṛgyajuṣam | acaturetyādinā nipātaḥ | yāvat sāmaśabdaḥ śrūyate tāvad ṛgvedaṃ yajurvedaṃ ca nādhīyīta | ṣaṣṭhyantapāṭhas tu nāsmabhyaṃ rocate || 16.21 ||
ākālikā nirghātabhūmikamparāhudarśanolkāḥ || 16.22 ||
nirghāto ‘śanipātaḥ | bhūmikampo bhūcalanam | rāhudarśanaṃ grahaṇam | ulkolkāpātaḥ [ulka ulkāpātaḥ?] | eta ākālikā anadhyāyahetava iti prakaraṇād gamyate | yasmin kāla ete bhavanti paredyus tatparyantaṃ kāla ākālaḥ | tatsaṃbaddha ākālikaḥ || 16.22 ||
stanayitnuvarṣavidyutaś ca prāduṣkṛtāgniṣu || 16.23 ||
stanayitnur meghaśabdaḥ | prasiddham anyat | prāduṣkṛteṣv agniṣv agnihotrahomakāle saṃdhyāyāṃ stanayitnuprabhṛtayo bhavantaḥ pratyekam ākālikān adhyāyahetavaḥ | apartāv idam || 16.23 ||
ṛtāv āha |
ahar ṛtau || 16.24 ||
varṣartāv ete yadi bhaveyuḥ saṃdhyāyāṃ tadā prātaś ced aharmātram anadhyāyaḥ | sāyaṃ tu rātrāv anadhyāya ity arthasiddhatvād anuktam || 16.24 ||
vidyuti naktaṃ cāpararātrāt || 16.25 ||
yadi naktaṃ vidyud dṛśyate na saṃdhyāyāṃ tadāpararātrād rātres tṛtīyo bhāgo ‘pararātra ā tasmād anadhyāyaḥ | tato ‘dhyeyam | prātas tu saṃdhyāyāṃ vidyuti jābāla āha: “vidyuti prātar ahar anadhyāyaḥ” iti || 16.25 ||
tribhāgādipravṛttau sarvam || 16.26 ||
yady ahnas tṛtīyād bhāgād ārabhya vidyut pravartate na kevalāyāṃ saṃdhyāyāṃ nāpi naktaṃ tadā sarvarātram anadhyāyaḥ || 16.26 ||
ulkā vidyutsamety ekeṣām || 16.27 ||
ulkā ca vidyuttulyā | yathā vidyuty anadhyāyo vidyuti naktaṃ cāpararātrād ity evam ulkāpāte ‘pīty ekeṣāṃ matam || 16.27 ||
stanayitnur aparāhṇe || 16.28 ||
stanayitnur aparāhṇe yadi bhavati na saṃdhyāyāṃ tadā vidyutsamo bhavati | ā apararātrād anadhyāyaṃ karoti || 16.28 ||
api pradoṣe || 16.29 ||
pradoṣe ‘pi bhavaḥ stanayitnur vidyutsamaḥ | āpararātrād anadhyāyahetuḥ || 16.29 ||
sarvaṃ naktam ārdharātrāt || 16.30 ||
prathamād rātribhāgād ārabhya ārdharātrāt pravṛttaḥ stanayitnuḥ sarvaṃ naktam anadhyāyahetuḥ || 16.30 ||
ahaś cet sajyotiḥ || 16.31 ||
ahaś cet stanayitnur bhavati prāg aparāhṇāt tadā sajyotir anadhyāyaḥ | sakalaṃ divasam ity arthaḥ || 16.31 ||
viṣayasthe ca rājñi prete || 16.32 ||
yasmin viṣaye svayaṃ vasati tatrasthe tasyādhipatau rājñi prete sajyotir anadhyāyaḥ | ākālikam ity anye || 16.32 ||
viproṣya cānyonyena saha || 16.33 ||
yadā sahādhyāyinaḥ parasparaṃ vipravaseyuḥ kecic cācāryeṇa saṃgatās tadā sajyotir anādhyāyaḥ | ā pareṣāṃ melanād ity eke | ākālikam ity anye || 16.33 ||
saṃkulopāhitavedasamāpticchardiśrāddhamanuṣya-
yajñabhojaneṣv ahorātram || 16.34 ||
saṃkulaś corādibhir grāmādyupadravaḥ | upāhito ‘gnidāhaḥ | vedasamāptiḥ śākhāsamāptiḥ | chardanaṃ bhuktodgāraḥ | śrāddham ekoddiṣṭādi | manuṣyayajño vasantotsvādiḥ | bhojanaśabda ubhābhyāṃ saṃbadhyate | śrāddhabhojane manuṣyayajñabhojana iti | eteṣu nimitteṣv ahorātram anadhyāyaḥ | manuṣyaprakṛtīnāṃ devānāṃ yajño manuṣyayajña ity anye | yathāha āpastambaḥ: “manuṣyaprakṛtīnāṃ devānāṃ yajñe bhuktvety eke” [ĀpDh 1.11.3] iti | ye manuṣyā bhūtvā prakṛṣṭena tapasā devāḥ saṃpannās tadyajñas tatprītyarthaṃ brāhmaṇabhojanam || 16.34 ||
amāvāsyāyāṃ ca || 16.35 ||
amāvāsyāyām ahorātram anadhyāyaḥ || 16.35 ||
dvyahaṃ vā || 16.36 ||
tad ahaḥ pūrvedyuś ca dvyaham anadhyāyaḥ | śuklacaturdaśyāṃ tv anadhyāyasya mūlāntaraṃ mṛgyam | evaṃ pratipatsu ca || 16.36 ||
kārtikī phālguny āṣāḍhī paurṇamāsī || 16.37 ||
kārtikyādyās tisraḥ paurṇamāsyo ‘nadhyāyahetavo ‘horātram | paurṇamāsyantareṣv anadhyāye mūlaṃ mṛgyam || 16.37 ||
tisro ‘ṣṭakās trirātram || 16.38 ||
ūrdhvam āgrahāyaṇyās triṣv aparapakṣeṣu tisro ‘ṣṭakāḥ | tās trirātram anadhyāyahetavaḥ tadahaḥ pūrvedyur aparedyuś ca || 16.38 ||
antyām eke || 16.39 ||
eke ‘ntyām ekāṣṭakām anadhyāyahetuṃ manyante || 16.39 ||
abhito vārṣikam || 16.40 ||
śravaṇādi vārṣikam iti yad uktaṃ vārṣikam anadhyayanaṃ tad abhitas tasyobhayoḥ pārśvayor ye karmaṇī upākaraṇotsarjane tayor api kṛtayor tryaham anadhyāyam eke icchanti | tathā ca manuḥ |
upākarmaṇi cotsarge trirātraṃ kṣapaṇaṃ smṛtam || iti | [MDh 4.119]
uśanā: “upākarmaṇi cotsarge tryaham anadhyāyaḥ” iti || 16.40 ||
sarve varṣāvidyutstanayitnusaṃnipāte || 16.41 ||
varṣādīnāṃ trayāṇāṃ yugapatsaṃnipāte trirātram anadhyāya iti sarva evācāryā
manyante || 16.41 ||
prasyandini || 16.42 ||
prakṛṣṭaṃ syandanaṃ varṣaṃ prasyandas tadvati ca kāle yāvat prasyandanam anadhyāyo dvyahaṃ tryahaṃ caturahaṃ vā || 16.42 ||
ūrdhvaṃ bhojanād utsave || 16.43 ||
upanayanādāv utsave bhojanād ūrdhvaṃ tadahar anadhyāyaḥ || 16.43 ||
prādhītasya ca niśāyāṃ caturmuhūrtam || 16.44 ||
upākṛtyādhyetuṃ pravṛttaḥ prādhītaḥ | ādikarmaṇi ktaḥ kartari ca | tasya niśāyāṃ caturmuhūrtaṃ caturo muhūrān aṣṭau nāḍikā anadhyāyaḥ | “śrāvaṇyāṃ paurṇamāsyām adhyāyam upākṛtya māsaṃ pradoṣe nādhīyīta” [ĀpDh 1.9.1] ity āpastambīyena samānārtham idam | cakārāt trayodaśīpradoṣe ‘pi niśāyāṃ caturmuhūrtaṃ niṣedho darśitaḥ || 16.44 ||
nityam eke nagare || 16.45 ||
eka ācāryā nagare nityam evādhyayanaṃ necchanti | nityagrahaṇaṃ niśādhikāranivṛttyartham
|| 16.45 ||
mānasam apy aśuciḥ || 16.46 ||
aprayataḥ san mānasam apy adhyayanaṃna kuryāt | evaṃ cānyeṣv anadhyāyahetuṣu mānasam aniṣiddham || 16.46 ||
śrāddhinām ākālikam || 16.47 ||
śrāddham asyāstīti śrāddhī śrāddhasya kartā | ata iniṭhanau | na tu śrāddham anena bhuktam iti, bhoktari pūrvam eva niṣiddhatvāt | teṣāṃ śrāddhadātṝṇām ākālikam anadhyāyaḥ | apara āha – ye śrāddhe kevalaṃ bhuktavanto na pitrādyarthaṃ pātratayā teṣāṃ pūrvako ‘horātraniṣedhaḥ | ayaṃ tv ākālikaniṣedhaḥ pitrādyarthaṃ pātratayā bhuktavatām iti || 16.47 ||
akṛtānnaśrāddhikasaṃyoge ‘pi || 16.48 ||
bhojanāsaṃbhave yadya(yada)kṛtānnaṃ pitṛbhyo dīyate tad akṛtānnaśrāddhikam | tatsaṃyoge ‘py ākālikam anadhyāyaḥ | na kevalaṃ bhuktavataḥ | tatra manuḥ |
prāṇi vā yadi vāprāṇi yat kiṃcic chrāddhikaṃ bhavet |
tad ālabhyāpy anadhyāyaḥ pāṃyāsyā brāhmaṇāḥ smṛtāḥ || iti | [MDh 4.117]
āmaśrāddhasyaitad eva liṅgam || 16.48 ||
pratividyaṃ ca yān smaranti [yān smaranti] || 16.49 ||
pratividyaṃ pratidharmaśāstraṃ yān anadhyāyān smaranti smartāras teṣv api hetuṣu nādhīyīta | tatra vasiṣṭhaḥ: “digdāhaparvataprapāteṣūpalarudhirapāṃsuvarṣeṣv ākālikam” [VaDh 13.35] iti |
śleṣmātakasya śālmalyā madhūkasya tathāpy adhaḥ |
kadācid api nādhyeyaṃ kovidārakapitthayoḥ || iti |
evam anyatrāpi draṣṭavyam | [abhyāso ‘dhyāyasamāptyarthaḥ] || 16.49 ||
iti śrīgautamīyavṛttau haradattaviracitāyāṃ mitākṣarāyāṃ
dvitīyapraśne saptamo ‘dhyāyaḥ
atha saptadaśo ‘dhyāyaḥ
mānasam apy aśucir ity uktam | tac cāśucitvam āhārajanitam api bhavatīti bhakṣyābhakṣyaprakaraṇam ārabhyate |
praśastānāṃ svakarmasu dvijātīnāṃ brāhmaṇo
bhuñjīta || 17.1 ||
svakarmasu varṇaprayukteṣv āśramaprayukteṣūbhayaprayukteṣu ca ye praśastāḥ “aho ayaṃ svakarmānutiṣṭhati” iti teṣāṃ dvijātīnāṃ gṛhe brāhmaṇo bhuñjīta | kṣudupaghātārthā bhojane pravṛttiḥ | śakyā ca yasya kasyacid gṛhe bhuñjānena kṣud upahantum | tatra parisaṃcaṣṭa eṣām eva gṛhe brāḥmaṇo bhuñjīta nānyeṣām iti || 17.1 ||
pratigṛhṇīyāc ca || 17.2 ||
pratigraho ‘py eṣām eva sakāśān nānyeṣām iti || 17.2 ||
asyāpavādaḥ |
edhodakayavasamūlaphalamadhvabhayābhyudyata-
śayyāsanāvasathayānapayodadhidhānāśapharīpriyaṅgu-
sraṅmārgaśākāny apraṇodyāni sarveṣām || 17.3 ||
edhaḥ kāṣṭham | udakaṃ ghaṭādistham api | yavasaṃ tṛṇādi | mūlam ārdrakādi | phalam āmrādi | madhu mākṣikam | abhayaṃ paritrāṇam | abhyudyatam ayācitenāpi dātrā svayam ānītam idaṃ gṛhāṇeti | śayyā kaṭādi | āsanaṃ pīṭhādi | āvasathaḥ pratiśrayaḥ | yānaṃ śakaṭādi | dadhipayasī prasiddhe | dhānā bhṛṣṭā yavāḥ | śapharī matsyaviśeṣaḥ | [priyaṅgū rājikā] | sraṅ mālā | mārgaṃ mṛgamāṃsam, panthā vā mārgaḥ | śākaṃ vāstukādi | etāny edhādīny apraṇodyāni sarvataḥ pratigrāhyāṇi yācitvāpi | abhyudyataṃ pakvānnādy apraṇodyam apratyākhyeyaṃ pratyākhyāne doṣaḥ | tathā ca āpastambaḥ |
udyatām āhṛtāṃ bhikṣāṃ purastād apraveditām |
bhojyāṃ mene prajāpatir api duṣkṛtakāriṇaḥ ||
na tasya pitaro ‘śnanti daśa varṣāṇi pañca ca |
na ca havyaṃ vahaty agnir yas tām abhyavamanyate || iti | [ĀpDh 1.19.13]
asyāpavādaḥ |
cikitsakasya mṛgayoḥ śalyakṛntasya pāśinaḥ |
kulaṭāyāś ca ṣaṇḍhasya teṣām annam anādyam || [ĀpDh 1.19.14] iti || 17.3 ||
pitṛdevagurubhṛtyabharaṇe ‘py anyat || 17.4 ||
pitṛbharaṇam avicchedena śrāddhakaraṇam | devabharaṇam agnihotrādi | guravaḥ pitrādayaḥ | bhṛtyāḥ putradāsādayaḥ | teṣāṃ bharaṇaṃ bhaktādidānam | eteṣu nimitteṣv anyad apy uktād anyad apy apraṇodyam | manuś ca |
gurūn bhṛtyāṃś coddhariṣyann arciṣyan devatātithīn |
sarvataḥ pratigṛhṇīyān na tu tṛpyet svayaṃ tataḥ || [MDh 4.251] iti || 17.4 ||
vṛttiś cen nāntareṇa śūdrāt || 17.5 ||
yadi śūdrapratigraham antareṇa vṛttir jīvanaṃ na nirvartate tadā śūdrād api pratigṛhṇīyāt || 17.5 ||
paśupālakṣetrakarṣakakulasaṃgatakārayitṛparicārakā
bhojyānnāḥ || 17.6 ||
yo yasya paśūn pālayati kṣetraṃ ca karṣati, yaś ca yasya kule saṃgataḥ pāramparyeṇa mitrarūpeṇāgataḥ, yaś ca yasya paricārako dāsas te teṣāṃ bhojyānnāḥ | pakvam apy annaṃ teṣāṃ bhuñjīran | kāruḥ kārayitā | “ūrdhvaṃ nāpitaḥ śmaśrūṇi kārayati” iti hi dṛśyate | sa ca viprād vaiśyāyām anūḍhāyāṃ jātaḥ so ‘pi bhojyānnaḥ | tatra manuḥ |
kṣetrikaḥ kulamitraś ca gopālo dāsanāpitau |
ete śūdreṣu bhojyānnā yaś cātmānaṃ nivedayet || iti | [MDh 4.253]
etac cātyantāpadviṣayam || 17.6 ||
vaṇik cāśilpī || 17.7 ||
vaṇik ca bhojyānnaḥ, se ced aśilpī kumbhakārādiko na bhavati || 17.7 ||
athābhojyam āha |
nityam abhojyam || 17.8 ||
nityaṃ paragṛhe na bhoktavyam | gṛhasthasyāyaṃ pratiṣedhaḥ,
upāsate gṛhasthā ye parapākam abuddhayaḥ | [MDh 3.104]
iti mānave darśanāt | anyebhyo yāvat pratyahaṃ dīyate tan nityam abhojyam || 17.8 ||
keśakīṭāvapannam || 17.9 ||
yac cānnaṃ keśaiḥ kīṭair vā saṃbaddhaṃ tad apy abhojyam | atra vasiṣṭhaḥ: “kāmaṃ tu keśakīṭān utsṛjyādbhiḥ prokṣya bhasmanāvakīrya vācā praśastam upayuñjīta” [VaDh 14.23] iti | manus tu |
pakṣijagdhaṃ gavā ghrātam avadhūtam avakṣutam |
keśakīṭāvapannaṃ ca mṛtprakṣepeṇa śudhyati || iti | [MDh 5.125]
tad eṣāṃ rucito vyavasthā | apara āha – pākād ārabhya yat keśakīṭāvapannaṃ tatra gautamīyam ūrdhvaṃ tu vāsiṣṭhamānava iti || 17.9 ||
rajasvalākṛṣṇaśakunipadopahatam || 17.10 ||
kṛṣṇaśakuniḥ kākaḥ | padagrahaṇaṃ tuṇḍāder apy avayavasyopalakṣaṇam | rajasvalayā kṛṣṇaśakunipadena vopahataṃ spṛṣṭam annam abhojyam | prabhūte tv anne parāśaraḥ |
śṛtaṃ droṇādhikaṃ cānnaṃ śvakākair upaghātitam |
na tyājyaṃ tasya śuddhyarthaṃ brāhmaṇebhyo nivedayet ||
gāyatryaṣṭasahasreṇa mantrapūtena vāriṇā |
bhojyaṃ tat prokṣitaṃ vipraiḥ paryagnikṛtam eva ca || iti |
upahataṃ pradeśam uddhṛtyedaṃ kāryam || 17.10 ||
bhrūṇaghnāvekṣitam || 17.11 ||
bhrūṇahā brahmahā | tathā ca vasiṣṭhaḥ: “brāhmaṇaṃ hatvā bhrūṇahā bhavati” [VaDh 20.23] iti | tena prekṣitam apy abhojyam || 17.11 ||
bhāvaduṣṭam || 17.12 ||
bhojayitrāvajñānena dattaṃ bhoktur va manaso duṣṭikaraṃ bhāvaduṣṭam | tad apy abhojyam
|| 17.12 ||
gavopaghrātam || 17.13 ||
gavā copa samīpe ghrātam abhojyam || 17.13 ||
śuktaṃ kevalam adadhi || 17.14 ||
yat pakvaṃ kālavaśād amlarasaṃ tat kevalaṃ śuktam | tad abhojyam | kevalagrahaṇāt kṣīrodakādisaṃpṛktam amlam api bhojyam | dadhi tu kevam apy almlaṃ bhojyam | takrakāñjikayor apakvatvān nāyaṃ pratiṣedhaḥ | ācāro ‘pi takre nirvivādaḥ | kāñjike savivādaḥ
|| 17.14 ||
punaḥ siddham || 17.15 ||
sakṛtpakvasya tādṛśa eva pākaḥ punaḥ kriyate pūrvaṃ śuktapakvam iti tat punaḥsiddam | tad abhojyam | anyathāpakvasya tu pākāntare bharjanādau na doṣaḥ || 17.15 ||
paryuṣitam aśākabhakṣasnehamāṃsamadhūni || 17.16 ||
udayāstamayāntaritaṃ paryuṣitam | divā pakvaṃ rātrau rātripakvaṃ divā tad aśuktam apy abhojyam | śākādi tu paryuṣitam api bhojyam | śākam uktam | bhakṣāḥ pṛthukāpūpādayaḥ | sneho ghṛtatailādiḥ | māṃsaṃ prasiddham | madhu ca | etāni paryuṣitāny ap bhojyāni | snehamadhvādīnām apakvatvād evāparyuṣitatvam | tasmāt snehamadhugrahaṇaṃ tatsaṃsṛṣṭasyāpi paryuṣitasya paryudāsārtham | tena tatsaṃsṛṣṭaṃ paryuṣitam api bhojyam agarhitam |
tat paryuṣitam apy ādyaṃ haviḥśeṣaṃ ca yad bhavet |
iti || 17.16 ||
utsṛṣṭapuṃścalyabhiśastānapadeśyadaṇḍikatakṣa-
kadaryabandhanikacikitsakamṛgayvaniṣucāryucchiṣṭa-
bhojigaṇavidviṣāṇānām || 17.17 ||
utsṛṣṭaḥ pitṛbhyāṃ parityaktaḥ |
gaṇḍasyoparijātānāṃ parityāgo vidhīyate |
ityādinā kāraṇena durbhikṣe rakṣaṇāśaktyā, prātikūlyena vā | puṃścalī – aniyatapuṃsakā vyabhicāriṇī gaṇikā ca | abhiśastaḥ satāsatā vā doṣeṇa khyātaḥ | anapadeśyaivaṃbhūto ‘yam iti vyapadeśānarhaḥ | strītvapuṃstvābhyām anirdeśyā tṛtīyā prakṛtir ity anye | daṇḍiko rājñā daṇḍādhikāre niyuktaḥ | śūdrāt prātilomyena vaiśyāyāṃ jātas takṣā | vaiśyāt kṣatriyāyāṃ jāto māhiṣyaḥ | śūdrāyām ūḍhāyāṃ vaiśyāj jātā karaṇī, tasyāṃ māhiṣyāj jāto rathakāraḥ | sa takṣety anye | kadaryo lubdhaḥ, yam adhikṛtya manur āha |
śrotriyasya kadaryasya vadānyasya ca vārdhuṣeḥ | iti | [MDh 4.224]
bandhaniko bandhakāgārarakṣī | cikitsiko vaidyaḥ śalyakartā vā | yo mṛgayuḥ sann iṣucārī na bhavati kiṃ tu pāśacārī sa mṛgayvaniṣucārī vāgurikaḥ | ucchiṣṭabhojī nigadasiddhaḥ | gaṇo janasamudāyaḥ | vidviṣāṇaḥ śatruḥ | eteṣām utsṛṣṭādīnām annam abhojyam | ye ‘tra praśastā dvijātayo na bhavanti teṣāṃ grahaṇam uditapratiṣedhārtham | tathā ca āpastambaḥ: “cikitsikasya mṛgayoḥ” [ĀpDh 1.19.14] ityādi | āpady api pratiṣedhārtham ity anye || 17.17 ||
apaṅktyānāṃ prāg durvālāt || 17.18 ||
ye cāpaṅktyāḥ prāg upadiṣṭās tyakātmaparyantās teṣām apy annam abhojyam [cf. GDh 15.18]
|| 17.18 ||
vṛthānnācamanotthānavyapetāni || 17.19 ||
yad ātmārthaṃ pacyate nātithyādyarthaṃ tad vṛthānnam | śrūyate hi “mogham annaṃ vindate apracetāḥ” ityādi | bhojanamadhye yatra kopādinā punar ācamyata utthīyate vā | apetād anyad vyapetaṃ sahitam iti | ete ācamanotthānavyapete anne | etāni vṛthānnādīny abhojyāni | atra uśanā: “agurubhir ācamanotthānaṃ ca” iti | ekasyāṃ paṅktau bahuṣu bhuñjāneṣv ekenāpi guruvyatirikenācamana utthāne vā kṛta itareṣām apy abhojyam iti | gurubhiḥ kṛte na doṣaḥ
|| 17.19 ||
samāsamābhyāṃ viṣamasame pūjātaḥ || 17.20 ||
kulaśīlādibhis tulyaḥ samaḥ | viparīto ‘samaḥ | viṣamasamaśabdau bhāvaparau | viṣamasama iti samāhāradvandvaḥ | pūjātaḥ pūjāyām āsanaparicaraṇādikāyāṃ samena saha pūjāyāṃ viṣame ‘samena ca sāmye kriyamāṇe tadannam abhojyam || 17.20 ||
anarcitaṃ ca || 17.21 ||
yac cānarcitaṃ dīyate “vaidhaveya bhakṣaya” iti tad apy abhojyam | pratigrahe ‘pi tulyam etat | yathāha manuḥ |
yo ‘rcitaṃ pratigṛhṇāti dadāty arcitam eva yaḥ |
tāv ubhau gacchataḥ svargaṃ narakaṃ tu viparyaye || iti | [MDh 4.235]
“sāyaṃ pātar aśanāny abhipūjayet” [VaDh 3.69] iti vasiṣṭhaḥ | tadakaraṇam anarcitam ity anye || 17.21 ||
goś ca kṣīram anirdaśāyāḥ sūtake || 17.22 ||
sūtakaṃ prasavaḥ | prasūtāyā anatikrāntadaśāhāyāḥ goḥ kṣīram ābhojyam || 17.22 ||
ajāmahiṣyoś ca || 17.23 ||
ajāmahiṣyoḥ sūtake ‘nirdaśāhayoḥ kṣīram apeyam || 17.23 ||
nityam āvikam apeyam auṣṭram aikaśaphaṃ ca || 17.24 ||
nityagrahaṇān na kevalam anirdaśāham eva | avir evāvikaḥ | uṣṭraḥ prasiddhaḥ | ekaśaphā ekakhurā aśvādayaḥ | avikādīnāṃ saṃbandhi kṣīraṃ nityam apeyam | manus tu |
ārāṇyānāṃ tu sarveṣāṃ mṛgāṇāṃ mahiṣaṃ vinā |
strīkṣīraṃ caiva varjyāni sarvaśuktāni caiva hi || [MDh 5.9] iti || 17.24 ||
syandinīyamasūsaṃdhinīnāṃ ca || 17.25 ||
yasyāḥ stanebhyaḥ kṣīraṃ syandate sā syandinī | yamasūr yugmavatsasūtikā | yā garbhiṇī dugdhe sā saṃdhinī | ekakāladohanety anye | evaṃbhūtānāṃ gavādīnāṃ kṣīram apeyam || 17.25 ||
vivatsāyāś ca || 17.26 ||
vatsena viyuktā vivatsā | tasyāś ca gavādeḥ kṣīram apeyam | atra prakaraṇe pratiṣiddhavikārasyāpi dadhyādeḥ pratiṣedham icchanti | ācāras tv anirdaśāyāṃ tathānyatrāniyataḥ || 17.26 ||
idānīṃ svarūpata evābhakṣyān āha |
pañcanakhāś cāśalyakaśaśaśvāvidgodhākhaḍga-kacchapāḥ || 17.27 ||
abhakṣyā ity uttaratra vakṣyati | yeṣāṃ pāṇipādeṣu pañcoccā nakhās te pañcanakhā vānarādayo ‘bhakṣyāḥ | śalyādīn varjayitvā | śalyako varāhaviśeṣo yasya nārācākārāṇi lomāni | śaśaḥ prasiddhaḥ | śvāvit kalpako yasya carmaṇā tanutrāṇaṃ kriyate | godhā kṛkalāsākṛtir mahākāyaḥ | khaḍgo mṛgāvaśeṣaḥ [mṛgaviśeṣaḥ?] | śṛṅgamṛtyuḥ | kacchapaḥ prasiddhaḥ | atra paṭhanti |
abhakṣyāṇāṃ tu yan mūtraṃ tad ucchiṣṭaṃ tathaiva ca |
abhojyam iti nirdiṣṭaṃ viṣṭhā caiva prayatnataḥ || iti || 17.27 ||
ubhayatodatkeśyalomaikaśaphakalaviṅkaplava-
cakravākabākahaṃsāḥ || 17.28 ||
ubhayatodantā aśvādayaḥ | dadbhāva ārṣaḥ | keśinaḥ keśātiśayayuktāś camaryādayaḥ | alomānaḥ sarpādayaḥ | ekaśaphā ekakhurāḥ | anubhayatodantārtham idam | kalaviṅko grāmacaṭakaḥ | plavaḥ śakaṭabilākhyaḥ pakṣī | haṃsacakravākau prasiddhau | ete cābhakṣyāḥ || 17.28 ||
kākakaṅkagṛdhraśyenā jalajā raktapādatuṇḍā
grāmyakukkuṭasūkarāḥ || 17.29 ||
kākādayaḥ prasiddhāḥ | jalajā api pakṣiṇa eva kākādisaṃnidhānāt | teṣāṃ viśeṣaṇaṃ raktapādatuṇḍā iti | grāme bhavo grāmyaḥ | uttarayoś caitad viśeṣaṇaṃ grāmyakukkuṭo grāmyasūkara iti | āraṇyayor apratiṣedhaḥ || 17.29 ||
dhenvanaḍuhau ca || 17.30 ||
dhenuḥ payasvinī gauḥ | anaḍvān anovahanayogyo balīvardaḥ | dvandve ‘caturetyādisamāsāntanipātanād dhenvanaḍvāhāv iti [na] prāpno[tī]iti tad anādṛtam | apapāṭho vā | dhenvanaḍuhau cābhakṣyau | āpastambīye tu gotrābhyāṃ (yā) māṃsaṃ bhakṣyam uktvā “dhenvanuḍuhor bhakṣyaṃ medhyam ānaḍuham iti vājasaeyakam” [ĀpDh 1.17.30–31] ity uktam | ānaḍuhaṃ ca kevalaṃ bhakṣyaṃ kiṃ tarhi medhyam apīty arthaḥ | bahvṛcabrāhmaṇeṣu śrūyate – “tad yathaivādo manuṣyarāja āgate ‘nyasmin vārhaty ukṣāṇaṃ vā vehataṃ vākṣadanta” iti | tatrātither bhakṣyam anyeṣām abhakṣyam iti | vadho ‘pi kila tatrānujñātaḥ “dāśagoghnau saṃpradāne” gaur yasmai hanyate sa goghno ‘tithir iti | evaṃ kila pūvam ācāraḥ | idānīṃ gandho ‘pi (?) || 17.30 ||
apannadannavasannavṛthāmāṃsāni || 17.31 ||
apannadann apatitadantaḥ | so ‘pratiṣiddho ‘pi na bhakṣyaḥ | “yadā vai paśor dantāḥ padyante ‘tha sa medhyo bhavati” iti bahvṛcabrāhmaṇam | “yo ‘pannadan malaṃ tat paśūnām iti vijñāyate” ity āpastambaḥ | avasanno vyādhitaḥ | vṛthāmāṃsaṃ vṛthānnena vyākhyātam | punaḥ pratiṣedhas tu māṃsasya prāyaścittagauravārthaḥ || 17.31 ||
kisalayakyākulaśunaniryāsāḥ || 17.32 ||
kisalayaḥ pallavo ‘graprarohaḥ | kyākuś chatrākaḥ | laśunaṃ prasiddham | niryāso vṛkṣatvagbhūto ghanībhūto raso hiṅgvādiḥ | kisalayādayo ‘py abhakṣyāḥ || 17.32 ||
lohitā vraścanāḥ || 17.33 ||
vṛkṣādiṣu vṛkṇapradeśe bhavā vaścanā niryāsās te lohitāś cen na bhakṣyāḥ | svayaṃsūnā niryāsā lohitā alohitāś cābhakṣyāḥ | vraścanaprabhavās tu lohitā eva | manus tu |
lohitān vṛkṣaniryāsān vraścanaprabhavāṃs tathā | iti | [MDh 5.6]
kecit tu lohitaśabdaṃ kisalayādiṣv api paṭhanti | hiṅgus tu niryāso vraścanaprabhavo na veti cintyam | sarvathā śiṣṭā api bhakṣayanti | karpūras tu na niryāso na vraścanaprabhavo na lohitaḥ, tasmād bhakṣya eva || 17.33 ||
nicudārubakabalākāśukamadguṭiṭṭibhamāsthāla-
naktaṃcarā abhakṣyāḥ || 17.34 ||
nicudārur dārvāghāṭaḥ | madgur jalavāyasaḥ | māsthālo vāgvadaḥ | naktaṃcarā ulūkādayaḥ | anye prasiddhāḥ | abhakṣyā iti pañcanakhā ity ārabhya saṃbadhyate || 17.34 ||
bhakṣyāḥ pratudaviṣkirajālapādāḥ || 17.35 ||
tuṇḍena pratudya pratudya ye bhakṣayanti te pratudāḥ | ye pādābhyāṃ vikīrya bhakṣayanti mayūrādayas te viṣkirāḥ | jālākārau pādau yeṣāṃ te jālapādāḥ | ete bhakṣyāḥ | yady apy abhakṣyeṣūkteṣv anye bhakṣyā iti gamyate tathāpi bhakṣyā ity upādānam anuktānām āpady eva bhakṣaṇam [yathā] syād anāpadi mā bhūd iti || 17.35 ||
matyāś cāvikṛtāḥ || 17.36 ||
vikṛtā manuṣyaśiraskādayas tadviparītā avikṛtā bhakṣyā iti || 17.36 ||
vadhyāś ca dharmārthe || 17.37 ||
ye bhakṣyā uktās te na kevalaṃ svayaṃ mṛtā anyahatā vā bhakṣyā api tu vadhyāś ca dharmārthe ‘tithipūjādau | aparaś cāha – ye dharmārthe yajñādau vadhyā ha --- tiṣā api bhakṣyā anṛtvijām apīti | dharmartha iti vacanād avakīrṇipaśor māṃsam abhakṣyam, tasya prāyaścittārthatvāt || 17.37 ||
vyālahatādṛṣṭadoṣavākpraśastān abhyukṣyopayuñjītopa-
yuñjīta || 17.38 ||
atithīn apy āśayed bhakṣayec ca | na tu śvāder ucchiṣṭam iti varjayet | manur apy āha: “śvā mṛgagrahaṇe śuciḥ” [MDh 5.130] iti | dviruktir uktā | atra manuḥ |
anumantā viśasitā nihantā krayavikrayī |
saṃskartā copahartā ca khādakaś ceti ghātakāḥ || [MDh 5.51]
na māṃsabhakṣaṇe doṣo na madye na ca maithune |
pravṛttir eṣā bhūtānāṃ nivṛttis tu mahāphalam || iti | [MDh 5.56]
apratiṣiddheṣv api bhakṣaṇān nirvṛttir eva jyāyasīty arthaḥ || 17.38 ||
iti śrīgautamīyavṛttau haradattaviracitāyāṃ mitākṣarāyāṃ
dvitīyapraśne ‘ṣṭamo ‘dhyāyaḥ
atha aṣṭādaśo ‘dhyāyaḥ
atha strīdharmān āha |
asvatantrā dharme strī || 18.1 ||
śraute gārhye ca dharme strī bhartur evānuṣṭhānam anupraviśati | vratopavāsādibhir api smārtaiḥ paurāṇaiś ca dharmair nāntareṇa bhartur anujñāṃ svātantreṇādhikriyate | āha śaṅkhaḥ: “na ca vratopavāsair niyamejyādānadharmo vānugrahakaraṇaṃ strīṇām anyatra patiśuśrūṣāyāḥ | karma tu bhartur anujñayā vratopavāsaniyamādīnām abhyāsaḥ strīdharmaḥ” iti | nārado ‘py āha |
strīkṛāny apramāṇāni kāryāṇy āhur anāpadi |
viśeṣato gṛhakṣetradānādhyayanavikrayāt ||
etāny eva pramāṇāni bhartā yady anumanyate || iti | [NSm 1.22–23]
manus tu |
bālye pitur vaśe tiṣṭhet pāṇigrāhasya yauvane |
putrasya sthavirābhāve na strī svātantryam arhati || [MDh 5.148]
bālayā vā yuvatyā vā vṛddhayā vāpi yoṣitā |
na svātantryeṇa kartavyaṃ kāryaṃ kiṃcid gṛheṣv api || [MDh 5.147] iti || 18.1 ||
nāticared bhartāram || 18.2 ||
bhartāraṃ nātikrāmed bhartur anyaṃ manasāpi na cintayet || 18.2 ||
vākcakṣuḥkarmasaṃyatā || 18.3 ||
yāvadarthasaṃbhāṣiṇī vākasṃyatā | prekṣakādīnām aprekṣiṇī cakṣuḥsaṃyatā | svakuṭumbārthakarmavyatiriktānāṃ karmaṇām akartrī karmasaṃyatā | evaṃbhūtā syāt || 18.3 ||
atha nāticared bhartāram ity asyāpavādaḥ |
apatir apatyalipsur devarāt || 18.4 ||
anapatyāyā yasyāḥ patir mṛtaḥ sāpatyaṃ lipsamānā satī devarāl lipseta | patyur bhrātā devaraḥ kaniṣṭha ity upadeśaḥ || 18.4 ||
tatra prakāraḥ |
guruprasūtā nartum atīyāt || 18.5 ||
gurubhiḥ patipakṣaiḥ pitṛpakṣair vā niyuktā satī saṃyujyeta | tatrāpi nartum atīyād ṛtukālaṃ nātikrāmet | tatrāpi prathame gamane garbhasaṃbhavaḥ | śrūyate hi tavalakārāṇāṃ brāhmaṇe: “yad vā prathame ‘hani retaḥ sicyate sa garbhaḥ saṃbhavaty atha yatra tat sicyate mudheva tat parāsicyate” iti | tataś catrāv api sakṛd eva gamanam | atrauśanaso viśeṣaḥ: “niyuktā sarvāṅgaṃ ghṛtābhyaktam | tena sarvāṅgam ātmānam abhyajya gacchet” iti || 18.5 ||
devarābhāve krameṇa gamanīyān āha |
piṇḍagotrarṣisaṃbandhebhyo yonimātrād vā || 18.6 ||
piṇḍasaṃbandhaḥ sapiṇdaḥ | gotrasaṃbandhaḥ sagotraḥ | ṛṣisaṃbandhaḥ samānapravarā haritakutsādayaḥ | etebhyaḥ kramenāpatyaṃ lipseta | yonimātrād vā | atra smṛtyantaram: “sarvābhāve yonimātrād brāhmaṇajātimātrāt” iti || 18.6 ||
nādevarād ity eke || 18.7 ||
eke manyante devarād eva lipseta nādevarād iti || 18.7 ||
nātidvitīyam || 18.8 ||
prathamam apatyam atīya dvitīyaṃ na janayed iti || 18.8 ||
athaivam utpāditam apatyaṃ kṣetriṇo bījino veti viṣaye nirṇayam āha |
janayitur apatyam || 18.9 ||
janayitus tad apatyaṃ bhavati na kṣetriṇaḥ | āpastambo ‘pi: “utpādayituḥ putra iti hi brāhmaṇam” [ĀpDh 2.13.5] ityādi || 18.9 ||
samayād anyasya || 18.10 ||
yadi jñātayaḥ samayaṃ kṛtvā niyuñjate kṣetriṇo ‘patyam astv iti yathā vicitravīryasya kṣetraṃ satyavatī tasyāṃ vyāsenotpāditam apatyam iti || 18.10 ||
jīvataś ca kṣetre || 18.11 ||
yadā ca jīvann eva kṣetrī vandhyo rugṇo vā prārthayate mama kṣetre putram utpādayeti tadā kṣetriṇa evāpatyaṃ na bījinaḥ || 18.11 ||
parasmāt tasya || 18.12 ||
parasmād devarādivyatiriktāt tadaniyuktāyām apy apatyavatyām anapatyāyāṃ cotpannaḥ punas tasyaiva bījino bhavati na kṣetriṇaḥ || 18.12 ||
dvayor vā || 18.13 ||
evamutpāditam apatyaṃ dvayor vā bhavati bījikṣetriṇoḥ | idaṃ niyuktāviṣayam | tathā ca yājñavalkyaḥ |
aputreṇa parakṣetre niyogotpaditaḥ sutaḥ |
ubhayor apy asau rikthī piṇḍadātā ca dharmataḥ || [YDh 2.127] iti || 18.13 ||
rakṣaṇāt tu bhartur eva || 18.14 ||
yadi bhartā kṣetry eva rakṣaṇaṃ bharaṇaṃ poṣaṇaṃ saṃskārādi karoti na bījī tadā bhartur eva tad apatyam iti | evaṃ mṛte || 18.14 ||
śrūyamāṇe ‘bhigamanam || 18.15 ||
yadā tu bhartā śrūyate tasmin deśe sthita iti tadā tam abhigacchet || 18.15 ||
pravrajite tu nivṛttiḥ prasaṅgāt || 18.16 ||
yadi tu bhartā pravrajito bhavati mokṣāśramaṃ prāpto bhavati tadā sarvasmāt prasaṅgān nivṛttiḥ | svayam api nivṛttimukhī saṃyataiva syād iti || 18.16 ||
dvādaśa varṣāṇi brāhmaṇasya vidyāsaṃbandhe || 18.17 ||
vidyādhigamārthaṃ proṣitasya brāhmaṇasya bhāryā dvādaśa varṣāṇi kṣapayet | nāpatyotpattir nābhigamanam || 18.17 ||
bhrātari caivaṃ jyāyasi yavīyān kanyāgnyupayameṣu
|| 18.18 ||
jyeṣṭhe bhrātary akṛtadāre ‘nāhitāgnau ca proṣite kanīyān bhrātaivaṃ dvādaśa varṣāṇi pratīkṣeta | tataḥ kanyām upayacched agnīṃś cādadhīta | atra vāsiṣṭho viśeṣaḥ: “aṣṭau daśa dvādaśa varṣāṇi jyeṣṭhaṃ bhrātaram aniviṣṭaṃ na pratīkṣamāṇaḥ prāyaścittīyo bhavati” iti |
dvādaśaiva tu varṣāṇi jyāyān dharmārthayogyataḥ |
nyāyyaḥ pratīkṣituṃ bhrātā śrūyamāṇaḥ punaḥ punaḥ || iti ca || 18.18 ||
ṣaḍ ity eke || 18.19 ||
eke manyante ṣaḍ eva varṣāṇi pratīkṣeteti | proṣite cātyantavṛddhe sthite cātyantadharmapara idam || 18.19 ||
gataṃ prāsāṅgikam, punar api strīdharmān āha |
trīn kumāry ṛtūn atīya svayaṃ yujyetāninditenotsṛja
pitryān alaṃkārān || 18.20 ||
yadi kanyāṃ pitrādir na dadyat tatas trīn ṛtūn atītya svayam evāninditena kulavidyāśīlādiyuktena bhartrā yujyeta pitryān pitṛkulāyātān alaṃkārān utsṛjya | atra manuḥ |
alaṃkāraṃ nādadīta pitryaṃ kanyā svayaṃvarā |
mātṛkaṃ bhrātṛdattaṃ vā steyaṃ syād yadi kiṃcana || [MDh 9.92] iti || 18.20 ||
ata eva |
pradānaṃ prāg ṛtoḥ || 18.21 ||
ṛtudarśanāt prāg eva deyā kanyā || 18.21 ||
aprayacchan doṣī || 18.22 ||
tasmin kāle ‘prayacchan pitrādir doṣavān bhavati | atra yājñavalkyaḥ |
pitā pitāmaho bhrātā sakulyo jananī tathā |
kanyāpradaḥ pūrvanāśe prakṛtisthaḥ paraḥ paraḥ ||
aprayacchan samāpnoti bhrūṇahatyām ṛtāv ṛtau || [YDh 1.63–64] iti || 18.22 ||
prāg vāsasaḥ pratipatter ity eke || 18.23 ||
eke manyante yadā kanyā vāsaḥ pratipadyate ‘tha vā lajjate tāvad eva pradeyeti || 18.23 ||
dravyādānaṃ vivāhasiddhyarthaṃ dharmatantra-
saṃyoge ca śūdrāt || 18.24 ||
dravyam ananujñātam api śūdrāc cailādikam ādeyaṃ vivāhasiddhyarthaṃ yāvatā vivāhaḥ sidhyati tāvat | adhike doṣaḥ | tathā dharmasya paśubandhādeḥ pravṛttasya yat tantram aṅgam aśvādi tasya saṃyoge ‘vicchedasiddhyarthe yāvatā tan nivartate [nirvartate] tāvad ananujñātam apy ādeyaṃ śūdrāt | adhike doṣaḥ || 18.24 ||
anyatrāpi śūdrād bahupaśor hīnakarmaṇaḥ || 18.25 ||
“itarābhyo ‘pi dṛśyante” iti pañcamyās tral | śūdrād anyato ‘pi dravyam ādeyaṃ sa ced bahupaśus tathā hīnakarmā bhavati | tadanurūpaṃ karma na karoti niṣiddhaṃ vā karma sevate | śūdragrahaṇaṃ vidhir ayaṃ yathā syād iti | tena śūdrālābhe vaiśyat | tadalābhe kṣatriyāt || 18.25 ||
uktam evārtham udāharaṇena darśayati |
śatagor anāhitāgneḥ || 18.26 ||
gograhaṇam upalakṣaṇam | yas tāvad dravyo bhavaty agnīṃś ca nādhatte | niṣiddhakarmasevī tu daṇḍāpūpikayā vyākhyātaḥ || 18.26 ||
sahasragoś cāsomapāt || 18.27 ||
pūrveṇa gatam | yaḥ sahasraguś ca bhavati somaṃ ca na pibati tasmād iti || 18.27 ||
saptamīṃ cābhuktvānicayāya || 18.28 ||
saptamyarthe dvitīyā | ṣaṭsu velāsu bhojyālābhenābhuktvā saptamyāṃ velāyāṃ yāvatā vṛttis tāvad ananumatam apy ādeyam | anicayaḥ punas tena nicayo na kartavyaḥ | śvo bhojyam api nādeyam | atra manuḥ |
tathaiva saptame bhakte bhaktāni ṣad anaśṇatā |
aśvastanavidhānena hartavyaṃ hīnakarmaṇā || [MDh 11.16] iti || 18.28 ||
apy ahīnakarmabhyaḥ || 18.29 ||
asyām avasthāyām ahīnakarmabhyo ‘py ādeyam | apiśabdaḥ kathaṃcid asyānujñātam iti darśayati | tena prāṇasaṃśaya evedaṃ bhavati || 18.29 ||
ācakṣīta rājñā pṛṣṭaḥ || 18.30 ||
yady asāv evaṃ kurvan svāmibhir gṛhīto rājasakāśaṃ nītas tena pṛṣṭaḥ kim ittham akārṣīr iti tadā svām avasthām ācakṣīta | na tu mithyā vaded iti || 18.30 ||
tena hi bhartavyaḥ śrutaśīlasaṃpannaś cet || 18.31 ||
hiścārthe | tena ca rājñā sa na kevalam adaṇḍyaḥ kiṃ tarhi tata ārabhya bhartavyas taveyam avasthā mayā na jñāteti sāntvayitvā | sa cec chrutavṛttaśīlasaṃpanno bhavati | śrutaṃ śāstraparijñānam | śīlaṃ tadanukūla ācāraḥ | itaro ‘pi na daṇḍyaḥ | bharaṇaṃ tu tasya tādṛśaṃ na kāryam | daṇḍābhāvaḥ pūrvayor api nimittayoḥ samānaḥ || 18.31 ||
dharmatantrapīḍāyāṃ tasyākaraṇe doṣo ‘karaṇe doṣaḥ
|| 18.32 ||
yadi paśubandhādau dharme pravṛttasya tadaṅgaṃ pāśvādi kenacit pīḍitaṃ bhavati hatam apahṛtaṃ vā tasmin nivedite tadaiva tasya pratividhānaṃ kāryaṃ rājñā | akaraṇe doṣo bhavati | abhyāso ‘dhyāyasamāptyarthaḥ || 18.32 ||
iti śrīgautamīyavṛttau haradattaviracitāyāṃ mitākṣarāyāṃ
dvitīyapraśne navamo ‘dhyāyaḥ
atha tṛtīyaḥ praśnaḥ
ekonaviṃśo ‘dhyāyaḥ
pañcavidho dharmaḥ – varṇadharma āśramadharma ubhayadharmo guṇadharmo naimittika[dharma]ś ceti | tatra varṇaprayukto dharmo varṇadharmaḥ “upanayanaṃ brāhmaṇasyāṣṭame” [GDh 1.6] iti | āśramaprayukta āśramadharmo brahmacāryādeḥ samidādhānādir iti | ubhayaprayukta ubhayadharmo brāhmaṇasya brāhmacāriṇaḥ pālaśo daṇda ityādi | abhiṣekaguṇayuktaḥ prajāpālanādir guṇadharmaḥ | brahmahatyādau nimitte kartavyo naimittiko dharmaḥ prāyaścittam | tatra naimittikaṃ vakṣyann uktam anubhāṣate |
ukto varṇadharmaś cāśramadharmaś ca || 19.1 ||
ubhayadharmaguṇadharmayor apy upalakṣaṇam etat | yady apy anyatroktaṃ nānubhāṣyate ‘nanubhāṣaṇe ‘pi vakṣyamāṇaṃ śakyate vaktum iti tathāpīhānubhāṣyata āśaṅkānivṛttyartham | anyathopariṣṭād daivikāni punaḥstomādīni prāyaścittaṇy udāhariṣyante tāni ca śūdrasya na saṃbhavanty atas tadvad eva punaḥstomādīni prāyaścittāntarāṇy api śūdrasya na syur iti kaścid āśaṅketa | apara āha – ya ukto dharmaḥ sa eva varṇinām āśramiṇāṃ ca dharmaḥ | vakṣyamāṇas tu puruṣamātradharmaḥ | yad āha “atha khalv ayaṃ puruṣaḥ” [GDh 19.2] iti | kiṃ siddhaṃ bhavati | pratilomānām api prāyaścitteṣv adhikāraḥ siddho bhavati | yady api teṣāṃ bhakṣyābhakṣyaviveko nāsti tathāpi gobrāhmaṇādivadhe brāhmaṇasvarṇādiharaṇe ca prāyaścittaṃ bhavaty eva | akurvāṇā eva tu prāyaścittaṃ rājñā vadhyāḥ | ahiṃsāsatyāsteyabrahmacaryāparigrahādimanuṣyamātradharmā na varṇeṣv āśrameṣu vā niyatāḥ | atas tadatikrame yuktam eva prāyaścittam | yat tu pūrvam uktam “pratilomās tu dharmahīnāḥ” [GDh 4.20] iti, tad aihikāmuṣmikaśreyaḥsādhaneṣu karmasv adhikāranivṛttiparam iti || 19.1 ||
prāyaścittasya nimittāny āha |
atha khalv ayaṃ puruṣo yāpyena karmaṇā lipyate
yathaitad ayājyayājanam abhakṣyabhakṣaṇam
avadyavadanaṃ śiṣṭasyākriyā pratiṣiddhasevanam
iti || 19.2 ||
atha khalv iti vākyālaṃkāre | ayaṃ puruṣa iti saṃghātavartinaṃ pratyagātmānaṃ nirdiśati | yāpyaṃ kutsitam | yāpyena pāpena karmaṇā lipyate | tajjanyenādharmeṇa lipyamāne karmaṇā lipyata iti bhākto vādaḥ | yāpyasya karmaṇa udāharaṇaprapañco yathaitad ityādi | yathety udāharaṇe | ayājyāḥ patitādayas teṣāṃ yājanam | abhakṣyā laśunādayas teṣāṃ bhakṣaṇam | avadyam anṛtāsabhyādi tasya vadanaṃ kathanam | śiṣṭaṃ vihitaṃ saṃdhyopāsanādi tasyākriyākaraṇam | pratiṣiddhasya hiṃsādeḥ sevanaṃ karaṇam | iti samāptau | etāvad eva yāpyaṃ karmeti | pratiṣiddhasevanam ity eva siddher ayājyayājanādigrahaṇaṃ yājanādhyāpanapratigrahāḥ sarveṣām ity āpady anujñā tatrāpi prāyaścittārtham | tatra uśanā: “āpadvihitaiḥ karmabhir āpadaṃ tīrtvā punas teṣāṃ prāyaścittaṃ caturbhāgaṃ kuryāt” iti | abhakṣyabhakṣaṇagrahaṇam apy āpadi vyādhyādau laśunādibhakṣaṇaviṣayaṃ ca | avadyavadanagrahaṇaṃ tu,
prāṇināṃ tu vadho yatra tatra sākṣy anṛtaṃ vadet |
ityādiviṣayam ca | tathā yatra brāhmaṇa iti jñāte tāḍayeyur arthaṃ vā hareyus tatra tadgrahaṇārtham | asabhyānṛtabhāṣaṇenāpi tan nivārya paścāt tām āpadaṃ tīrtvā prāyaścittaṃ caturbhāgaṃ cared iti || 19.2 ||
tatra prāyaścittaṃ kuryān na kuryād iti
mīmāṃsante || 19.3 ||
tatra tasmin yāpyakarmalepe prāyaścittam,
prāyo nāma tapaḥ proktaṃ cittaṃ niścaya ucyate |
taponiścayasaṃyogāt prāyaścittam iti smṛtam ||
ityevaṃlakṣaṇaṃ kartavyaṃ na kartavyam iti vicārayanti brahmavādinaḥ || 19.3 ||
tatra kecit |
na kuryād ity āhuḥ || 19.4 ||
tatra hetuḥ |
na hi karma kṣīyata iti || 19.5 ||
hiśabdo hetau | yasmāt kṛtaṃ karma puṇyaṃ pāpaṃ ca nāntareṇopabhogaṃ kṣīyata iti | tathā ca śaṅkhaḥ |
yathā pṛthivyāṃ bījāni ratnāni nidhayo yathā |
evam ātmani karmāṇi tiṣṭhanti prasavanti ca || iti |
utpanne tu phale naśyati yathā bījam aṅkure | prāyaścittāni tu nimitte karmāntarāṇi | yathā gṛhadāhādau kṣāmavatyadayaḥ || 19.5 ||
kuryād ity aparam || 19.6 ||
kuryāt prāyaścittam ity aparaṃ darśanam | nāsmāt param astīty aparasiddhāntaḥ || 19.6 ||
tatra pramāṇatvena śrutivākyāny udāharati |
punaḥstomeneṣṭvā punaḥ savanam āyāntīti
vijñāyate || 19.7 ||
“apratigrāhyād brahma pratigṛhya punaḥstomena yajeta” iti śrūyate | “abhakṣyabhakṣaṇam avadyavadanaṃ punaḥstomena tarata” iti ca | asatpratigrahādidoṣadūṣitāḥ punaḥstomanāmnaikāheneṣṭvā punaḥ savanam āyānti | savanaśabdena karmocyate | punar api śrautāni smārtāni ca karmāṇy āyānty āpnuvanti | tadyogyā bhavanti || 19.7 ||
vrātyastomaiś ceṣṭvā || 19.8 ||
punaḥ savanam āyāntīty anuṣaṅgaḥ | vrātyā yathākālam anupanītāḥ | teṣāṃ kartavyāḥ prāyaścittayāgā vrātyastomāḥ | bahuvacananirdeśād bahavas te pratyetavyāḥ || 19.8 ||
tarati sarvaṃ pāpmānaṃ tarati brahmahatyāṃ
yo ‘śvamedhena yajata iti ca || 19.9 ||
iti ceti vakṣyamāṇam apekṣyate | vijñāyata ity anuṣaṅgaḥ [should this go under 19.10?]
|| 19.9 ||
agniṣṭutābhiśasyamānaṃ yājayed iti ca || 19.10 ||
agniṣṭun nāmaikāhas tenābhiśasyamānaṃ yājayet | atra punaḥstomādīnāṃ doṣanirghātārthatayā śrutatvād upabhogeneva prāyaścittenāpi pāpakaṃ karma kṣīyate | śaṅkhavacanaṃ cākṛtaprāyaścittaviṣayaṃ puṇyaviṣayaṃ ca | atha kasmād vacanagamye ‘rthe vicāraḥ kriyate | kuryān na kuryād iti | na hy upanayanādāv evaṃ vicāraḥ kṛta iti ucyate | prāyaścittastutyartho ‘yaṃ vicāraḥ || 19.10 ||
idānīṃ yeṣv āhatya na prāyaścittaṃ vihitaṃ teṣu prāyaścittāny upadiśati |
tasya niṣkrayaṇāni japas tapo homa upavāso
dānam || 19.11 ||
tasya yāpyasya karmaṇo japādīni pañca niṣkrayaṇāni śodhanāni || 19.11 ||
tatra japa ity uktaṃ japān āha |
upaniṣado vedāntaḥ sarvacchandaḥsu saṃhitā madhūny
aghamarṣaṇam atharvaśiro rudrāḥ puruṣasūktaṃ
rājatarauhiṇe sāmanī bṛhadrathantare puruṣagatir
mahānāmnyo mahāvairājaṃ mahādivākīrtyaṃ
jyeṣṭhasāmnām anyatamad bahiṣpavamānaṃ
kūṣmāṇḍāni pāvamānyaḥ sāvitrī ceti pāvanāni || 19.12 ||
upaniṣado rahasyabrāhmaṇāny ādhyātmikāni | tadvyatiriktā āraṇyakabhāgāḥ vedāntāḥ | sarvachandaḥsu sarveṣu pravacaneṣu saṃhitā saṃhitāpāṭho na padakramādipāṭhaḥ | madhūni madhuśabdayuktāni yajūṃṣi “brahma metu mām” [TaitĀ10.48.1] ityādīni | aghamarṣaṇam “ṛtaṃ ca satyaṃ ca” iti sūktaṃ ṣaḍṛcam aghamarṣaṇena ṛṣiṇā dṛṣṭam | atharvaśiro ‘tharvavede prasiddham, “devā ha vai svargaṃ lokam agaman” ityādi | rudrāḥ “namas te rudra manyave” [VS 16.1] ityādyā anuvākā ekādaśa | ekaśataṃ yajuḥśākhās tāsu sarvāsu paṭhyante | puruṣasūkam “sahasraśīrṣā” [RV 10.90] ityādi | rājatarauhiṇe sāmanī “indraṃ naro nemadhitā havante” [RV 7.27.1] ity asyām ṛci gīyete | “tvām iddhi havāmahe” [RV 6.46.1] ity ābhyāṃ bṛhat | “abhi tvā śūra nonumaḥ” [RV 7.32.22] ity atra rathaṃtaram | “aham asmi prathamajā ṛtasya” [TaitBr 2.8.8.1] ity asyāṃ puruṣagatiḥ | mahānāmnyaḥ “vidā maghavan” [AitĀ 4.15] ityādyā ṛcaḥ | mahāvairājam “pibā somam” [RV 6.17.1] ity asvyāṃ gītaṃ sāma | mahādivākīrtyam “vibhrāḍ bṛhat pibatu” [RV 10.170.1] ity asyām | jyeṣṭhasāmāni talavakāriṇām “ud u tyam” [RV 1.50.1] “citram” [RV 1.115.1] ity etayor gītāni | chandogās tv āhuḥ: “mūrdhānaṃ divaḥ” [RV 6.7.1] ity asyāṃ gītāni trīṇi sāmāny ājyadohādīni, brāhmaṇe tathā śrutatvāt | bahiṣpavamānam “upāsmai gāyatā naraḥ” [RV 9.11.1] ity etāsu gītam | kūṣmāṇḍāni taittirīyake svādhyāyabrāhmaṇe “yad devā devaheḍanam” [VS 20.14], “yad adīvyann ṛṇām” [TaitĀ2.4.1], “āyuṣ ṭe viśvato ‘dadhat” [TaitS1.3.14.4] iti trayo ‘nuvākāḥ | yajuṣṭvābhiprāyo napuṃsakanirdeśaḥ | tatraivācchidrākhye praśne “yad devā devahelanam” [VS 20.14] ity anuvāke yā ṛcas tāḥ kūṣmāṇḍyaḥ | pavamānaḥ somo devatā yāsāṃ tāḥ pāvamānyaḥ “svādiṣṭhayā madiṣṭhayā” [RV 9.1.1] ityādyā ā maṇḍalasamāpteḥ | “tat savitur vareṇyam” ity eṣā sāvitrī prasiddhā | na yā kācana savitṛdevatyā | itiśabdaḥ prakāravacanaḥ | evaṃprakānāṇy anyāny api pāvamānānīti | tatra manuḥ |
kautsaṃ japtvāpa ity etad vāsiṣṭhaṃ ca tṛcaṃ prati |
māhitraṃ śuddhaliṅgaṃ ca surāpo ‘pi viśudhyati ||
sakṛj japtvāsyavāmīyaṃ śivasaṃkalpam eva ca |
suvarṇam apahṛtyāpi kṣaṇād bhavati nirmalaḥ ||
haviṣyāntīyam abhyasya na tamaṃha itīti ca |
japtvā tu pauruṣaṃ sūktaṃ mucyate gurutalpagaḥ ||
