somāraudraṃ tu bahvenā māsam abhyasya śudhyati || [MDh 11.249–250, 254]
ityādi | prāyaścittaprakaraṇe punaḥ pāvamānānītivacanāt prāyaścittavyatirekeṇāpy ṛddhikāmasyāhar ahar etāni japyāni || 19.12 ||
jape pravṛttasyāhāraniyamam āha |
payovratatā śākabhakṣatā phalabhakṣatā prasṛtayāvako
hiraṇyaprāśanaṃ ghṛtaprāśanaṃ somapānam iti
medhyāni || 19.13 ||
payovratatā kṣīrāhāratā | vratagrahaṇād upavāsanyāyena | śākaṃ vāstukādi | phalaṃ kadalyādeḥ | prasṛtayāvakaḥ prasṛtaparimitair yavaiḥ pakva odanaḥ | tatra auśanaso viśeṣaḥ: “snātaḥ śucir bhūtvoditeṣu nakṣatreṣu tāmrabhājane prasṛtayāvakaṃ śrapayed yathā yavāgūr bhavati | tasya śrapaṇakāle rakṣāṃ kuryāt | namo rudrāya bhūtādhipataye parvatānāṃ pataye tvam imam rakṣasva’ iti | śṛte ‘varopya ‘devasya tvā savitur’ ityādinotpūya tato ‘bhimantrayet |
yavo ‘si dhānyarājo ‘si vāruṇo madhusaṃyutaḥ |
nirṇodaḥ sarvapāpānāṃ pavitram ṛṣibhiḥ smṛtam ||
vācā kṛtaṃ karmakṛtaṃ manasā durvicintanam |
alakṣmīṃ kālakaṇṭhīṃ ca sarvaṃ punata me yavāḥ ||
mahāpātakasaṃyuktaṃ dāruṇaṃ rājakilbiṣam |
bālavṛttam adharmaṃ ca sarvaṃ punata me yavāḥ ||
suvarṇastainyam avratyam ayājyasya ca yājanam |
brāhmaṇānāṃ parīvādaṃ sarvaṃ punata me yavāḥ ||
śvasūkarāvabhūtaṃ ca kākādyucchiṣṭam eva ca |
mātāpitror aśuśrūṣāṃ sarvaṃ punata me yavāḥ ||
gaṇānnaṃ gaṇikānnaṃ ca śūdrānnaṃ śrāddhasūtakam |
corasyānnaṃ tathābhakṣyaṃ sarvaṃ punata me yavāḥ ||
ity etaiḥ ṣaḍbhiḥ |
tato ‘brahmā devānām’ iti prāśya tataḥ prāṇāya svāhetyādibhir yathoktaṃ sarvaṃ prāśnīyāt ṣaḍrātram | tato niyamātikramajāt pratiṣiddhasevanajād abhyakṣyabhakṣaṇajāc ca sarvasmāt pāpāt pramucyate | saptarātraṃ pītvā bhrūṇahatyāṃ gurutalpaṃ suvarṇastainyaṃ surāpānaṃ ca punāti | ekādaśarātraṃ pītvā sarvakṛtapāpaṃ nudati | ekaviṃsatirātraṃ pītvā gaṇān paśyati gaṇādhipatiṃ paśyati vidyāṃ paśyati vidyādhipatiṃ paśyati | evam ahar ahar ananyāhāro yavāgūṃ prāśnīyāt” iti |
sarpirādau hiraṇyaṃ vighṛṣya prāśanaṃ hiraṇyaprāśanam | ghṛtaprāśanaṃ prasiddham | somapānaṃ kratāv uktam | bahir apy anye | itikaranād yac cānyad evam uktaṃ pañcagavyaśaṅkhapuṣpādi tasya prāśanaṃ medhyaṃ vijñeyam || 19.13 ||
atha japādīnāṃ sthānam āha |
sarve śiloccayāḥ sarvāḥ sravantyaḥ puṇyā hradās
tīrthāny ṛṣinivāsā goṣṭhapariskandhā iti deśāḥ || 19.14 ||
śiloccayāḥ śailāḥ | sravantyo nadyaḥ | sarvagrahaṇāt puṇyāpuṇyavibhāgo nādaraṇīyaḥ | puṇyā hradāḥ puṣkariṇyādayaḥ | prayāgādīni tīrthāni | ṛṣinivāsā vasiṣṭhādīnām āśramāḥ | goṣṭhaḥ gavāṃ sthānam | pariskandho devālayaḥ | itikaraṇān naimiṣāraṇyādīni || 19.14 ||
vyākhyātaḥ sahaparikareṇa japaḥ | tapaḥsvarūpam āha |
brahmacaryaṃ satyavacanaṃ savaneṣūdakopa-
sparśanam ārdravastratādhaḥśāyitānāśaka iti
tapāṃsi || 19.15 ||
brahmacaryaṃ maithunatyāgaḥ | satyavacanaṃ dṛṣṭārthavāditvam | savaneṣu prātar madhyaṃdine sāyaṃ codakasparśanaṃ snānam | ārdravastratā snānasamaye parihitasya vāsasas tathaivāpīḍitasya dhāraṇam | adhaḥśāyitā sthaṇḍilaśāyitā | aśanam āśaḥ | sa evāśakas tasyābhāvo ‘nāśako ‘naśanam | atrāpītikaraṇāt prāṇāyāmādīnāṃ grahaṇam | atra manuḥ |
savyāhṛtikāḥ sapraṇavāḥ prāṇāyāmās tu ṣoḍaśa |
api bhrūṇahanaṃ māsāt punanty ahar ahaḥ kṛtāḥ || iti |
homāḥ kūṣmāṇḍagaṇahomādayaḥ prasiddhatvād ihānuktāḥ | tatra śrutiḥ: “kūṣmāṇḍair juhuyād yo ‘pūta iva manyeta” ityādi | gaṇahomas tu baudhāyanoktaḥ |
kṣāpavitraṃ sahasrākṣo mṛgāro ‘ṃhomucau gaṇau |
pāvamānyaś ca kūṣmāṇḍyo vaiśvānarya ṛcaś ca yāḥ ||
ghṛtaudanena tā juhvat saptāhaṃ savanatrayam |
maunavratī haviṣyāśī nigṛhītendriyakriyaḥ |
mucyate sarvapāpebhyo mahataḥ pātakād api || iti | [BDh 4.7.5–7]
yājñavalkyaḥ |
yatra yatra ca saṃkīrṇam ātmānaṃ manyate janaḥ |
tatra tatra tilair homaḥ sāvitryāḥ pratyahaṃ japaḥ || iti | [YDh 3.309]
manuḥ |
na sāvitrīsamaṃ japyaṃ nājyāhutisamaṃ hutam |
nānnatoyasamaṃ dānaṃ na cāhiṃsāparaṃ tapaḥ || iti |
upavāso bhaktatyāgaḥ | sa eva tapaḥsv api punaḥ punaḥ paṭhyata ādarakhyāpanārtham | apara āha – niṣkrayaṇeṣu paṭhita upavāsa indriyanigrahaḥ |
vyāvṛttasyaiva doṣebhyo yas tu vāso guṇaiḥ saha |
upavāsaṃ tam āhus tu na śarīrasya śoṣaṇāt ||
iti purāṇe darśanād iti || 19.15 ||
atha deyāny āha |
hiraṇyaṃ gaur vāso ‘śvo bhūmis tilā ghṛtam annam
iti deyānīti || 19.16 ||
nigadavyākhyātam etat || 19.16 ||
atha kiyān kālo japādīnām ity ata āha |
saṃvatsaraḥ ṣaṇ māsāś catvāras trayo vā dvau vaikaś
caturviṃśatyaho dvādaśāhaḥ ṣaḍahar tryaho ‘horātra
iti kālāḥ || 19.17 ||
eteṣu yāvatā śuddho manyate tāvān kālaḥ || 19.17 ||
etāny evānādeśe vikalpena kriyeran || 19.18 ||
etāny eva japādīni niṣkrayaṇāny anādeśe yatrāhatya prāyaścittam anirdiṣṭaṃ tatra viṣaye vikalpena kartavyāni | evakāraḥ paunarvacanikaḥ | tad yathā – devadatto grāmaṃ gacchatu sa evāraṇyam iti | kiṃ siddhaṃ bhavati | yeṣu niyate(mitte)ṣv āhatya prāyaścittam uktaṃ teṣv apy abhyāsānubandhādau pratipūraṇāpekṣāyāṃ japādīnām anupraveśaḥ siddho bhavati || 19.18 ||
kiṃ tulyavad vikalpo nety āha |
enaḥsu guruṣu gurūṇi laghuṣu laghūni || 19.19 ||
abhisaṃdhikṛtam eno guru tadviparītaṃ laghu | evam abhyāsānubandhādāv api draṣṭavyam | yathāha āpastambaḥ: “yaḥ pramatto hanti prāptaṃ doṣaphalaṃ saha saṃkalpena bhūya evam anyeṣv api doṣavatsu karmasu tathā puṇyaphaleṣu yathā karmābhyāse” [ĀpDh 1.29.2–5 iti || 19.19 ||
kṛcchrātikṛcchrau cāndrāyaṇam iti sarvaprāyaścittaṃ
[sarvaprāyaścittam] || 19.20 ||
kṛcchrātikṛcchrau cāndrāyaṇaṃ copariṣṭād vakṣyante | sarvagrahaṇān na kevalam anādeśe | etāni ca gurūny enāṃsi samastāni samuditāni prāyaścittaṃ laghūny ekam ekaṃ laghutare ‘tikṛcchro laghutame kṛcchraḥ | manur apy āha |
saṃvatsarasyaikam api caret kṛcchraṃ dvijottamaḥ |
ajñātabhuktaśuddhyarthaṃ jñātasya tu viśeṣataḥ || iti | [MDh 5.21]
itikaraṇād yac cānyad evam uktam | tatra manuḥ |
yatātmano ‘pramattasya dvādaśāham abhojanam |
parāko nāma kṛcchro ‘yaṃ sarvapāpapraṇāśanaḥ || iti | [MDh 11.215]
[abhyāso ‘dhyāyasamāptyarthaḥ] || 19.20 ||
iti śrīgautamīyavṛttau haradattaviracitāyāṃ mitākṣarāyāṃ
tṛtīyapraśne prathamo ‘dhyāyaḥ
atha viṃśo ‘dhyāyaḥ
atha yo yāpyaṃ kṛtvāpi prāyaścittaṃ na pratipadyate sa kiṃ kartavyaḥ | tyājya ity āha |
tyajet pitaraṃ rājaghātakaṃ śūdrayājakaṃ
śūdrārthayājakaṃ vedaviplāvakaṃ bhrūṇahanaṃ
yaś cāntyāvasāyibhiḥ saha saṃvased antyāvasāyinyāṃ
ca || 20.1 ||
rājātrābhiṣikto na jātimātropajīvī | taṃ hatavān rājaghātakaḥ | śūdraṃ yo yājayaty etayā niṣādasthapatiṃ yājayed ityādau viṣaye sa śūdrayājakaḥ | yaḥ śūdrād artham adhigamya yajate sa śūdrārthayājakaḥ | anadhyāyānadhyāpyādhyayanādinā yo vedaṃ viplavāyati vyākulī karoti sa vedaviplāvakaḥ | bhrūṇahā brahmahā | etan mahāpātakinām apy upalakṣaṇam |
caṇḍālaḥ śvapacaḥ kṣattā sūto vaidehikas tathā |
māgadhāyogavau caiva saptaite ‘ntyāvasāyinaḥ ||
ity aṅgirāḥ | taiḥ sahaikasmin sthāne yo vasati sa tathoktaḥ | antyāvasāyinām | yaḥ saha vasatīty apekṣate | tasyāṃ saṃvāso maithunācaraṇam | eteṣu nimitteṣu pitaram api tyajet | pitṛgrahaṇāt kim uta putrādikam iti || 20.1 ||
atha tyāgaprakāraḥ |
tasya vidyāgurūn yonisaṃbandhāṃś ca saṃnipātya
sarvāṇy udakādīni pretakāryāṇi kuryuḥ || 20.2 ||
tasya tyājyasya ye vidyāgurava ācāryagurūpādhyāyā yonisaṃbandhā mātulādayas tān sarvān saṃnipātyaikatra samavetān kṛtvodakādīni śrāddhāntāni sarvāṇi pretakarmāṇi kuryuḥ | ke | putrādayo jñātayaḥ, pitaram ity upakramād bahuvacananirdeśāc ca || 20.2 ||
pātraṃ cāsya viparyasyeyuḥ || 20.3 ||
asya tyājyasya pātraṃ kiṃcit kalpayitvā te eva viparyasyeyuḥ | viparyāso ‘dhomukhīkaraṇam | yathā tad anudakaṃ bhavati || 20.3 ||
tatra prakāraḥ |
dāsaḥ karmakaro vāvakarād amedhyapātram ānīya
dāsīghaṭāt pūrayitvā dakṣiṇāmukho yadā viparyasyed
amukam anudkaṃ karomīti nāmagrāham || 20.4 ||
dāsaḥ prasiddhaḥ | karmakaro bhṛtakaḥ | tayor anyataro ‘vakarād avaskarāt | varcaske ‘vaskaraḥ | amedhyāt sathānād aśuci pātraṃ kiṃcid upādāya yena dāsy udakam āharati tasmād ghaṭād gṛhītenodakena pūrayitvā dakṣiṇāmukho bhūtvā yadāpasavyena viparyasyed apasavyam adhomukhaṃ vikṣipet | tatra mantra – amukam anudakaṃ karomīti | nāmagrāham | amukam iti sthāne tyājyasya nāma dvitīyāntaṃ gṛhītvā | nāmnyādiśigrahor iti ṇamul | grāha it pāṭhe rūpasiddhiś cintyā || 20.4 ||
taṃ sarve ‘nvālabheran prācīnāvītino
muktaśikhāḥ || 20.5 ||
taṃ viparyasyantaṃ sarve jñātayaḥ prācīnāvītino muktaśikhāḥ santo ‘nvālabheran spṛśeyuḥ
|| 20.5 ||
vidyāguravo yonisaṃbandhāś ca vīkṣeran || 20.6 ||
na tu saṃspṛśeyuḥ || 20.6 ||
apa upaspṛśya grāmaṃ praviśanti || 20.7 ||
evam idaṃ karma kṛtvāpa upspṛśya snātvā grāmaṃ saṃviśanti praviśeyuḥ | ata eva jñāyate grāmād bahir idaṃ karmeti || 20.7 ||
ata uttaraṃ tena saṃbhāṣya tiṣṭhed ekarātraṃ japan
sāvitrīm ajñānapūrvam || 20.8 ||
atas tyāgād ūrdhvaṃ tena tyaktena saha saṃbhāṣaṇam ajñānāt kṛtvaikam ahorātraṃ tiṣṭhen na bhuñjīta na śayīta nāsīteti | ajñānapūrvam iti vacanād ekavacananirdeśāc ca jñātivyatiriktasyāpīdaṃ bhavati || 20.8 ||
jñānapūrvaṃ ca trirātram || 20.9 ||
yas tu tena jñānapūrvaṃ saṃbhāṣate sa trirātram uktakrameṇa tiṣṭhet | kāryākāryanirūpaṇādāv idam | paripraśnādau tu parāśaroktam |
kṣute niṣṭhīvane caiva dantaspṛṣṭe tathaiva ca |
patitānāṃ ca saṃbhāṣe dakṣiṇaṃ śravaṇaṃ spṛśet || iti || 20.9 ||
yas tu prāyaścittena śudhyet tasmiñ śuddhe
śātakumbhamayaṃ pātraṃ puṇyatamād dhradāt
pūrayitvā sravantībhyo vā tata enam apa
upasparśayeyuḥ || 20.10 ||
prāyaścitteneti vacanād rājadaṇḍena śuddhasya vakṣyamāṇasvīkaraṇavidhir na bhavati | tasya kevalaṃ paratraiva śuddhiḥ |
rājabhir dhṛtadaṇḍās tu kṛtvā pāpāni mānavāḥ |
nirmalāḥ svargam āyānti santaḥ sukṛtino yathā || iti || [MDh 8.318]
tasmiṃl lokasamakṣaṃ śuddhe śātakumbhamayaṃ sauvarṇaṃ pātraṃ puṇyatamād dhradān nadibhyo vāhṛtena jalena pūrayitvā tatas tasmād āvarjitā apa enaṃ caritaprāyaścittam upasparśayeyus tābhir adbhiḥ snāpayeyur jñātayaḥ || 20.10 ||
athāsmai tat pātraṃ dadyus tat saṃpratigṛhya japec
chāntā dyauḥ śāntā pṛthivī śāntaṃ śivam antarikṣaṃ
yo rocanas tam imaṃ gṛhṇāmīti || 20.11 ||
atha snāpanāntantaram asmai snātāya tat sauvarṇaṃ pātraṃ dadyur jñātayaḥ | sa ca tat pātraṃ pratigṛḥya japec chāntā dyaur ityādi gṛhṇāmītyantam || 20.11 ||
etair yajurbhiḥ pāvamānībhis taratsamandībhiḥ
kūṣmāṇḍaiś cājyaṃ juhuyād dhiraṇyaṃ brāhmaṇāya
dadyāt || 20.12 ||
homānte dānam || 20.12 ||
gāṃ vā || 20.13 ||
icchānto vikalpaḥ || 20.13 ||
ācāryāya ca || 20.14 ||
ya ātmana ācāryas tasmā api hiraṇyaṃ dadyād gāṃ vā || 20.14 ||
yasya tu prāṇāntikaṃ prāyaścittaṃ sa mṛtaḥ
śudhyet || 20.15 ||
uttaravivakṣayedam ucyate | prāyaścittasya śuddhyarthatvād eva siddhā śuddhiḥ || 20.15 ||
sarvāṇy eva tasminn udakādīni pretakarmāṇi
kuryuḥ || 20.16 ||
yady api tasya nāsmiṃl loke pratyāpattis tathāpi maraṇād eva śuddha iti sarvāṇy eva pretakarmāṇi kartavyāni | sarvagrahaṇād āśaucam api | yo ‘pi dvādaśavārṣikādau prāyaścitte pravṛtto madhye mriyate tadvaiṣaye vyāsa āha |
yajamānaḥ sadā dharmye mriyate yadi madhyataḥ |
prāpnoty eva tu tat sarvam atra me nāsti saṃśayaḥ || iti || 20.16 ||
etad eva śāntyudakaṃ sarveṣūpapātakeṣu
sarveṣūpapātakeṣu || 20.17 ||
etad evānantaroktaṃ śāntā dyaur ityādibhir abhimantritaṃ sarveṣūpapātakeṣu kartavyaṃ prāyaścittasyānte | ādāv ity anye | dviruktiḥ pūrvavat || 20.17 ||
iti śrīgautamīyavṛttau haradattaviracitāyāṃ mitākṣarāyāṃ
tṛtīyapraśne dvitīyo ‘dhyāyaḥ
atha ekaviṃśo ‘dhyāyaḥ
patitānāṃ tyāgavidhir uktaḥ | ke punas te | tān āha |
brahmahasurāpagurutalpagamātṛpitṛyonisaṃbandhāga-
stenanāstikaninditakarmābhyāsipatitātyāgyapatita-
tyāginaḥ patitāḥ || 21.1 ||
brāhmaṇasya hantā brahmahā |
gauḍī mādhvī ca paiṣṭī ca vijñeyā trividhā surā |
yathaivaikā na pātavyā tathā sarvā dvijottamaiḥ || [MDh 11.94]
iti manuḥ | tatra yā yasya surā pratiṣiddhā sa tasyāḥ pātā surāpaḥ | guruḥ pitācāryaś ca | talpaśabdena bhāryā lakṣyate | gamanaṃ maithunam | mātṛsaṃbandhā mātṛpakṣe prākpañcamād bhavāḥ pitṛsaṃbandhāḥ pitṛpakṣe ‘rvāk saptamād bhavāḥ | yonisaṃbaṃdhā bhaginyādayaḥ | ya etā bhāryātvenādhigacchati sa mātṛpitṛyonisaṃbandhāgaḥ | steno brāhmaṇasuvarṇāpahārī | nāstikaḥ pretyabhāvāpavādī | ninditaṃ pratiṣiddham | tasya karmaṇo buddhipūrvaṃ sātatyenānuṣṭhātā ninditakarmābhyāsī | patitā naiva sataḥ putrādīn snehādīnā (?) yo na tyajati sa patitātyāgī | yas tv apatitān eva dveṣādinā tyajati so ‘patitatyāgī | ete brahmahadaya patitāḥ
|| 21.1 ||
pātakasaṃyojakāś ca || 21.2 ||
pātakaiḥ karmabhir ye parān saṃyojayanti tatra “pravartyanti dravyapradānādinā macchatrum itthaṃ vyāpādaya tvacchatror vyāpādane ‘yam abhyupāyaḥ” iti | tathā kenacij jighāṃsitaṃ palāyamānaṃ yo nivārayati nivāritaś ca hanyate so ‘pi prayojakaḥ | yasyānumatim antareṇārtho na nivartate [nirvartate] sa mantā | sa iha pṛthaṅ nopādīyate prayojakakoṭir evāyam iti | āpastambas tu kiyatāpy avāntarabhedena tam eva pṛthag uditavān: “prayojitā mantā kartā ceti svarganarakaphaleṣu karmasu bhāginaḥ | yo bhūya ārabhate tasmin phalaviśeṣaḥ” iti [ĀpDh 2.21.1–2] || 21.2 ||
taiś cābdaṃ samācaran || 21.3 ||
taiḥ patitaiḥ saha yo ‘bdaṃ samācarati yānāsanaśayanādīni so ‘pi patitaḥ | atra kaṇvaḥ |
saṃvatsareṇa patati patitena samācaran |
yānāsanāśanair nityam ity āhur brahmavādinaḥ || iti |
yājanādau tu yājñavalkya āha |
yājanaṃ yonisaṃbandhaṃ svādhyāyaṃ sahabhojanam |
kṛtvā sadyaḥ pataty eva patitena samācaran || iti | [MDh
sahabhojanam ekasmin pātre |
saṃvatsareṇa patati patitena samācaran |
yājanādhyāpanād yaunān na tu yānāsanāśanāt ||
iti mānavam apy evaṃ vyākhyeyam | yānādibhiḥ saṃvatsareṇa patati na tu yājanādibhiḥ kiṃ tu taiḥ sadya eveti || 21.3 ||
kiṃ punar eṣāṃ patitatvam |
dvijātikarmabhyo hāniḥ patanam || 21.4 ||
dvijātīnāṃ yāni kāryāny [karmāṇy] adhyayanādīni śrautāni gārhyāṇi smārtāni ca tebhyo hānis teṣv anadhikāraḥ || 21.4 ||
tathā paratra cāsiddhiḥ || 21.5 ||
yāny anena prāg arjitāni kuśalāni karmāṇi tāny apy asya paratra na sidhyanti phaladāni na bhavanti | tad etad etābhyāṃ patitaśabdasya nirvacanaṃ kṛtaṃ kartavyebhyaḥ karmabhyaḥ pūrvājitānāṃ sukarmaṇāṃ phalebhyaś ca pātaḥ pracyavanaṃ patitatvam iti || 21.5 ||
tam eke narakam || 21.6 ||
yeyaṃ karmabhyo hānir yā ca paratrāsiddhis tām evaike narakaṃ manyante | narakasāmānādhikaraṇyāt puṃliṅgam ekavacanaṃ ca | karmabhyo hīnasya bandhubhis tyaktasya duḥkham utpadyate paratrāsiddheḥ sukhalavo na bhavaty ato naraka evāyam iti | svamataṃ tu viśiṣṭe deśe duḥkhaikatānasya vāso naraka iti || 21.6 ||
trīṇi prathamāny anirdeśyāny anu || 21.7 ||
anukrāntānāṃ pātakānāṃ madhye prathamāni trīṇi brahmahatyāsurāpānagurutalpagamanāny anirdeśyāny anirdeśyaprāyaścittāni | teṣāṃ prāyaścittam anirdeśyam iti | brahmavadhe manur āha |
kāmato brāhmaṇavadhe niṣkṛtir na vidhīyate || iti | [MDh 11.89]
surāpāne |
matipūrvam anirdeśyaṃ prāṇāntikam iti sthitiḥ | iti | [MDh 11.146]
gurutalpe mṛgyam || 21.7 ||
na strīṣv agurutalpaṃ patatīty eke || 21.8 ||
eke manyante strīṣu pravṛtto gurutalpa eva patito nānyatreti | svayaṃ tv anyatrāpi patatīti | āha manur api |
cāṇḍālāntyastriyo gatva bhuktvā ca pratigṛhya ca |
pataty ajñānato vipro jñānāt sāmyaṃ tu gacchati || [MDh 11.175] iti || 21.8 ||
atha striyāḥ patanahetum āha |
bhrūṇahani hīnavarṇasevāyāṃ ca strī patati || 21.9 ||
bhrūṇaheti bhāvaparam | bhrūṇo garbhaḥ | ātmāpekṣāyāṃ garbhahatyāyāṃ strī patati | yo hīnavarṇo brāhmaṇyāḥ kṣatriyādiḥ kṣatriyāyā vaiśyādir vaiśyāyāḥ śūdras tatsevāyāṃ ca strī patati | cakārāḍ brahmahatyādiṣu ca | apara āha – bhrūṇahanaṃ hīnavarṇaṃ ca yā sevate na tasya bhāryā bhavati sā patati | cakārād brahmahatyādiṣu ceti | bhrūṇagrahaṇaṃ patitopalakṣaṇam || 21.9 ||
kauṭsākṣyaṃ rājagāmi paiśunaṃ guror
anṛtābhiśaṃsanaṃ mahāpātakasamāni || 21.10 ||
kūṭasākṣiṇo bhāvaḥ kauṭasākṣyaṃ sākṣiṇo ‘nṛtavacanam | sato ‘sato vā paradoṣasya khyāpanaṃ paiśunam | rājanīti vaktavye rājagāmīti vacanaṃ yatroktaṃ pāraṃparyeṇāpi rājānaṃ gacchati tad api varjyam ity evamartham | guroḥ pitur ācāryasya vānṛtenāsatyena doṣeṇabhiśaṃsanaṃ duṣṭatākhyāpanaṃ guror anṛtābhiśaṃsanam | etāni [mahā]pātakasamāni | sāmyātideśe prāyaścittam ardham iti smārto vyavahāraḥ || 21.10 ||
apaṅktyānāṃ prāg durvālād gohantṛbrahmaghna-
tanmantrakṛdavakīrṇipatitasāvitrīkeṣūpapātakam
|| 21.11 ||
stenādayo gītaśīlāntā ekapañcāśan na bhojayitavyā ity uktāḥ śrāddhaprakaraṇe ‘paṅktyāḥ | teṣām apapaṅktyānāṃ madhye durvālāt prāg yāvantas tyaktātmaparyantā [cf. GDh 15.18] ekatriṃśat teṣu patitaḥ kūṭasākṣī cāntarbhūtaḥ | tatra patitasya patitatvaṃ kūṭasākṣiṇas tatsāmyam uktam | vyatirikteṣūpapātakaṃ pāpam | nāstiko ‘pi teṣu paṭhitaḥ | sa ca trividhaḥ | yathāhuḥ paurāṇikāḥ |
nāstikā trividhā jñeyā dharmajñais tattvadarśibhiḥ |
kriyāduṣṭo manoduṣṭo vāgduṣṭaś ceti te trayaḥ || iti |
atra vāgduṣṭa upapātako ‘bhipretaḥ | itarayoḥ pātaka eva | gohantā hananaṃ daṇḍakāṣṭhādinā tāḍanam | brahma vedas tam adhītaṃ yaḥ pramādād abhihatavān vismṛtavān sa brahmaghnaḥ | buddhipūrve mānavam |
brahmaghnatvaṃ vedanindā kauṭasākṣyaṃ suhṛdvadhaḥ |
garhitānnādyayor jagdhiḥ surāpānasamāni ṣaṭ || iti |
tanmantrakṛd gohantṛbrahmaghanor yājanādikṛt | yo brahmacārī striyam upeyāt so ‘vakīrṇī | asyāpaṅktyeṣu paṭhitasya punarvacanaṃ kṛte ‘py avakīrṇiprāyaścitte pṛthag upapātakaprāyaścittam api kartavyam ity evamartham | etac cāpatyotpādanaparyantagamane draṣṭavyam | patitasāvitriko yathākālam anupanīto vrātyaḥ | eteṣūpapātakaṃ pāpam iti || 21.11 ||
ajñānād anadhyāpanād ṛtvigācāryau patanīyasevāyāṃ
ca heyau || 21.12 ||
ajñānād anadhyāpanād iti | yadi (yaḥ) karmaṇi pravṛtta ṛtviṅmantrān karmapaddhatiṃ vā na jānāti sa ca, ya ālasyādinā nādhyāpayaty ācāryas tāv ubhau heyau tyājyau | idaṃ patitena saha śayanāsanādeḥ sevāyāṃ prāg apy abdāt parityāgārtham | tarhi saṃvatsareṇa patatīti vacanam anarthakam | na tādṛśas tyāgo ‘tra vivakṣitaḥ | kiṃ tarhy ṛtvigācāryāntaram upādeyam | anupādāne doṣa iti || 21.12 ||
anyatra hānāt patati || 21.13 ||
anyatrājñānād anadhyāpanād anyatra tayos tyāgo na kartavyaḥ | kurvan patati || 21.13 ||
tasya ca pratigrahītety eke || 21.14 ||
tasyartvijam ācāryam īdṛśaṃ tyajataḥ pratigrahītā taṃ yaḥ pratigṛhṇāti yājyatvena śiṣyatvenartvigācāryau vā so ‘pi patatīty eke manyante | eke grahaṇāj jñātvā pratigrahe pātityaṃ nānyatreti || 21.14 ||
na karhicin mātāpitror avṛttiḥ || 21.15 ||
na kasyāṃcid apy avasthāyāṃ mātāpitror avṛttr aśuśrūṣā kartavyā kiṃ tu patitayor api tayor namaskārādikā śuśrūṣā kartavyā | tathā ca āpastambaḥ: “mātā bhūyāṃsi karmāṇy ārabhate tasyāṃ śuśrūṣā nityā patitāyām api” [ĀpDh 1.28.9] iti || 21.15 ||
dāyaṃ tu na bhajeran || 21.16 ||
tadīyaṃ tu dhanaṃ tadabhāve na bhajeran putrādayaḥ | rājagāmi tad bhavati || 21.16 ||
brāhmaṇābhiśaṃsane doṣas tāvān || 21.17 ||
yo brāhmaṇam abhiśaṃsati tasya santaṃ doṣaṃ prathamaṃ khyāpayati tasya doṣas tāvān bhavati yāvān kartur iti | yathāha āpastambaḥ: “doṣaṃ dṛṣṭvā na pūrvaḥ parebhyaḥ patitasya samākhyāne syād varjayet tv enaṃ dharmeṣu” [ĀpDh 1.21.20] iti || 21.17 ||
dvir anenasi || 21.18 ||
anenasi viṣaye doṣābhiśaṃsane dvir doṣo ‘sya bhavati | dvir dviguṇaḥ | atrābhiśaṃsanamātre doṣa uktaḥ | mānave tu |
patitaṃ patitety uktvā coraṃ coreti vā punaḥ |
vacanāt tulayadoṣaḥ syān mithyā dvir doṣabhāg bhavet || iti | [=NSm 15–16.22]
pātityacauryaviṣayam abhiśaṃsanam uktam | vasiṣṭhas tu: “brāhmaṇam anṛtenābhiśaṃsya patanīyenopapatanīyena vā māsam abbhakṣaḥ śuddhavatīr āvartayet pūto bhavati” [VaDh 23.39] iti vijñāyate || 21.18 ||
durbalahiṃsāyāṃ ca vimocane śaktaś cet || 21.19 ||
durbale prabalena hiṃsyamāne yaḥ śaktaḥ san na mocayati tasyāpi tāvān doṣo yāvān hiṃsituḥ || 21.19 ||
abhikruddhāvagoraṇaṃ brāhmaṇasya varṣaśatam
asvargyam || 21.20 ||
yo ‘bhikruddhaḥ san brāhmaṇaṃ prahartuṃ hastam āyudhaṃ vāvagurata udyamya kampayati tasya tadavagoraṇaṃ varṣāṇāṃ śatam asvargyaṃ bhavati svargaprāptiṃ niruṇaddhi | tannimittāni sukṛtāni hantīty arthaḥ | asvargyam iti narakapāto vā lakṣyate | sajātīyaviṣayam idam | vijātīyaviṣaye tu –
dviguṇaṃ triguṇaṃ caiva caturguṇam athāpi ca |
kṣattraviṭśūdrajātīnāṃ brāḥmaṇasya vadhe smṛtam || iti |
anenaiva nyāyena brāhmaṇenāvagoraṇe kṛte tripādyaṃ dvipādyaṃ pādyaṃ pādaś ceti kṣatriyādiṣu draṣṭavyam | evam anyatrāpi pratilomānulomabhedenādharottarabhāve tāratamyaṃ kalpyam || 21.20 ||
nighāte sahasram || 21.21 ||
yaḥ svarṇena hanti tasya varṣasahasram asvargyam | upasamastaṃ varṣapadam apekṣate
|| 21.21 ||
lohitadarśane yāvatas tat praskandya pāṃsūn
saṃgṛhṇīyāt [saṃgṛhṇīyat] || 21.22 ||
yadi tena nighātena lohitam utpādayet tatas tal lohitaṃ praskandya niḥsṛtya yāvataḥ pāṃsūn saṃgṛhṇīyāt piṇḍān kuryāt tāvanti varṣāṇi tad asvargyaṃ bhavati | tasmād brāhmaṇāya nāvagureta na nihanyān na lohitaṃ kuryād iti gamyamānatvād anuktam | [abhyāso ‘dhyāyasamāptyarthaḥ] || 21.22 ||
iti śrīgautamīyavṛttau haradattaviracitāyāṃ mitākṣarāyāṃ
tṛtīyapraśne tṛtīyo ‘dhyāyaḥ
atha dvāviṃśo ‘dhyāyaḥ
evaṃ prāyaścittanimittāny uktāni | atha prāyaścittāny ucyante |
prāyaścittam || 22.1 ||
adhikāro ‘yam | niścitya tapaso ‘nuṣṭhānaṃ prāyaścittam | tathā ca aṅgirāḥ |
prāyo nāma tapaḥ proktaṃ cittaṃ niścaya ucyate |
taponiścayasaṃyogāt prāyaścittam iti smṛtam || iti || 22.1 ||
agnau saktir brahmaghnas trir avacchātasya || 22.2 ||
saktiḥ saṅgaḥ patanam | avacchāto ‘vaśīrṇo bhaktatyāgena kṛśībhūtaḥ | brahmahā bhaktatyāgena kṛśo bhūtvāgnau triḥ pated utthāyotthāya | idam asya prāyaścittam | atra mānavo viśeṣaḥ |
prāsyed ātmānam agnau vā samiddhe trir avākśirāḥ || iti | [MDh 11.73]
kāṭhakaśrutiḥ: “anaśanakarśito ‘gnim ārohet” iti | nedaṃ maraṇāntikaṃ trir iti niyamāt | triḥ patane jīvann api śūdhyatīti || 22.2 ||
lakṣyaṃ vā syāj janye śastrabhṛtām || 22.3 ||
janyaṃ yuddham | śastrabhṛta iṣvāsāḥ | lakṣyam iti vacanād yuddha iṣvāsānām iṣūn asyatāṃ lakṣyaṃ bhūtvā tiṣṭhet | tair viddho jīvan mṛto vā śudhyati | yājñavalkyaḥ |
saṃgrāme vā hato lakṣyabhūtaḥ śuddhim avāpnuyāt |
mṛtakalpaḥ prahārārto jīvann api viśudhyati || [YDh 3.248] iti || 21.3 ||
khaṭvāṅgakapālapāṇir vā dvādaśa saṃvatsarān
brahmacārī bhaikṣāya grāmaṃ praviśet
karmācakṣāṇaḥ || 22.4 ||
khaṭvāṅgaṃ pāśupatānāṃ prasiddham | kapālaṃ svavyāpāditasya brāhmaṇasya śiraḥ kapālam | te pāṇyor yasya sa khaṭvāṅgakapālapāṇiḥ | khaṭvāṅgaṃ dakṣiṇe pāṇau kapālaṃ savye bhikṣārthaṃ pānīyapānārthaṃ bhojanārthaṃ ca | tathā āpastambaḥ: “puruṣaśiraḥ pratipānārtham ādāya khaṭvāṅgaṃ daṇḍārtham” [ĀpDh 1.28.21–29.1] iti | manus tu “kṛtvā śavaśirodhvajam” [MDh 11.72] iti | tasmin pakṣe khaṭvāṅgasyāgre dhvajaḥ | tanmūle śavaśiraḥ | bhikṣācaraṇaṃ tu lohitena khaṇḍaśarāveṇa āpastambīyadarśanāt | evaṃbhūto bhaikṣāya grāmaṃ praviśet | etāvān asya grāme praveśo ‘nyadāraṇye | bhaikṣaṃ ca karmācakṣāṇaś caret |
veśmano dvāri tiṣṭhāmi bhikṣārthī brahmaghātakaḥ |
iti parāśaraḥ | dvādaśa saṃvatsarān evaṃ caran brahmacārī bhavet | strīṣu na prasajet | “bhikṣācaraṇe saptāgārāṇy asaṃkalpitāni” ity āpastambaḥ | saṃvartas tu |
bhikṣāyai praviśed grāmaṃ vanyair yadi na jīvati | iti |
“ekakālāhāraḥ” iti vasiṣṭhaḥ || 22.4 ||
patho ‘pakrāmet saṃdarśanād āryasya || 22.5 ||
āryas traivarṇikas tasmin dṛṣṭe patho ‘pakrāmed apayāyāt | atra vyāghraḥ |
cāṇḍālaṃ patitaṃ dṛṣṭvā dūrataḥ parivarjayet |
govālavyajanād arvāk sacailaṃ snānam ācaret || iti |
śūdro ‘pi sparśaṃ varjayet | yathāha laugākṣiḥ |
mahāpātakisaṃsparśe varṇānāṃ snānam ucyate |
asnātvā bhojane caiva saptarātraṃ samāviśet ||
trirātraṃ syād amatyā cec chaṅkhapuṣpīśṛtaṃ payaḥ |
evam ārtavicaṇḍālaśavānām api kīrtayet || iti || 22.5 ||
sthānāsanābhyāṃ viharan savaneṣūdakopasparśī
śudhyet || 22.6 ||
“tiṣṭhed ahani rātrāv āsīta” [GDh 26.6] yathāśakti prātar madhaṃdine sāyam iti svaneṣu trisaṃdhyam udakopasparśī syāt snāyāt | evaṃ dvādaśa varṣāṇi carann ante śudhyet | snānavidhānād eva tadantarbhūtamantrādiprāptir iti gamyate | śucinā kartavyam iti ca sarvakarmasādhāraṇam | ataḥ saṃdhyopāsanam apy asya bhavati,
saṃdhyāhīno ‘śucir nityam anarhaḥ sarvakarmasu |
yat kiṃcit kurute karma na tasya phalabhāg bhavet ||
iti dakṣasmaraṇāt | dvijātikarmabhyo hāniḥ patanam ity anena tu prāyaścittavratacaryānaṅgabhūtānāṃ karmaṇāṃ hānir na sarveṣām | atra ca yasya dve brahmahatye, sa caturviṃśativarṣāṇi vrataṃ caret | yasya tisraḥ, sa ṣaṭtriṃśataṃ na punar dehakālakartraikyāt prāyaścittasya tantratā | yasya catasro, na tasyeha loke niṣkṛtiḥ | etad eva vratam ottamād ucchvāsāc caret | tathā ca manuḥ |
vidheḥ prāthamikād asmād dvitīye dviguṇaṃ caret |
tṛtīye triguṇaṃ proktaṃ caturthe nāsti niṣkṛtiḥ || iti |
yājñavalkyaḥ |
dviguṇaṃ savanasthe tu brāhmaṇe vratam ādiśet || [YDh 3.252] iti || 22.6 ||
prāṇalābhe vā tannimitte brāhmaṇasya || 22.7 ||
yadi coravyāghrādibhiḥ pramāpyamāṇasya brāhmaṇasya tannimittaḥ prāṇalābho bhavati tadā śudhyet | ekasya chinnāḥ prāṇāḥ aparasya dattāḥ ko nv atra viśeṣaḥ | anenaiva nyāyena sarveṣām eva hanane tajjātīyasya taddhetuke prāṇalābhe śuddhir draṣṭavyā || 22.7 ||
dravyāpacaye tryavaraṃ pratirāddhaḥ || 22.8 ||
brāhmaṇasyeti vartate | brāhmaṇasya dravye corādibhir apacīyamāne ‘pahriyamāṇe tasya pratyānayanāya corādisamīpaṃ gatas taiḥ śastrādibhiḥ kṣato varji(to ‘varji)taḥ sakṛt, punaḥ punaś caivaṃ trivārān nyānaṃ (trivāranyūnaṃ) pratirāddho ‘bhiyuktaḥ sann apratyānīte ‘pi dravye śudhyet | pratyānīte tu sakṛt prayoge ‘pi śudhyet |
tryavaraṃ pratirāddho vā sarvasvam avajitya ca | [MDh 11.80]
iti manuḥ | anenaiva nyāyena svadravyapradānenāpi śuddhir jñeyā | tathā ca manuḥ |
sarvasvaṃ vā vedavide brāhmaṇāyopapādayet |
dhanaṃ vā jīvanāyālaṃ gṛhaṃ vā saparicchadam || [MDh 11.76] iti |
yājñavalkyas tu |
pātre dhanaṃ vā paryāptaṃ datvā śuddhim avāpnuyāt |
ādātuś ca viśuddhyartham iṣṭir vaiśvānarī smṛtā || [YDh 3.250] iti || 22.8 ||
aśvamedhāvabhṛthe vā || 22.9 ||
snātveti śeṣaḥ | parakīyasyāśvamedhasyāvabhṛte svayaṃ snātvā vā śudhyet | prāṇalābha ityādisūtreṣu vāśabdo vikalpārthaḥ | atra mānavo viśeṣaḥ |
śiṣṭvā vā bhūmidevānāṃ naradevasamāgame |
svam eno ‘vabhṛte snātvā hayamedhe vimucyate || [MDh 11.82]
bhūmidevā brāhmaṇā ṛtvijaḥ | naradevo rājā yajamānaḥ | teṣāṃ samavāye svam enaḥ śiṣṭvā vikhyāpya || 22.9 ||
anyayajñe ‘py agniṣṭudantaś cet || 22.10 ||
aśvamedhād anyayajñe ‘py avabhṛte snātvā śudhyet | kim aviśeṣeṇa | na | tasya ced antar madhye ‘gniṣṭunnāmaikāho bhavati | pañcadaśarātrāder grahaṇam | apara āha – agniṣṭudanto ‘gniṣṭutsamāptiko bhavatīti | atra pakṣe sarvamedhāder grahaṇam | atra ca śudhyed iti dvādaśavārṣikam upasaṃhṛtya vidhānād vāgrahaṇāc ca sarvāṇy etāni svatantrāṇi vaikalpikāni prayojakāni prayojakādiviṣayāṇi vā draṣṭavyāni | anye tu dvādaśavārṣikapravṛttasyety āhuḥ | tathā ca śaṅkhaḥ “dvādaśe varṣe śuddhim āpnoti” ity abhidhāyāha: “antarāle vā brāhmaṇaṃ mocayitvā gavāṃ vā dvādaśānāṃ paritrāṇāt” iti | vāśabdas tu parasparāpekṣayā vikalpārthaḥ
|| 22.10 ||
sṛṣṭaś ced brāhmaṇavadhe ‘hatvāpi || 22.11 ||
sarga utsāho niścayaś ca | tadvān sṛṣṭaḥ | yadi brāhmaṇavadhe sṛṣṭo bhavati kenacid daivād vānivāritaḥ so ‘hatvāpi brahmahā bhavati | atas tasyāpy anantarokteṣu prāyaścitteṣu yal laghu tad bhavati || 22.11 ||
ātreyyāś caivam || 22.12 ||
“ṛtusnātām ātreyīm āhuḥ | tatra yad apatyaṃ bhavati” [VaDh 20.35–36] iti vasiṣṭhaḥ | tasyām api brāhmaṇyāṃ hatāyām evaṃ brahmahā bhavatīti tadīyam eva prāyaścittam iti | kṣatriyādyātreyīvadhe tattatpuruṣavadhanimittaṃ prāyaścittam | anye tv agtrigotrām ātreyīm āhuḥ || 22.12 ||
garbhe cāvijñāte || 22.13 ||
brāhmaṇasya garbhe strīpuṃnapuṃsakatvenāvijñāte brāhmaṇyām āhita auṣadhādinā hate brahmahatyāprāyaścittam | vijñāte tu yathaliṅgam | kṣatriyādigarbhe ‘pi tadanuguṇam | rājanyavaiśyayor api savanaṃ gatayor vadha etad eva | yathāha manuḥ |
hatvā garbham avijñātam etad eva vrataṃ caret |
rājanyavaiśyabījānāṃ cātreyīm api ca striyam || iti | [MDh 11.87]
atra parāśaraḥ |
cāturvidyopapannas tu vidhivad brahmaghātake |
samudrasetugamanaṃ prāyaścittaṃ vinirdiśet ||
setubandhapathe bhikṣāṃ cāturvarṇyāt samāharet |
varjayitvā vikarmasthāṃś chatropānahavarjitaḥ ||
ahaṃ duṣkṛtakarmā vai mahāpātakakārakaḥ |
veśmano dvāri tiṣṭhāmi bhikṣārthī brahmaghātakaḥ ||
gokuleṣu ca goṣṭheṣu grāmeṣu nagareṣu ca |
tapovaneṣu tīrtheṣu nadīprasravaṇeṣu ca ||
eteṣu khyāpayed enaḥ puṇyaṃ gatvā tu sāgaram |
brahmahā vipramucyeta snātvā tasmin mahodadhau ||
tataḥ pūto gṛhaṃ prāpya kṛtvā brāhmaṇabhojanam |
gavāṃ cāpi śataṃ datvā cāturvaidyāya dakṣiṇām ||
evaṃ viśuddhim āpnoti cāturvarṇyānumoditaḥ || iti | [PārSm 12.65–69]
atra sumantuḥ: “brahmahā saṃvatsaraṃ kṛcchraṃ cared adhaḥśāyī triṣavaṇī karmāvedako bhikṣāhāro divyanadīpulinasaṃgamāśramagoṣṭhaparvataprasravaṇatapovanavihārī syān na vīrāsanī | saṃvatsare pūrṇe hiraṇyamaṇigodhānyatilabhūmisarpīṃṣi brāhmaṇebhyo dadyāt pūto bhavati” iti | atra varṇaviśeṣa āśramaviśeṣe ca aṅgirāḥ |
parṣad yā brāhmaṇānāṃ tu sā rājñāṃ dviguṇā matā |
vaiśyānāṃ triguṇā proktā parṣadvac ca vrataṃ smṛtam ||
gṛhasthokāni pāpāni kurvanty āśramiṇo yadi |
śaucavac chodanaṃ kuryād arvāg brahmanidarśanāt || iti
etac chaucaṃ gṛhasthānāṃ dviguṇaṃ brahmacāriṇām |
triguṇaṃ syād vanasthānāṃ yatīnāṃ ca caturguṇam || iti |
atra bhārgavaḥ |
aśītir yasya varṣāṇi bālo vāpy ūnaṣōḍaśaḥ |
prāyaścittārtham arhanti striyo vyādhita eva vā || iti |
hārītaḥ |
prāyaścitte pravṛttas tu madhye yadi vipadyate |
śuddhas tad ahar evāsāv iha loke paratra ca || iti ca || 22.13 ||
uktaṃ brāhmaṇavadhe prāyaścittam | atha rājanyavadhe |
rājanyavadhe ṣaḍvārṣikaṃ prākṛtaṃ brahmacaryam
rṣabhaikasahasrāś ca gā dadyāt || 22.14 ||
rājanyavadhe kṛte brāhmaṇavadhe yad uktaṃ brahmacaryaṃ tat ṣaḍvārṣikaṃ kartavyam | tad api prākṛtaṃ svābhāvikaṃ khaṭvāṅgādirahitaṃ brahmahatyāprāyaścittaṃ ṣaḍ varṣāni kuryāt | ekādhikaṃ sahasram ṛṣabha ekasahasrāṃ yāsāṃ tā ṛṣabhaikasahasrā gā dadyāt | atra uśanā: “rājanyavadhe ṣaḍvārṣikaṃ brahmavrataṃ tasyānta ṛṣabhaikasahasragodānaṃ ca” iti | idam abhiṣiktasya śrotriyasya vratavato buddhipūrvavadhe | tasyaivāśrotriyasya vratahīnasya vadhe kevalaṃ godānam | ubhayahīnasyānabhiṣiktasya vadhe kevalaṃ ṣaḍvārṣikam | anabhiṣiktasya tūbhayavato vāsiṣṭham: “brāhmaṇo rājanyaṃ hatvāṣṭau varṣāṇi vrataṃ caret” iti | eteṣām evābuddhipūrve ‘rdhaṃ kalpyam | jātimātravadhe,
strīśūdraviṭkṣatravadho nāśtikyaṃ copapātakam | [MDh 11.66]
ity uktvā,
etad eva vrataṃ kuryur upapātakino dvijāḥ |
avakīrṇivarjaṃ śuddhyarthaṃ cāndrāyaṇam athāpi vā || [MDh 11.117]
iti manunoktaṃ draṣṭavyam | kiṃcidguṇavato vadhe “agnyutsādinirākṛtyupapātakeṣu caivam” [GDh 22.34] iti vakṣyamāṇaṃ sāṃvatsarikaṃ prākṛtaṃ brahmacaryam | evam uttaratrāpi draṣṭavyam | sarvatrābuddhipūrve ‘rdhaṃ buddhipūrve kṛtsnam iti || 22.14 ||
atha vaiśyavadhe |
vaiśye tu traivārṣikam ṛṣabhaikaśatāś ca gā
dadyāt || 22.15 ||
idam atyantaguṇavato buddhipūrvavadhe | etena paraṃ vyākhyātam || 22.15 ||
śūdre saṃvatsaram ṛṣabhaikādaśāś ca gā
dadyāt || 22.16 ||
idam apy atyantaguṇavadviṣayam | atyantanirguṇasya śūdrasya vadha auśanasam: “śūdraṃ hatvā taptakṛcchram” iti | athānulomaviṣaye vyāghraḥ |
sarveṣām anulomānāṃ tanmātrahanane tu yat |
tad eva nirdiśed vidvān strīṇām ardhaṃ tathaiva ca ||
ātreyīhanane brūyād yad bhartur upadiśyate |
garbhe caiva tathā jñāte vyāghrasya vacanaṃ yathā || iti |
pratilomavadhe laugākṣiḥ |
hanane pratilomānāṃ śūdrajānāṃ kathaṃ bhavet |
jñānapūrve parākaḥ syād ajñāne tv aindavaṃ smṛtam ||
itareṣāṃ caturbhāgaṃ pitur uktaṃ manīṣibhiḥ || iti || 22.16 ||
anātreyyāṃ caivam || 22.17 ||
ātreyīvyatiriktāyā vadhe caivaṃ śūdre saṃvatsaram ṛṣabhaikādaśāś ca gā dadyād iti | idaṃ brāhmaṇyāś cāritravatyāḥ kuṭumbinyā buddhipūrvavadhe | yājñavalkyaś ca |
apraduṣṭāṃ striyaṃ hatvā śūdrahatyāvrataṃ caret || iti | [YDh 3.269]
ṣaṇmāsāñ śūdrahāpy etad dhenur dadyād daśātha vā || iti | [YDh 3.267]
durvṛttā brahmaviṭkṣatraśūdrayoṣāḥ pramāpya tu |
dṛtiṃ dhanur bastam aviṃ kramād dadyād viśuddhaye || iti | [YDh 3.268]
yat tu hārītenoktam: “ṣaḍvarṣāṇi rājanye prākṛtaṃ brahmacaryam | vaiśye trīṇi | sārdham abdaṃ śūdre, kṣatriyavad brāhmaṇeṣu, vaiśyavat kṣatriyāyām, śūdravad vaiśyāyām, śūdrāṃ hatvā nava māsān” iti tad atyantotkṛṣṭācāryādiviṣayam || 22.17 ||
gāṃ ca vaiśyavat || 22.18 ||
gāṃ ca hatvā vaiśyavadhe yat prāyaścittaṃ “vaiśyavadhe traivāṣikam ṛṣabhaikaśatāś ca gā dadyāt” [GDh 22.15] iti tac caret | idaṃ vṛttasvādhyāyavato durgatasya bahukuṭumbasya yā gaur bahukṣīrā taruṇī tasyā buddhipūrvavadhe | tādṛśyā ekaphalāyā garbhiṇyāḥ karmāṅgabhūtāyā vadhe yāmyam |
gosahasraṃ śataṃ vāpi dadyāt sucaritavrataḥ |
avidyamāne sarvasvaṃ vedavidbhyo nivedayet || iti |
dvaimāsikaṃ vratam atra prakṛtam | atraiva buddhipūrve kātyāyanīyaṃ godānarahitaṃ traivārṣikam |
goghnas taccarmasaṃvīto vased goṣṭhe ‘tha vā punaḥ |
gāś cānugacchet satataṃ mauñjīcīrājinādibhiḥ ||
varṣaśītātapakleśavahnipaṅkabhayārditaḥ |
mokṣayet sarvayatnena pūyate vatsarais tribhiḥ || iti |
vasiṣṭhaḥ: “gāṃ ced dhanyāt tasyāś carmaṇārdreṇa pariveṣṭitaḥ ṣaṇmāsān kṛcchraṃ taptakṛcchraṃ vā tiṣṭhed ṛṣabhavehatau ca dadyātām” [VaDh 21.18, 22] iti | vehad vṛṣbhopahatā gauḥ | dadyātām iti karmaṇi kartṛpratyayaḥ | yājñāvalkyaḥ |
pañcagavyaṃ piban goghno māsam āsīta saṃyataḥ |
goṣṭheśayo go’nugāmī gopradānena śudhyati ||
kṛcchraṃ caivātikṛcchraṃ ca cared vāpi samāhitaḥ |
dadyāt trirātraṃ copoṣya vṛṣabhaikādaśās tu gāḥ || iti | [YDh 3.263–264]
jābālaḥ |
prājāpatyaṃ caren māsaṃ gohantā ced akāmataḥ |
gohito go’nugāmī syād gopradānena śudhyati || iti |
viṣṇuḥ |
goghnasya pañcagavyena māsam ekaṃ palatrayam |
pratyahaṃ syāt parāko vā cāndrāyaṇam athāpi vā || iti |
kāśyapaḥ: “gāṃ hatvā taccarmaṇā parivṛto māsaṃ goṣṭheśayas triṣavaṇasnāyī nityaṃ pañcagavyāhāraḥ” iti | śātātapaḥ: “māsaṃ pañcagavyāhāraḥ” iti | śaṅkha-pracetasau: “goghnaḥ pañcagavyāhāraḥ | pañcaviṃśatirātram upavaset saśikhaṃ vapanaṃ kṛtvā gocarmaṇā parivṛto gāś cānugacched goṣṭheśayo gāṃ ca dadyāt” iti | paiṭhīnasiḥ: “goghno māsaṃ yavāgūṃ prasṛtataṇḍulaśṛtāṃ bhuñjāno gobhyaḥ priyaṃ kurvañ śudhyati” iti | manuḥ |
upapātakasaṃyukto goghno bhuñjīta yāvakam |
kṛtavāpo vased goṣṭhe carmaṇārdreṇa saṃvṛtaḥ ||
caturthakālam aśnīyād akṣāralavaṇaṃ mitam |
gomūtreṇācaret snānaṃ dvau māsau niyatendriyaḥ || [MDh 11.108–109]
ity ārabhya,
anena vidhinā yas tu goghno gā anugacchati |
sa gohatyākṛtaṃ pāpaṃ tribhir māsair vyapohati ||
ṛṣabhaikādaśā gāś ca dadyāt sucaritavratam || iti | [MDh 11.115–116]
sumantuḥ: “goghnasya gopradānaṃ goṣṭhe śayanaṃ dvādaśarātraṃ pañcagavyaprāśanaṃ gavām anugamanaṃ ca” iti | saṃvartaḥ |
saktuyāvakabhaikṣāśī payo dadhi ghṛtaṃ śakṛt |
etāni kramaśo ‘śnīyān māsārdhaṃ susamāhitaḥ ||
brāhmaṇān bhojayitvā tu gāṃ dadyād ātmaśuddhaye || iti |
bṛhaspatiḥ: “dvādaśarātraṃ pañcagavyāhāraḥ” iti | eteṣāṃ buddhipūrvābuddhipūrvabhedena brāhmaṇādiparigraheṇa yathārhaṃ viṣayavibhāga ūhitavyaḥ | ṣaṭtriṃaṇmate viśeṣaḥ |
pāda utpannamātre tu dvau pādau dṛḍhatāṃ gate |
pādonaṃ vratam ādiṣṭaṃ hatvā garbham acetanam ||
aṅgapratyaṅgasaṃpūrṇe garbhe cetaḥsamanvite |
dviguṇaṃ govrataṃ kuryād eṣā goghnasya niṣkṛtiḥ ||
bṛhatpracetāḥ |
ekavarṣe hate vatse kṛcchrapādo vidhīyate |
abuddhipūrve puṃsaḥ syād dvipādas tu dvihāyane ||
trihāyane tripādaṃ syāt prājāpatyam ataḥ param || iti |
smṛtyantaram |
ativṛddhām atikṛśām atibhālāṃ ca rogiṇīm |
hatvā pūrvavidhānena cared ardhavrataṃ dvijaḥ ||
brāhmaṇān bhojayec chaktyā dadyād dhematilāṃs tathā || iti |
saṃvarta-āpastambau |
ekā ced bahubhiḥ kaiścid daivād dvyāpāditā kvacit |
pādaṃ pādaṃ tu hatyāyāś careyus te pṛthak pṛthak ||
vyāpannānāṃ bahūnāṃ tu rodhane bandhane ‘pi vā |
bhiṣaṅ mithyopacāre ca dviguṇaṃ govrataṃ caret || iti |
bahūnām api vyāpādane dviguṇam eva vacanabalāt | na tu pratinimittaṃ nauprettikāvṛttiḥ (?) |
vyāsaḥ |
auṣadhaṃ lavaṇaṃ caiva puṇyārtham api bhojanam |
atiriktaṃ na dātavyaṃ kāle svalpaṃ tu dāpayet ||
atirikte vipattiś cet kṛcchrapādo vidhiyate || iti |
āpastambaḥ |
pāṣāṇair laguḍair vāpi śastrair vānyena vā balāt |
nighātayanti ye gās tu tasmin kuryur vrataṃ hi te ||
pādam ekaṃ cared rodhe dvau pādau bandhane caret |
yojane pādahīnaṃ syāc caret sarvaṃ nipātane || iti |
vasiṣṭhaḥ |
na nālikereṇa na śāṇavālair na cāpi mauñjena na vardhraśṛṅkhalaiḥ |
etais tu gāvo na nibandhanīyā baddhvānutiṣṭhet paraśuṃ pragṛhya || iti || 22.18 ||
maṇḍūkanakulakākabimbadaharamūṣakaśvahiṃsāsu
ca || 22.19 ||
bimbaḥ kāmarūpī kṛkalāsaḥ | daharaḥ svalpakāyo mūṣakaḥ | chucchundarīty eke | anye prasiddhāḥ | eteṣāṃ samuditānāṃ vadhe vaiśyavat prāyaścittam | idaṃ buddhipūrvābhyāsaviṣayam | anyatra āpastambīyam: “vāyasabakabalākabarhiṇacakravāka-haṃsabhāsamaṇḍūkanakulasairikāśvahiṃsāyāṃ śūdravat prāyaścittam” [ĀpDh 1.25.13] iti | manur api |
mārjāranakulau hatvā cāṣamaṇḍūkam eva ca |
śvagodholūkakākāṃś ca śūdrahatyāvrataṃ caret || iti | [MDh 11.131]
pratyekaṃ vadhe tu buddhipūrve |
mārjāragodhānakulamaṇḍūkaśvapatatriṇaḥ |
hatvā tryahaṃ pibet kṣīraṃ kṛcchraṃ vā pādikaṃ caret || [YDh 3.270]
iti yājñavalkyoktaṃ draṣṭavyam | buddhipūrve mānavam |
payaḥ pibet trirātraṃ vā yojanaṃ vādhvano vrajet || [MDh 11.132] iti || 22.19 ||
athanvatāṃ sahasraṃ hatvā || 22.20 ||
asthimatāṃ kṛkalāsādīnāṃ sahasraṃ hatvā vaiśyavat prāyaścittam || 22.20 ||
anasthimatām anaḍudbhāre ca || 22.21 ||
ye ‘sthimanto na bhavanti daṃśamaśakādayas teṣāṃ yāvato ‘naḍvān bhartuṃ śaknoti tāvato hatvā vaiśyavat prāyaścittam | idaṃ dvayam api pūrvābhyāsaviṣayam | anyatra
yājñavalkyoktam |
asthanvatāṃ sahasraṃ tu tathānasthimatām anaḥ | [YDh 3.269]
śūdrahatyāvrataṃ ṣāṇmāsikaṃ prakṛtaṃ daśa dhenūr vā dadyāt iti ca || 22.21 ||
api vāsthanvatām ekaikasmin kiṃcid dadyāt || 22.22 ||
api veti vikalpe | asthanvatāṃ yāvanto hatāḥ sahasram ūrdhvam arvāg vā tāvataḥ saṃkhyāya pratyekaṃ kiṃcit kiṃcid dadyāt | idaṃ cāsthimatsu prāyaścittaṃ pūrvkaṃ veti |
aṣṭamuṣṭi bhavet kiṃcit kiṃcid aṣṭau tu puṣkalam |
puṣkalāni tu catvāri āḍhakaḥ parikīrtitaḥ ||
caturāḍhako bhaved droṇa iti mānasya lakṣaṇam ||
iti smṛtiḥ | anasthimatāṃ tu tāvantah prāṇāyāmāḥ | tathā ca manuḥ |
kiṃcid eva tu viprāya dadyād asthimātāṃ vadhe |
anasthnāṃ caiva hiṃsāyāṃ prāṇāyāmena śudhyati || [MDh 11.141] iti || 22.22 ||
ṣaṇḍhe palālabhāraḥ sīsamāṣaś ca || 22.23 ||
yaṃ prati devala āha |
ṣaṇḍho yo hīnaliṅgaḥ syāt saṃskārārhaś ca naiva saḥ |
iti, tasmin hate puruṣabāhyaḥ palālabhāraḥ sīsamāṣaś cety ubhayaṃ militaṃ deyaṃ buddhipūrve | itaratra tv ekaikam | sīsaṃ lohaviśeṣo rajatasadṛśaḥ kṣaṇadrutiḥ | māṣapramāṇaṃ pūrvam eva vyākhyātam | atra ca na kvāpi smṛtau jāviviśeṣaḥ śrūyate, ṣaṇḍhaḥ ṣaṇḍhaka ity etāvad eva śrūyate | tatra yathā jāvisamavāye ‘pi brāhmaṇādiprayuktaḥ saṃskāro na bhavati tathā tadvadhanimittaṃ prāyaścittam api na bhavati yāvad uktam eva tu bhavati | anye mṛgapakṣiviṣayaṃ manyante | mṛgeṣu pakṣiṣu ca ye ṣaṇḍhās teṣu hateṣv iti || 22.23 ||
varāhe ghṛtaghaṭaḥ || 22.24 ||
varāhe hate ghṛtapūrṇo ghaṭo deyaḥ || 22.24 ||
sarpe lohadaṇḍaḥ || 22.25 ||
sarpe hate lohadaṇḍo deyaḥ | lohaśabdena kārṣṇāyasam ucyate,
abhrīṃ kārṣṇāyasīṃ dadyāt sarpaṃ hatvā dvijottamaḥ | [MDh 11.133]
iti mānave darśanāt | “sarpaṃ hatvā māṣaṃ dadyāt” ity auśanasaṃ buddhipūrvaviṣayam
|| 22.25 ||
brahmabandhvāṃ calanāyāṃ nīlaḥ || 22.26 ||
jātimātrabrāhmaṇī brahmabandhūḥ | calanā vyabhicāriṇī | tasyāṃ hatāyāṃ nīlo deyaḥ | nīlo vṛṣa iti | manus tu varṇānupūrvyam āha |
nīlakārmukabastāvīḥ pṛthag dadyād viśuddhaye |
caturṇām api varṇānāṃ nārīr hatvānavasthitāḥ || [MDh 11.138] iti || 22.26 ||
vaiśikena kiṃcit || 22.27 ||
abhigacchati yā nārī puruṣair bahubhir mithaḥ |
vyabhicāriṇīti sā jñeyā pratyakṣaṃ gāṇiketi ca ||
iti prajāpatiḥ | vaiśikena veśyākarmaṇā jīvantyāṃ brahmabandhvāṃ hatāyāṃ kiṃcid deyam “aṣṭamuṣṭi bhavet kiṃcit” ity etat || 22.27 ||
talpānnadhanalābhavadheṣu pṛthag varṣāṇi || 22.28 ||
talpaśabdena śayanavācinā bhāryā lakṣyate | annaṃ kṛtānnam | dhanaṃ suvarṇādi | eteṣāṃ lābhasya vadhe vighna eṣu labhyamāneṣu doṣopanyāsādinā yo hanti sa pṛthak pratinimittaṃ bhedena saṃvatsaraṃ prākṛtaṃ brahmacaryaṃ caret | “kanyānnadhanavighne prājāpatyam” ity auśanasam buddhipūrvaviṣayam | brāhmaṇalābhaviṣayam idam | kṣatriyādiṣv ardham ardham
|| 22.28 ||
dve paradāre || 22.29 ||
paradāragamane dve varṣe prākṛtaṃ brahmacaryam | ṛtukālagamane buddhipūrva idam | akāminaḥ punar etad evārdhakḷptyā yojyam || 22.29 ||
trīṇi śrotriyasya || 22.30 ||
pūrvokta eva viṣaye śrotriyasya brāhmaṇasya dārān gacchatas trīṇi varṣāṇi brahmacaryam | atrāpy akāmato ‘rdham | atra śaṅkhaḥ: “vaiśyāyām avakīrṇaḥ saṃvatsaraṃ brahmacaryaṃ triṣavaṇaṃ cānutiṣṭhet | kṣatriyāyāṃ dve varṣe | trīṇi brāhmaṇyām | vaiśyāvac ca śūdrāyāṃ brāhmaṇapariṇītāyām” iti | saṃvartaḥ |
śūdrāṃ tu brāhmaṇo gatvā māsaṃ māsārdham eva vā |
gomūtrayāvakāhāras tiṣṭhet tatpāpamokṣakaḥ || iti |
kāmato māsam akāmato ‘rdhamāsam iti vyavasthito vikalpaḥ | anṛtukāle tu brāhmaṇyādidvijātiṣu mānavāni traimāsikadvaimāsikacāndrāyaṇāni | kṣatriyādīnāṃ ca kṣatriyādistrīṣu brāhmaṇavat prāyaścittam | atra uśanā |
gamane tu vrataṃ yat syād garbhe taddviguṇaṃ caret || iti || 22.30 ||
dravyalābhe cotsargaḥ || 22.31 ||
yadi ca parastrīto yat kiṃcid dravyaṃ labdhaṃ tasyotsargas tyāgaḥ kāryaḥ || 22.31 ||
yathāsnānaṃ vā gamayet || 22.32 ||
yatra snāne labdhaṃ tad vā gamayet || 22.32 ||
pratiṣiddhamantrayoge sahasravākaś cet || 22.33 ||
mantrayoge ye pratiṣiddhāḥ patitādayas taiḥ saha mantrayoge ‘dhyayanādhyāpanayājyayājanalakṣaṇe saṃvatsaraṃ prākṛtaṃ brahmacaryaṃ sa cen mantrayogaḥ sahasravāko bhavati | vaktīti vākaḥ padam | sahasrapadaś cet | abuddhipūrva idam | buddhipūrve tu patitatvaṃ syād iti | upapātake tu vāsiṣṭham: “patitacaṇḍālaśavasūtakaśravaṇe tu trirātraṃ vāgyatā āsīran sahasrāvaraṃ vā tad abhyasyantaḥ pūtā bhavantīti vijñāyate | etenaiva garhitādhyāpakayājakā vyākhyātāḥ | dakṣiṇātyāgāc ca pūtā bhavantīti vijñāyate” [VaDh 23.34–36] iti | anye tu sahādhyayanaṃ sahayajanaṃ ca mantrayogaṃ vyācakṣate || 22.33 ||
agnyutsādinirākṛtyupapātakeṣu caivam || 22.34 ||
agnim utsādayituṃ śīlam asyeti buddhipūrvam agnyutsādī | nirākṛtiḥ śaktau satyām anadhyetā | upapātakāni, apaṅktyānāṃ prāg durvālād gohantṛbrahamahatyādīni vyākhyātāni [GDh 21.11] | eṣv agnyutsādyādiṣv evaṃ saṃvatsaraṃ brahmacaryam iti | yo nāstikyād deśopaplavādinā vāgnīn apavidhyati punas tacchāntāv api bahukālaṃ nādhatte tadviṣayam idam | tatraivālpakāle vāsiṣṭham: “yo ‘gnīn apavidhyāt kṛcchraṃ dvādaśarātraṃ caritvā punar ādadhīta” [VaDh 21.27] | ālasyena tyajato mānavam |
agnihotry apavidhyāgnīn brāhmaṇaḥ kāmakārataḥ |
cāndrāyaṇaṃ caren māsaṃ vīrahatyāsamaṃ hi tat || iti | [MDh 11.41]
māsam apavidhyety anvayaḥ |
agnihotry apavidhyāgīn māsād ūrdhvaṃ tu kāmataḥ |
kṛcchraṃ cāndrāyaṇaṃ caiva kuryād atrāvicārayan || iti |
māsād arvāg api cāndrāyaṇam icchanti | smārte tv agnau,
yo ‘gniṃ tyajati nāstikyāt prājāpatyaṃ cared dvijaḥ |
anyatra punarādhānaṃ dānam eva tathaiva ca || iti |
mānavaṃ tu |
ṣaṣṭhānnakālatā māsaṃ saṃhitājapa eva ca |
homaś ca śākalair nityam apaṅktyānāṃ viśodhanam || [MDh 11.200] iti || 22.34 ||
strī yāticāriṇī guptā piṇḍaṃ tu labheta || 22.35 ||
yā strī bhartāram aticarati vyabhicarati puruṣāntareṇa saṃgacchate sā caitad eva prāyaścittaṃ kuryāt saṃvatsaraṃ brahmacaryam | sā ca yāvat samāpyate prāyaścittaṃ tāvad guptā satī piṇḍamātraṃ labhate | buddhipūrve sakṛdgamana idam | anyatra,
yat puṃsaḥ paradāreṣu tac caitāṃ cārayed vratam | [MDh 11.176]
ity etat | sajātīyaviṣaye cedam | brāhmaṇyāḥ kṣatriyaviṣaye vāsiṣṭham: “vyavāye saṃvatsaraṃ ghṛtapaṭaṃ dhārayed gomayakardame kuśaprastare vā bhuñjānādhaḥ śayītordhaṃ saṃvatsarād apsu nimagnāyāḥ sāvitryaṣṭasahasreṇa śirobhir juhuyāt” [VaDh 21.8] iti | vaiśyaviṣaye tv auśanasam: “vyabhicāriṇī kṛcchrābdaṃ caret” iti | atra bṛhatprecetāḥ |
viprāḥ śūdreṇa saṃpṛktā na caitasmāt prasūyate |
prāyaścittaṃ smṛtaṃ tasyāḥ kṛcchraṃ cāndrāyaṇatrayam ||
cāndrāyaṇe dve kṛcchraṃ ca viprāyā vaiśyasaṃgame |
kṛcchracāndrāyaṇe syātāṃ tasyāḥ kṣatriyasaṃgame ||
kṣatriyā śūdrasaṃparke kṛcchre cāndrāyaṇadvayam |
cāndrāyaṇaṃ sakṛcchraṃ ca cared vaiśyena saṃgatā ||
śūdraṃ gatvā cared vaiśyā kṛcchraṃ cāndrāyaṇottaram |
ānulomyena kurvīta kṛcchraṃ pādāvaropitam || iti |
prajātāyā brāhmaṇyāś caturviṃśatimate viśeṣaḥ |
vipragarbhe parākaḥ syāt kṣatriyasya tathaindavam |
aindavaṃ ca parākaś ca vaiśyasyākāmakārataḥ ||
śūdragarbhe bhavet tyāgaś caṇḍālo jāyate yataḥ |
garbhasrāve dhātudoṣaiś carec cāndrāyaṇatrayam || iti |
kāmakāre punaḥ parākādikaṃ dviguṇaṃ kuryāt | vasiṣṭhas tu |
brāhmaṇakṣatriyaviśāṃ bhāryāḥ śūdreṇa saṃgatāḥ |
aprajātā viśudhyanti prāyaścittena netarāḥ || [VaDh 21.12]
āhitapatigarbhāyās tu paścāc chūdrādisaṃgame |
antarvatnī tu yā nārī sametākramya kāminā |
prāyaścittaṃ na sā kuryād yāvad garbho na niḥsṛtaḥ ||
jāte garbhe vrataṃ paścāt kuryān māsaṃ tu yāvakam |
na garbhadoṣas tatrāsti saṃskāryaḥ sa yathāvidhi ||
iti smṛtyantaroktaṃ draṣṭavyam | yā tu dauḥśīlyāt prāyaścittaṃ na karoti tadā,
prātilomye vadhaḥ puṃsāṃ strīṇāṃ nāsādikartanam | [YDh 2.286]
ity etad bhavati |
hīnavarṇopabhuktā yā sāṅkyā vadhyātha vā bhavet |
iti parāśaraḥ | aṅkanaṃ puṃliṅgena || 22.35 ||
amānuṣīṣu govarjaṃ strīkṛte kūṣmāṇḍair ghṛtahomo
ghṛtahomaḥ || 22.36 ||
govarjitāsv amānuṣīṣu mahiṣādistrīṣu strīkṛte maithuna ācarite kūṣmāṇḍair ghṛtahomaḥ kartavyaḥ | govarjam iti vacanaṃ vispaṣṭārtham | vakṣyati “gavi ca gurutalpasamaḥ” [GDh 23.12] iti | tataś ca tad eva gogamane bhaviṣyati | sakṛdgamana idam | abhyāse śaṅkhoktam: “paśuveśyābhigamane prājāpatyam” iti | atra kaṇvaḥ |
prasūto yas tu veṣyāyāṃ bhaikṣabhuk saṃyatendriyaḥ |
śatasāhasram abhyasya sāvitrīm eti śuddhatām || iti |
dviruktir uktā || 22.36 ||
iti śrīgautamīyavṛttau haradattaviracitāyāṃ mitākṣarāyāṃ
tṛtīyapraśne caturtho ‘dhyāyaḥ
atha trayoviṃśo ‘dhyāyaḥ
kramaprāptaṃ surāpānaprāyaścittam āha |
surāpasya brāhmaṇasyoṣṇām āsiñceyuḥ surām āsye
mṛtaḥ śudhyet || 23.1 ||
trividhā surā | yathāha manuḥ |
gauḍī mādhvī ca paiṣṭī ca vijñeyā trividhā surā |
yathaivaikā na pātavyā tathā sarvā dvijottamaiḥ || iti | [MDh 11.94]
dvijottamā brāhmaṇāḥ | kṣatriyavaiśyayos tu paiṣṭy eva | yathā sa evāha |
surā vai malam annānāṃ pāpmā ca malam ucyate |
tasmād brāhmaṇarājanyau vaiśyaś ca na surāṃ pibet || iti | [MDh 11.93]
annānāṃ malaṃ surā paiṣṭī | atra brāhmaṇagrahaṇaṃ dvijātyupalakṣaṇam | yasya yā pratiṣiddhā surā tasyāḥ pātā surāpaḥ | tasya dvijāter āsye tām eva surām uṣṇām āsiñceyuḥ | upadeṣṭṛṣv ayam āsecanāropaḥ | āsiñcanty eva hi te surām āsye | yena surāpeṇa suroṣṇā pātavyā tasyeyaṃ niṣkṛtir ity upadiśantīti | svayam eva tv āsecanakartā | tathā ca āpastambaḥ: “surāpo ‘gnisparśāṃ surāṃ pibet” [ĀpDh 1.25.3] iti | āsiñceyur iti bahuvacanam upadeṣṭṝṇāṃ bahutvaṃ sūcayati | manur apy āha |
teṣāṃ vedavido brūyus trayo ‘py enaḥsu niṣkṛtim |
sā teṣāṃ pāvanā yasmāt pavitraṃ viduṣāṃ hi vāk || iti | [MDh 11.85]
mṛtaḥ śudhyed ity vacanāt tathā surā tāpayitavyā yathā pātur maraṇaṃ bhavati | “ārdravāsāḥ pibet” iti paiṭhīnasiḥ | “āyasena tāmreṇa vā pātreṇa” iti precatāḥ | atha yājñavalkyaḥ |
surāmbughṛtagomūtrapayasām agisaṃnibham |
surāpo ‘nyatamaṃ pītvā maraṇāc chuddhim ṛcchati ||
vālavāsā jaṭī vāpi brahmahatyāvrataṃ caret |
piṇyākaṃ vā kaṇān vāpi bhakṣayet tu samāṃ niśi || iti | [YDh 4.253–254]
tatra maraṇāntikaprāyaścittaṃ buddhipūrvābhyāsaviṣayam | tatraiva sakṛtpānaviṣayaṃ brahmahatyāvrataṃ dvādaśavārṣikam | atra striyo ‘dhikṛtya śaṅkhaḥ: “surālaśunapalāṇḍugṛñjanamāṃsādīny abhakṣyāṇi varjayed āhāramayaṃ śarīram” iti | vasiṣṭho ‘pi |
pataty ardhaṃ śarīrasya bhāryā yasya surāṃ pibet |
patitārdhaśarīrasya nikṣtir na vidhīyate || iti | [VaDh 21.15]
atra strīṇām api pratiṣiddhā surā | prāyaścittaṃ ca bhavati | tatra strīṇām ardhaprāyaścittam ity uktaṃ purastāt | tatra maraṇāntike ‘rdhakḷpter aśakyatvād buddhipūrvasakṛtpāne dvādaśavārṣikasyārdham | abhyāse tasyaivābhyāsaḥ || 23.1 ||
amatyā pāne payo ghṛtam udakaṃ vāyuṃ pratitryahaṃ
taptāni sa kṛcchras tato ‘sya saṃskāraḥ || 23.2 ||
yas tv amatyābuddhipūrvaṃ yavāgvadibuddhyā surāṃ pibati sa payaādīni catvāri dravyāṇi taptāny uṣṇāni | dvitīyāyā nirdeśāt pibed iti gamyate | pratitryahaṃ prathame tryahe payo dvitīye ghṛtaṃ tṛtīya udakaṃ caturthe vāyum | vāyor uṣṇatvaṃ sātape pradeśe | sa kṛcchraḥ sa evaṃbhūtas taptakṛcchro ‘sya prāyaścittam | tataḥ kṛcchrānantaraṃ punaḥ saṃskāraḥ punar upanayanam asya kartavyam | tatra mānavo viśeṣaḥ |
vapanaṃ mekhalā daṇḍo bhaikṣacaryā vratāni ca |
etāni tu nivartante punaḥsaṃskārakarmaṇi || iti | [MDh 11.151]
idam īṣadabhyāsaviṣayam |
ajñānād vāruṇīṃ pītvā saṃskāreṇaiva śudhyati | [MDh 11.146]
iti mānavaṃ sakṛtpānaviṣayam | yat tu,
piṇyākaṃ vā kaṇān vāpi bhakṣayet tu samāṃ niśi | [YDh 3.254]
iti yājñavakyavacanam | yac ca āpastambīyam: “steyaṃ kṛtvā surāṃ pītvā” [ĀpDh 1.25.10] ityādi tad ubhayam api bahukṛtvo ‘bhyāsa eva |
kaṇān vā bhakṣayed abdaṃ piṇyākaṃ vā sakṛn niśi |
surāpānāpanuttyarthaṃ vālavāsā jaṭī dhvajī || [MDh 11.92]
ityādīni mānavādīny abuddhipūrvaviṣaya evābhyāsatāratamyāpekṣayā vyavasthāpyāni || 23.2 ||
mūtrapurīṣaretasāṃ ca prāśane || 23.3 ||
mūtrādīnāṃ ca prāśane taptakṛcchrasahitaḥ punaḥsaṃskāraḥ prāyaścittam | idaṃ buddhipūrvaviṣayam |
bhuktvāto ‘nyatamasyānnam amatyā kṣapaṇaṃ tryaham |
matyā bhuktvā caret kṛcchraṃ reto viṇmūtram eva ca || [MDh 4.222]
iti |
ajñānāt prāśya viṇmūtraṃ surāsaṃsṛṣṭam eva ca |
punaḥ saṃskāram arhanti trayo varṇā dvijātayaḥ || [MDh 11.150]
iti ca |
kaṇvaś ca spaṣṭam āha |
retomūtrapurīṣāṇāṃ prāśane matipūrvake |
nāśnīyāc ca tryahaṃ matyā taptakṛcchraṃ cared dvijaḥ || iti || 23.3 ||
śvāpadoṣṭrakharāṇāṃ cāṅgasya || 23.4 ||
vyāghrādayo vanacarāḥ śvāpadāḥ | uṣṭrakharau prasiddhau | teṣām aṅgaṃ māṃsacarmādi | tasya prāśane taptakṛcchraḥ punaḥsaṃskāraś ca | buddhipūrvābhyāsa ubhayaṃ militam | sakṛdbuddhipūrve cābuddhipūrvābhyāse ca taptakṛcchraḥ | sakṛdmatipūrve saṃskāra eva bhavati || 23.4 ||
grāmyakukkuṭasūkarayoś ca || 23.4 ||
grāmyakukkuṭasūkarayoś cāṅgasya prāśana etad eva prāyaścittam | viṣayavyavasthā ca pūrvavat || 23.5 ||
gandhāghrāṇe surāpasya prāṇāyāmā ghṛtaprāśanaṃ
ca || 23.6 ||
yas tu surāpas tasya taṃ surāgandham ājighrati na punaḥ śarīragandhaṃ nāpi bhāṇḍasthāyāḥ gandhaṃ tasya prāṇāyāmās trayo ghṛtaprāśanaṃ ca prāyaścittam | brāhmaṇasya militam, kṣatriyasya prāṇāyāmāḥ | vaiśyasya ghṛtaprāśanam iti | somapasya viśeṣo manunā darśitaḥ |
brāhmaṇasya surāpasya gandham āghrāya somapaḥ |
prāṇān apsu trir ācamya ghṛtaṃ prāśya viśudhyati || [MDh 11.149]
iti |
brāhmaṇasya rujākṛtyaṃ ghrātir aghreyamadyayoḥ |
jaihmyaṃ puṃsi ca maithunyaṃ jātisaṃkaraṃ smṛtam || [MDh 11.67]
iti |
jātibhraṃśakaraṃ karma kṛtvānyatamam icchayā |
caret sāṃtapanaṃ kṛcchraṃ prājāpatyam anicchayā || [MDh 11.124]
iti [ca] mānavaṃ bhāṇḍasthāyāḥ surāyā gandhāghrāṇe || 23.6 ||
pūrvaiś ca daṣṭasya || 23.7 ||
pūrvaiḥ śvāpadādibhir daṣṭasya ca prāṇāyāmā ghṛtaprāśanaṃ ca prāyaścittam | manus tu |
śvasṛgālakharair daṣṭo grāmyaiḥ kravyādibhir eva ca |
narāśvoṣṭrair varāhaiś ca prāṇāyāmena śudhyati || iti | [MDh 11.199]
brāhmaṇaviṣaye vāsiṣṭho viśeṣaḥ |
brāhmaṇas tu śunā daṣṭo nadīṃ gatvā samudragām |
prāṇāyāmaśataṃ kṛtvā ghṛtaṃ prāśya viśudhyati || iti | [VaDh 23.31]
jātūkarṇyaḥ |
brāhmaṇī kṣatriyā vaiśyā śunā ca śvāpadair api |
daṣṭā sacailam āplutya śudhyatīti na saṃśayaḥ || iti || 23.7 ||
tapte lohaśayane gurutalpagaḥ śayīta || 23.8 ||
gurur atra pitā |
niṣekādīni karmāṇi yaḥ karoti yathāvidhi |
saṃbhāvayati cānyena sa vipro gurur ucyate || [MDh 2.142]
iti manuḥ | vipragrahaṇaṃ varṇopalakṣaṇam | talpaśabdena śayanavācinā bharyā lakṣyate | tatrāpi jananī tatsapatnī ca | tadgāmī gurutalpagaḥ | lohaśayane kṛṣṇāyasanirmite tapte yathā maraṇam eva bhavati tathā tapte śayīta || 23.8 ||
sūrmīṃ vā śliṣyej jvalantīm || 23.9 ||
lohamayī strīprakṛtiḥ sūrmī | tāṃ jvalantīm agnivarṇāṃ taptāṃ śliṣyed ā prāṇaviyogāt || 23.9 ||
liṅgaṃ vā savṛṣaṇam utkṛtyāñjalāv ādhāya
dakṣiṇāpratīcīṃ vrajed ajihmam ā śrīranipātāt || 23.10 ||
sabījaṃ liṅgam utpāṭya kṣurādinā nikṛtya svasyāñjalau sthāpayitvā nairṛtīṃ diśam ā śarīranipātād vrajed ajihmam | “kūpādy apariharan yatraiva pratihatas tatraiva tiṣṭhed āpralayāt” [VaDh 20.13] iti vasiṣṭhaḥ || 23.10 ||
mṛtaḥ śudhyet || 23.11 ||
sarvaśeṣo ‘yam | pūrvokteṣu prakāreṣv anyatamena mṛta eva gurutalpagaḥ śudhyen nānyatheti | tritayam apy etaj jananīgamane svabhāryādibuddhyābuddhipūrvaṃ tatsapatnyāṃ ca, savarṇāyāṃ buddhipūrvagamane |
pitṛbhāyāṃ tu vijñāya savarṇāṃ yo ‘bhigacchati |
jananīṃ vāpy avijñāya nāmṛtaḥ sa viśudhyati |
iti ṣaṭtriṃśanmate darśanāt | jananyāṃ kāmakṛte vāsiṣṭham: “niṣkālako ghṛtābhyakto gomayāgninā pādaprabhṛty ātmānam avadāhayet” [VaDh 20.42] iti | akāmato ‘bhyāse ‘py evam eva | akāmatas tu mātuḥ sapatnyāḥ savarṇāyā utkṛṣṭhāyāś ca gamanābhyāse śaṅkhoktam |
adhaḥśāyī jaṭādhārī parṇamūlaphalāśanaḥ |
ekakālaṃ samaśnan vai varṣe tu dvādaśe gate ||
rukmasteyī surāpaś ca brahmahā gurutalpagaḥ |
vratenaitena śudhyanti mahāpātakinas tv ime || iti |
sakṛdgamana ubhayor icchātaḥ pravṛttau mānavam |
khaṭvāṅgī cīravāsā vā śvaśrulo vijane vane |
prājāpatyaṃ caret kṛcchram abdam ekaṃ samāhitaḥ || iti | [MDh 11.105]
tayā protsāhitasya svena vā protsāhitāyām auśanasaṃ prāyaścittadvayaṃ krameṇa draṣṭavyam: “gurutalpagāmī saṃvatsaraṃ brahmacārivrataṃ ṣaṇmāsāṃs taptakṛcchraṃ ca” iti | evam uttareṣv api prāyaścitteṣu yad guru tad ātmanā protsāhitāyāṃ yal laghu tat tayā protsitasya madhyamaṃ tūbhayor icchātaḥ pravṛttāv iti draṣṭavyam | tatra vyāghraḥ |
kṛcchraṃ caivātikṛcchraṃ ca tathā kṛcchātikṛcchrakam |
caren māsatrayaṃ vipraḥ kṣatriyāgamane guroḥ || iti |
idaṃ sakṛd gamane ‘buddhipūrve | buddhipūrvābhyāsa ekavarṣam |
matyā gatvā punar bhāryāṃ guroḥ kṣatrasutāṃ dvijaḥ |
vṛṣaṇavarjitaṃ liṅgam utkṛtya sa mṛtaḥ śuciḥ || iti |
kaṇvaḥ |
cāndrāyaṇaṃ taptakṛcchram atikṛcchraṃ tathaiva ca |
sakṛd gatvā guror bhāryām ajñānāt kṣatriyāṃ dvijaḥ || iti |
jātūkarṇyaḥ |
guroḥ kṣatrasutāṃ bhāryāṃ punar gatvā tv akāmataḥ |
vṛṣaṇamātram utkṛtya śuddho jīvan mṛto ‘pi vā || iti |
kaṇvaḥ |
taptakṛcchraṃ parākaṃ ca tathā sāntapanaṃ guroḥ |
bhāryāṃ vaiśyāṃ sakṛd gatvā buddhyā māsaṃ cared dvijaḥ || iti |
laugākṣiḥ |
guror vaiśyāṃ punar gatvā (sakṛd) gatvā vāpi punaḥ punaḥ |
liṅgāgraṃ chedayitvā tu tataḥ sudhyet sa kilbiṣāt || iti |
prajāpatiḥ |
pañcarātraṃ tu nāśnīyāt saptāṣṭau vā tathaiva ca |
vaiśyāṃ bhāryāṃ guror gatvā sakṛd ajñānato dvijaḥ || iti |
hārītaḥ |
abhyasya vipro vaiśyāyāṃ guror ajñānamohitaḥ |
saṣaḍaṅgaṃ brahmacaryaṃ sa cared yāvad āyuṣam || iti |
jābāliḥ |
atikṛcchraṃ taptakṛcchraṃ parākaṃ ca tathaiva ca |
guroḥ śūdrāṃ sakṛd gatvā buddhyā vipraś caret tataḥ || iti |
upamanyuḥ |
punaḥ śūdrāṃ guror gatvā buddhyā vipraḥ samāhitaḥ |
brahmacaryam aduṣṭātmā dvādaśābdaṃ samācaret || iti |
dīrghatapāḥ |
prājāpatyaṃ sāntapanaṃ saptarātropavāsanam |
guroḥ śūdrāṃ sakṛd gatvā cared ajñānato janaḥ || iti |
tatraivābhyāse mānavaṃ draṣṭavyam |
cāndrāyaṇaṃ vā trīn māsān abhyasen niyatendriyaḥ |
haviṣyeṇa yavāgvā vā gurutalpāpanuttaye || iti | [MDh 11.106]
atra vyāghraḥ |
jātyuktaṃ pāradāryaṃ ca gurutalpatvam eva ca |
sādhāraṇastriyā nāsti kanyādūṣaṇam eva ca || iti || 23.11 ||
sakhīsayonisagotrāśiṣyabhāryāsu snuṣāyāṃ
gavi ca gurutalpasamaḥ || 23.12 ||
sakhī mitrabhūtā | sayonir bhaginī | sagotraikagotrā | snuṣā putrabhāryā | etāsu śiṣyabhāyāyāṃ gavi ca mithunībhāve yāvān gurutalpadoṣas tāvān asyeti | yājñavalkyaḥ |
sakhibhāryākumārīṣu svayoniṣv antyajāsu ca |
sagotrāsu sutastrīṣu gurutalpasamaṃ smṛtam ||
pituḥ svasāraṃ mātuś ca mātulānīṃ snuṣām api |
mātuḥ sapatnīṃ bhaginīm ācāryatanayāṃ tathā ||
ācāryapatnīṃ svasutāṃ gacchaṃs tu gurutalpagaḥ |
liṅgaṃ chittvā vadhas tasya sakāmāyāḥ striyā api || iti | [YDh 3.231–233]
nāradaḥ |
mātā mātṛṣvasā śvaśrūr mātulānī pitṛṣvasā |
pitṛvyasakhiśiṣyastrī bhaginī tatsakhī snuṣā ||
duhitācāryabhāryā ca sagotrā śaraṇāgatā |
rājñī pravrajitā dhātrī sādhvī varṇottamā ca yā ||
āsām anyatamāṃ gacchan gurutalpaga ucyate |
śiśnasyotkartanāt tatra nānyo daṇḍo vidhīyate || iti | [NSm 12.72–74]
kātyāyanaḥ |
jananyāś ca bhaginyāś ca svasutāyās tathaiva ca |
snuṣāyā gamanaṃ caiva vijñeyam atipātakam ||
atipātakinas tv ete praviśeyur hutāśanam ||
bṛhadyamaḥ |
retaḥ siktvā kumārīṣu svayoniṣv antyajāsu ca |
sapiṇḍāsv anyadāreṣu prāṇatyāgo vidhīyate || iti |
sa eva |
cāṇḍālīṃ pulkasīṃ mlecchīṃ snuṣāṃ ca bhaginīṃ sakhīm |
mātāpitroḥ svasāraṃ ca nikṣiptāṃ śaraṇāgatām ||
mātulānīṃ pravrajitāṃ sagotrāṃ nṛpayoṣitam |
śiṣyabhāryāṃ guror bhāryāṃ gatvā cāndrāyaṇaṃ caret || iti ca || 23.12 ||
avakara ity eke || 23.13 ||
eke manyante sakhyādigamane ‘vakaro doṣaḥ | atra prāyaścittam apy avakīrṇivrataṃ na gurutalpavratam iti | yāny etāni sakhyādigamane ‘nukrāntāni prāyaścittāni teṣu maraṇāntikāni sapratyayānubandhātyantābhyāsaviṣayāṇi | yāni punar atyantalaghūni tāni svabhāryābuddhyā pravṛttasya madhye jñātvā retaḥsekād arvāṅ nivṛttaviṣayāṇi | madhye madhyāni kalpyāni |
na jātu brāhmaṇaṃ hanyāt sarvapāpeṣv avasthitam | [MDh 8.380]
iti mānavaṃ tu maraṇāntikayogyamahāpātakādivyatiriktaviṣayam || 23.13 ||
atra prāyaścittam akurvatīnāṃ strīṇāṃ daṇḍam āha |
śvabhir ādayed rājā nihīnavarṇagamane striyaṃ
prakāśam || 23.14 ||
nihīnavarṇena saha yā maithunam ācarati tāṃ prakāśaṃ sarveṣām eva paśyatāṃ parṣatsthānagato rājā śvabhir ādayet khādayet | atra manuḥ |
bhartāraṃ laṅghayed yā tu jātistrīguṇagarvitā |
tāṃ śvabhiḥ khādayet rājā saṃsthāne bahubhiḥ sthitaḥ || iti | [MDh 8.371]
vasiṣṭhas tu jātiviśeṣeṇa viśeṣam āha –
śūdraś ced brāhmaṇīm abhigacchet tṛṇair veṣṭayitvā śūdram agnau prāsya brāhmaṇyāḥ śirasi vapanaṃ kārayitvā sarpiṣābhyajya nagnāṃ kharam āropya mahāpatham anusaṃvrājayet pūtā bhavatīti vijñāyate | vaiśyaś ced brāhmaṇīm abhigacchel lohitadarbhair veṣṭayitvā vaiśyam agnau prāsyet | brāhmaṇyāḥ śirasi vapanaṃ kārayitvā sarpiṣābhyajya nagnāṃ kharam āropya mahāpatham anusaṃvrājayet pūtā bhavatīti vijñāyate | rājanyaś ced brāhmaṇīm abhigacchec charapatrair veṣṭayitvā rājanyam agnau prāsyet | brāhmaṇyāḥ śirasi vapanaṃ kārayitvā sarpiṣābhyajya nagnāṃ kharam āropya mahāpatham anusaṃvrājayet pūtā bhavatīti vijñāyate | evaṃ vaiśyo rājanyāṃ śūdraś ca rājanyavaiśyayoḥ | iti | [VaDh 21.1–5]
anulomeṣu pratilomaṃ gacchatsu vyāghra āha |
varṇānām anulomānāṃ parasparasamāgame |
vyutkrameṇa tato rājā khādayed vānaraiḥ striyam ||
śṛgālair buddhipūrvaṃ cet puruṣo vadham arhati |
ayam evānulomānāṃ svajātivyutkrameṣv iti || iti || 23.14 ||
pumāṃsaṃ ghātayet || 23.15 ||
anantarokte viṣaye gantā pumān rājñā ghātayitavyaḥ | vadhaprakāraś cānantaram eva vasiṣṭhavacanena darśitaḥ || 23.15 ||
yathoktaṃ vā || 23.16 ||
liṅgoddhāra ityādi yathoktaṃ vā daṇḍapraṇayanaṃ kartavyam | sapratyayāpratyayābhyāsānabhyāsāpekṣo ‘yaṃ vikalpaḥ || 23.16 ||
gardabhenāvakīrṇī nirṛtiṃ catuṣpathe yajet || 23.17 ||
avakīrṇī bhaved gatvā brahmacārī tu yoṣitam | [YDh 3.280]
iti yājñavalkyaḥ | sa catuṣpathe gardabhena paśunā nirṛtiṃ yajet | atra mānavo viśeṣaḥ |
avakīrṇī tu kāṇena gardabhena catuṣpathe |
pākayajñavidhānena yajeta nirṛtiṃ diśi || iti | [MDh 11.118]
vasiṣṭhas tu |
brahmacārī cet striyam upeyād araṇye catuṣpathe laukike ‘gnau rakṣodaivataṃ gardabhaṃ paśum ālabheta, nairṛtaṃ vā caruṃ nirvapet tasya juhuyāt kāmāya svāhā, kāmakāmāya svāhā, nirṛtyai svāhā, rakṣodevatābhyaḥ svāhā | [VaDh 23.1–3] iti || 23.17 ||
tasyājinam ūrdhvavālaṃ paridhāya lohitapatraḥ sapta
gṛhān bhaikṣaṃ caret karmācakṣāṇaḥ || 23.18 ||
evaṃ gardabheneṣṭvā tasyaiva gardabhasyājinam ūrdhvavālaṃ paridhāya lohitapātraḥ pākena lohitaṃ mṛnmayaṃ pātraṃ haste gṛhītvā karmācakṣāṇo ‘vakīrṇine bhikṣāṃ dehīti bruvāṇaḥ sapta gṛhān bhaikṣaṃ caret | saptasu gṛheṣu yāval labdhaṃ tāvad evāśanam | alābha upavāsaḥ
|| 23.18 ||
saṃvatsareṇa śudhyet || 23.19 ||
saṃvatsaram etad vrataṃ carec chuddho bhavati | atra manuḥ |
tebhyo labdhena bhaikṣeṇa vartayann ekakālikam |
upaspṛśaṃs triṣavaṇam abdenaikena śudhyati || iti | [MDh 11.123]
idaṃ ca vārṣikaṃ śrotriyasya viprasya vaiśyapatnyāṃ draṣṭavyam | āhatuḥ śaṅkhalikhitau: “guptāyāṃ vaiśyāyām avakīrṇaḥ saṃvatsaraṃ triṣavaṇam anutiṣṭhet kṣatriyāyāṃ dve varṣe brāhmaṇyāṃ trīṇi varṣāṇi” iti | guptāyāṃ cec chrotriyapatnītvādiguṇaśālinyām | aṅgirāḥ |
avakīrṇinimittaṃ tu brahmahatyāvrataṃ caret |
cīravāsās tu ṣaṇmāsāṃs tathā mucyeta kilbiṣāt || iti |
tad akāmato gautamīyaṃ kāmaviṣayam | punaḥ śaṅkhalikhtau:
svairiṇyāṃ vṛṣalyām avakīrṇaḥ sacailaṃ snāta udakumbhaṃ dadyād brāhmaṇāya | vaiśyāyāṃ caturthakālāhāro brāhmaṇān bhojayed yavabhāraṃ ca gobhyo dadyāt | kṣatriyāyāṃ trirātram upoṣito ghṛtapātraṃ dadyāt | brahmaṇyāṃ ṣaḍrātram upoṣito gāṃ dadyāt | goṣv avakīrṇaḥ prājāpatyaṃ caret | ṣaṇḍhāyām avakīrṇaḥ palālabhāraṃ sīsamāṣakaṃ ca dadyāt | iti |
idaṃ cāvakīrṇaprāyaścittaṃ sarveṣām eva traivarṇikabrahmacāriṇāṃ samānam | tathā ca śāṇḍilyaḥ |
avakīrṇī dvijo rājā vaiśyaś cāpi khareṇa tu |
iṣṭvā bhaikṣāśano nityaṃ śudhyaty abdāt samāhitaḥ || iti || 23.19 ||
retaḥskandane bhaye roge svapne
‘gnīndhanabhaikṣacaraṇāni saptarātram
akṛtvājyahomaḥ samidho vā retasyābhyām || 23.20 ||
bhaye roge svapne vā yadi brahmacāriṇo retaḥ skandet tato retasyābhyāṃ mantrābhyām ājyahomaḥ kartavyaḥ | samidho vā | homa ity upasamastam apekṣyate | etat tu bhaye roga ityādi brahmacārivyatiriktasyāpi | tathāgnīndhanaṃ samidādhānaṃ bhaikṣacaraṇaṃ ca saptarātram akṛtvā pūrvavad dhomaḥ | retasye ṛcau “punar mām aitv indriyam” iti | “punar manaḥ punar ātmā ma āgāt” ity eke | āśvalāyanena tu “punar mām aitv indriyam” iti, “ime ye ‘dhiṣṭhayāso ‘gnaye” iti |
bhaye roge tathā svapne siktvā śukram akāmataḥ |
ādityam arcayitvā tu punar mām ity ṛcaṃ japet || iti |
prājāpatyaṃ sakṛtsekaviṣayam | gautamīyam abhyāsaviṣayam | hārītaḥ |
yaḥ kuryād upakurvāṇaḥ kāmato ‘kāmato ‘pi vā |
tad eva dviguṇaṃ kuryād brahmacārī tu naiṣṭhikaḥ || iti |
atra vasiṣṭhaḥ: “etad eva retasaḥ prayatnotsarge divā svapne ca vratāntareṣu caivam” [VaDh 23.4] iti | gardabhaṃ paśum ālabheta nairṛtaṃ vā caruṃ nirvaped iti prakṛtam |
vānaprastho yatiś caiva khaṇḍane sati kāmataḥ |
parākatrayasaṃyuktam avakīrṇivrataṃ caret ||
iti śāṇḍilyaḥ |
puṃsi maithunam āsevya yatnotsarge ca retasaḥ |
brahmacārī yathābhyāsaṃ snātvātha haviṣā yajet ||
puṃsi maithunam āsādya vānaprastho yatis tathā |
kṛcchraṃ cāndrāyaṇaṃ caiva kṛtvā śudhyati kilbiṣāt ||
iti kaṇvaḥ |
sūryasya trīn namaskārān svapne siktvā gṛhī caret |
yatiś caiva vanasthaś ca triḥ kuryād aghamarṣaṇam ||
iti kāśyapaḥ |
maithunaṃ tu samāsādya puṃsi yoṣiti vā punaḥ |
goyāne ‘psu divā caiva svāpe ca snānam ācaret || [MDh 11.174]
iti mānavam | gṛhasthasya –
ṛtau tu garbhaśaṅkitvāt snānaṃ maithuninaḥ smṛtam |
anṛtau tu yadā gacchec chaucaṃ mūtrapurīṣavat ||
ity aṅgirāḥ | vṛddhavasiṣṭhaḥ |
yas tu pāṇigṛhītāyām asye kurvīta maithunam |
tasya retasi taṃ māsaṃ pitaras tasya śerate || iti || 23.20 ||
sūryābhyudito brahmacārī tiṣṭhed ahar abhuñjāno
‘bhyastamitaś ca rātriṃ japan sāvitrīm || 23.21 ||
yas tu sūrya udayati svapiti sa sūryābhyudito brahmacārī sarvam ahar abhuñjānas tiṣṭhet | abhyastamitaś ca rātiṃ sarvām āsīta, “tiṣṭhed ahani rātrāv āsīta” [GDh 26.6] iti kṛcchre darśanāt | japan sāvitrīm ity ubhayatra samānam | brahmacārigrahaṇād gṛhasthādīnām anyat prāyaścittam | “ā tamitoḥ prāṇam āyacched ity eke” [ĀpDh 2.12.15] ity āpastambīyaṃ gṛhasthasya | āha vasiṣṭhaḥ |
vanasthaś ca yatiś caiva sūryeṇābhyudito yadi |
brahmakūrcāśinau bhūtvā japetāṃ drupadāṃ tv ahaḥ || iti |
abhyastamitayor apīdam eva | āha prajāpatiḥ |
pālāśaṃ padmapatraṃ vā tāmraṃ vātha hiraṇmayam |
gṛhītvā sādayitvā ca tataḥ kūrcaṃ samārabhet ||
gāyatryādāya gomūtraṃ gandhadvāreti gomayam |
āpyāyasveti ca kṣīraṃ dadhikrāvṇeti vai dadhi ||
śukram api jyotir asīty ājyaṃ devasya tvā kuśodakam |
caturdaśīm upoṣyātha paurṇamāsyāṃ samācaret ||
gomayād dviguṇaṃ mūtraṃ sarpir dadyāc caturguṇam |
kṣīram aṣṭaguṇaṃ deyaṃ dadhi pañcaguṇaṃ tathā ||
sthāpayitvātha darbheṣu pālāśaiḥ patrakair atha |
tat samuddhṛtya hotavyaṃ devatābhyo yathākramam ||
agnaye caiva somāya sāvitryā caiva mantrataḥ |
praṇavena tathā hutvā sviṣṭakṛt praṇavena tu ||
etad brahmakṛtaṃ kūrcaṃ pavitraṃ ca tathaiva ca |
evaṃ hutvā tataḥ śeṣaṃ pāpaṃ dhyātvā samāhitaḥ ||
āloḍya praṇavenaiva nirmathya praṇavena tu |
uddhṛtya praṇavenaiva pibec ca praṇavena tu ||
etad brahmakṛtaṃ kūrcaṃ māsi māsi cared dvijaḥ |
sarvapāpaviśūddhātmā svargalokaṃ sa gacchati ||
yat tvagasthigataṃ pāpaṃ dehe tiṣṭhati dehinām |
brahmakūrco dahet sarvaṃ pradīptāgnir ivendhanam || iti |
buddhipūrve ‘buddhipūrve sādhāraṇam idam | tathā ca manuḥ |
taṃ ced abhyudiyāt sūryaḥ śayānaṃ kāmakārataḥ |
nimloced vāpy avijñānāj japann upavased dinam || iti | [MDh 2.220]
dinam ity upalakṣaṇaṃ nimlocane rātrim upavased iti | abhayarogasya iti jābālivacanād bhaye roge ca prāyaścittam na bhavati || 23.21 ||
aśuciṃ dṛṣṭvādityam īkṣeta prāṇāyāmaṃ kṛtvā || 23.22 ||
aśuciś caṇḍālādiḥ | taṃ dṛṣṭvā prāṇāyāmam ekaṃ kṛtvā sūryam īkṣeta | japādiniyamakāla idaṃ brahmacāriṇaḥ |
ācamya prayato nityaṃ japed aśucidarśane |
sauryān mantrān yathotsāhaṃ pāvamānīś ca śaktataḥ || [MDh 5.86]
iti mānavaṃ naiṣṭhikādīnām | aśucidarśane dvijaḥ praṇavaṃ japed iti jābāligṛhyavacanaṃ gṛhasthaviṣayam | aśucidarśana ādityadarśanaṃ brāhmaṇadarśanaṃ gavām agner vety auśanasaṃ niyamakālād anyatra | jābāligṛhye dvijagrahaṇāc chūdrasya na vidhir na pratiṣedhaḥ
|| 23.22 ||
abhojyabhojane ‘medhyaprāśane vā
niṣpurīṣībhāvaḥ || 23.23 ||
“nityam abhojyaṃ keśakīṭāvapannam” [GDh 17.8–9] ity ārabhyābhojyāny uktāni | tāni ca bahuprakārāṇi | jātiduṣṭāni laśunādīni | kāladuṣṭāni paryuṣitādīni | parigrahaduṣṭāny utsṛṣṭādīnām annāni | saṃsargaduṣṭāni keśakīṭādyupahatāni | kriyāduṣṭāny ācamanotthānavyapetādīni | teṣām abhojyānāṃ bhojane ca | medhyaṃ pavitram | amedhyam apariśuddhaṃ sthānapātrapākasparśapradātrādinā | teṣām amedhyānāṃ prāśane ca niṣpurīṣībhāvaḥ kāryaḥ | yathā niṣpurīṣam udaraṃ bhavati tathā kāryam || 23.23 ||
tat katham |
trirātrāvaram abhojanam || 23.24 ||
tisro rātrīr na kiṃcid bhuñjīta | na kiṃcit khādayet | na kiṃcit pibet | evaṃ niṣpurīṣībhāvo ’vāpyate | avaragrahaṇāc catūrātrāder api bhāvaḥ (lābhaḥ) | parameṇa saptarātram | tathā ca āpastambaḥ: “abhojyaṃ bhuktvā naiṣpurīṣyaṃ tat saptarātreṇāvāpyate” [ĀpDh 1.27.3–4] iti
|| 23.24 ||
saptarātraṃ vā svayaṃśīrṇāny upabhuñjānaḥ
phalāny anatikrāman || 23.25 ||
śudhyatīti śeṣaḥ | atha vā nopavaset kiṃ tu svayaṃśīrṇāni svayaṃpatitāni phalāni bhuñjāno ‘natikrāmann asvāduphalopalambhe tadatikrameṇa svāduphalāntaragrahaṇārtham agacchan saptarātram evaṃ kurvañ śudhyati || 23.25 ||
prāk pañcanakhebhyaś chardanaṃ ghṛtaprāśanaṃ
ca || 23.26 ||
tatraivābhojyaprakaraṇe pañcanakhāś ca śalyaketyādibhir aṣṭabhiḥ sūtrair yāny abhojyāny uktāni tebhyaḥ prāg yāny abhojyāni “nityam abhojyam” [GDh 17.8] ityādibhir ekonaviṃśatisūtrair uktāni teṣu bhukteṣu chardayitvā ghṛtaṃ prāśya viśudhyati | evaṃ ca pūrvakaṃ prāyaścittadvayaṃ svabhāvaduṣṭeṣu pañcanakhādiṣv evāvatiṣṭhate | atra viṣṇuḥ: “malānāṃ madyānām anyatarasya prāśane cāndrāyaṇaṃ kuryāl laśunapalāṇḍukagṛñjanatajjaviḍvarāhagrāmakukkuṭanaramāṃsabhakṣaṇe ca sarveṣv eteṣu dvijātīnāṃ prāyaścittaṃ punaḥ saṃskāraḥ [ViDh 51.2–4] | bṛhaspatiḥ |
alehyānām apeyānām abhakṣyāṇāṃ ca bhakṣaṇe |
retomūtrapurīṣāṇāṃ śuddhiś cāndrāyaṇaṃ smṛtam ||
aṅgirāḥ |
alehyānām apeyānām abhakṣyāṇāṃ ca bhakṣaṇe |
retomūtrapurīṣāṇām ṛṣikṛcchro viśodhanam ||
padmodumbarabilvānāṃ kuśaparṇapalāśayoḥ |
eteṣām udakaṃ pītvā taptenaiva viśudhyati ||
kāśyapaḥ:
laśunapalāṇḍugṛñjanakukkuṭabhakṣaṇe medaḥśukrapāne ‘yājyayājane ‘bhojyabhojane ‘bhakṣyabhakṣaṇe ‘gamyāgamane caivaṃ prāyaścittaṃ brāhmaṇebhyo nivedya ṣaḍrātropoṣitaś cīrṇānte prācyām udīcyāṃ diśi gatvā yatra grāmyapaśūnāṃ śabdo na śrūyate tasmin deśe ‘gniṃ prajvālya brahmāsanam āstīrya tatpraṇītena vidhinā punaḥ saṃskāram arhati |
sumantuḥ:
laśunapalāṇḍugṛñjanabhakṣaṇe vīraśrāddhe sūtikābhojyānnamadhumāṃsamūtra-reto’medhyābhakṣyabhakṣaṇe sāvitryaṣṭasahasreṇa mūrdhni saṃpātān avanayet | etāny evāturasya bhiṣakkriyāyām apratiṣiddhāni bhavanti | yāni cānyāny evaṃprakārāṇi teṣv apy adoṣaḥ |
palāṇḍuṃ laśunaṃ caiva gṛñjanaṃ kavakaṃ tathā |
catvāry ajñānato jagdhvā taptakṛcchraṃ cared dvijaḥ ||
manus tu |
chatrākaṃ viḍvarāhaṃ ca laśunaṃ grāmakukkuṭam |
palāṇḍuṃ gṛñjanaṃ caiva matyā bhuktvā bhaved [pated?] dvijaḥ ||
amatyaitāni ṣaḍ jagdhvā kṛcchraṃ sāntapanaṃ caret |
yaticāndrāyaṇaṃ vāpi śeṣeṣūpavased ahaḥ ||
saṃvatsarasyaikam api caret kṛcchraṃ dvijottamaḥ |
ajñātabhuktaśuddhyarthaṃ jñātasya tu viśeṣataḥ || [MDh 5.19–21]
śātātapaḥ: “laśunapalāṇḍugṛñjanakusumbhaśarakavakāmedhyabhakṣaṇe taptakṛcchraḥ” | viṣṇuḥ: “vṛntākakavakāśane sāntapanam” [ViDh 51.34] | paiṭhīnasiḥ: “laśunapalāṇḍugṛñjanabhakṣaṇe prājāpatyam” | devalaḥ: “abhakṣyabhakṣaṇe kṛcchram” | paiṭhīnasiḥ: “abhakṣyabhakṣaṇe taptakṛcchram” | saṃvartaḥ |
abhojyabhojanaṃ kṛtvā brahmakṣatraviśāṃ gaṇaḥ |
gomūtrayāvakāhāraḥ saptarātreṇa śudhyati ||
bṛhaspatiḥ|
pītvā śuklakaṣāyāṇi bhuktvā cānnaṃ vigarhitam |
bhaved aprayato vipraḥ karmaṇaḥ syād adhogatiḥ ||
viṣṇuḥ: “dadhivarjitāni sarvaśuklāni [-śuktāni?] cātra prāsyopavaset” [cf. ViDh 51.42] | iti prakṛtam | śaṅkhaḥ |
lohitān vṛkṣaniryāsān vraścanaprabhavāṃs tathā |
bhuktvā ṛbīsapakvaṃ ca trirātraṃ tu vratī bhavet ||
śaṅkhalikhitau: “sarvāsāṃ dvistanīnāṃ kṣīraprāśane ‘jāvarjam etad eva | atra ṣaḍrātram abhojanaṃ cāndrāyaṇaṃ ca” iti prakṛtam | anirdaśāvigokṣīraprāśane tadahar abhojanaṃ sacailasnānaṃ ca | śātātapaḥ |
uṣṭrīkṣīram avikṣīram annaṃ ca mṛtisūtake |
corasyānnaṃ navaśrāddhe bhuktvā cāndrāyaṇaṃ caret ||
paiṭhīnasiḥ: “avikharoṣṭramānuṣadugdhaprāśane punar upanayanaṃ prājāpatyaṃ ca” | baudhāyanaḥ: “aveḥ payaḥpāne kṛcchro ‘nyatra gavyāt | gavi trirātropavāsaḥ” [BDh 1.12.12–13] | śaṅkhaḥ |
anirdaśāyā goḥ kṣīram ājaṃ māhiṣam eva ca |
goś ca kṣīraṃ vivatsāyāḥ pītvā pakṣavrataṃ caret ||
kṣīrāṇi yāny apeyāni tadvikārāśane budhaḥ |
saptarātravrataṃ kuryād yad etat parikīrtitam ||
sumantuḥ: “ekaśaphoṣṭrasyandinyavistrīkṣīraprāśane gomahiṣyajānāṃ cānirdaśāhānāṃ kṣīraprāśane trirātraṃ yāvakas triṣavaṇaṃ ca” | viṣṇuḥ: “go’jāvimahiṣīvarjaṃ sarvapayāṃsi ca tāny apy anirdaśāhāni syandinīsaṃdhinīvivatsākṣīraṃ cāmedhyabhujaś ca kṣīraṃ prāśyopavaset” [ViDh 51.38–41] iti prakṛtam | hārītaḥ: “anuktānāṃ sattvānāṃ bhakṣaṇe ‘tikṛcchro grāmyāṇāṃ cāndrāyaṇam” | yamaḥ |
varāhaikaśaphānāṃ ca kākakukkuṭayos tathā |
kravyādānāṃ ca sarveṣām abhakṣyā ye ca kīrtitāḥ ||
māṃsamūtrapurīṣāṇi prāśya gomāṃsam eva ca |
śvagomāyukapīnāṃ ca taptakṛcchraṃ viśodhanam ||
upoṣya dvādaśāhaṃ vā kūṣmāṇḍair juhuyād ghṛtam ||
vasiṣṭhaḥ: “śvakukkuṭagrāmyasūkarakākagṛdhrabhāsavāyasapārāvatamānuṣakākolūkānāṃ māṃsādane saptarātram upavāso niṣpurīṣībhāvo ghṛtaprāśanaṃ punaḥ saṃskāraś ca kāryaḥ” [VaDh 23.30] |
biḍālakākākhūcchiṣṭaṃ jagdhvā ca nakulasya ca |
keśakīṭāvapannaṃ ca pibed brahmasuvarcalām || [= MDh 11.159]
keśakīṭāvapannaṃ ca strībhiḥ svādaṃs tathaiva ca |
śvodakyābhyāṃ ca saṃspṛṣṭaṃ pañcagavyena śudhyati ||
yamaḥ |
mākṣikaṃ phāṇitaṃ śākaṃ gorasaṃ lavaṇaṃ ghṛtam |
etāni hastadattāni bhuktvā sāntapanaṃ caret ||
śaṅkhaḥ |
ekapaṅktyupaviṣṭānāṃ viṣamaṃ yaḥ prayacchati |
yaś caivāśnāty ayaṃ sarvaḥ kuryād brahmahaṇi vratam ||
yamaḥ |
brāhmaṇakṣatriyaviśāṃ śūḍrāṇāṃ sahabhojanam |
prājāpatyaṃ taptakṛcchram atikṛcchraṃ tathaiva ca ||
cāndrāyaṇam iti proktaṃ prāyaścittaṃ krameṇa tu ||
śātātapaḥ |
yo ‘gṛhītvā vivāhāgniṃ gṛhastha iti manyate |
annaṃ tasya na bhoktavyaṃ vṛthāpāko hi sa smṛtaḥ ||
vṛthāpākasya bhuktvānnaṃ prāyaścittaṃ cared dvijaḥ |
prāṇāyāmaṃ trir abhyasya ghṛtaṃ prāśya viśudhyati ||
aṅgirāḥ |
brahmakṣatraviśāṃ bhuktvā na doṣo ‘sty agnihotriṇām |
sūtake śāva āśauce asthisaṃcayanāt param ||
cāṇḍālaḥ śvapacaḥ kṣattā sūto vaidehakas tathā |
māgadhāyogavau caiva saptaite ‘ntyāvasāyinaḥ ||
antyāvasāyinām annam aśnīyād yas tu kāmataḥ |
sa tu cāndrāyaṇaṃ kuryāt taptakṛcchram athāpi vā ||
yamaḥ |
brāhmaṇānnaṃ dadac chūdraḥ śūdrānnaṃ brāhmaṇo dadat |
ubhāv etāv abhojyānnau bhuktvā cāndrāyaṇaṃ caret || 23.26 ||
ākrośānṛtahiṃsrāsu trirātraṃ paramaṃ tapaḥ || 23.27 ||
mahāpātakopapātakayuktād anyatrākrośe satāsatā vā dośeṇātivāde sākṣyādiviṣayād anyatrānṛte tatroktatvāt | prāṇibhyo ‘nyatra hiṃsāyām, prāṇiṣūktatvāt | eteṣu nimitteṣu paramaṃ tapaḥ parameṇa trirātram anaśanaṃ brahmacaryaṃ kartavyam | paramagrahaṇād ekarātrāder api lābhaḥ | tatra brāhmaṇa ākrośe trirātram, kṣatriya dvirātram, vaiśya ekarātram, śūdre ‘har iti vyavasthā |
anṛte ‘py evam | phalāphalādyapekṣayā brāhmaṇādisvāmikeṣu vṛkṣādiṣu hiṃsāyām apy evam | atra prajāpatiḥ |
anṛte somapaḥ kuryāt trirātraṃ paramaṃ tapaḥ |
pūrṇāhutiṃ vā juhuyāt sapta te agna ity ṛcā ||
iti |
anṛtoktau ṣṭhīvane ca dantasparśana eva ca |
patitānāṃ ca saṃbhāṣe dakṣiṇaṃ śravaṇaṃ spṛśet || iti |
idaṃ parihāsādinimittānṛtaviṣayam | hārītaḥ |
pratyāśrutyānṛtaṃ bhrūyān mithyā satyam athāpi vā |
sa taptakṛcchrasahitaṃ carec cāndrāyaṇavratam ||
prajāpatiḥ |
māṃsaṃ bhuktvā brahmacārī punaḥ saṃskāram arhati |
abhyāsa aindavaṃ caiva naiṣṭhiko dviguṇaṃ caret ||
vanasthas triguṇaṃ kuryād yatiḥ kuryāc caturguṇam |
māṃsāśane ‘nṛtoktau ca śavanirharaṇe tathā || iti | 23.27 ||
ākrośe viśeṣaḥ |
satyavākye vāruṇīmānavībhir homaḥ | 23.28 ||
ākrośe satyavākye sati vāruṇībhir mānavībhiś cājyahomaḥ kartavyaḥ | trirātraṃ paramam ity eva | “yat kiṃcedam,” “imaṃ me varuṇa,” “tat tvā yāmi,” “avate heḍa” iti vāruṇyaḥ | “agnir ukthe purohita” ity ārabhyādhyāyaparisamāpter mānavya ṛca ekonaṣaṣṭir manunā dṛṣṭāḥ | tāsv antyāś catasro “makṣū devavata” ityādyās taittirīyake saumāraudryamiṣṭau dhāyyatvena viniyuktāḥ | “asāv āditya” ity asminn anuvāke mānavī ṛcau dhāyye kuryād iti | sūtrakāro ‘py āha: “mānavī ṛcā dhāyye makṣū devavata” ity etāsāṃ dve iti | tatrāntyābhir ābhiś catasṛbhir homa ity eke | anye tu ṛgvedapāṭhitābhiḥ sarvābhir ṛgbhir iti || 23.28 ||
vivāhamaithunanarmārtasaṃyogeṣv adoṣam
eke ‘nṛtam || 23.29 ||
vivāhakāle kanyāvarayor asatsv api guṇeṣu kathiteṣv idaṃ te dāsyāmīti pratiśrutyāpradāne ca na dośaḥ | tathā maithunasaṃyoga idaṃ te dāsyāmīty uktvā maithune kṛte tasyādāne ‘pi na doṣaḥ | narma parihāsas tatsaṃyoge ‘nṛtavacane na doṣaḥ | tad yathā bhoktukāmaṃ gṛham āgataṃ śyālādikaṃ pratyucyate – ehi manya odanaṃ bhokṣyase bhuktaḥ so ‘tithibhir ity evaṃprāyam | ārtasaṃyoga ārtasya duḥkhopaśamāyānṛtavacane na doṣaḥ | tenaiteṣu nimitteṣv anṛtavacaneṣu na prāyaścittam iti || 23.29 ||
na tu khalu gurvartheṣu || 23.30 ||
guruprayojaneṣu vivāhādiṣv apy anrtaṃ na vaktavyam || 23.30 ||
kasmād yataḥ |
sapta puruṣān itaś ca parataś ca hanti manasāpi
guror anṛtaṃ vadann alpeṣv apy artheṣu || 23.31 ||
ita ity ātmānaṃ nirdiśati | ātmānam ārabhya sapta puruṣān putrapautrādīn parataś ca sapta puruṣān pitṛpitāmahādīn hanti pīḍayati pāpena yojayatīti | manasāpi guror anṛtaṃ cintayann alpeṣv api prayojaneṣu kim aṅga mahatsu vācā vadann iti || 23.31 ||
antyāvasāyinīgamane kṛcchrābdaḥ || 23.32 ||
antyāvasāyinīnāṃ gamane maithunācaraṇe kṛcchrābdaḥ prāyaścittaṃ saṃvatsaraṃ prājāpatyavidhināvasthānam | buddhipūrva idam || 23.32 ||
amatyā dvādaśarātraḥ || 23.33 ||
kṛcchre prakṛte dvādaśarātragrahaṇaṃ parākopasaṃgrahaṇārtham | tathā ca –
antyajānāṃ tu gamane bhojane ca pramāpaṇe |
parākeṇa viśuddhaḥ syād bhagavān aṅgirābravīt || iti |
idam api retaḥsekāt prāg evoparatasya | ūrdhvaṃ tu vāsiṣṭham: “dvādaśarātram abbhakṣo dvādaśarātram upavased aśvamedhāvabhṛthaṃ vā gacchet | etena cāṇḍālīvyavāyo vyākhyātaḥ” [VaDh 23.38, 40–41] iti || 23.33 ||
udakyāgamane trirātras [trirātraḥ] || 23.33 ||
udakyāgamane sati brahmacaryānaśanadinā prāyaścittena trirātro gamayitavyaḥ | buddhipūrve sakṛdgamana idam | abhyāse mānavam |
amānuṣīṣu govarjam udakyāyām ayoniṣu |
retaḥ siktvā jale caiva kṛcchraṃ sāntapanaṃ caret || iti | [MDh 11.173]
abuddhipūrve sakṛdgamane śātātapoktam: “anudakamūtrapurīṣakaraṇe ca kākasparśane sacailasnānaṃ mahāvyāhṛtibhir homaś ca | rajasvalāgamane caivam” iti | abhyāse vāsiṣṭham: “rajasvalāgamane śuklam ṛṣabhaṃ dadyāt kṛṣṇapiṅga[la?]m” iti | [dviruktis uktārthā] || 23.33 ||
iti śrīgautamīyavṛttau haradattaviracitāyāṃ mitākṣarāyāṃ
tṛtīyapraśne pañcamo ‘dhyāyaḥ
atha caturviṃśo ‘dhyāyaḥ
rahasyaṃ prāyaścittaṃ vakṣyate \
rahasyaṃ prāyaścittam avikhyātadoṣasya || 24.1 ||
yasya pāpasya doṣaḥ parair na vikhyātas tasya prāyaścittaṃ rahasyaṃ bhavati | yathā parair na jñāyate tathā kartavyam iti yāvat | yair vinā yat pātakaṃ kartuṃ na śakyate tadvyatiriktair jñātatvaṃ niṣidhyate | tena pāradārye patitasaṃvāse ca tair jñātatve ‘pi vakṣyamāṇaṃ bhavaty eva || 24.1 ||
caturṛcaṃ taratsamandīty apsu japed apratigrāhyaṃ
pratijighṛkṣan pratigṛhya vā || 24.2 ||
jātiduṣṭasya vā karmaduṣṭasya vā puruṣasya svabhūtaṃ svayaṃduṣṭaṃ ca kṛṣṇājinādy apratigrāhyam | gatyabhāvāt pratijighṛkṣan pratigrahītum icchaṃs taratsamandīdhāvatīti caturṛcaṃ sūktam apsu japet | nābhidadhne jale sthita ity eke | nimagna ity anye | pratigṛhya vā pratigrahāt paścād vā japet | evaṃ tulyavad vikalpaḥ | anye pratigrahāt pūrvam evāpratigrāhyam iti jñāte prāg japaḥ | paścāj jñāte paścāj japa iti | atra manuḥ |
pratigṛhyāpratigrāhyaṃ bhuktvā cānnaṃ vigarhitam |
japaṃs taratsamandīyaṃ mucyate mānavas tryahāt || iti | [MDh 11.253]
prajāpatiḥ |
japādipūraṇā kuryāt khyātadoṣo dvijottamaḥ |
rahaḥkṛtasya doṣasya tat tad evābhyaset tathā || iti |
idam abhyāsaviṣayam || 24.2 ||
abhojyaṃ bubhukṣamāṇaḥ pṛthivīm āvapet || 24.3 ||
“nityam abhojyaṃ keśakīṭāvapannam” [GDh 17.8–9] ity uktam | yadi gatyabhāve tad eva bhoktum icchati tadā pṛthivīm āvapen mṛdaṃ prakṣipet tato bhuñjīta || 24.3 ||
ṛtvantarāramaṇa udakopasparśanāc chuddhim
eke || 24.4 ||
ṛtumadhya āramaṇa udakyāgamana udakopasparśanāt sacailasnānāc chuddhim āhur eke | “udakyāgamane trirātraḥ” [GDh 23.33] iti prakāśaviṣayam | ekegrahaṇaṃ paratrāpi saṃbadhyate || 24.4 ||
strīṣu || 24.5 ||
eke svastrīṣūdakopasparśanam anyatra trirātraṃ manyante | apara āha – strīṣu vaḍavādyāsv api govarjaṃ maithuna ācarita udakopasparśanāc chuddhim eke manyante || 24.5 ||
atha brāhmaṇavadhe rahasyam |
payovrato vā daśarātraṃ ghṛtena dvitīyam adbhis tṛtīyaṃ divādiṣv ekabhaktiko jalaklinnavāsā lomāni nakhāni tvacaṃ māṃsaṃ śoṇitaṃ snāyv asthi majjānam iti homā ātmano mukhe mṛtyor āsye juhomīty antataḥ sarveṣāṃ prāyaścittaṃ bhrūṇahatyāyāḥ || 24.6 ||
bhrūṇahatyā brahmahatyā | tasyāḥ prāyaścittam idam ucyate | ādita ekaṃ daśarātraṃ payovrataḥ kṣīrāhāraḥ syāt | dvitīyaṃ daśarātraṃ ghṛtena vartayet | tṛtīyam adbhiḥ | vāśabdād dhaviṣyabhojano vā | śaktito vikalpaḥ | eteṣu divaseṣv ekabhaktikaḥ payaḥprabhṛti kim api pūrvoktaṃ sakṛd evopabhuñjīta | kadā? divādiṣu prātaḥkāleṣu na sāyaṃ na madhyāhne | jalaklinnavāsā eṣu divaseṣv ārdravāsāś ca syāt | tathā homāś cāṣṭau pratyaham ājyena kartavyāḥ | tatra mantrāḥ – lomāni nakhāni tvacaṃ māṃsaṃ śoṇitaṃ snāyv asthi majjānam iti | teṣāṃ sarveṣām ātmano mukhe mṛtyor āsye juhomi svāhety ante prayoktavyam | juhoticodanā svāhākārapradāneti vacanāt | tad yathā | lomāny ātmano mukhe mṛtyor āsye juhomi svāhā, nakhāny ātmano mukhe mṛtyor āsye juhomi svāhety evaṃprakārā homāḥ || 24.6 ||
atha bhrūṇahatyāyā evānyat prāyaścittam ucyate |
ukto niyamaḥ || 24.7 ||
“payovrato vā” [GDh 24.6] ityādir vakṣyamāṇe ‘pi veditavyaḥ || 24.7 ||
agne tvaṃ pārayeti mahāvyāhṛtibhir juhuyāt
kūṣmāṇḍaiś cājyam || 24.8 ||
“agne tvaṃ pārayā” [RV 1,189.2] ity ṛcā mahāvyāhṛtibhir bhūrādibhiḥ kūṣmāṇḍair “yad devā devaheḍanam” [VS 20.14] ityādibhiś ca krameṇa sakṛd ājyaṃ juhuyāt || 24.8 ||
tadvrata eva vā brahmahatyāsurāpānasteyagurutalpeṣu prāṇāyāmais tānto ‘ghmarṣaṇaṃ japan samam aśvamedhāvabhṛthenedaṃ ca prāyaścittam || 24.9 ||
tadvrata eva vā tenaiva “payovrato vā” [GDh 24.6] ityādinā vratenopetaś caturṣu brahmahatyādiṣu pāpeṣu prāyaścittaṃ kuryāt | prāṇāyāmais tānto mlāno yāvadbhiḥ prāṇāyāmais tānto bhavati tāvadbhiḥ kuryād aghamarṣaṇam | aghamarṣaṇena ṛṣiṇā dṛṣṭam “ṛtaṃ ca satyaṃ ca” ityādināghamarṣaṇaṃ | tac cāśvamedhāvabhṛthena samaṃ tulyam | japann iti vartamānaprayogeṇa pratyaham eva triṃśadrātraṃ vrataṃ kuryāt | atra manuḥ |
yathāśvamedhaḥ kraturāṭ sarvapāpapraṇāśanaḥ |
tathāghamarṣaṇaṃ sūktaṃ sarvapāpapraṇāśanam || [MDh 11.260] || 24.9 ||
sāvitrīṃ vā sahastrakṛtva āvartayan punīte
haivātmānam || 24.10 ||
tadvrata evety anuvartate | prāṇāyāmais tānta iti ca | sāvitrīṃ sahasrakṛtva āvartayann iti japyamātraṃ bhidyate | anyat samānam | evaṃ kurvann ātmānaṃ punīte brahmahatyādibhyaś caturbhyaḥ śodhayati | heti prasiddhau | evety avadhāraṇe | tataś cānyeṣv api pāpeṣu sāvitryabhyāsaḥ śuddhihetuḥ | tathā ca vasiṣṭhaḥ |
sahasraparamāṃ devīṃ śatamadhyāṃ daśāvarām |
śuddhikāmaḥ prayuñjīta sarvapāpeṣv api sthitaḥ || iti | [VaDh 26.15]
vyāghro ‘py āha |
na sāvitrīsamam japyaṃ na vyāhṛtisamaṃ hutam |
nānnatoyasamaṃ dānaṃ na cāhiṃsāsamaṃ tapaḥ || iti || 24.10 ||
prāyaścittāntaram āha |
antarjale vāghamarṣaṇaṃ trir āvartayan sarvapāpebhyo
vimucyate [vimucyate] || 24.11 ||
tadvrata evodakasyāntarnimagnas triṃśadrātram aghamarṣaṇaṃ trir abhyasya sarvasmāt pāpāj jñānakṛtād ajñānakṛtāc ca mucyate | dviruktiś ca vyākhyātā || 24.11 ||

tad āhuḥ katidhāvakīrṇī praviśatīti || 25.1 ||
