marutaḥ prāṇenendre balena bṛhaspatiṃ
brahmavarcasenāgnim ivetareṇa sarveṇeti || 25.2 ||
prāṇena pañcavṛttinā maruto vāyūny praviśati | indraṃ balena praviśati | vṛttasvādhyāyasaṃpadbrahmavarcasena bṛhaspatiḥ | itareṇa sarveṇa cakṣurādinendriyavargeṇāgnim eva praviśati | evam alpāyur nirutsāho brahmavarcasahīnaś cakṣurādihīnaś cāvakīrṇī bhavati | ataś caritavyaṃ prāyaścittam || 25.2 ||
tadānīm āha |
so ‘māvāsyāyāṃ niśy agnim upasamādhāya
prāyaścittājyāhutīr huhoti || 25.3 ||
so ‘vakīrṇy amāvāsyāyāṃ niśy ardharātre gṛhyoktena mārgeṇāgniṃ pratiṣṭhāpyopasamādhāyājyabhāgānte prāyaścittarūpā ājyāhutīr juhoti || 25.3 ||
tatra mantrau |
kāmāvakīrṇo ‘smy avakīrṇo ‘smi kāma kamāya svāhā | kāmābhidugdho ‘smy abhidugdho ‘smi kāma kāmāya svāheti samidham ādhāyānuparyukṣya yajñavāstu kṛtvopotthāya samāsiñcatv ity etayā trir upatiṣṭheta || 25.4 ||
homānta ekāṃ samidhaṃ tūṣṇīm ādhāya “adite ‘nv amaṃsthāḥ” [ĀpGṛ 1.2.8] ityādibhir agnim anuparyukṣati | tato yajñavāstu karoti | atra chandogānāṃ gṛhye sviṣṭakṛto ‘nantaraṃ paṭhyate: “samidham ādhāya darbhān ājye havīṃṣi ca trir avadhāyāgramadhyamūlāny “aktaṃ rihāṇā vi yantu vayaḥ” [TaitS 1.1.13.1] ity abhyukṣyāgnāv anuprahared yaḥ (yo?) bhūtānām adhipatī rudras tanticaro vṛṣā paśūn asmākaṃ mā hiṃsīr etad astu hutaṃ tava svāheti tad yajñavāstu sarvatra kuryād iti | tad etad yajñavāstu kṛtvopotthāyāgnisamīpe sthitvā samāsiñcatu, ity etayā ca trir agnim upatiṣṭheta || 25.4 ||
trirupasthānasyārthavādaḥ |
traya ime lokā eṣāṃ lokānām abhijityā abhikrāntyā
iti || 25.5 ||
trayo hi lokā bhūr bhuvaḥ svar iti | teṣām abhijitir bhogayogyatāpādanam | abhikrāntis tatraivādhipatyenādhiṣṭhāyāvasthānam | tadartham evaṃ kartavyam iti saṃbandhaḥ || 25.5 ||
etad evaikeṣāṃ karmādhikṛtya yo ‘prayata iva syāt sa itthaṃ juhuyād ittham anumantrayeta varo dakṣiṇeti prāyaścittam aviśeṣāt || 25.6 ||
yo ‘pūta iva syād anyo ‘py ātmānam apūtam iva manyate na kevalam avakīrṇī so ‘py etad evoktaṃ karmādhikṛtyetthaṃ juhuyād ittham anumantrayeta homam upasthānaṃ caivaṃ kuryāt | varo dakṣiṇā | gaur vai varaḥ | sā svayaṃkartṛkatvād brahmaṇe deyeti śravaṇaviśeṣāt | aviśeṣeṇa sarveṣām upapātakinām idaṃ prāyaścittam ity ekeṣāṃ matam || 25.6 ||
anārjavapaiśunapratiṣiddhācārānādyaprāśaneṣu śūdrāyāṃ ca retaḥ siktvāyonau ca doṣavati ca karmaṇy api saṃdhipūrve ‘bliṅgābhir apa upaspṛśed vāruṇībhir anyair vā pavitraiḥ || 25.7 ||
anārjavaṃ śāṭhyam | paiśunaṃ paradoṣasūcanam | pratiṣiddhācāro niṣiddhānuṣṭhānam | anādyam abhakṣyaṃ tasya prāśanam | eteṣu śūdrāyāṃ retaḥ siktvāyonau cāsyādiṣu vā retaḥ siktvā, doṣavati karmaṇi parapīḍātmake steyātmake ca saṃdhipūrve buddhipūrve, apiśabdād abuddhipūrve kṛte ‘bliṅgābhir “āpo hi ṣṭhā mayobhuvaḥ” [RV 10.9.1] iti tisṛbhir “hiraṇyavarṇāḥ śucayaḥ pāvakāḥ” [TS 5.6.1.1] iti catasṛbhiḥ, vāruṇībhir “yat kiṃcedam imaṃ me varuṇa” [RV 7.89.5], “tat tvā yāmi” [RV 1.24.11], “ava te heḍaḥ” [RV 1.24.14] ity etābhir anyair vā pavitraiḥ pavamānaḥ “suvarjana” ityādibhir apa upaspṛśet pūrvaṃ snātvā paścād añjalinā mūrdhni mantrair etair apaḥ kṣipet | yady apy anādyaprāśanam api pratiṣiddhācāras tathāpi pṛthagupādānāt teṣu bahubhir mārjanam anādyaprāśane yathāsaṃbhavaṃ draṣṭavyam || 25.7 ||
pratiṣiddhavāṅmanasāpacāre vyāhṛtayaḥ pañca
satyāntāḥ || 25.8 ||
pratiṣiddhaviṣaye yo vāṅmanasayor apacāraḥ kutsitā pravṛttis tatra vyāhṛtayaḥ pañca japyā bhūrādayaḥ satyāntāḥ prathame ‘dhyāya uktāḥ | vāṅmanasor iti pāṭho ‘smabhyaṃ na rocate | acatureti samāsāntavidhiprasaṅgāt, pratiṣiddhagrahaṇasya ca duranvayatvāt || 25.8 ||
sarvāsv apo vācāmed ahaś ca mādityaś ca punātv iti prātā rātriś ca mā varuṇaś ca punātv iti sāyam || 25.9 ||
sarvāsu pāpakriyāsv anārjavādiṣv ābhyāṃ mantrābhyām apo ‘bhimantryācāmed “ahaś ca” iti prātaḥ pibed “rātriś ca” iti sāyaṃ pibet || 25.9 ||
aṣṭau vā samidha ādadhyād devakṛtasyeti hutvaiva
sarvasmād enaso mucyate [mucyate] || 25.10 ||
atha vā “devakṛtasya” [VS 8.13] ityādibhir mantrair aṣṭau samidha ādadhyāj juhuyāt | hutvaiva sarvasmād enaso na kevalam anārjavādibhyaḥ kiṃ tv ayājyayājanāder apy enaso mucyate | asya homasya mukhyatvapradarśanārtham evakāraḥ | hutvaivānyad akṛtveti | tataś ca sati saṃbhava idam eva jyāyaḥ | “devakṛtasyainaso ‘vayajanam asi svāhā” ityādayo ‘ṣṭau mantrāḥ [dviruktir uktārthā] || 25.10 ||
iti śrīgautamīyavṛttau haradattaviracitāyāṃ mitākṣarāyāṃ
tṛtīyapraśne saptamo ‘dhyāyaḥ
atha ṣaḍviṃśo ‘dhyāyaḥ
kṛcchrātikṛcchrau cāndrāyaṇam ity uktam | tatra krameṇa kṛcchrādisvarūpam āha |
athātaḥ kṛcchrān vyākhyāsyāmaḥ || 26.1 ||
athaśabdo ‘dhikārārthaḥ | ataḥśabdo hetau | ya eta ākhyātā na śakyante kartum ataḥ kṛcchrān vyākhyāsyāma iti || 26.1 ||
haviṣyān prātarāśān bhuktvā tisro rātrīr nāśnīyāt || 26.2 ||
yajñiyair varakādivyatiriktair niṣpāditān ghṛtādisaṃyuktān kṣāralavaṇavarjitān prātarāśān | aśnanta [aśyanta?] ity āśā grāsāḥ | divā bhojyān grāsān bhuktvā | “athāparaṃ tryaham” GDh 26.3] iti vakṣyamāṇatvād atrāpi tryaham iti gamyate | tisro rātrīr nāśnīyād iti tasmiṃs tryahe rātryaśanapratiṣedhaḥ śrutyanusāreṇa kṛtaḥ | punar ayaṃ vaktavyaḥ | katham –
sāyaṃ prātar dvijātīnām aśanaṃ śruticoditam |
iti parisaṃkhyānād dvir eva bhojanaṃ prāptam | tatra prātarāśān bhuktvety ukte parisaṃkhyānāt siddhā rātrāv aśananivṛttir yathottaratra divā bhojananivṛttiḥ || 26.2 ||
athāparaṃ tryahaṃ naktaṃ bhuñjīta || 26.3 ||
evaṃ divā haviṣyabhojanena tryahaṃ nītvānantaraṃ tryahaṃ naktam eva bhuñjīta | haviṣyān ity eva || 26.3 ||
athāparaṃ tryahaṃ na kaṃcana yāceta || 26.4 ||
atha naktabhojanatryahānantaraṃ tryahaṃ na kaṃcana bandhum api yāceta | yācñāpratiṣedho ‘yam | svadravyasya vāyācitalabdhasyāpratiṣedhaḥ | evam ukte haviṣyaniyamo na prāpnoti | kālaviśeṣāśravaṇād dvirbhojanaṃ ca prāpnoti | na yācetety atrāpi haviṣyān ity evānuvartate | ayācitalabdhe ‘pi sakṛd eva siddham | kutaḥ | athāparam iti vacanasya pūrveṇa sadṛśārthatvāt | tat tu divā naktaṃ vā yatheccham | anye tu “tryaham ayācitavrataḥ” [ĀpDh 1.27.7] ity āpastambīye darśanād ayācitalabdhenaiva tryahaṃ vṛttir na svadravyeṇa | nāpi yācitalabdheneti varṇayanti | anuṣṭhānam apy evam eva || 26.4 ||
athāparaṃ tryaham upavaset || 26.5 ||
spaṣṭam | evam ayaṃ dvādaśāhasādhyaḥ kṛcchraḥ | vasiṣṭhena prakārāntaram api darśitam |
ahaḥ prātar ahar naktam ahar ekam ayācitam |
ahaś copavased ekam evaṃ caturahau parau ||
anugrahārthaṃ viprāṇāṃ manur dharmabhṛtāṃ varaḥ |
bālavṛddhāturāṇāṃ ca śiśukṛcchram uvāca ha || iti | [VaDh 23.43]
bharadvājaḥ |
prājāpatyaṃ caran vipro yady aśakto dine dine |
viprān pañcāvarāñ śuddhān bhojayet samyag arcitān || iti |
yasmin dine ‘śaktis tatraivam, dināntareṣu pūrvavat | tatrāpy aśakto brāhmaṇabhojanam upavāsadineṣv aśakto vā brāhmaṇabhojanaṃ datvā haviṣyān samyag bhuñjīta || 26.5 ||
atha kṛcchrasya guṇavidhiḥ |
tiṣṭhed ahani rātrāv āsīta kṣiprakāmaḥ || 26.6 ||
yaḥ kāmayeta kṣipraṃ śudhyeyam iti sa tiṣṭhann evāhar nayet | bhojanādyavirodhena rātrāv āsīta | svāpo ‘py āsīnasyaiva | vasiṣṭhas tu kṣiprakāmasya prakārāntaram āha |
atha cet tvarate kartuṃ divasaṃ mārutāśanaḥ |
rātrau caiva jale tiṣṭhet prājāpatyena tat samam ||
sāvitryaṣṭasahasraṃ tu japyaṃ kṛtvotthite ravau |
mucyate pātakaiḥ sarvair yadi na bhrūṇahā bhavet || [VaDh 27.17–18] || 26.6 ||
satyaṃ vadet || 26.7 ||
satyaṃ yathādṛṣṭam | vivāhādiviṣaye ‘pi satyam eva vadet || 26.7 ||
anāryair na saṃbhāṣeta || 26.8 ||
dvijātivyatiriktair liṅgasyāvivakṣitatvāt tatstrībhir api na saṃbhāṣeta || 26.8 ||
rauravayaudhājape nityaṃ prayuñjīta || 26.9 ||
rauravayaudhājape sāmanī | “punānaḥ soma dhāraya” [RV 9.63.28] ity asyām ṛci gīte | nityaṃ pratyahaṃ prayuñjīta gāyet | apara āha – nityaṃ punaḥ prayuñjīteti || 26.9 ||
anusavanam udakopasparśanam āpo hi ṣṭheti tisṛbhiḥ pavitravatībhir mārjayīta hiraṇyavarṇāḥ śucayaḥ pāvakā ity aṣṭābhiḥ || 26.10 ||
udakopasparśanaṃ snānam | tadanuvasanaṃ triṣu savaneṣu kartavyam | tadanantaraṃ ca mārjanam āpo hi ṣṭhetyādibhiḥ | pavamānaḥ “suvarjanaḥ” ity anuvāke yā ṛcas tāḥ pavitravatyaḥ, liṅgasamavāyāt | tābhiś ca taittirīye pañcame kāṇḍe ṣaṣṭhe praśne “hiraṇyavarṇāḥ śucayaḥ pāvakāḥ” ityādyā ṛcaḥ paṭhyante | tā daś bhavanti | tatrāṣṭābhiḥ | yadi tu śākhāntare kvacid aṣṭāv eva paṭhyante tatas tā eva grāhyāḥ || 26.10 ||
athodakatarpaṇam || 26.11 ||
atha mārjanānantaram udakena tarpaṇaṃ kartavyam || 26.11 ||
tatra mantrāḥ |
namo ‘hamāya mohamāya maṃhamāya dhunvate tāpasāya punarvasave namaḥ | namo mauñjyāyorvyāya vasuvindāya sārvavindāya namaḥ | namaḥ pārāya supārāya mahāpārāya vārayiṣṇave namaḥ | namo rudrāya paśupataye mahate devāya tryambakāyaikacarāyādhipataye harāya śarvāyeśānāyogrāya vajriṇe ghṛṇine kapardine namaḥ | namaḥ sūryāyādityāya namaḥ | namo nīlagrīvāya śitikaṇṭhāya namaḥ | namaḥ kṛṣṇāya piṅgalāya namaḥ | namo jyeṣṭhāya śreṣṭhāya vṛddhāyendrāya harikeśāyordhvaretase namaḥ | namaḥ satyāya pāvakāya pāvakavarṇāya kāmāya kāmarūpiṇe namaḥ | namo dīptāya dīptarūpiṇe namaḥ | namas tīkṣṇāya tīkṣṇarūpiṇe namaḥ | namaḥ sobhyāya supuruṣāya mahāpuruṣāya madhyamapuruṣāyottamapuruṣāya brahmacāriṇe namaḥ | namaś candralalāṭāya kṛttivāsase namaḥ || 26.12 ||
nāyam eko mantraḥ, etāś cājyāhutaya iti bahuvacananirdeśāt | kiṃ tarhi | trayodaśaite mantrāḥ | namaskārādayo namaskārāntāś ca sarve | tatra prathame caturthyantāni ṣaḍ devasya nāmāni | dvitīye catvāri | tathā tṛtīye | caturthe trayodaśa | mahate devāyeti mahādevapadam eva vyastam uktam | pañcamādiṣu triṣu dve | aṣṭame ṣaṭ | namave pañca | daśame dve | tathaikādaśe | dvādaśe ṣaṭ | iti ṣaṭpañcāśad devanāmāni | ebhir mantrais tarpaṇam anusavanam || 26.12 ||
etad evādityopasthānam || 26.13 ||
āditya upasthīyate yena tad ādityasyopasthānam | etena kṛtsnena mantreṇāditya upastheya ity uktaṃ bhavati | etad apy anusavanaṃ pratyaham | sakṛd ity anye, pṛthag yogakaraṇāt | anyathāthodakatarpaṇam ādityopasthānaṃ cety ekam eva yogam akariṣyat || 26.13 ||
etā evājyāhutayaḥ || 26.14 ||
etā iti mantram api parāmṛśati | etac chandasyāhutisāmānādhikaraṇyāt strīliṅgatā | etair eva trayodaśabhir mantrair ājyam api hotavyam ity uktaṃ bhavati | tatra “juhoticodanā svāhākārapradānā” iti svāhākārān tair homaḥ pratyahaṃ sakṛt kartavyaḥ || 26.14 ||
dvādaśarātrasyānte caruṃ śrapayitvaitābhyo devatābhyo
juhuyāt || 26.15 ||
evam uktena prakāreṇa dvādaśarātraṃ nītvā tadante trayodaśe ‘hani gṛhyoktena mārgeṇa caruṃ śrapayitvaitābhyo vakṣyamāṇābhyo devatābhyo juhuyāt || 26.15 ||
tā āha |
agnaye svāhā somāya svāhāgnīṣomābhyām indrāgnibhyām viśvebhyo devebhyo brahmaṇe prajāpataye ‘gnaye sviṣṭakata iti || 26.16 ||
sviṣṭakṛtā saha navāhutayaḥ | dviḥ svāhākārapāṭho ‘nuṣaṅgaprakāradarśanārthaḥ | sarva eva svāhākārāntāḥ [ity arthaḥ] || 26.16 ||
tato brāhmaṇatarpaṇam || 26.17 ||
tato homānantaraṃ brāhmaṇās tarpayitavyā bhojanādibhiḥ | “śucīn mantravataḥ sarvakṛtyeṣu bhojayet” [ĀpDh 2.15.11] ity āpastambaḥ || 26.17 ||
etenaivātikṛcchro vyākhyātaḥ || 26.18 ||
spaṣṭam || 26.18 ||
yas tv asya viśeṣas tam āha |
yāvat sakṛd ādadīta tāvad aśnīyāt || 26.19 ||
ekena pāṇinā yāvat sakṛd ādātuṃ śaknuyāt tāvad evāśnīyāt | haviṣyaṃ divā naktam ayācitam upavāsa iti viśeṣāḥ sthitā eva | atra manuḥ |
ekaikaṃ grāsam aśnīyāt tryahāṇi trīṇi pūrvavat |
tryahaṃ copavased antyam iti kṛcchraḥ sa ucyate || [MDh11.213] || 26.19 ||
abbhakṣas tṛtīyaḥ sa kṛcchrātikṛcchraḥ || 26.20 ||
pūrvokteṣv eva bhojanakāleṣu kevalam udakam eva pibet sa eṣa tṛtīyaḥ kṛcchrātikṛcchro nāma veditavyaḥ | atropavāsadineṣv ācamanavyatirekeṇodakapānam api na bhavati | ta ete trayaḥ kṛcchrā uktāḥ || 26.20 ||
teṣu –
prathamaṃ caritvā śuciḥ pūtaḥ karmaṇyo
bhavati || 26.21 ||
prathamaṃ prājāpatyaṃ caritvā śuciḥ,
saṃdhyāhīno ‘śucir nityam anarhaḥ sarvakarmasu |
ityādinā vihitākaraṇanimittena doṣeṇa hīnaḥ | pūtaḥ pratiṣiddhācaraṇajanyenādharmeṇa rahitaḥ | karmaṇyaḥ karmasu yogyaś ca bhavati | karmaṇya iti vacanād aprajñātadoṣasyāpi kṛcchrānuṣṭhānād iṣṭeṣu karmasu yogyateti jñāpyate || 26.21 ||
dvitīyaṃ caritvā yat kiṃcid anyan mahāpātakebhyaḥ
pāpaṃ kurute tasmāt pramucyate || 26.22 ||
dvitīyam atikṛcchraṃ caritvā mahāpātakavyatiriktaiḥ pāpair mucyate || 26.22 ||
tṛtīyaṃ caritvā sarvasmād enaso mucyate || 26.23 ||
tṛtīyaṃ kṛcchrātikṛcchraṃ caritvā sarvasmān mahāpātakād apy enaso ‘nabhisaṃdhikṛtān mucyate || 26.23 ||
evaṃ vyastānāṃ phalam uktvā samastānām āha |
athaitāṃs trīn kṛcchrāṃś caritvā sarveṣu vedeṣu snāto
bhavati sarvair devair jñāto bhavati || 26.24 ||
ya etāṃs trīn kṛcchrān avyavadhānenānutiṣṭhati tasya sarvān vedān adhītya snātasya yat phalaṃ tattulyaṃ phalaṃ bhavati | sarveṣāṃ devānāṃ lokā jitās tena || 26.24 ||
atha viduṣaḥ praśaṃsā |
yaś caivaṃ veda [yaś caivaṃ veda] || 26.25 ||
yaś caitān kṛcchrān svarūpeṇetikartavyatayā phalena vijānāti so ‘pi sarveṣu vedeṣu snāto bhavati | sarvair devair jñāto bhavati | evaṃ jñātaṃ praśastam ity arthaḥ | [dviruktir uktārthā] || 26.25 ||
iti śrīgautamīyavṛttau haradattaviracitāyāṃ mitākṣarāyāṃ
tṛtīyapraśne ‘ṣṭamo ‘dhyāyaḥ
atha saptaviṃśo ‘dhyāyaḥ
athātaś cāndrāyaṇam || 27.1 ||
pūrvavad vyākhyeyam | candraprāptinimittabhūtaṃ karma cāndrāyaṇam | tathā cānte vakṣyati – candramasaḥ salokatām āpnotīti || 27.1 ||
tasyokto vidhiḥ kṛcchre || 27.2 ||
“tiṣṭhed ahani” [GDh 26.6] ityadiko yo vidhiḥ kṛcchra uktaḥ sa cāndrāyaṇasyāpi draṣṭavyaḥ || 27.2 ||
yas tu viśeṣaḥ sa ucyate |
vapanaṃ vrataṃ caret || 27.3 ||
vratam iti prāyaścittam āha,
etair dvijātayaḥ śodhyā vratair āviṣkṛtainasaḥ | [MDh 11.226]
ityādau darśanāt | yadi prāyaścittārthaṃ cāndrāyaṇaṃ kriyate tadā vapanam api kartavyam | aviśeṣe ‘pi puruṣāṇām eva | “tad eva striyāḥ keśavapanavarjam” [BDh 2.2.45] iti baudhāyanasmaraṇam | cāndrāyaṇe vapanavidhānāt kṛcchre prāyaścittārthe ‘pi na bhavati | vrataṃ cared iti vacanād adṛṣṭārthe karmaṇyatārthe ca cāndrāyaṇe na vapanam || 27.3 ||
śvobhūtāṃ paurṇamāsīm upavaset || 27.4 ||
śvaḥ paurṇamāsī bhavitety avagamya pūrvedyuś caturdaśyām upavaset | upavāso bhojanalopaḥ
|| 27.4 ||
ā pyāyasva saṃ te payāṃsi navo nava iti caitābhis tarpaṇam ājyahomo haviṣaś cānumantraṇam upasthānaṃ candramasaḥ || 27.5 ||
āpyāyasvetyādibhir mantrais tarpaṇādīni catvāri karmāṇi kartavyāni | vaiṣamyād yathāsaṃkhyaṃ na bhavati | tatra tarpaṇahomau pratimantraṃ bhavataḥ | anumantraṇam upasthānaṃ ca samuccayena | kṛcchravidhyatideśād raudreṇa ya udakatarpaṇādayaḥ prāptās teṣāṃ ca samuccaya ity eke | upadiṣṭair atidiṣṭānāṃ bādha ity anye || 27.5 ||
yad devā devaheḍanam iti catasṛbhir juhuyāt || 27.6 ||
“yad devā devaheḍanam” [VS 20.14] ity anuvāka āditaś catasṛbhir ṛgbhir anādeśād ājyaṃ juhuyāt | pūrvābhis tisṛbhiś ceti saptājyāhutayaḥ || 27.6 ||
devakṛtasyeti cānte samidbhiḥ || 27.7 ||
ājyahomānte “devakṛtasya” [VS 8.13] ityādibhiḥ pūrvoktair aṣṭabhir mantraiḥ samidbhir homaḥ kartavyaḥ | upadeśakramād eva siddhe ‘ntagrahaṇaṃ prāptānuvādaḥ | anye punaś cāndrāyaṇānta iti vyācakṣate | teṣāṃ caśabdo na saṃgacchate || 27.7 ||
oṃ bhūr bhuvaḥ svas tapaḥ satyaṃ yaśaḥ śrīr ūrgiḍ aujas tejo varcaḥ puruṣo dharmaḥ śiva ity etair grāsānumantraṇaṃ pratimantraṃ manasā || 27.8 ||
praṇavādayaḥ pañcadaśa mantrās teṣām ekaikena mantreṇaikaikasya grāsasya manasānumantraṇaṃ kartavyam | anumantraṇakrameṇa bhojanam | yadā tu nyūnā grāsās tadā yāvad grāsam ādito mantrā grāhyāḥ | antato lupyante | grāsānumantraṇam iti vacanān naite bhojanamantrāḥ | tataś ca prāṇāhutimantrāṇām anivṛttiḥ | yadā catvāro grāsās tadā dvābhyāṃ pūrvaṃ yadā trayo dvābhyāṃ dvābhyāṃ pūrvau yadā dvau dvābhyāṃ pūrvam uttaraṃ tribhiḥ | sarvair ekam | haviṣaś cānumantraṇam iti pūrvoktam iha tu grāsānumantraṇam iti prāṇāhutimantrāś ca sthitāḥ | tatra prayogaḥ – sarvaṃ bhojyaṃ pātre nidhāyāpyāyasvetyadibhir anumantrya grāsān kṛtvā praṇavādibhiḥ krameṇānumantrya prāṇāhutīḥ kṛtvā prāśnīyād iti
|| 27.8 ||
namaḥ svāheti vā sarvān || 27.9 ||
atha vā sarvān eva grāsān namaḥ svāhety anumantrayet | namaḥ svāhety anayor vikalpaḥ | samudito mantra ity anye || 27.9 ||
grāsapramāṇam āsyāvikāreṇa || 27.10 ||
yāvatpramāṇe grāse grasyamāna āsyam avikṛtaṃ bhavati tāvat tasya pramāṇam || 27.10 ||
carubhaikṣasaktukaṇayāvakaśākapayodadhighṛtamūla-phalodakāni havīṃṣy uttarottaraṃ praśastāni || 27.11 ||
haviṣyair upakalpito navasrāvito viśadasiddhaudanaś caruḥ | bhaikṣaṃ brahmacāriṇā śiṣyādinā svayam ānītam, gṛhasthasya bhikṣācaraṇaniṣedhāt | cūrṇīkṛtā lājāḥ saktavaḥ | kaṇāḥ phalīkaraṇāni | yāvakaḥ pūrvam uktaḥ | anyāni prasiddhāni | dvādaśaitāni havīṃṣi | teṣu ca pūrvasmāt pūrvasmād uttaram uttaraṃ praśastam | tatra dravāṇāṃ patrapuṭādinā grāsakalpanā | tapāṃsi cainaḥsu guruṣu gurūṇi laghuṣu laghūni || 27.11 ||
paurṇamāsyāṃ pañcadaśa grāsān
bhuktvaikāpacayenāparapakṣam aśnīyāt || 27.12 ||
evaṃ caturdaśyām upoṣyāparedyuḥ pañcadaśyāṃ pañcadaśa grāsān aśitvā tataḥ param ekāpacayena dvirvacane saty artha spaṣṭo bhavati pratyaham ekaikāpacayeneti | sarvam evāparapakṣam aśnīyāt | tithihrāse kramaprāpte navamībhojane yadā prātaḥ pañca nāḍyo navamī, aparedyuś ca daśamī nāsti tadā pūrvedur āgatāyām eva navamyāṃ nava grāsān bhuktvāparedyur ekādaśīprāptān ekādaśa grāsān bhuñjīta | daśamīprāptānāṃ daśagrāsānāṃ lopaḥ | evaṃ tithivṛddhāv ekādaśīgrāse prāpte yadā ṣaḍviṃśatināḍikā divā daśamī catasra ekādaśī, aparedyu rātrāv api kiyaty apy ekādaśī tadā pūrvedyur ekādaśyāṃ praniviṣṭāyām ekādaśa grāsān bhuktvāparedyur api tān evaikādaśa bhuñjīta | tasyāparedyur dvādaśeti prayogaḥ |
yathā kathaṃcit piṇḍānāṃ tisro ‘śītīḥ samāhitaḥ |
māsenāśnan haviṣyasya candrasyeti salokatām || [MDh 11.220]
iti mānave cāndrāyaṇāntaraṃ vidhīyate | na punar upacayāpacayarūpa ukte cāndrāyaṇe piṇḍasaṃkhyāniyamaḥ | tathā ca yājñavalkyena spaṣṭam uktam |
yathā kathaṃcit piṇḍānāṃ catvāriṃśac chatadvayam |
māsenaikena bhuñjīta cāndrāyaṇam athāparam || iti [YDh 3.324] || 27.12 ||
amāvāsyāyām upoṣyaikopacayena pūrvapakṣam || 27.13 ||
evam ekāpacayena grasyamāneṣu caturdaśyām eko grāso bhavati | amāvāsyāyām upavāsaḥ | amāvāsyāyām upoṣya pūrvpakṣapratipady ekaṃ grāsam aśitvaikaikopacayenaikaikagrāsavṛddhyā kṛtsnam eva pūrvapakṣaṃ aśnīyāt | paurṇamāsyāṃ pañcadaśa bhavanti | tad etat tanumadhyatvāt pipīlikāmadhyaṃ cāndrāyaṇam || 27.13 ||
viparītam ekeṣām || 27.14 ||
ekeṣām ācāryāṇāṃ matenedam eva vidhānaṃ viparītaṃ bhavati | amāvāsyāyām upoṣyaikopacayena pūrvapakṣam aśitvā kṛṣṇapratipadam ārabhyaikāpacayenāparapakṣam aśnīyāc caturdaśyām eko grāso bhavati | amāvāsyāyām upavāsaḥ | tad etat sthūlamadhyatvād yavamadhyaṃ cāndrāyaṇam || 27.14 ||
evaṃ cāndrāyaṇo māsaḥ || 27.15 ||
evaṃ māsasādhyaṃ cāndrāyaṇaṃ tadyogād eṣa māsaś cāndrāyaṇaḥ | yady apy ukte prakāre pipīlikāmadhye dvātriṃśad ahāni yavamadhye caikatriṃśat tathāpi na vaikenākṣareṇeti nyāyenaiṣa māsa ity uktam || 27.15 ||
evam āptvā vipāpo vipāpmā sarvam eno hanti || 27.16 ||
evam evaṃvidhaṃ cāndrāyaṇaṃ māsam āptvā māsasādhyam etad vrataṃ kṛtvā vipāpo vihitākaraṇajanyapāpahīno bhavati | vipāpmā niṣiddhācaraṇabhavapāpahīnaḥ | sarvam eno hanti yac cānyaj janmāntarārjitaṃ sūkṣmam enas tad api sarvaṃ hanti || 27.16 ||
dvitīyam āptvā daśa pūrvān daśa parān ātmānaṃ
caikaviṃśaṃ paṅktiṃ ca punāti || 27.17 ||
dvāv āptveti vaktavye dvitīyam iti vacanaṃ nairantaryārthaṃ dvitīyaṃ māsaṃ nirantaram āptveti | kathaṃ punar nairantaryasya saṃbhavaḥ | yāvatā pipīlikāmadhye śvobhūtāṃ paurṇamāsīm upavased ity uktaṃ paurṇamāsyāṃ pañcadaśa grāsān bhuktveti ca tathāmāvāsyāyām upoṣyaikaikopacayena pūrvapakṣam aśnīyād iti taddvitīyapaurṇamāsyantaḥ sa prayogaḥ | tadanantaraṃ dvitīyasyārambhe caturdaśyām upavāsaḥ | pañcadaśyāṃ pañcadaśa grāsān iti ca nopapadyate | tasmād evam atra vaktavyam – nātra dvayoś cāndrāyaṇayor vidhānam | kiṃ tarhi | māsadvayasādhyam ekaṃ cāndrāyaṇam | tasyaiṣa phalavidhiḥ | tasyādau caturdaśyām upavāsas tṛtīye paurṇamāsyantaś ca prayogaḥ | madhye yathoktam | dvitīyā ca paurṇamāsī tantreṇa prathamasyāntyā dvitīyasyādyā | evaṃ yavamadhye dvitīyāmāvāsyā | etena saṃvatsaraṃ vāptveti vyākhyātam || 27.17 ||
saṃvatsaraṃ cāptvā candrasamaḥ salokatām āpnoti
salokatām āpnoti || 27.18 ||
yas tu saṃvatsaram avyavadhānena cāndrāyaṇavrataṃ carati sa candramasaḥ sālokyam āpnoti | dviruktir vyākhyātā | atra manuḥ |
aṣṭāv aṣṭau samaśnīyāt piṇḍān madhaṃdine sthite |
niyatātmā haviṣyasya yaticāndrāyaṇaṃ caran ||
caturaḥ prātar aśnīyād dvijaḥ piṇḍān samāhitaḥ |
caturo ‘stamite sūrye śiśucāndrāyaṇaṃ caran || iti | [MDh 11.218–219]
“yathā kathaṃcit piṇḍānām” [MDh 11.220] iti ca || 27.18 ||
iti śrīgautamīyavṛttau haradattaviracitāyāṃ mitākṣarāyāṃ
tṛtīyapraśne navamo ‘dhyāyaḥ
atha aṣṭāviṃśo ‘dhyāyaḥ
atha dāyavibhāgaḥ |
ūrdhvaṃ pituḥ putrā rikthaṃ bhajeran || 28.1 ||
ūrdhvaṃ pitur pitari mṛte tadīyaṃ rikthaṃ svagṛhakṣetradāsagavāśvasvarṇādikaṃ putrā bhajeran, putrās tatra bhāginaḥ | putrāṇāṃ tatsvāmitvam ity uktaṃ bhavati | ūrdhvaṃ pitur iti vacanāj jīvati tasmin na tatra putrāṇāṃ svāmyam | tathā ca manuḥ |
ūrdhvaṃ pituś ca mātuś ca sametya bhrātaraḥ saha |
bhajeran paitṛkaṃ riktham anīśās te hi jīvatoḥ || iti | [MDh 9.104]
pitṛśabdasya saṃbandhiśabdatvād eva siddhe putragrahaṇaṃ niyamārtham | tena pitur ūrdhvaṃ vibhajatāṃ mātāpy aṃśaṃ samaṃ hared ityādivacanajātam ācāryasyābhimataṃ na bhavati | putrā evaṃ sarvaṃ dhanādikaṃ gṛhītvā mātaraṃ yathāvad rekṣeyur iti manyate | śrūyate ca: “tasmāt striyo nirindriyā adāyādāḥ” iti | manur apy āha |
pitā rakṣati kaumāre bhartā rakṣati yauvane |
putrās tu sthavirībhāve na strī svātantryam arhati || iti [MDh 9.3] || 28.1 ||
nivṛtte rajasi mātur jīvati cecchati || 28.2 ||
atha vā jīvaty api pitari putrā rikthaṃ bhajerann iti | icchati sati | tadanujñayety arthaḥ | tasya kālaḥ – nivṛtte rajasi mātuḥ | uparatarajaskāyāṃ nivṛttaprasavāyām ity uktaṃ bhavati || 28.2 ||
sarvaṃ vā pūrvajasyetarān bibhṛyāt pitṛvat || 28.3 ||
jyeṣṭha eva sarvaṃ dhanaṃ svīkṛtya gṛhītvetarān kaniṣṭhān bibhṛyāt | te ‘pi tasmin putravad bhajeran || 28.3 ||
vibhāge tu dharmavṛddhiḥ || 28.4 ||
tuśabdaḥ pakṣaṃ vyāvartayati | naitad evaṃ jyeṣṭha eva bibhṛyād iti | yad uktaṃ vibhāga eva jyāyān yatas tatra dharmavṛddhiḥ | yathāha bṛhaspatiḥ |
ekapākena vasatāṃ pitṛdevadvijārcanam |
ekaṃ bhaved vibhaktānāṃ tad eva syād gṛhe gṛhe || 28.4 ||
adhunā pitur ūrdhvaṃ jīvati ca tasmin vibhāgaprakāram āha |
viṃśatibhāgo jyeṣṭhasya mithunam ubhayatodadyukto
ratho govṛṣaḥ || 28.5 ||
sarvasmāt pitṛdhanād viṃśatitamo bhāgaḥ mithunaṃ gavādiṣu strīpuṃsayor yugmam | ubahatodantā aśvāśvataragardabhās teṣām anyatamābhyāṃ yukto rathaḥ govṛṣaḥ puṃgavaḥ | ayam uddhāro jyeṣṭhasya || 28.5 ||
kāṇakhorakūṭavaṇeṭā madhamasyānekāś cet || 28.6 ||
kāṇa ekanetraḥ | vikalāṅga iti yāvat | khoro vṛddhaḥ | khoṭa iti pāṭhe vikalapādaḥ | kūṭaḥ śṛṅgahīnaḥ | vaṇeṭo vikalavāladhiḥ | gavāśvādiṣu ya evaṃrūpaḥ sa madhyamasyoddhāraḥ | sa ca kāṇādir yady aneko bhavati | itareṣām apy asti coditi || 28.6 ||
avir dhānyāyasī gṛham anoyuktaṃ catuṣpadāṃ
caikaikaṃ yavīyasaḥ || 28.7 ||
avir ūrṇāyuḥ | jātāv ekavacanam | yāvanto ‘vayaḥ, ekasya catuṣpadāṃ caikaikam ity eva siddhatvāt | apara āha: yady api pitur eka evāvis tathāpi sa yavīyasaḥ | catuṣpadāṃ caikaikam iti tu bahuviṣayam iti | dhānyaṃ vrīhyādi | aya āyasaṃ dātrādi | dhānyam ayaś ceti dhānyāyasī | etad ubhayaṃ yāvat kiṃcid gṛhe | gṛhaṃ yatrāsyate | anaḥ śakaṭaṃ yuktaṃ bāhyābhyām | catuṣpadāṃ ca gavādīnām ekam iṣṭaṃ gṛhṇīyāt | ayaṃ kanīyasa uddhāraḥ | ayaṃ ca sarvakanīyasaḥ | itareṣām uddhāro yo madhyamasya || 28.7 ||
samadhā cetarat sarvam || 28.8 ||
itarad uddhṛtaśiṣṭaṃ sarvaṃ sarve samadhā gṛhṇīyuḥ | samam ity arthaḥ | dvidhā bahudhetyādau dṛṣṭo dhāpratyayaḥ prayuktaḥ || 28.8 ||
ekaikaṃ vā dhanarūpaṃ kāmyaṃ pūrvaḥ pūrvo
labhate || 28.9 ||
kalpāntareṣu bahuṣu kṣetrādiṣv ekaikaṃ dhanarūpaṃ jyeṣṭhānupūrvyād gṛhṇīyuḥ | kāmyaṃ yasya yad iṣṭaṃ sa tad gṛhṇīyād iti | sarveṣv iṣṭaṃ jyeṣṭas tadrahiteṣv iṣṭam anantara iti | ayam uddhāraḥ sarveṣām || 28.9 ||
atraiva paśuṣu viśeṣaḥ |
daśakaṃ paśūnām || 28.10 ||
daśāvayavā asya daśakaḥ | paśūnāṃ gavādīnāṃ madhye daśakaṃ daśakaṃ pūrvo labhate na tv ekam iti || 28.10 ||
asyāpavādaḥ |
naikaśaphadvipadām || 28.11 ||
ekaśaphānām aśvādīnāṃ dvipadāṃ dāsyādīnāṃ ca daśakaṃ na gṛhṇīyuḥ | kiṃ tu pūrvoktam aikaikam eveti | dvipādānām iti pāṭhe pādaśabdena samānarthaḥ padaśabdaḥ | evam ekamātṛkāṇāṃ soddhāro vibhāga uktaḥ || 28.11 ||
athānekamātṛkāṇām āha |
ṛṣabho ‘dhiko jyeṣṭhasya || 28.12 ||
uttarasūtre jyaiṣṭhineyasyeti vacanād ayaṃ jyeṣṭhaḥ kāniṣṭhineyaḥ | yadi kanīyasyāḥ putro bhavati tadā tasya ṛṣabha uddhāraḥ | samam anyat || 28.12 ||
ṛṣabhaṣoḍaśā jyaiṣṭhineyasya || 28.13 ||
jyeṣṭhasyeti vartate | jyeṣṭhāyāḥ putraś ca bhavati yo jyeṣṭhasya bhavati tasya pañcadaśa gāva ṛṣabhaś caika uddhāraḥ | samam anyat || 28.13 ||
atha ṛṣabho ‘dhiko jyeṣṭhasyety asyāpavādaḥ |
samadhā vājyaiṣṭhineyena yavīyasām || 28.14 ||
jyeṣṭhasyeti vatate | tac cājyaiṣṭhineyenety anena sāmānādhikaraṇyāt tṛtīyāntaṃ saṃpadyate | ajyaiṣṭhineyena kaniṣṭhāyāṃ jātena jyeṣṭhena saha yavīyasāṃ jyaiṣṭhineyānāṃ samo vā vibhāgaḥ | ekasya janmato jyaiṣṭhyam anyeṣāṃ mātṛta iti || 28.14 ||
pratimātṛ vā svasvavarge bhāgaviśeṣaḥ || 28.15 ||
viṃśatibhāgo jyeṣṭasyetyādir ya ukto bhāgaviśeṣaḥ sa pratimātṛ vā sve sve varge viśeṣaḥ kartavyaḥ | etad uktaṃ bhavati – yāvatyo mātaraḥ putravatyas tāvatā vibhakte dhana ekasyā yāvantaḥ putrās teṣāṃ bhāgān ekīkṛtya tatra tatra varge yo yo jyeṣṭhas tasya viṃśatibhāgo jyeṣṭhasyetyādir bhāgaviśeṣa iti | evaṃ putravato vibhāga uktaḥ || 28.15 ||
athāputrasyāha |
pitotsṛjet putrikām anaptyo ‘gniṃ prajāpatiṃ
ceṣṭvāsmadartham apatyam iti saṃvādya || 28.16 ||
pitā nāma tām utsṛjed dadyāt | bhāvisaṃjñānirdeśo ‘yam | yathā yūpaṃ chinattīti | putrikāṃ bhaviṣyantī duhitaram anapatyo ‘putro ‘gniṃ prajāpatiṃ ceṣṭvāgnaye svāhā prajāpataye svāhety ājyabhāgān anantaram aupāsana ājyena hutvāsmadartham apatyam iti saṃvādya yasmai dadāti tena saṃvādaṃ kārayitvā | tatra prakāro vasiṣṭhena darśitaḥ |
abhrātṛkāṃ pradāsyāmi tubhyaṃ kanyām alaṃkṛtām |
asyāṃ janiṣyate putraḥ sa me putro bhaved iti || [VaDh 17.17]
evaṃ dattā sā putrikā tasyāṃ jāto mātāmahasyaiva putro notpādayituḥ | ata eva manuḥ |
mātuḥ prathamataḥ piṇḍaṃ nirvapet putrikāsutaḥ |
dvitīyaṃ tu pitus tasyās tṛtīyaṃ tu pituḥ pituḥ || iti | [MDh 9.140]
evaṃ sarve garbhāḥ putrikāpy eṣā pituḥ putrapratinidhiḥ | “ive pratikṛtau” [Pāṇ 5.3.96] saṃjñāyām kann iti | saiva ca rikthagrāhiṇī | tathā ca manuḥ |
putrikāyāṃ kṛtāyāṃ tu yadi putro ‘nujāyate |
samas tatra vibhāgaḥ syāj jyeṣṭhatā nāsti hi striyāḥ || iti | [MDh 9.134]
gotram api tasyāḥ pitur eva gotram | bhartus tu kevalaṃ dharmeṣu sahacāriṇī ratiphalā ca | putrārthaṃ tu vivāhāntaraṃ kartavyaṃ svakulasaṃtānārtham anyathā doṣaḥ || 28.16 ||
abhisaṃdhimātrāt putrikety ekeṣām || 28.17 ||
eke manyate pradānasamaye pitur yo ‘bhisaṃdhir iyaṃ me putrikāstv iti tāvanmātrakād eva duhitā putrikā bhavati na homasaṃvādanādyapekṣeti || 28.17 ||
tataś ca |
tatsaṃśayān nopayacched abhrātṛkām || 28.18 ||
tatsaṃśayād abhisaṃdhisaṃśayāt putrikāsaṃśayād vā | manur apy āha |
yasyās tu na bhaved bhrātā na vijñayeta vā pitā |
nopayaccheta tāṃ prājñaḥ putrikādharmaśaṅkayā || iti [MDh 3.11] || 28.18 ||
piṇḍagotrarṣisaṃbandhā rikthaṃ bhajeran strī
vānapatyasya || 28.19 ||
yasya putrikārūpam apy apatyaṃ nāsti so ‘napatyaḥ | tasya rikthaṃ piṇḍādisaṃbandhā bhajeran strī vā | piṇḍasaṃbandhāḥ sapiṇḍāḥ | gotrasaṃbandhāḥ sagotrāḥ | hārītasya hārīta itivat | ṛṣisaṃbandhāḥ samānapravarāḥ haritakutsapiśaṅgaśaṅkhadarbhahaimagavāḥ parasparam | evam anyatrāpi | tatra sapiṇḍādyāḥ pratyāsattikrameṇa gṛhṇīyuḥ | tathā ca āpastambaḥ: “putrābhāve yaḥ pratyāsannaḥ sapiṇḍaḥ” [ĀpDh 2.14.2] iti | tad yathā – pitā mātā ca sodaryas tatputrā bhinnodarā bhrātaras tatputrāḥ pitṛvya ityādi | sapiṇḍābhāve sagotrās tadabhāve samānapravarāḥ | strī tu sarvaiḥ sagorādibhiḥ samuccīyate | yadā sapiṇḍādayo gṛhṇanti tadā taiḥ saha patny apy ekam aṃśaṃ haret | tathā |
pitur ūrdhvaṃ vibhajatāṃ mātāpy aṃśaṃ samaṃ haret | iti | [YDh 2.123]
ata eva strī pṛthaṅnirdiṣṭā | sapiṇḍādayaḥ samānena | patnīdāyas tv ācāryasya pakṣe na bhavati | manur api |
nirindriyā adāyādā striyo nityam iti sthitiḥ || iti | [MDh 9.18]
atra sapiṇḍādyabhāve bṛhaspatiḥ |
anyatra brāhmaṇāt kiṃ tu rājā dharmaparāyaṇaḥ |
tat strīṇāṃ jīvanaṃ dadyād eṣa dāyavidhiḥ smrtaḥ ||
annārthaṃ taṇḍulaprastham aparāhṇe tu sendhanam |
vasanaṃ tripaṇakrītaṃ deyam ekaṃ trimāsataḥ ||
etāvad eva sādhvīnāṃ coditaṃ vidhināśanam || iti |
tad evaṃ manu-bṛhaspatibhyāṃ patnīdāyasyātyantābhāva uktaḥ | yājñavalkyena tu patnīdāyaḥ sa uktaḥ: “patnī dutaraś ca” [YDh 2.135] ityādi | atra vyāsaḥ |
dvisahasrapaṇo dāyaḥ patnyai deyo dhanasya tu |
yac ca bhartrā dhanaṃ dattaṃ sā yathākāmam āpnuyāt || iti |
ācāryeṇa tu sapiṇḍādisamāṃśagrahaṇam uktam | tatra sarvam eva dhanaṃ sapiṇḍādyā gṛhītvā striyo yāvajjīvaṃ rakṣeyur iti mukhyaḥ kalpaḥ | tadasaṃbhave ‘śanavasanayoḥ paryāptaṃ dhanakṣetrādikam aṃśatvena vyapohya śeṣaṃ gṛhṇīyuḥ | tathā ca bṛhaspatinā patnīdāyaṃ pratiṣiddhyānta uktam |
vasanasyāśanasyaiva tathaiva rajatasya ca |
trayaṃ vyapohya tacchiṣṭaṃ dāyādānāṃ prakalpayet ||
dhūmāvasārikaṃ dravyaṃ sahāyās tān ataḥ purā |
tathaivāśanavāsāṃsi vigaṇayya dhane mṛtā || iti || 28.19 ||
bījaṃ vā lipseta || 28.20 ||
atha vā strī sapiṇḍādibhyo bījaṃ lipseta | apatyam utpādayed ity uktaṃ bhavati | asmin pakṣe tu na sapiṇḍādyā dhanaṃ gṛhṇīyur eṣyanto ‘patyasyārthāya rakṣeyuḥ || 28.20 ||
asmin pakṣe viśeṣaḥ |
devaravatyām anyajātam abhāgam || 28.21 ||
devare vidyamāne yady anyato bījaṃ lipseta tatas tasyāṃ jātam apatyam abhāgaṃ bhāgarahitam | na tasya dhanagrahaṇam asti | asati tu devare ‘nyato jātam apy apatyaṃ sabhāgam eva || 28.21 ||
strīdhanaṃ duhitṝṇām aprattānām apratiṣṭhitānāṃ
ca || 28.22 ||
pitṛmātṛsutabhrātṛdattam adhyagny upāgatam |
ādhivedanikādyaṃ ca strīdhanaṃ parikīrtitam || [YDh 2.143]
iti yājñavalkyaḥ | tat strīdhanaṃ tasyāṃ mṛtāyāṃ duhitṝṇām aprattānāṃ bhavati | yadi sarvā api prattā apratiṣṭhitānāṃ bhavati | prattāsu pratiṣṭhitāḥ kāścit kāścid apratiṣṭhitāḥ | prattāprattāsamavāye ‘prattā gṛhṇāti | pratiṣṭhitāpratiṣṭhitāsamavāye ‘pratiṣṭhitā gṛhṇāti | yadā prasūtādidhanaṃ tadā sarvāsāṃ bhavati | eṣā mātur ūrdhvaṃ jīvantyāṃ pitṛkulalabdhasya strīdhanasya gatiḥ | tathā ca manuḥ |
mātus tu yautakaṃ yat syāt kumārībhāga eva saḥ | [MDh 9.131]
vasiṣṭhaś ca “mātuḥ pāriṇeyaṃ striyo vibhajeran” [VaDh 17.46] iti | yat tu śākkhalikhitābhyām uktam: “samaṃ sarve sodaryā mātṛkaṃ dravyam arhāḥ strīkumāryaś ca” iti | tadbhartṛkulalabdheḥ prattāsu duhitṛṣu | tatra prattāviṣaye prabhūtatame mānavam |
jananyāṃ saṃsthitāyāṃ tu samaṃ sarve sahodarāḥ |
bhajeran mātṛkaṃ riktaṃ bhaginyaś ca sanābhayaḥ ||
yās tv āsāṃ syur duhitaras tāsām api yathārhataḥ |
mātāmahyā dhanāt kiṃcid pradeyaṃ prītipūrvakam || iti | [MDh 9.192–193]
tatraivālpe dhane bārhaspatyam |
strīdhanaṃ tadapatyānāṃ duhitṝṇāṃ tadāśinī |
aprattā cet samūḍhā sā labheta tu samātṛkam || iti || 28.22 ||
bhaginīśulkaḥ sodaryāṇām ūrdhvaṃ mātuḥ || 28.23 ||
bhaginīpradānanimittaṃ pitrā yad gṛhītaṃ dravyam āsurārṣavivāhayos tasmin mṛte tasyā bhaginyā eva sodaryā bhrātaras teṣāṃ bhavati | tac ca mātur ūrdhvaṃ jīvantyāṃ mātari tasyā eva na tu mṛtasya pitur etat svam iti | tatra ye bhāgino bhinnodarā bhrātaro mātṛsapatnī ceti te sarve ‘ṃśaṃ na gṛhṇīyur iti | yatra vivāhasamaye bhartrādikulena bhaginyādyau dattam ābharaṇakṣetrādikaṃ tat tasyā eva | mṛtāyāṃ ca tasyām aprajasi yājñavalkyenoktam |
bandhudattaṃ tathā śulkam anvādheyakam eva ca |
aprajāyām atītāyāṃ bāndhavās tad avāpnuyuḥ ||
yena yad dattaṃ sa tad avāpnuyād iti | satyāṃ tu prajāyāṃ saiva gṛhṇīyād iti || 28.23 ||
pūrvaṃ caike || 28.24 ||
prāg api mātur maraṇād bhaginīśulkaṃ sodaryāṇāṃ bhavatīty eke manyante | tasyā vṛttāpekṣo vikalpaḥ || 28.24 ||
asaṃsṛṣṭivibhāgaḥ pretānāṃ jyeṣṭhasya || 28.25 ||
asaṃsṛṣṭino vibhaktabhrātaraḥ | vibhaktavyo vibhāgaḥ | asaṃsṛṣṭināṃ vibhāgo ‘saṃsṛṣṭivibhāgaḥ | pretānām ity etad upasarjanībhūtānām apy asaṃsṛṣṭināṃ viśeṣaṇam | anapatyasya ceti vartate | asaṃsṛṣṭināṃ vibhaktānām anapatyānāṃ bhrātṝṇāṃ pretānāṃ yo vibhāgo vibhaktavyo dhanādiḥ sa jyeṣṭhasya bhrātur bhavati netareṣāṃ bhrātṝṇāṃ nāpi patnyā na ca pitror ity ācāryasya pakṣaḥ | tathā ca śaṅkhalikhita-patiṭhīnasī: “aputrasya svaryātasya bhrātṛgāmi dravyaṃ tadabhāve mātāpitarau haretāṃ patnī vā jyeṣṭhā sagotraśiṣyasya brahmacāriṇaś ca” iti | manus tu |
pitā hared aputrasya rikthaṃ bhrātara eva ca | iti | [MDh 9.185]
devalaś ca |
tato dāyam aputrasya vibhajeran sahodarāḥ |
sakulyā duhitā vāpi dhriyamāṇaḥ pitāpi ca || iti || 28.25 ||
saṃsṛṣṭini prete saṃsṛṣṭī rikthabhāk || 28.26 ||
bhrātrādibhiḥ saṃsṛṣṭaṃ dhanaṃ yasya sa saṃsṛṣṭī sādhāraṇadhano ‘vibhakto vibhajya saṃsṛṣṭaś ca,
vibhakto yaḥ punaḥ pitrā bhrātrā vaikatra saṃvaset |
pitṛvyeṇātha vā prītyā sa tatsaṃsṛṣṭa ucyate ||
iti bārhaspatye darśanāt | anapatyasyeti vartate | saṃsṛṣṭīty anapatye prete tasya rikthaṃ saṃsṛṣṭī bhajet | tatrāpi sodaryeṇāsodaryeṇa ca saṃsṛṣṭe sodarye [sodaryo] bhajet | “sodarasya tu sodaraḥ” [YDh 2.138] iti yājñavalkyadarśanāt | tad evaṃ vibhakte bhrātary anapatye mṛte taddhanaṃ jyeṣṭhasya | asati jyeṣṭha itareṣāṃ bhrātṝṇām | avibhakte tu mṛte tadaṃśaḥ sarveṣāṃ bhrātṝṇām iti || 28.26 ||
vibhaktajaḥ pitryam eva || 28.27 ||
yas tu vibhāgād ūrdhvaṃ jātaḥ putras tasyām anyasyāṃ vā bhāryāyāṃ sa pitryam eva gṛhṇīyāt | vibhāgād ūrdhvaṃ pitrā yad arjitaṃ vibhāgakāle vā gṛhītaṃ tad eva bhajed alpaṃ prabhūtaṃ vā | atra bṛhaspatiḥ |
putraiḥ saha vibhaktena pitrā yat svayam arjitam |
vibhaktajasya tat sarvam anīśāḥ pūrvajāḥ smṛtāḥ || iti |
yadā tu pitur na kiṃcid asti tadā vaiṣṇavam: “pitṛvibhaktā vibhāgottarotpannasya bhāgaṃ dadyuḥ” [ViDh 17.3] iti | yājñavalkyo ‘py āha |
vibhakteṣu suto jātaḥ savarṇāyāṃ vibhāgabhāk |
dṛśyād vā tadvibhāgaḥ syād āyavyayaviśodhitāt || iti | [YDh 2.122]
atra manu-nāradau |
ūrdhvaṃ vibhāgāj jātas tu pitryam eva hared dhanam |
saṃsṛṣṭās tena vā ye ‘sya vibhajeta sa taiḥ saha || iti [MDh 9.216] || 28.27 ||
svayam arjitam avaidyebhyo vaidyaḥ kāmaṃ
na dadyāt || 28.28 ||
vidyām adhīta iti vaidyaḥ | svayam arjitaṃ vidyārahitebhyo bhrātṛbhyaḥ kāmaṃ na dadyāt | adāne ‘pi na pratyavāyo dāne tv abhyudaya iti || 28.28 ||
avaidyāḥ samaṃ vibhajeran || 28.29 ||
yadā tu sarve bhrātaro mūrkhāḥ kṛṣyādinopārjayeyus tadā samaṃ vibhajeran | vaidyenāpi kṛṣyādinā yad arjitaṃ na vidayā labdhaṃ yadi pitṛdravyāvirodhi tatra sāmyam eva | tatra sūtradvayam api caitad bhrātṛviṣayam eva | pitari tu jīvati viduṣāviduṣā vāvibhaktenārjitaṃ pitur eva |
bhāryā putraś ca dāsaś ca traya evādhanāḥ smṛtāḥ |
yat te samadhigacchanti yasyaite tasya tad dhanam || [MDh 8.416]
iti manuḥ || 28.29 ||
ācāryeṇa putrā rikthaṃ bhajerann ity uktaṃ tatraurasā eva putrā iti saṃpratyayo mā bhūd ity āha |
putrā aurasakṣetrajadattakṛtrimagūḍhotpannāpaviddhā
rikthabhājaḥ || 28.30 ||
auraso dharmapatnījaḥ | atra yājñavalkyaḥ |
aputreṇa parakṣetre niyogotpāditaḥ sutaḥ |
ubhayor apy asau rikthī piṇḍadātā ca dharmataḥ || iti | [YDh 2.127]
ayam evotpādayitur na bījinaś ca bhartuḥ | dattaviṣaye vasiṣṭhaḥ: “na jyeṣṭhaṃ putraṃ dadyāt pratigṛhṇīyād vā | sa hi santānāya pūrveṣām | na strī putraṃ dadyāt pratigṛhṇīyād vānyatrānujñānād bhartuḥ | putraṃ pratigrahīṣyan bandhūn āhūya rājani vāvedya niveśanasya madhye vyāhṛtibhir hutvādūre bāndhavasaṃnikṣṭam eva pratigṛhṇīyāt” [VaDh 15.3–6] iti | sa dattaḥ | kṛttrimaviṣaye manuḥ |
sadṛśaṃ tu prakuryād yaṃ guṇadoṣavivarjitam |
putraṃ putraguṇair yuktaṃ sa vijñeyas tu kṛttrimaḥ |
utpadyate gṛhe yasya na ca jñāyeta kasyacit |
sa gṛhe gūḍha utpannas tasya syād yasya talpajaḥ ||
mātāpitṛbhyām utsṛṣṭaṃ tayor anyatareṇa vā |
yaṃ putraṃ pratigṛhṇīyād apaviddhas tu sa smṛtaḥ || iti | [MDh 9.169–171]
ṣaḍ ete rikthabhājaḥ putrāḥ || 28.30 ||
kānīnasahoḍhapaunarbhavaputrikāputrasvayaṃdatta-
krītā gotrabhājaḥ || 28.31 ||
pitṛveśmani kanyā tu yaṃ putraṃ janayed iha |
taṃ kānīnaṃ vaden nāmnā voḍhuḥ kanyāsamudbhavam || iti | [MDh 9.172]
atra vasiṣṭhaḥ |
aprattā duhitā yasya putraṃ vindeta tulayataḥ |
pautrī mātāmahas tena dadyāt piṇḍaṃ hared dhanam || iti | [VaDh 17.23]
yājñavalkyaḥ |
kānīnaḥ kanyakājāto mātāmahasuto yataḥ || iti | [YDh 2.129]
tatrāprattāyām eva mṛtāyāṃ mātāmahasya putraḥ pautro vā | ūḍhāyāṃ voḍhuḥ | atra manuḥ |
yā garbhiṇī saṃskriyate jñātājñātāpi vā satī |
voḍuḥ sa garbho bhavati sahoḍha iti cocyate ||
yā patyā vā parityaktā vidhavā vā svecchayā |
utpādayed punarbhūtvā sa paunarbhava ucyate || [MDh 9.173, 175]
putrikāputraḥ pūrvam evoktaḥ | manuḥ |
mātāpitṛvihīno yas tyakto vā syād akāraṇāt |
ātmānaṃ sparśayed yasmai svayaṃdattas tu sa smṛtaḥ ||
krīṇīyād yas tv apatyārthaṃ mātāpitror yam antikāt |
sa krītakaḥ sutas tasya sadṛśo ‘sadṛśo ‘pi vā || iti | [MDh 9.177,174]
ete tu gotrabhājo gotram eva kevalaṃ bhajante na riktham | pūrve tu rikthabhājo gotrabhājaś caurasena sahābhidhānāt | sarve caite sajātīyāḥ,
sajātīyeṣv ayaṃ proktas tanayeṣu mayā vidhiḥ | [YDh 2.133]
iti yājñavalkyavacanāt || 28.31 ||
caturthāṃśina aurasādyabhāve || 28.32 ||
atha vā naite kānīnādayo na rikthabhājaḥ kiṃ tu caturthāṃśinaḥ | pitṛdhanasya caturtham aṃśaṃ bhajeran | pūrvoktānāṃ ṣaṇṇām aurasādīnām abhāve | bhāve tu ta eva bhajeran | caturthāṃśavyatiriktaṃ ca sapiṇḍā gṛhṇīyuḥ | yad atra putrikāputrasyaurasādyabhāve ‘pi caturthāṃśabhāktvam uktaṃ tad apakṛṣṭaputrikāputraviṣayam | yo hīnavarṇāyā bhāryāyā duhitaraṃ putrikāṃ karoti tatrāpy abhisaṃdhimātreṇa tatputraviṣayam ity arthaḥ | atra manuḥ |
putrikāyāṃ kṛtāyāṃ tu yadi putro ‘nu jāyate |
samas tatra vibhāgaḥ syāj jyeṣṭhatā nāsti hi striyaḥ || [MDh 9.134]
iti |
ṣaṣṭhaṃ tu kṣetrajasyāṃśaṃ pradadyāt paitṛkād dhanāt |
auraso vibhajan dāyaṃ pitryaṃ pañcamam eva vā ||
aurasakṣetrajau putrau pitṛrikthasya bhāginau |
daśāpare tu kramaśo gotrarikthāṃśabhāginaḥ || [MDh 9.164–165]
iti ca | atra dattaputragrahaṇānantaraṃ vasiṣṭhaḥ: “yasmiṃś cet pratigṛhīta aurasaḥ putra utpadyate caturthabhāgabhāgī syāt” iti | atra kātyāyanaḥ |
utpanne tv aurase putre tṛtīyāṃśaharāḥ sutāḥ |
savarṇā asavarṇās tu grāsācchādanabhāginaḥ || iti |
atra bṛhaspatiḥ |
eka evaurasaḥ pitrye dhane svāmī prakīrtitaḥ |
tattulyaḥ putrikāputro bhartavyās tv apare smṛtāḥ ||
kṣetrajādyāḥ sutās tv anye pañcaṣaṭsaptabhāginaḥ || iti |
hārītaḥ: “vibhajiṣyamāṇa ekaviṃśaṃ kānīnāya dadyād, viṃśaṃ paunarbhavāyaikonaviṃśaṃ dvyāmuṣyāyaṇāyāṣṭādaśaṃ kṣetrajāya saptadaśaṃ putrikāputrāyetarān aurasāya” iti | yājñavalkyo dvādaśa putrān anukramyāha |
piṇḍado ‘ṃśaharaś caiṣāṃ pūrvābhāve paraḥ paraḥ | iti | [YDh 2.132]
manur api |
śreyasaḥ śreyaso ‘bhāve yavīyān riktham arhati | iti | [MDh 9.184]
nārado ‘pi |
kramād ete pravartante mṛte pitari taddhane |
jyāyaso jyāyaso ‘bhāve jaghanyas tad avāpnuyāt || iti |
vasiṣṭho ‘pi: “yasya tu pūrveṣāṃ ca na kaścid dāyādaḥ syād ete tasya dāyaṃ hareyuḥ” [VaDh 17.39] iti |
atraurasaḥ putrikāputraḥ kṣetrajaḥ kānīno guḍhotpanno ‘paviddhaḥ sahoḍhaḥ paunarbhavo dattaḥ svayamupāgataḥ kṛtakaḥ krīta iti krameṇa putrān abhidhāya devalaḥ |
ete dvādaśa putrās tu santatyartham udāhṛtāḥ |
ātmajāḥ parajāś caiva labdhā yādṛcchikās tathā ||
teṣāṃ ṣaḍ bandhudāyādāḥ pūrvaṃ ye pitur eva ṣaṭ |
viśeṣaś cāpi putrāṇām ānupūrvyād viśiṣyate ||
sarve ‘py anaurasasyaite putrā dāyaharāḥ smṛtāḥ |
aurase punar utpanne teṣu jyaiṣṭhyaṃ na gacchati ||
teṣāṃ savarṇā ye putrās te tṛtīyāṃśabhāginaḥ |
hīnāḥ samupajīveyur grāsācchādanasaṃbhṛtāḥ || iti |
bandhudāyādā iti bandhūnāṃ sapiṇḍānām apy ete dāyaṃ hareyur na kevalaṃ pitur eva | itare pitur eveti | eṣa eva smṛtyantareṣv api bandhudāyādaśabdasyārthaḥ | tad evam –
aurasaḥ putrikā bījikṣetriṇau putrikāsutaḥ |
paunarbhavaś ca kānīnaḥ sahoḍho gūḍhasaṃbhavaḥ ||
dattakrītasvayaṃdattāḥ kṛttrimaś cāpaviddhakaḥ |
yatra kva cotpāditaś ca putrākhyā daśa pañca ca ||
anenaiva krameṇaiṣāṃ pūrvābhāve paraḥ paraḥ |
piṇḍado ‘ṃśaharaś ceti yuktā guṇavaśā sthitiḥ || iti || 28.32 ||
uktaḥ savarṇaputrāṇāṃ vibhāgaḥ | atha kramavivāheṣv asavarṇāputreṣu viśeṣam āha |
brāhmaṇasya rājanyāputro jyeṣṭho guṇasaṃpannas
tulyabhāk || 28.33 ||
brāhmaṇasya rājanyāyāṃ jātaḥ putro yadi guṇasaṃpanno jyeṣṭhaś ca bhavati tadā brāhmaṇīputreṇa yavīyasā tulyabhāk | ekasya vayasā jyaiṣṭhyam aparasya jātyeti || 28.33 ||
jyeṣṭhāṃśahīnam anyat || 28.34 ||
viṃśatibhāgo jyeṣṭhasyetyādir ya uddhāraḥ pūrvam uktas tadvyatiriktam anyad vibhajeteti prakaraṇād gamyate | guṇahīne jyeṣṭhe ca rājanyāputre mānavam |
sarvaṃ vā rikthajātaṃ tad daśadhātra vibhajya tu |
dharmyaṃ vibhāgaṃ kurvīta vidhānena tu dharmavit ||
caturo ‘ṃśān hared vipras trīn aṃśān kṣatriyāsutaḥ |
vaiśyāputro hared dvyaṃśam ekaṃ śūdrāsuto haret || iti [MDh 9.152–153]
|| 28.34 ||
rājanyāvaiśyāputrasamavāye yathā sa
brāhmaṇīputreṇa || 28.35 ||
yadā brāhmaṇīputras tu nāste tadā rājanyāputro brāhmaṇīputreṇa samavāye yathā tulyabhāk, evaṃ kṣatriyāputreṇa vaiśyāputras tulyabhāk || 28.35 ||
kṣatriyāc cet || 28.36 ||
cecchabdaś caśabdasyārthe | kṣatriyāc cotpannayoḥ putrayoḥ samavāye vaiśyāputro jyeṣṭho guṇasaṃpannaḥ kṣatriyāputreṇa yavīyasā tulyabhāk | evaṃ vaiśyād utpannasya śūdrāputrasyāpy eke manyante draṣṭavyam iti | nety anye ‘nuktatvāt || 28.36 ||
śūdrāputro ‘py anapatyasya śuśrūṣuś cel labheta
vṛttimūlam antevāsividhinā || 28.37 ||
brāhmaṇasyeti vartate | anapatyasyāvidyamānadvijātiputrasya brāhmaṇasya śūdrāputro ‘pi vṛttimūlaṃ labheta | yāvatā kṛṣyādikarmasamartho bhavati tāval labheta | sa yady antevāsividhinā śuśrūṣur bhavati | yathā śiṣya ācāryaṃ śuśrūṣate tathā śuśrūṣuś ced iti | evaṃ kṣatriyavaiśyayor api śūdrāputro vṛttimūlaṃ labheta || 28.37 ||
savarṇāputro ‘py anyāyyavṛtto na
labhetaikeṣām || 28.38 ||
yas tv anyāyyavṛtto ‘dharmeṇa dravyāṇi pratipādayati veśyādibhyaḥ prayacchati [sa] savarṇāputro ‘py apiśabdāj jyeṣṭho ‘pi dāyaṃ na labhetety ekeṣāṃ matam | tathā ca āpastambaḥ: “yas tv adharmeṇa dravyāṇi pratipādayati jyeṣṭho ‘pi tam abhāgaṃ kurvīta” [ĀpDh 2.14.15] iti || || 28.38 ||
śrotriyā brāhmaṇasyānapatyasya rikthaṃ
bhajeran || 28.39 ||
apatyagrahaṇaṃ piṇḍagotrarṣisaṃbandhāder upalakṣaṇam | anapatyasyāvidyamānadhanabhājo brāhmaṇasya śrotriyā hi rikthaṃ bhajeran || 28.39 ||
rājetareṣām || 28.40 ||
itareṣāṃ kṣatriyādīnāṃ riktham anapatyānāṃ rājā bhajeta || 28.40 ||
jaḍaklībau bhartavyau || 28.41 ||
jaḍo naṣṭacittaḥ | klības tṛtīyāprakṛtiḥ | etāv aśanācchādanadānena bhartavyau | manus tu |
anaṃśau klībapatitau jātyandhabadhirau tathā |
unmattajaḍamūkāś ca ye ca kecin nirindriyāḥ || iti [MDh 9.201] || 28.41 ||
apatyaṃ jaḍasya bhāgārham || 28.42 ||
yadi tu jaḍasyāpatyaṃ bhavati tadā tadbhāgārhaṃ bhavati | tasmai sa bhāgo deyas tatpituḥ | atra manuḥ |
yady arthitā tu dāraiḥ syāt klībādīnāṃ kathaṃcana |
teṣām utpannatantūnām apatyaṃ dāyam arhati || iti [MDh 9.203] || 28.42 ||
śūdrāputravat pratilomās tu || 28.43 ||
prātilomyena jātānāṃ sūtādīnām api guṇotkṛṣṭānāṃ śūdrāputravad vṛttimūlaṃ dātavyam iti
|| 28.43 ||
udakayogakṣemakṛtānneṣv avibhāgaḥ || 28.44 ||
udakaṃ kūpādi | yogakṣemāv iṣṭāpūrte | tathā ca laugākṣiḥ |
yogaḥ pūrtaṃ kṣema iṣṭā ity āhus tattvadarśinaḥ |
avibhājye tu te prokte śayanaṃ cānnam eva ca || iti |
kṛtānne tūtsavādiṣu kalpite prabhūte ‘pi | eteṣu vibhāgo na kartavyaḥ | yathāvasthiteṣv eva sodaryānurūpena bhogaḥ || 28.44 ||
strīṣu ca saṃyuktāsu || 28.45 ||
yāś ca striyo dāsyo bhrātrādiṣu kenacit saṃyuktā upabhogaparigṛhītās tās tasyaiva | yady anyāḥ santy anyatrānyeṣāṃ bhāgaḥ | yadi na santi tadā dravyeṇa sāmyam āpādanīyam | yadā punar ekaiva dāsyasaṃyuktā ca tadā paryāyeṇa karma karotu || 28.45 ||
anājñāte daśāvaraiḥ śiṣṭair ūhavidbhir alubdhaiḥ
praśastaṃ kāryam || 28.46 ||
jñāyata ivājñātam | tadviparītam anājñātam | yo ‘rtho yathāvad avijñātaḥ saṃdigdho vā tatrānājñāte daśāvarair daśabhyo ‘nyūnaiḥ śiṣṭaiḥ |
dharmeṇādhigato yais tu vedaḥ saparibṛṃhaṇaḥ |
te śiṣṭā brāhmaṇā jñeyāḥ śrutipratyakṣahetavaḥ || [MDh 12.109]
iti manunoktaiḥ | ūhavidbhir ūhāpohakuśalaiḥ | alubdhair utkocādiṣu niḥspṛhaiḥ | evaṃbhūtair brāhmaṇair yat praśastaṃ stutam idam atra yuktam iti tat kāryaṃ kartuṃ yuktam || 28.46 ||
ke punas te daśāvarās tān āha |
catvāraś caturṇāṃ pāragā vedānāṃ prāg uttamāt traya
āśramiṇaḥ pṛthag dharmavidas traya etān daśāvarān
pariṣad ity ācakṣate || 28.47 ||
caturṇāṃ vedānāṃ pāragāḥ sāṅgānām adhyetāro ‘rthajñāś ca | evaṃbhūtās catvāro na cāturvaivya ekaḥ | āśramiṇas tṛtīye ‘dhyāya uktā brahmacārī gṛhastho bhikṣur vāikhānasa iti | teṣūttamād vaikānasāt pūrve traya āśramiṇaḥ | pṛthag dharmaśāstravidas trayaḥ | pṛthaggrahaṇam ekam eva dharmaśāstraṃ viduṣāṃ trayāṇāṃ grahaṇaṃ mā bhūd iti | tān etān daśāvarān pariṣad ity ācakṣate dharmajñāḥ || 28.47 ||
asaṃbhave tv eteṣāṃ śrotriyo vedavic chiṣṭo
vipratipattau yad āha || 28.48 ||
eteṣāṃ vyastānāṃ samastānāṃ ca bahūnām asaṃbhave śrotriyaḥ sāṅgasya vedasyādhyetā | vedavit tadarthajñaḥ | śiṣṭaḥ svadharmanirataḥ | evaṃbhūtaḥ eko ‘pi vipratipattiviṣaye yad āhedam atra yuktam idaṃ kāryam iti tat kāryam | tathā ca manuḥ |
eko ‘pi vedavid dharmaṃ yaṃ vyavasyet samāhitaḥ |
sa dharmaḥ paramo jñeyo nājñānām udito ‘yutaiḥ || iti | [MDh 12.113]
|| 28.48 ||
kasmāt punar ekasyāpi śrotriyasya vedavidaḥ śiṣṭasya vacanaṃ kartavyam ity ata āha |
yato ‘yam aprabhavo bhūtānāṃ hiṃsānugrahayogeṣu
|| 28.49 ||
prabhavaty asmād iti prabhavaḥ kāraṇam | tan na vidyate yasya so ‘prabhavaḥ | yasmād ayaṃ bhūtānāṃ hiṃsānugrahayogeṣu daṇḍaprāyaścittādiṣv agṛhyamāṇakāraṇaḥ kevalaṃ śāstranetras tasmād ekasyāpi vacanam anuṣṭheyam iti | apara āha – prabhavanaṃ prabhavaḥ prabhutvaṃ tad yasya nāsti so ‘prabhavaḥ | na hy asau śāstranirapekṣaḥ svatantraḥ kiṃcid anugṛhṇāti nigṛhṇāti vā | tasmād asya vacanam anuṣṭheyam iti || 28.49 ||
sāṃprathaṃ ye kevalaṃ dharmam anutiṣṭhanti tebhyo jñātvānutiṣṭhan viśiṣṭa ity āha |
dharmiṇāṃ viśeṣeṇa svargaṃ lokaṃ dharmavid
āpnoti jñānābhiniveśābhyām || 28.50 ||
dharmiṇo dharmavanto dhārmikāḥ | teṣāṃ madhye yo dharmavid dharmaśāstraṃ yāvato ‘rthato ‘dhītya dharmaṃ tattvato vetti saḥ | jñānābhiniveṣābhyām | jñānaṃ samarthāvagatiḥ abhiniveśas tātparyeṇānuṣṭhānam | jñānenābhiniveśena na kevalānuṣṭhātṛbhyo viśeṣeṇa svargaṃ lokam āpnoti || 28.50 ||
iti dharmo dharmaḥ || 28.51 ||
so ‘yam ādito “vedo dharmamūlam” (GDh 1.1) dharmamūlam ity ārabhyaivamanto dharmaḥ uktaḥ | dviruktiḥ śāstraparisamāptyarthā || 28.51 ||
gautamokte dharmaśāstre haradattakṛtāv iha |
aṣṭāviṃśo ‘yam adhyāyo vṛttau dāyaḥ samāpitaḥ ||
iti śrīgautamīyavṛttau haradattaviracitāyāṃ mitākṣarāyāṃ
tṛtīyapraśne daśamo ‘dhyāyaḥ ||
samāpto ‘yaṃ granthaḥ ||
[1] This sutra is omitted in the commentaries of both Maskarin and Haradatta. But evidently, Haradatta knew this sutra, indicating that the commentaries we have may be defective.
